### [1] [Check Your Blind Spots: Decision-Guided Perception by Probing the Planner with Hypothetical Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23030)
*Hoang Pham Cong,Long D. Nguyen,Ho Viet Duc Luong,Nguyen Tran Minh Nhat,Binh P. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DGSP将低分辨率感知结果与其动作视为"草稿",通过向规划器插入假想物体探测决策敏感区域,仅对这些区域及当前动作所依赖的检测进行高分辨率验证,以约四分之一像素量复现全帧高分辨率感知的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应分辨率感知依据图像线索分配高分辨率计算,但图像线索既无法可靠指向低分辨率遗漏的目标,也无法判断哪些目标对规划器真正重要。作者观察到:在未观测前,规划器即可判断某位置上的物体是否会改变其动作。

Method: 提出决策引导的推测式感知(DGSP):先用低分辨率场景及其动作作为草稿,通过在位置插入假想物体并探测规划器求得"决策敏感场"(未见物体将改变动作的位置),再仅对该场与支撑集(当前动作所依赖的检测)做高分辨率验证,迭代至动作稳定。

Result: 对两类规划器证明:若裁剪检测与全帧一致且裁剪覆盖决策敏感场,DGSP即精确返回全帧高分辨率感知的动作;真实图像近似满足该条件。在三个驾驶数据集上,约98%帧复现该动作,仅用约四分之一像素;在同等像素代价下,较均匀降采样修复更多草稿分歧、更少误刹,并找回更多草稿遗漏的决策相关物体。

Conclusion: 以决策相关性而非图像显著性分配计算资源,可使感知在显著降低像素开销的同时保持规划动作一致,为感知-规划联合的高效计算提供理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Not every perception error changes the planner's action. Yet adaptive-resolution perception chooses where to spend high-resolution computation from image cues, which can neither reliably point to an object that low resolution missed nor tell which objects matter to the planner. We observe that the planner can say where an object would matter before anything is seen: inserting a hypothetical object at a location tests whether its action would change. Decision-Guided Speculative Perception (DGSP) treats the low-resolution scene and its action as a draft and probes the planner in this way to obtain a decision-sensitivity field, the locations where an unseen object would change the action. Only this field and the support, the detections on which the current action rests, are verified at high resolution, repeatedly until the action is stable. For two planner classes, we prove that DGSP returns exactly the action of whole-frame high-resolution perception if crop detections match whole-frame ones and the crops cover the field, conditions that real images meet approximately. On three driving datasets, it reproduces this action on about 98% of frames with about a quarter of the pixels. At the pixel cost of uniform downscaling, it repairs more of the draft's disagreements with this action, brakes falsely less often, and recovers many more of the decision-relevant objects that the draft misses.

</details>


### [2] [Know When to Zoom: How Small Is Too Small for Vision-Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22709)
*Siddharth Vohra,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究量化了在线视觉语言模型在大图上丢失小目标细节的程度，并证明分块（tiling）与两步缩放能显著恢复细节：tiling 为 Claude/Gemini 提升 11.1–14.5 个百分点，并使 GPT-6.1 Sol 从 50.7% 升至 83.7%。作者提出以模型自身"不确定回复"作为触发缩放的可靠信号，并总结出五条 agent 缩放规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线视觉语言模型服务在大图输入时会压缩或直接拒收图片，导致模型尚未看到图像就已丢失小目标信息（如 8000×6000 无人机照片中 20 像素宽的汽车在 Claude 端仅剩约 6 像素，OpenAI 默认设置直接拒收）。论文希望系统测量这种细节损耗，并寻找可廉价恢复细节的策略。

Method: 跨四家厂商七个模型进行实证测量：(1) 用印刷码"视力表"测定模型可辨识的最小文字尺寸；(2) 用 601 个关于真实照片中小目标的问题评估任务准确率；(3) 对比默认设置、将图像切分为最多 9 块、以及两步缩放（模型先指定所需区域再放大）三种策略的准确率与图像 token 消耗；(4) 分析细节丢失时模型"拒答/不确定"与"自信错误"的行为差异；(5) 检验调用前的细节尺寸预判检查与基于模型不确定回复的触发式 tiling。

Result: 默认设置下模型半数可读的最小文字从 6 像素以下增至 10–13 像素（7680×4320 照片）；tiling 使 Claude 与 Gemini 准确率提升 11.1–14.5 个百分点，并因规避拒收使 GPT-6.1 Sol 从 50.7% 升至 83.7%；两步缩放以约 40% 的图像 token 达到或超过 tiling（Claude 上差 2.5 点以内）。细节丢失时两模型约一半情况下承认无法判断，但 Gemini 在其余情况有 43%–48% 给出自信错误答案，Claude 仅 5%–9%。基于细节尺寸的调用前检查无法有效捕获 tiling 收益，而"仅在不确定回复后 tiling"仅比始终 tiling 低 1.4 点，且只用 62%–70% 的 token。

Conclusion: 大图细节损耗可量化且可通过缩放策略大幅补偿；模型自报的不确定性是比尺寸预判更可靠的缩放触发信号。作者据此提出五条"agent 何时应缩放"的规则，并公开视力表、全部模型回答与代码。

Abstract: Online vision-language models often miss small details in large images, because the service shrinks the image, or rejects it, before the model ever sees it. A car 20 pixels wide in an 8000 by 6000 drone photo reaches Claude about 6 pixels wide, and OpenAI's default rejects the photo outright. We measure how much detail survives for seven models from four vendors, using an eye exam of printed codes and 601 questions about small objects in real photos. The smallest text a model reads half the time grows from 6 pixels or less on a small image to a predicted 10 to 13 pixels on a 7680 by 4320 photo at default settings. Zooming wins much of the detail back. Splitting each photo into up to nine tiles raises accuracy by 11.1 to 14.5 percentage points for Claude and Gemini, and by avoiding rejections it lifts GPT-6.1 Sol from 50.7% to 83.7%. A two-step zoom, in which the model first names the region it needs, matches or beats tiles on Gemini and comes within 2.5 points of them on Claude, with about 40% of their image tokens. When a detail is lost, Claude and Gemini both say they cannot tell about half the time, but they fail differently in the rest: Gemini gives a confident wrong answer in 43% to 48% of these cases, Claude in only 5% to 9%. A check based on detail size, run before the call, misses most of the gain from tiles on Claude and Gemini, but Claude's own reply is a reliable trigger: tiling only after an unsure reply comes within 1.4 points of always tiling with 62% to 70% of the image tokens. We turn these findings into five rules for when an agent should zoom, and release the eye exam, every model answer and code at https://siddvoh.com/know-when-to-zoom.

</details>


### [3] [Motion-Aware Reward Modeling for Instruction-Guided Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22495)
*Li Li,Xiangbo Gao,Bo Ni,Samyadeep Basu,Viet Dac Lai,Jing Shi,Seunghyun Yoon,Gang Wu,Yu Wang,Thien Huu Nguyen,Tyler Derr,Franck Dernoncourt,Zhengzhong Tu,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示视频编辑奖励模型存在"运动坍缩"（motion collapse）失效模式：在97%情况下偏好被抑制运动的候选，且潜在优化与奖励引导微调会利用该漏洞。作者提出显式建模帧间动力学的运动感知奖励模型，将选择运动抑制候选的比例降到0%，并与人类评分达到0.57的Spearman相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令引导视频编辑的奖励模型倾向于以牺牲运动为代价换取时序一致性，导致"运动坍缩"。现有学习的视频奖励与VLM评判对轻度运动抑制也不敏感，且该缺陷会被潜在优化和奖励引导微调放大（奖励随运动减少而上升）；基线奖励骨干的线性探针在轻度抑制下接近随机水平，说明缺乏显式帧间动态信息。

Method: 构建受控的运动抑制变体以研究该失效；提出运动感知奖励模型：由RGB帧差流显式编码帧间动态，并结合对"运动抑制负样本"与"伪造运动负样本"的偏好训练；引入蒸馏锚点以保持与人类质量判断的对齐。

Result: 在VE-Bench运动阶梯上，新模型将选择运动抑制候选的比例由97%降至0%，与人类质量评分的Spearman相关达0.57；可检测未见过的抑制变体，并在两个编辑管线的奖励引导微调中保持运动。

Conclusion: 结果支持在视频编辑奖励中显式建模运动，是缓解运动坍缩、提升奖励与人类判断一致性的关键。

Abstract: Reward models for instruction-guided video editing can favor temporal consistency at the expense of motion. We study this failure, termed \emph{motion collapse}, using controlled motion-suppression variants of real edits. On VE-Bench, the evaluated composite reward prefers a motion-suppressed candidate in $97\%$ of cases. Learned video rewards and a VLM judge also struggle with mild suppression, while latent optimization and reward-guided fine-tuning exploit this weakness to increase reward as motion declines. A linear probe of our base reward backbone performs near chance on mild suppression, motivating an explicit inter-frame dynamics input. We propose a motion-aware reward model that combines an RGB frame-difference stream with preference training against motion-suppressed and fabricated-motion negatives. A distillation anchor preserves alignment with human quality judgments. On the VE-Bench motion ladder, our model reduces the rate of selecting motion-suppressed candidates to $0\%$ while achieving a Spearman correlation of $0.57$ with human quality ratings. It also detects unseen suppression variants and preserves motion during reward-guided fine-tuning on two editing pipelines. These results support explicitly modeling motion in rewards for video editing.

</details>


### [4] [LeViT-UNet++: Towards Balanced Fine-Grained Details and Context for Efficient Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22482)
*Guoping Xu,Jinling Zhu,Liuyue Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LeViT-UNet++高效编解码架构，通过Haar小波多尺度下采样(HWMD)与深度可分离卷积解码器，在四类基准上实现精度-效率良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床分割(手术导航、放疗、实时引导)需快速且精确。CNN与Transformer在有限算力下难以兼顾细粒度结构细节与全局上下文，降复杂度常损失精度，形成不利的精度-效率权衡。

Method: 1) HWMD模块将特征分解为低/高频分量，提取多尺度结构信息，缓解下采样细节损失；2) 基于深度可分离卷积的解码器，融合Transformer块的全局多尺度表示与局部空间特征，渐进式精化特征并降低开销；3) 支持2D与3D设置。

Result: 在Synapse、ACDC、LA2018、MSD Heart四个基准(2D/3D)上均具竞争力精度、紧凑参数与快速推理。Synapse上DSC达76.57%，仅8.025M参数、241.72 FPS。

Conclusion: 小波多尺度特征提取与轻量局部-全局特征融合相结合，可有效实现高效医学图像分割；代码已开源。

Abstract: Efficient medical image segmentation is essential for clinical applications requiring fast and accurate delineation, such as surgical navigation, radiation therapy, and real-time image-guided intervention. Despite substantial advances in convolutional neural networks and Transformers, attaining high accuracy under limited computational resources remains difficult, because reducing model complexity often sacrifices fine-grained structural details and global context modeling, yielding an unfavorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. To address this, we propose LeViT-UNet++, an efficient encoder-decoder architecture that better balances local detail preservation and global context modeling. Specifically, we introduce a Haar Wavelet Multi-Scale Downsampling (HWMD) module that decomposes feature representations into low- and high-frequency components to extract multi-scale structural information, thereby mitigating detail loss during downsampling. We further develop a depthwise convolution-based decoder that integrates multi-scale global representations from Transformer blocks with local spatial features, enabling progressive feature refinement while reducing computational overhead. Evaluated on four benchmarks (Synapse, ACDC, LA2018, and MSD Heart) under both 2D and 3D settings, LeViT-UNet++ achieves competitive accuracy with a compact parameter footprint and fast inference. On Synapse, for instance, it attains a DSC of 76.57% with only 8.025M parameters and 241.72 FPS, confirming a favorable accuracy-efficiency balance. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of combining wavelet-based multi-scale feature extraction with lightweight local-global feature fusion for efficient medical segmentation. The source code is available at https://github.com/apple1986/levit_unet_pp.

</details>


### [5] [Benchmarking Deep Learning Models for Wheelchair Configuration Classification in Accessible Automated Vehicle Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21945)
*Samir Ahmed,Shaaz Saleem,Devin Dhami,Wesley Maia,Angel Martinez-Sanchez,Parthib Roy,Jannatul Ferdous Srabonee,Ahmed Sabbir Arif,Ross Greer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轮椅配置图像数据集WCID（467张人脸模糊图像、192个采集组），标注手动/电动二分类、子类型、乘员、视角、场景、光照、遮挡、驱动方式与控制方式，并检验三种CNN骨干与冻结视觉语言探针的跨数据集泛化与高斯模糊鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需服务包括轮椅使用者在内的弱势道路使用者，但轮椅在车架几何、驱动方式与外观上高度个性化，乘员或配件还会遮挡视觉线索；现有辅助出行数据集仅标注"是否出现轮椅"，缺乏对具体配置的描述，无法支撑个性化车辆设计。

Method: 构建WCID数据集：467张人脸模糊图像、192个采集组，每张标注(i)手动/电动二分类、(ii)轮椅子类型、(iii)乘员、视角、场景、光照、遮挡、驱动与控制属性；划分为273张验证集与194张测试集（仅用于评测）。在外部门控图像数据集上训练三种CNN骨干与一个冻结视觉语言探针，在WCID上评估跨数据集泛化，并用高斯模糊测试鲁棒性。

Result: 卷积模型在清晰图像上表现良好，但在强模糊条件下性能明显下降，距离实际部署仍有差距。

Conclusion: WCID填补了轮椅"配置级"标注的空白，为心理/配置细粒度识别与跨域泛化提供了纯评测基准；当前模型在退化条件下仍不足，需提升鲁棒性。代码、划分、标签、训练权重与人脸模糊图像已在GitHub (Mi3-Lab/MAID) 开源。

Abstract: Making automated vehicles accessible requires consideration of vulnerable road users, including wheelchair users. To offer a more tailored experience that supports independence for this group, vehicle design needs to accommodate the wide range of wheelchair configurations. However, wheelchairs are individualized, varying in frame geometry, propulsion, and visual appearance. Moreover, occupants or accessories may obscure visual cues. Existing mobility-aid datasets only label the presence of a wheelchair, rather than describing its specific configuration. To fill this gap, this paper introduces the Wheelchair Configuration Image Dataset (WCID), comprising 467 face-blurred images across 192 capture groups solely for evaluation. Each image is annotated with (i) a two-class label of manual or powered, (ii) a chair subtype, (iii) occupancy, viewpoint, setting, lighting, occlusion, propulsion, and control, with a 273-image validation split and 194-image test split. Three convolutional neural network backbones and a frozen vision-language probe are trained on an external image dataset and evaluated on WCID to measure cross-dataset generalization, while also testing robustness using Gaussian blur. Convolutional models do well on clean images, but performance drops under strong blur, leaving a gap before deployment. Code, split assignments, labels, trained weights, and the face-blurred images is available at: https://github.com/Mi3-Lab/MAID

</details>


### [6] [AR-WAM: efficient streaming world-action modeling for autonomous driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21851)
*Baiting Luo,Shatadal Mishra,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Nejib Ammar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AR-WAM：因果视频骨干+单次前向未来特征条件的流式世界-动作模型，NAVSIM-v1上91.5/94.0 PDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需同时具备预测交通演化与快速响应新观测的能力。视频基础模型具备前者，但重复编码历史与迭代式视频生成代价高昂，难以用于实际驾驶。

Method: 提出AR-WAM：自回归时序建模+单次前向（single-pass）未来条件。视频骨干为因果结构，增量更新视觉上下文并复用历史计算，以观测到的运动作为未来预测条件；不做迭代视频生成，仅一次前向获得未来特征用于条件化轨迹生成；再用强化学习直接优化驾驶奖励以改进策略。

Result: 仅用单前视相机，NAVSIM-v1上监督训练达91.5 PDMS，强化学习后达94.0 PDMS，优于对比的强端到端世界-动作模型；在连续驾驶的动态设置中，监督版AR-WAM跨决策保留视觉历史，优于强世界-动作基线。

Conclusion: 因果视频骨干+单次前向未来特征+RL的组合可在不依赖迭代视频生成的前提下兼顾预测能力与实时效率，并在动态连续驾驶场景中保持优势。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires anticipating how traffic will evolve while responding quickly to new observations. Video foundation models offer the first ability, but repeated history encoding and iterative video generation make them costly to use for driving. We present AR-WAM, an efficient streaming world-action model that combines autoregressive temporal modeling with single-pass future conditioning. Because its video backbone is causal, AR-WAM updates its visual context incrementally, reusing past computation and conditioning future prediction on observed motion. We further hypothesize that future features computed from recent observations can improve generalization in dynamic traffic without iterative video generation. AR-WAM therefore obtains these features in one forward pass and uses them to condition trajectory generation, and reinforcement learning then improves the policy through direct optimization of driving rewards. With a single front camera, AR-WAM achieves 91.5 PDMS after supervised training and 94.0 after reinforcement learning on NAVSIM-v1, outperforming the compared strong end-to-end world-action models. In a dynamic setting of continuous driving, supervised AR-WAM keeps its visual history across decisions and outperforms strong world-action baselines.

</details>


### [7] [EgoHandWAM: Probing the Role of Hand Motion in Egocentric World Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22151)
*Kailin Wang,Haoxiang Jie,Yaoyuan Yan,Zhiyou Heng,Zhaosong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出EgoHandWAM，用当前图像与近期手部状态联合预测三维手腕/指尖轨迹及未来视觉特征（DINOv2冻结编码器，751,065个EgoDex窗口）。手部动作对短期轨迹预测有小幅增益但低估长距离伸展；视觉特征预测收益依赖真实手部历史，迁移到机器人任务无稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究第一人称视角下"手部运动能在多大程度上预示未来画面"，即检验手部状态作为未来视觉预测先验的信息量与实用性边界。

Method: 联合建模手部轨迹（三维手腕与指尖）与未来视觉特征；在751,065个EgoDex窗口上训练，使用冻结DINOv2编码器；设置消融：仅动作模型、加入未来特征监督、打乱跨会话历史、四任务探针（观测上下文+未来位移+跟踪有效性掩码）、以及RoboTwin仿真中用对齐人类样本替换辅助机器人批次。

Result: 两秒手腕终点误差由静止基线60.26 mm降至56.48 mm，但长距离伸展仍被低估；加入未来特征监督对轨迹精度几乎无影响；视觉头在smooth L1上相对复制当前特征改善1.32%，打乱历史后增益消失；四任务探针中位移+掩码降低视觉特征误差3.93%，仅掩码提升甚微，用预测轨迹替代真实位移贡献很小；RoboTwin两任务三个随机种子下，人类样例替换机器人辅助批次未带来一致成功率提升。

Conclusion: 手部状态对短期轨迹预测提供有限但一致的增益，对未来视觉特征的增益高度依赖真实手部历史而非预测轨迹；该信号在仿真机器人任务上的迁移收益不稳定，说明其作为跨域辅助监督的价值仍有待验证。

Abstract: How much does hand motion tell us about what a first-person camera will see next? EgoHandWAM forecasts three-dimensional wrist and fingertip trajectories and a future visual feature from the current image and recent hand states. We train on 751,065 EgoDex windows using a frozen DINOv2 encoder. On held-out development sessions, the action-only model reduces two-second wrist endpoint error from 60.26 to 56.48~mm relative to a stationary forecast. It still underestimates long reaches. Adding future-feature supervision gives nearly identical trajectory accuracy. With the recorded hand history, the visual head reduces smooth L1 error by 1.32\% relative to copying the current feature. Shuffling histories across sessions erases this gain. In a separate four-task probe, adding recorded future displacements to observed context and future tracking-validity masks reduces visual-feature error by 3.93\%. Masks alone give little improvement. A trajectory predicted from the same observations adds little. On two simulated RoboTwin tasks, substituting aligned human examples for an auxiliary batch of robot examples gives no consistent increase in task success across three training seeds.

</details>


### [8] [Retrieval-Augmented Correction for Generalist Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23189)
*Vincenzo Marcianò,Xiaoming Zhang,Michela Antonelli,Sebastien Ourselin,Maria A Zuluaga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRACE 是一种检索增强的分割掩膜修正器：在由已验证病例构成的共享知识库上下文条件下编辑输入掩膜，一次训练即可泛化到多器官，并支持零参数索引扩展至未见解剖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动修正残余结构误差的方法按解剖结构逐一建模，修正器数量随分割目标数线性增长，扩展成本高。

Method: 构建检索增强精修器：在冻结多模态编码器上加轻量形状感知投影头，将嵌入相似度映射为掩膜形状一致性，使知识库检索出可作为形状先验的病例；在多器官语料（紧凑器官、腔体、分支血管、肿瘤病灶）上训练一次。评估含留出数据集迁移（三个留出数据集查询仅由训练数据构建的固定知识库）与留一解剖结构（新器官病例入索引、精修器冻结）实验。

Result: 在 18 个公开数据集（CT、MRI、超声）、9 种精修配置、7 个上游分割器（弱/中/强）上评估。弱档输入 DSC 0.386，GRACE 最多提升 +0.35 DSC；强档输入 DSC 0.932，平均变化较小。向训练与索引中均未出现的成像模态迁移仅部分有效且依赖解剖结构。

Conclusion: 检索条件化精修可作为解剖特异修正模型的实用替代方案；未见模态迁移仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Automated segmentation is routinely used as the first step in quantitative image-based clinical workflows, where predicted masks are subsequently reviewed and corrected by human operators. Automated correction of the residual structural errors is currently addressed one anatomy at a time, so the number of trained correctors scales with the number of segmentation targets. We present GRACE, a retrieval-augmented refiner that edits an input mask under context drawn from a shared knowledge base of verified prior cases. The refiner is trained once on a multi-organ corpus spanning compact organs, cavities, branching vessels, and tumour lesions. In the held-out dataset-transfer evaluation, three held-out datasets query a fixed knowledge base built from training data only. A separate leave-one-anatomy-out experiment tests parameter-free index extension: verified cases of an unseen solid organ enter the knowledge base, and the refiner stays frozen. A light shape-aware projection head on a frozen multimodal encoder maps embedding similarity to the shape agreement of the masks, so the knowledge base returns cases that the refiner can use as a shape prior. We evaluate GRACE on 18 public datasets spanning CT, MRI and ultrasound, using nine refinement configurations and seven upstream segmentors that span weak, mid, and strong operating regimes. We report each change with the DSC of its input mask. On the weak tier, the input DSC is 0.386 and GRACE adds up to +0.35 DSC. On a strong segmentor, the input DSC is 0.932 and the mean change is small. Transfer to an imaging modality that is absent from training and from the index is partial and depends on the anatomy, and remains an open research direction. Our results suggest retrieval-conditioned refinement is a practical alternative to anatomy-specific correction models. Code: https://github.com/robustml-eurecom/grace

</details>


### [9] [Say Less, Hallucinate Less? Diagnosing and Restoring Object Coverage in Preference-Optimized Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23179)
*Hansang Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 偏好优化（PO）虽降低目标幻觉，但会削弱目标覆盖；通过EOS logit偏置的提前停止可复现58–86%的PO增益，并提出免训练的基座引导目标类别恢复方法，在保持低幻觉率的同时恢复召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PO方法以降低幻觉为目标，但作者发现四个公开的LLaVA-1.5-7B PO检查点在降低幻觉的同时也降低了目标类别覆盖（命名的类别更少），且CHAIAR上的集合级目标F1并未显著提升，因此需要厘清PO增益中有多少可由基座模型自身实现，并寻求兼顾召回与幻觉的方法。

Method: 1) 用正EOS logit偏置让基座模型提前停止，使其召回达到各PO模型水平，量化PO增益的可复现比例；2) 分析负EOS偏置（延长生成）能否找回丢失类别；3) 提出base-guided object-category restoration：无需训练，将高置信度的"仅基座命中"类别追加到PO字幕中，阈值在开发集上固定。

Result: 提前停止在两个不相交COCO集上复现了三个检查点CHAIAR指标增益的58%–86%；延长生成无法找回丢失类别，负EOS偏置仅恢复2%–9%的丢失召回，且94%–96%的新增目标提及是重复已命名类别；恢复方法使三个检查点在留出COCO集上将召回提升至距基座1个百分点以内，且distinct-object幻觉率低于基座；相对召回匹配的基座停止曲线，RLAIF-V在近基座召回处、RLAIF-V与CHAIR-DPO在中等召回处增益成立。

Conclusion: PO带来的部分收益可通过基座模型提前停止复现，其代价是目标覆盖下降；基座字幕仍保留约一半被漏掉的类别，据此提出的免训练恢复方法可在不提高幻觉的前提下弥补召回损失。作者将开源召回受控评估代码。

Abstract: Preference optimization (PO) is the standard way to reduce object hallucination in large vision-language models. We find that four public PO checkpoints of LLaVA-1.5-7B also lower object coverage. They name fewer object categories, and their lower hallucination rates bring no significant gain in set-level object F1 on CHAIR. We therefore ask how much of the PO gain the base model can reproduce by stopping earlier. A positive end-of-sequence (EOS) logit bias lets the base model reach the recall of each PO model, and this early stopping alone reproduces 58 to 86 percent of the CHAIRi gain of three checkpoints on two disjoint COCO sets. Writing longer does not bring the lost categories back. Under greedy decoding, a negative EOS bias recovers only 2 to 9 percent of the lost CHAIR recall of these checkpoints, and 94 to 96 percent of the added object mentions repeat categories already named. On CHAIR, the base caption of the same image still names about half of the missed object categories. We therefore propose base-guided object-category restoration, which appends high-confidence base-only categories to the PO caption without training. With thresholds fixed on a development set, it brings three checkpoints within one point of the base recall on a held-out COCO set with a lower distinct-object hallucination rate than the base model. Against the recall-matched base stopping curve, its gain holds for RLAIF-V near the base recall and for RLAIF-V and CHAIR-DPO at an intermediate recall. We will release the code of the recall-controlled evaluation.

</details>


### [10] [Attribution Through the Decoder: Prediction-Space Explanations for Structured Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23161)
*Zaer Bin Zaqy,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 预测角度、视线方向与旋转的网络并不直接输出这些量，而是输出经固定解码器（如归一化）映射后的原始坐标。常规归因方法作用于原始坐标，会把解码器丢弃的变化（如向量重缩放）错误归因。作者提出"通过解码器微分"进行归因，对归一化解码器即化为缩放切向投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始坐标归因会给对最终预测无影响的输入（如仅改变向量模长）分配重要性，产生误导性解释，因此需要与解码器几何一致的归因目标。

Method: 归因通过解码器雅可比（微分）传导，而非环境坐标；分析输出重缩放不变性，证明球面上任意固定光滑标量目标存在切向敏感度为零的点；比较端点投影式Integrated Gradients与直接角度式Integrated Gradients；在MPIIGaze上使用切线Grad-CAM，在PASCAL3D+的6D旋转解码器上使用完整微分归因，并测试双极性切线LayerCAM变体。

Result: 合成任务中环境归因最多将72%重要性分配给无关区域；MPIIGaze上删除10%像素时切线Grad-CAM的预测位移增大44%；PASCAL3D+上完整微分相比环境坐标求和平均删除位移改善14.1度，相比解码器不变标量改善13.8度；双极性切线LayerCAM在循环回归上优于环境求和，但在四元数位姿上未检出差异。

Conclusion: 结果区分了两类失效来源：径向污染与标量化损失。该方法复用现有归因方法但采用解码器感知目标，部分变体需修改归因聚合方式；不变归因仍可能遗漏驱动预测的输入，故不变性与删除性能应分别检验。

Abstract: Networks that predict angles, gaze directions, and rotations do not output these quantities directly: they emit raw coordinates that a fixed decoder, such as normalization, maps to the final prediction. Attribution methods are usually applied to the raw coordinates, so they can credit inputs for changes the decoder discards, such as rescaling a vector when only its direction matters. The resulting explanation can highlight inputs that have no effect on the prediction. We instead attribute through the decoder differential, which for normalization decoders reduces to a scaled tangent projection. We characterize invariance to output rescaling, show that every fixed smooth scalar target on a sphere has points of zero tangential sensitivity, and show that an endpoint-projected variant of Integrated Gradients can still attribute to scale-only inputs, whereas direct angular Integrated Gradients cannot. On synthetic tasks with analytically known relevance, ambient attribution assigns up to 72% of its mass to an irrelevant region. On MPIIGaze validation data, tangent Grad-CAM produces 44% greater prediction displacement under 10% pixel deletion. On PASCAL3D+ with a 6D rotation decoder, the full differential improves mean deletion displacement by 14.1 degrees over the ambient coordinate sum and by 13.8 degrees over a decoder-invariant scalar. Results for a two-polarity tangent LayerCAM variant are mixed: it exceeds ambient-sum LayerCAM on circular regression but no difference is detected on quaternion pose. These results separate two failure sources, radial contamination and scalarization loss. The approach uses existing attribution methods with decoder-aware targets, although some variants also require changes to attribution aggregation. An invariant attribution can still miss inputs that drive the prediction, so invariance and deletion performance should each be checked.

</details>


### [11] [Hierarchical Diffusion Transformer for End-to-End Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22962)
*Xiaoyu Wu,Yifei Wang,Weihang Guo,Chen Wei,Lydia E Kavraki,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Steven Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分层扩散Transformer（HiT），通过在学习中动态合并/拆分视觉token构建隐式层级结构，在像素空间端到端生成任务上显著提升效率与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散可联合学习视觉表征与生成，无需预训练编码器，但现有像素Transformer在整个骨干中保持"每patch一token"，未显式鼓励逐步粗粒化的视觉层级，导致生成质量与训练效率落后于两阶段潜空间扩散模型。

Method: HiT在骨干中渐进地将相关token分组，每组用单一token表示；主Transformer瓶颈处理压缩后的组token，ungrouping将其还原到原始图像位置。引入"压缩预算"约束批内平均压缩率，使分组归属与数量可随图像及扩散时间步变化（训练与生成阶段均适用），从而实现无监督的语义结构涌现。

Result: ImageNet 256×256上FID达1.48；以约40%累计训练FLOPs达到与JiT相当的生成质量。文生图任务中，以约6倍更少的去噪器参数和约3倍更少的前向FLOPs，在五项基准上平均分超过UniLiP。

Conclusion: 在生成器内部学习视觉层级可突破"无外部视觉预训练"的限制，实现更强的图像生成，证明分层token表示是缩小与潜空间扩散差距的关键。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion learns visual representations and image generation jointly in a single end-to-end training stage, without a separately pretrained visual encoder. However, plain pixel-space Transformers still trail leading two-stage latent diffusion models in generation quality and training efficiency. We hypothesize that part of this gap arises from retaining one token per patch throughout the backbone, which does not explicitly encourage a visual hierarchy that summarizes related content at progressively coarser levels. To address this, we introduce Hierarchical Diffusion Transformers (HiT), which progressively group related tokens and represent each group with a single token. The main Transformer bottleneck processes these compact group tokens, and ungrouping restores features to their original image positions. A compression budget regulates the average compression ratio across the batch, allowing group memberships and counts to vary across images and diffusion timesteps during both training and generation. The model exhibits emergent semantic structure, learning to group coherent objects and object parts without segmentation labels. In text-to-image generation, HiT further learns to group caption words with image patches depicting the same concepts, without region annotations. HiT achieves 1.48 FID on ImageNet $256\times256$ and reaches comparable generation quality to JiT using approximately 40\% of its cumulative training FLOPs. For text-to-image generation, HiT exceeds UniLiP's average score across five benchmarks with roughly $6\times$ fewer denoiser parameters and $3\times$ fewer forward FLOPs. These results suggest that learning a visual hierarchy within the generator breaks down the barrier to stronger image generation without external visual pretraining.

</details>


### [12] [Learning Agent-Centric Vision-Language-Action World Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22880)
*Guoqing Wang,Pin Tang,Xiangxuan Ren,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgentWorld提出以"智能体中心离散token"作为世界VLA模型推理与规划的世界状态，替代像素级未来帧生成，通过几何锚定、推理导向SFT与智能体级强化学习学习token，在开环与闭环实验中提升规划性能并提供可解释的世界状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有world VLA模型以像素级未来帧为推理对象，产生大量稠密视觉token，其中仅少部分编码对规划关键的因素（智能体几何、运动动力学、交互），造成性能瓶颈。核心问题：世界VLA应推理何种世界状态才能更好支撑规划？作者主张应采用紧凑、结构化、以智能体为中心、刻画时序交互的潜状态。

Method: 提出AgentWorld——智能体中心的VLA世界模型，使用离散agent token同时用于推理与轨迹规划；每个token动态锚定到周围某个智能体，并关联其语义身份、3D几何、运动与交互上下文。token学习依赖三个过程：(1) 显式几何锚定；(2) 推理导向的监督微调；(3) 智能体导向的强化学习。

Result: 在多样驾驶场景下的开环与闭环实验表明，AgentWorld提升规划能力，充分利用视觉证据与智能体动力学，并生成可解释的智能体中心世界状态。

Conclusion: 面向规划的世界建模应聚焦紧凑、结构化的智能体中心潜状态，而非像素级预测；agent token作为推理与规划的共享接口可行且有效，为world VLA提供可解释的世界表示范式。

Abstract: Recent progress in integrating vision-language-action (VLA) models with world modeling has significantly advanced end-to-end autonomous driving by allowing systems to reason about imagined futures instead of merely reacting to current observations. However, most world VLA models generate future frames at the pixel level with plenty of dense visual tokens. Although such generated futures can be visually plausible, they preserve many driving-irrelevant appearance details, i.e., only a small portion of the tokens directly encode critical planning-relevant factors, such as agent geometry, motion dynamics, and interactions. This introduces a significant performance bottleneck. An important question thus arises: what world state should a world VLA model reason over to support planning beyond pixel-level prediction? We argue that planning-oriented world modeling should focus on a compact and structured agent-centric latent state that captures the interactions between objects in the driving scene over time. As such, we introduce AgentWorld, an agent-centric VLA world model that uses discrete agent tokens jointly for reasoning and trajectory planning. Each token is dynamically grounded to a surrounding agent associated with its object's state, such as semantic identity, 3D geometry, motion, and interaction context. AgentWorld learns these tokens through three key processes: explicit geometric grounding, reasoning-oriented supervised fine-tuning, and agent-oriented reinforcement learning. Extensive open-loop and closed-loop experiments under diverse driving scenarios show that AgentWorld enhances planning capabilities, fully exploits visual evidence and agent dynamics, and yields interpretable agent-centric world states.

</details>


### [13] [Leveraging Segmentation Representations for Efficient Classification: A Self-Configuring Framework Based on nnU-Net](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22380)
*Renjun Cai,Antonio Javier Gallego,Félix Fuentes-Hurtado*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将nnU-Net自配置流程扩展到3D医学图像的联合分割与分类框架，复用分割表示进行分类，并在FLARE 2026 Task 2官方终评中综合排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在3D医学影像中，分割与分类往往被分别建模，训练与部署成本高；作者希望借助nnU-Net的自配置能力，用一套简单高效的框架同时完成两任务，并验证复用分割表示对分类的实际价值。

Method: 框架复用分割模型的编码器特征及其预测的分割概率，交由轻量分类器处理，并比较两种信息共享策略：(1) 冻结主干分支——在特征上拼接感兴趣区域信息、预测前景体积和分割置信度；(2) 适配分支——部分微调编码器末级用于分类，同时加入辅助分割损失以保留分割相关信息。另做五折补充实验考察更复杂配置的影响。

Result: 五折实验显示更复杂的配置并未稳定提升分类性能；公开验证榜Overall得分0.8056；在FLARE 2026 Task 2官方终评中总体排名第一，四项测试任务（含两项隐藏任务）上DSC、NSD和AUROC均为最高；本地推理满足每例60 s、4 GB显存的限制。

Conclusion: 复用分割表示可有效支撑图像级分类，且在保持框架简单高效的前提下取得领先性能，证明了该自配置多任务框架的实用价值。

Abstract: We propose a self-configuring framework that extends the nnU-Net workflow to both segmentation and classification in 3D medical imaging. Building on nnU-Net's self-configuring method, the framework reuses representations learned by a segmentation model for image-level classification. Its encoder features and predicted segmentation probabilities are used by a lightweight classifier. Two information-sharing strategies are investigated. In the frozen-backbone branch, features are augmented with region-of-interest information, predicted foreground volume, and segmentation confidence. In the adaptation branch, the final encoder stage is partially fine-tuned for classification, with an auxiliary segmentation loss to preserve segmentation-related information. Additional five-fold experiments showed that more complex configurations did not consistently improve classification performance. On the public validation leaderboard, our selected framework achieved an Overall score of 0.8056. In the official FLARE 2026 Task 2 final evaluation, our framework ranked first overall, achieving the highest DSC, NSD, and AUROC across all four testing tasks, including two hidden testing tasks. Local inference measurements also remained below the challenge limits of $60 \mathrm{s}$ per case and $4 \mathrm{GB}$ GPU memory. These results demonstrate the practical value of reusing segmentation representations for classification while maintaining a simple and efficient framework.

</details>


### [14] [When AUC Meets Generative Reconstruction: A Unified Principle for Balanced Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22094)
*Boyu Han,Qianqian Xu,Shilong Bao,Zhiyong Yang,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将生成式重建重构为生成输出流形上的判别性排序问题，提出即插即用的AUCGR与pAUCGR目标，在避免损失竞争的同时兼顾判别性与细粒度感知表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM需要既能提取细粒度视觉特征、又能形成语义解耦且具判别性的表示。对比学习通过压制类内变异增强类间区分，生成式重建强调感知保真但缺乏显式比较监督；两者以复合损失朴素聚合时，共享隐空间中的梯度干扰会导致优化冲突与不稳定。

Method: 提出统一的表示学习范式：在生成输出流形内将生成式重建重新表述为判别性排序问题。保留感知重建作为监督信号，并通过对匹配/不匹配重建输出进行系统排序引入基于排序的判别约束，使判别信息嵌入生成过程。具体给出AUCGR（优化ROC曲线下面积，保证匹配对得分高于不匹配对）与pAUCGR（聚焦可靠特征子空间内的不匹配比较以缓解监督偏差）。目标即插即用，无需改动现有生成式重建框架架构。

Result: 理论分析建立了与AUC/pAUC优化的严格联系并给出泛化保证；大量实验验证方法同时提升了判别准确性与细粒度感知表示质量。

Conclusion: 通过将判别监督以排序形式融入生成式重建，可规避复合损失的优化冲突，获得判别性与感知保真兼得的视觉编码器表示，且具备理论支撑与即插即用特性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) require visual encoders that extract fine-grained visual features while constructing semantically disentangled and discriminative representations. Conventional methodologies encounter a fundamental trade-off: contrastive learning enhances inter-class discrimination by suppressing intra-class variability, whereas generative reconstruction emphasizes perceptual fidelity but lacks explicit comparative supervision. The naive aggregation of these objectives via composite loss functions often induces optimization conflicts and instability due to gradient interference within the shared latent space. To resolve this, we introduce a unified representation-learning paradigm that reformulates generative reconstruction as a discriminative ranking problem within the generative output manifold. Our approach maintains perceptual reconstruction as a supervisory signal and integrates a ranking-based discriminative constraint by systematically ordering matched and mismatched reconstruction outputs. This formulation embeds discriminative information seamlessly into the generative process, alleviating the risk of loss competition. Specifically, we propose AUC Generative Reconstruction (AUCGR), which optimizes the area under the ROC curve (AUC) by ensuring higher scores for matched pairs than for mismatched pairs, and Partial AUC Generative Reconstruction (pAUCGR), which addresses supervision bias by focusing on mismatched comparisons within reliable feature subspaces. The proposed objectives are designed to be plug-and-play, requiring no architectural modifications to existing generative reconstruction frameworks. Theoretical analysis establishes rigorous connections to AUC/pAUC optimization and provides generalization guarantees. Extensive experiments validate that our method advances both discriminative accuracy and fine-grained perceptual representation.

</details>


### [15] [Segmentation-Guided Direct Capacity Estimation of Rooftop Photovoltaics from Aerial Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22076)
*Guibin Chen,Emma Fan,Yuzhang Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Direct Head：在 U-Net 分割与软掩码池化基础上直接回归非负光伏容量，避免"面积×功率密度"的耦合，容量预测误差显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网规划需要安装级屋顶光伏容量清单，但并网记录常不完整或在空间上被聚合。传统图像方法先分割光伏阵列、将掩码转成物理面积、再乘以估计功率密度，使容量预测被掩码导出的面积强耦合，误差累积且受限于面积质量。

Method: Direct Head 是一种分割引导架构：保留 U-Net 分割与软掩码池化以提取光伏区域特征，同时结合面积描述符，直接回归非负容量值，而非通过面积乘密度得到容量。在 7,590 个法国屋顶安装上评估，采用原始划分与基于省份（department）的留出划分，各独立重复三次；与参数匹配的面积-密度神经模型比较，并做传统估计器对比、受控架构变体、辅助损失消融及典型案例分析。

Result: 相对参数匹配的面积-密度神经模型，Direct Head 在原始划分与省份留出划分两种设置下，平均绝对误差分别降低 8.7% 与 12.5%，对称平均绝对百分比误差分别降低 14.0% 与 20.7%。

Conclusion: 分割提供的空间证据对容量估计有用，但容量不必由面积-密度乘积决定；解耦"面积"与"容量"的回归路径可提升精度与泛化性。

Abstract: Distribution planners need installation-level inventories of rooftop photovoltaic (PV) capacity, but interconnection records may be incomplete or spatially aggregated. Conventional image-based estimators segment PV arrays, convert the mask to physical area, and multiply by an estimated power density, coupling capacity prediction to mask-derived area. We propose Direct Head, a segmentation-guided architecture that retains U-Net segmentation and soft-mask pooling while directly regressing nonnegative capacity from PV-region features and area descriptors. We evaluate three independent runs on 7,590 French rooftop installations under the original split and a department-based holdout. Relative to a parameter-matched area-density neural model, Direct Head reduces mean absolute error by 8.7% and 12.5%, and symmetric mean absolute percentage error by 14.0% and 20.7%, respectively. Comparisons with conventional estimators, controlled architecture variants, auxiliary-loss ablations, and representative cases show that segmentation provides useful spatial evidence without requiring capacity to be determined by an area-density product.

</details>


### [16] [Domain-Tuned Diffusion Super-Resolution for 4K Facial Asset Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21890)
*Elly Akhoundi,Mathieu Lamarre,Thibault Goyallon,Patrick Anderson,Étienne Danvoye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 面向面部资产的领域定制扩散上采样器，借助LoRA与Tile ControlNet将1K反照率/位移图提升至4K，细节更锐利忠实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影视级面部资产需4K反照率与位移贴图，但生成管线通常仅输出1K；通用摄影超分不适用，因为面部UV图具领域特定统计分布，位移为标量高度场，其误差会直接扭曲几何。

Method: 以双线性上采样图为起点，对冻结的SDXL主干按模态分别注入轻量LoRA进行领域适配，并用Tile ControlNet保持输入结构，使扩散先验恢复毛孔与皱纹级细节；未设计专门的超分架构。

Result: 在留出贴图上，较双线性及现成GAN/扩散超分器细节更锐利忠实，反照率LPIPS由0.21降至0.08，且避免位移起伏错位或过度。

Conclusion: 轻量领域适配即可产出可渲染的4K面部资产贴图，无需定制超分架构。

Abstract: Physically based facial assets require 4K albedo and displacement maps for cinematic close-ups, yet generative pipelines commonly produce them at only 1K. Naively applying photographic super-resolution is a poor fit: facial UV maps have domain-specific statistics, and displacement is a scalar height field whose errors directly distort geometry. We present a domain-tuned diffusion upscaler for 4x (1024 to 4096) facial asset super-resolution. Starting from a bilinearly upsampled map, we adapt a frozen SDXL backbone per modality with lightweight LoRA modules, while a Tile ControlNet preserves input structure as the diffusion prior restores pore- and wrinkle-scale detail. On held-out maps, our method produces sharper, more faithful detail than bilinear and off-the-shelf GAN and diffusion super-resolvers, reducing albedo LPIPS from 0.21 to 0.08 while avoiding misplaced or excessive displacement relief. These results show that lightweight domain adaptation can produce render-ready 4K facial asset maps without a custom super-resolution architecture.

</details>


### [17] [MAVE: Reconstructing Editable 3D Blockouts from Film Shots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22464)
*Yifan Chang,Jian Yang,Jiangnan Shao,Xiaofeng Wang,Zhaoxin Fan,Renjing Xu,Ming Lu,Xingang Wang,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAVEBench基准与MAVE视频到3D blockout框架：在统一度量坐标系中恢复相机、人体运动与几何证据，由智能体约束式构建可编辑Blender场景并经程序化校验迭代，评分提升27.5-41.1分，单镜头API成本0.054美元、耗时降53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动画3D blockout支撑预可视化并为视频生成提供结构条件，但从参考视频手工重建成本高，既阻碍创作者使用，也限制配对训练数据生产。

Method: 构建MAVEBench（50个电影镜头、416个关键帧，评测相机运动、空间布局与角色走位）；提出MAVE框架，结合度量观测（Metric observation）、智能体建模（Agentic modeling）与基于证据的校验（evidence-based VErification）：先在共享度量坐标系中恢复相机、人体运动与几何证据，再由智能体在观测约束下构造可编辑环境，程序化检查报告不一致以引导精化，输出可编辑Blender场景与帧对齐blockout视频；并构建3K规模配对数据集MAVEData-3K。

Result: MAVEBench上相比对应vanilla基线，三个智能体总评分提升27.5-41.1分；使用DeepSeek-1017达73.2分，平均每镜头API成本仅0.054美元，优于最强vanilla基线且总重建时间减少53%；发布3,000对blockout-视频数据用于模型训练。

Conclusion: 以度量观测+智能体建模+程序化校验的方式实现低成本、高质量、可编辑的视频到blockout重建，并提供基准、数据与代码；创作者可修改视角与角色路径后渲染编辑场景以条件化视频生成。

Abstract: Animated 3D blockouts support previsualization and provide structural conditions for video generation, but manually reconstructing them from reference videos is costly. This limits both their use by creators and the production of paired training data. We introduce MAVEBench, a benchmark of 50 film shots and 416 keyframes for evaluating camera motion, spatial layout, and character blocking. We propose MAVE, a video-to-blockout framework combining Metric observation, Agentic modeling, and evidence-based VErification. It first recovers cameras, human motion, and geometric evidence in a shared metric coordinate system. An agent then constructs an editable environment under constraints from these observations, while programmatic checks report inconsistencies to guide refinement. The outputs are editable Blender scenes and frame-aligned blockout videos. On MAVEBench, MAVE improves overall scores by 27.5-41.1 points across three agents over their corresponding vanilla baselines. With DeepSeek-1017, it achieves a score of 73.2 at an average API cost of only USD 0.054 per shot, outperforming the strongest vanilla baseline while reducing total reconstruction time by 53%. We also construct MAVEData-3K, a dataset of 3,000 blockout-video pairs for model training, and will release it publicly. Creators can change viewpoints and character paths, then render the edited scenes to condition video generation. Code is available at https://github.com/yfChang-cv/MAVE.

</details>


### [18] [As Student Rollouts Continue, Visual Backdoors Keep Growing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22446)
*Zane Fu,Yanzhao Zheng,Jinze Liu,Ziting Qin,Qianshan Wei,Jihuai Zhu,Yipeng Yu,Baohua Dong,Hangcheng Zhu,Ruohui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示On-Policy Distillation(OPD)中视觉后门传播的隐蔽风险，发现其呈阈值式突变、"无恶意响应"依赖概率质量累积驱动，并提出零显式触发注入的触发设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏中后门可从教师传给学生，但OPD这一新兴VLM后训练范式中的后门传播风险研究不足。直觉上OPD中学生自生成良性轨迹、无显式恶意目标监督，似乎具有内在抗性，本文旨在挑战该直觉。

Method: 跨多模型族与数据集的实验，结合理论分析，刻画OPD后门传播规律；分析教师异常概率质量的累积机制；据此设计无需显式触发注入的触发方案。

Result: 三点发现：(1)低毒化率下后门几乎不出现，但超过临界阈值后攻击成功率迅速逼近100%，区别于离线蒸馏的渐进传播；(2)传播不依赖显式恶意响应，而由教师持续累积的异常概率质量驱动；(3)基于此提出零显式触发注入的触发设计，可实现视觉后门传播。

Conclusion: OPD存在被忽视的后门传播机制与潜在安全风险，其"内在抗性"直觉不成立，需引起安全关注。

Abstract: Conventional knowledge distillation can facilitate the propagation of backdoors from teacher models to student models. However, how this risk manifests in On-Policy Distillation (OPD) remains insufficiently studied. OPD has recently emerged as an important post-training paradigm for Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Unlike traditional offline distillation, OPD does not require teacher-generated responses. Instead, it uses the teacher's probability distributions to supervise response trajectories generated online by the student. Since the initial student model typically generates benign responses and receives no explicit supervision from malicious target responses, OPD appears to offer some inherent resistance to backdoor propagation.
  In this paper, we challenge this intuition and reveal a more subtle risk of visual backdoor propagation in OPD. Through experiments across multiple model families and datasets, together with theoretical analysis, we identify three main findings. First, unlike the relatively gradual propagation observed in traditional offline distillation, backdoors rarely emerge in OPD at low poisoning ratios. However, the attack success rate rapidly approaches 100% once a critical threshold is exceeded. Second, backdoor propagation does not depend on explicit malicious responses. Instead, it is driven by the continuous accumulation of anomalous probability mass assigned by the teacher. Finally, based on these findings, we propose a novel trigger design that enables visual backdoor propagation with zero explicit trigger injection. These findings further reveal an overlooked mechanism of backdoor propagation in OPD and highlight its potential security risks.

</details>


### [19] [Depth-Aware Activation Steering via Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21655)
*Shilong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将Attention Residuals的跨层可学习路径与Jacobian-lens概念读出结合，提出轻量级深度感知激活引导（depth-aware steering）流程；在两个小规模开源模型上验证分层注入优于单层注入，并给出几何分解、强度窗口与指标纪律三点实践发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导方法通常只在单一层注入向量，缺乏深度感知的传播机制与内部验证手段，难以保证引导信号在多层间的有效传递与可验证性。

Method: 融合两项近期思路：AttnRes提供可学习的跨层通路权重，Jacobian-lens（J-space）提供概念读出；据此把人格（persona）向量按AttnRes式深度权重分配到多层。辅以符号反转、随机方向、LoRA微调消除印记等对照，以及下游注意力JS散度分析、逐层余弦几何分解、强度扫描，并以LLM-judge检验关键词规则指标。另外以引导目标训练深度权重（200步、主干冻结）。

Result: 在Qwen3-0.6B与Qwen3.5-0.8B上，分布注入在内部对齐（更深层概念余弦与秩，p<0.0001）与行为偏移上均强于单层注入；效应具符号特异性，反向向量使六项指标全部反转，随机方向降低对齐（p<0.0001），短LoRA微调可抹除印记；注入仅改造注入点下游注意力（上游JS=0.00000）。注入层余弦提升中123%为算术贡献，下游5-11层则100%为动力学贡献；效应在0.3-0.7强度区间单调，之后生成崩溃（617→306→25字符）；关键词规则指标未通过LLM-judge验证（kappa≤0.09），两项规则结论被撤回。以引导目标训练深度权重使留出提示上的最终对齐提升10倍；跨人格向量保留96-99%效应，特异性仅对随机方向成立、对语义方向不成立。

Conclusion: 提出一种低开销、深度感知的激活引导方法及配套严谨评测协议，强调分层传播的必要性、效应符号特异性与强度窗口，并警示规则型指标的有效性风险；代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Activation steering is a simple, training-free way to modulate LLM behavior, yet existing methods typically inject vectors at a single layer and lack mechanisms for depth-aware propagation or internal verification. We combine two recent ideas - Attention Residuals (AttnRes) providing learnable cross-layer pathways, and Jacobian-lens (J-space) concept readout - into a lightweight depth-aware steering pipeline. On two small open models (Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3.5-0.8B), distributing a persona vector according to AttnRes-style depth weights yields stronger internal alignment (deeper-layer concept cosine and rank, p<0.0001) and stronger behavioral shifts than single-layer injection. The effect is sign-specific: reverse vectors invert all six metrics, random directions degrade alignment (p<0.0001), and a short LoRA fine-tune erases the imprint. Injection rewires attention strictly downstream of the injection point (upstream JS = 0.00000). Three practical findings follow. First, a layer-wise geometric decomposition of cosine readouts: at the injection layer 123% of the increase is arithmetic, while 5-11 layers downstream 100% is dynamical. Second, an intensity window: effects are monotone over 0.3-0.7, then generation collapses (617 to 306 to 25 chars). Third, metric discipline: keyword-rule metrics fail LLM-judge validation (kappa <= 0.09), and two rule-based conclusions were retracted. Training the depth weights on a steering objective (200 steps, frozen backbone) raises final alignment 10x on held-out prompts. Cross-persona vectors retain 96-99% of the effect: specificity holds against random, not semantic, directions. We contribute a practical, low-overhead depth-aware steering method with a careful evaluation protocol. Code and data are released.

</details>


### [20] [Does the Model Use What It Cites? Counterfactual Tests of Grounded Reasoning Chains in Small Video-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21940)
*Jugal Gajjar,Kamalasankari Subramaniakuppusamy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需标注的"引用差"（cite gap）指标：遮挡模型自身推理链所引用的区域，与遮挡同帧同尺寸随机区域相比，比较正确答案翻转比例之差，用于检验接地推理链的引用是否真被答案依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对"接地推理链"的检验只能说明引用区域可验证（verifiable）且相关（relevant），却无法判断答案是否真正依赖所引用区域；可验证且有相关性的引用，答案未必依赖它。

Method: 采用干预式检验：将模型自身链中引用的帧片段与框遮黑，统计正确答案翻转比例，并与在同一帧上遮挡同尺寸的其他框作配对对照，得到配对差值即 cite gap；在 ClevrER 精确链与伪标注 NExT-QA 链上训练的 2–3B 视频语言模型上评估；另做整帧遮挡被引用实体的变体实验。

Result: 相同权重下，cite gap 在 ClevrER 为 0.089、NExT-QA 为 0.003；跨模型汇总，遮挡被引用框使正确答案翻转数是 ClevrER 对照的 2.2 倍、NExT-QA 的 1.1 倍，在依赖视频的答案子集中同样成立；遮挡整帧被引用实体为对照的 1.9 倍；两类任务的框均通过接地检验。

Conclusion: NExT-QA 链被监督为引用"多数帧可见"的实体的两帧，结果与该监督模式的预期一致；关键在于常规接地链检验无法发现该问题。引用可以可验证且相关，而答案并不依赖它；接地推理的检验应移除链所命名的内容，而不只是它指向的位置。

Abstract: A grounded reasoning chain cites a frame span and a box for every step, so a reader can check where the model looked. Whether the answer depends on the cited region is a different question. We test it by intervention: the regions a model cited in its own chain are blacked out, and the share of correct answers that flip is compared with blacking out boxes of the same size elsewhere on the same frames. The paired difference, the cite gap, needs no annotation. We apply it to 2-3B video-language models trained on exact CLEVRER chains and on pseudo-labelled NExT-QA chains. With the same weights, the cite gap is 0.089 on CLEVRER and 0.003 on NExT-QA. Pooled over models, masking the cited boxes flips 2.2 times as many correct answers as the control on CLEVRER and 1.1 times as many on NExT-QA, also among the answers that depend on the video at all, while the boxes pass a grounding check on both. Masking the cited entity on every frame, instead of the cited box, flips 1.9 times as many answers as its control. The NExT-QA chains were supervised to cite two frames of entities that stay visible on most frames, so this outcome is what the supervision predicts; the point is that the usual check on grounded chains cannot see it. A citation can be verifiable and relevant without the answer depending on it, and a test of grounded reasoning should remove what a chain names, not only where it points.

</details>


### [21] [FaceRiff: Data-Efficient Unified Text-to-3D Face Asset Synthesis via Geometric Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21867)
*Elly Akhoundi,Mathieu Lamarre,Thibault Goyallon,Patrick Anderson,Étienne Danvoye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaceRiff是一个数据与参数高效的统一框架，基于预训练SDXL加任务专用LoRA，以UV法线图为几何条件，从文本直接生成完整的3D人脸资产（网格、反照率、位移）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率、具有一致UV映射几何、反照率与物理基位移的人脸数据集稀缺，而这类资产是照片级数字人渲染的关键。现有3D人脸生成方法往往速度慢、仅支持单模态或依赖大规模专用数据集；大型图像扩散模型虽蕴含强人脸先验，却无法原生输出光照无关纹理、毛孔级位移或UV对齐几何。

Method: 在共享UV空间中改造预训练SDXL骨干，为每个任务配备专用LoRA适配器，并以UV法线图作为几何条件：逐实例法线引导反照率与位移合成，数据集平均法线为网格生成提供规范空间锚点。训练仅使用约350个身份的2,049次扫描，单GPU完成，每个任务更新不到3%的骨干参数，且无需逐实例优化即可产出可直接渲染的资产。

Result: 相较FaceG2E与DreamFace，FaceRiff在保持提示对齐与固定拓扑UV一致性的同时，具有更高的逐提示多样性；并能生成适用于PBR重打光的光照无关反照率以及此前文本驱动基线缺失的毛孔级位移。45位业界专业人士的盲测显示，其感知真实度显著高于DreamFace，接近真实采集。

Conclusion: FaceRiff表明，预训练扩散先验可在有限数据条件下被有效适配到统一的3D人脸资产合成任务中。

Abstract: High-resolution facial datasets with consistent UV-mapped geometry, albedo, and physically based displacement are scarce, yet such assets are essential for photorealistic digital-human rendering. Existing 3D face generation methods are often slow, single-modality, or require large specialized datasets. Meanwhile, large image diffusion models encode strong facial priors but do not natively produce lighting-independent textures, pore-scale displacement, or UV-aligned geometry. We present FaceRiff, a unified, data- and parameter-efficient framework for generating complete 3D face assets (mesh, albedo, and displacement) directly from text in a shared UV space. FaceRiff adapts a pretrained SDXL backbone with task-specific LoRA adapters and uses UV normal maps as geometric conditioning: per-instance normals guide albedo and displacement synthesis, while a dataset-mean normal provides a canonical spatial anchor for mesh generation. Using only 2,049 scans from approximately 350 identities and training on a single GPU, FaceRiff updates less than 3% of the backbone parameters per task and produces render-ready 3D face assets without per-instance optimization. Compared with FaceG2E and DreamFace, FaceRiff produces greater per-prompt diversity while preserving prompt alignment and fixed-topology UV consistency, and additionally generates lighting-independent albedo suitable for PBR relighting and pore-scale displacement absent from prior text-driven baselines. A blind study with 45 industry professionals shows that FaceRiff achieves substantially higher perceived realism than DreamFace, approaching that of real captures. Overall, FaceRiff demonstrates that pretrained diffusion priors can be adapted to unified 3D face asset synthesis under limited data.

</details>


### [22] [A tiny object detection dataset for high-altitude open-water search and rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22825)
*Hui Xu,Chenfei Hou,Henglai Wei,Hao Wang,Yidong Jin,Xiulin Niu,Haoyang Zhang,Hui Zhang,Tianshi Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TinyWOD——一个面向高空（1.5–2.0 km）开阔水域搜救（OW-SAR）的微小目标检测数据集，以救生圈为检测目标，共11,359张图像、19,505个标注实例，并定义APtiny指标对主流网络进行基准评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型空中平台常在1.5 km以上作业以提升OW-SAR效率，但成像距离远、视场宽、传感器分辨率有限，目标仅占几个像素，易受环境干扰，检测难度高于常规小目标；而现有数据集集中于低空场景，无法支撑1.5–2.0 km高空自动检测研究。

Method: 构建TinyWOD数据集：11,359张人工标注图像、19,505个实例，覆盖多样环境、光照、水色与水面波纹；统计显示平均等效边长（ESL）仅12.7像素，76.96%实例ESL低于15像素，目标显著小于现有数据集且微小实例占比更高；同时定义微小目标检测指标APtiny。

Result: 使用APtiny及常规指标对主流与最先进网络进行基准评测，所有被评估网络均能有效检测水面微小目标，验证了TinyWOD的可复用性与实用性。

Conclusion: TinyWOD填补了高空OW-SAR微小目标检测数据空白，预期可支撑该方向后续研究。

Abstract: Large aerial platforms often operate above 1.5 km to improve the efficiency of open water search and rescue (OW-SAR). However, long imaging distances, wide fields of view, and limited sensor resolution reduce objects to a few pixels, increasing their susceptibility to environmental interference and making detection harder than for conventional small objects. To our knowledge, existing datasets focus on low-altitude scenarios and do not support automatic detection at 1.5-2.0 km. We therefore present TinyWOD, a tiny object detection dataset for high-altitude OW-SAR using lifebuoys as detection targets. It comprises 11,359 manually annotated images and 19,505 annotated instances spanning diverse environments, lighting conditions, water colors, and surface ripples. Compared with existing datasets, TinyWOD contains much smaller objects and a substantially higher proportion of tiny instances; its average equivalent side length (ESL) is 12.7 pixels, with 76.96% of in stances having ESLs below 15 pixels, reflecting actual high-altitude water-rescue imaging conditions. We define APtiny, a tiny object detection metric, and benchmark mainstream and state-of-the-art networks using this and conventional metrics. All evaluated networks effectively detect tiny waterborne objects, demonstrating TinyWOD's reusability and utility. We expect this dataset to support research on tiny object detection in high-altitude OW-SAR.

</details>


### [23] [ForceBody: Force-Paired Parametric Body Motion with Torque Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22769)
*Joonwoo Kwon,Yufei Zhang,Xiaoming Liu,Zijun Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ForceBody，首个将真值力监督引入参数化人体模型表示的数据集，并附带逐样本关节力矩不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力与力矩是人体运动的核心，但现有基于学习的运动重建与生成流水线大多缺少力的标注；已有含力标注的数据集其运动序列与现代流程使用的参数化人体模型不兼容，且关节力矩通常由逆动力学得到，对输入运动学质量敏感却未量化其可靠性。

Method: 将 SKEL 参数化人体模型与地面反作用力、逆动力学关节力矩配对，覆盖 140 名受试者的 10,386 条运动试验（9.7M 帧、26.9 小时）。对每个力矩标签，通过蒙特卡洛采样遍历逆动力学流程，得到逐帧、逐关节的不确定性标注。并在 MLP、Conv1D、LSTM、GRU、Mamba2、Transformer 六种架构上基准测试由 SKEL 运动预测地面反作用力与关节力矩。

Result: 建立六种架构的力与力矩预测基准；以逐样本不确定性加权微调 Transformer，使关节力矩估计的 MAE 降低 5%。

Conclusion: ForceBody 弥合了真值力监督与参数化人体表示之间的缺口，并提供可靠性量化，可支撑力感知的运动重建、生成与仿真学习研究。

Abstract: Forces and torques drive human motion, central to biomechanics, robotics, and physics-aware animation, yet they are often absent from learning-based motion pipelines for body reconstruction and generation. Some motion datasets provide force annotations, but the paired motion sequences are incompatible with the parametric body models used in modern learning pipelines. In addition, existing datasets typically obtain joint torques through inverse dynamics, which is inherently sensitive to the quality of the input kinematics; however, they do not quantify the reliability of these torque estimates. We close this gap with ForceBody, the first dataset to bring ground truth force supervision into a parametric body representation, shipped with per-sample torque uncertainty. ForceBody pairs the SKEL body model with ground reaction forces and inverse-dynamics joint torques across 10,386 motion trials (9.7M frames, 26.9 hours) from 140 subjects. Each torque label is paired with a per-frame, per-joint uncertainty annotation obtained by Monte Carlo sampling through the inverse dynamics pipeline. We benchmark six architectures (MLP, Conv1D, LSTM, GRU, Mamba2, Transformer) on ground reaction force and joint torque prediction from SKEL motion. As one example use of the released uncertainty, finetuning a Transformer with per-sample uncertainty weighting reduces the MAE of joint torque estimation by 5%. Code and data are at https://github.com/DominoAI-Lab/ForceBody-NeurIPS-ED-2026.

</details>


### [24] [Analyzing Learned Convnet Features with Dirichlet Process Gaussian Mixture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/1702.07189)
*David Malmgren-Hansen,Allan Aasbjerg Nielsen,Rasmus Engholm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于狄利克雷过程高斯混合模型(DPGMM)聚类卷积网络内部表示的可视化技术，用于解释Convnet学习到的表示，并服务于迁移学习分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Convnet在视觉任务上表现良好，但其内部表示维度高、深层抽象性强，导致解释其学到的表示仍然困难，缺乏有效的可视化手段。

Method: 对每一层所有节点的内部表示进行跨节点聚类，采用Dirichlet Process Gaussian Mixture Model进行聚类，从而在高维情形下实现表示的聚类与可视化。

Result: 摘要未给出定量实验结果，仅报告该方法可应对高维表示，并说明其在迁移学习场景（将某数据集训练的模型用于另一任务）中的应用价值。

Conclusion: 对Convnet各层表示进行跨节点聚类是一种可行的高维可视化途径，有助于理解网络表示并支撑迁移学习分析。

Abstract: Convolutional Neural Networks (Convnets) have achieved good results in a range of computer vision tasks the recent years. Though given a lot of attention, visualizing the learned representations to interpret Convnets, still remains a challenging task. The high dimensionality of internal representations and the high abstractions of deep layers are the main challenges when visualizing Convnet functionality. We present in this paper a technique based on clustering internal Convnet representations with a Dirichlet Process Gaussian Mixture Model, for visualization of learned representations in Convnets. Our method copes with the high dimensionality of a Convnet by clustering representations across all nodes of each layer. We will discuss how this application is useful when considering transfer learning, i.e.\ transferring a model trained on one dataset to solve a task on a different one.

</details>


### [25] [FVScore: Specification-Driven Neuro-Symbolic Video Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22673)
*Do Xuan Long,Long T. Le,Yiwen Song,Min-Yen Kan,Tomas Pfister,Yale Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出神经符号框架FVSCORE，将视频生成评估转化为规范驱动的命题验证问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估器依赖MLLM整体判断或视觉-语言指标，难以与人类判断对齐，且无法将分数追溯到具体的时间性违规。

Method: 将视频编译为两类命题：显式命题（提示词要求）与隐式命题（未明示的物理、时间与跨模态约束）；用专门感知工具对每个命题进行接地，并以受线性时序逻辑启发的软时序算子评估置信轨迹；再经显著性感知校准，仅保留感知上显著的违规，输出带时间戳与证据的报告。

Result: 在公开及复杂多事件基准、当前最强视频模型上实验，FVSCORE在人类相关性上优于整体式与智能体式评估器，并提供高准确、可解释的反馈。

Conclusion: 规范驱动+神经符号验证的评估范式可同时提升与人类判断的一致性和反馈的可追溯性、可解释性。

Abstract: We introduce Faithfulness Video Score (FVSCORE), a neuro-symbolic framework for specification-driven video generation evaluation. Existing evaluators rely on holistic MLLM judgments or vision-language metrics, yet aligning their scores with human judgments and tracing them to specific temporal violations remain open challenges. FVSCORE addresses these limitations by compiling each video into explicit propositions capturing prompt requirements and implicit propositions capturing unstated physical, temporal, and cross-modal constraints. It grounds each proposition using specialized perceptual tools and evaluates the resulting confidence traces with soft temporal operators inspired by Linear Temporal Logic. A Significance-Aware Calibration step then aligns verification with human perception by retaining only perceptually significant violations before producing a timestamp- and evidence-grounded report. We conduct extensive experiments across public and complex multi-event benchmarks with the strongest video models to date, demonstrating that FVSCORE outperforms holistic and agent-based evaluators in human correlation while providing highly accurate and interpretable feedback.

</details>


### [26] [From Perception to Simulation: Unveiling and Empowering Mental Spatial Dynamics in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22541)
*Jiachun Li,Zhuoran Jin,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MentalGrid 动态空间模拟基准，并给出三阶段课程学习框架训练出 SpatialWalker 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已推进静态场景空间关系感知，但缺乏无中间视觉反馈下"模拟空间状态演化"的动态空间模拟能力研究，需填补该空白。

Method: 构建 MentalGrid 基准，包含需在无中间视觉反馈条件下模拟演化空间状态的空间推理任务；基于 16k 专用空间推理样本，设计三阶段课程学习框架训练 SpatialWalker。

Result: 评测显示现有模型空间模拟能力显著不足：简单场景受感知能力限制，复杂度提升时受多步推理不足制约。SpatialWalker 相对基座模型平均提升 33.8%，在空间模拟上超过 Gemini-3-Flash 等闭源模型；在域外基准上平均增益 4.9%。

Conclusion: 空间模拟是更广泛空间智能的基础构件，课程学习可有效提升 MLLM 的动态空间模拟能力并具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental component of world understanding for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). While existing research has advanced the perception of spatial relations in static scenes, the domain of dynamic spatial simulation remains relatively unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce MentalGrid, a benchmark focused on spatial reasoning tasks that require models to simulate evolving spatial states without intermediate visual feedback. Extensive evaluation on our benchmark reveals a significant deficiency in the spatial simulation capabilities of existing models. Specifically, we find that performance is constrained by perceptual limitations in simple simulation scenarios and hindered by inadequate multi-step reasoning as complexity increases. To address these challenges, we propose a three-stage curriculum learning framework utilizing 16k specialized spatial reasoning samples. This approach yields SpatialWalker, which achieves a 33.8% average improvement over its base model, surpassing proprietary models like Gemini-3-Flash in spatial simulation. Furthermore, SpatialWalker demonstrates robust generalization, maintaining a 4.9% average gain on out-of-domain benchmarks, demonstrating the pivotal role of spatial simulation as a fundamental building block for broader spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [27] [Reading Scene States: Uncovering Safety Failures in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23206)
*Tongxi Wu,Yuyang Cheng,Jiacheng Xian,Menglin Yang,Kai Chen,Jian Zhang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文识别出视觉语言模型的"场景-状态安全漏洞"：模型通过解读图像中的视觉事件即可推断并给出有害协助，即使问题本身未明确请求有害目标。作者构建SceneSafetyBench并据此提出S3TA攻击方法，模型平均ASR达71.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM安全研究主要关注输入内容筛查（文本过滤、视觉审核），但模型可能通过场景化推理自行推断出有害目标并给出协助。作者指出这一"场景-状态安全漏洞"尚未被系统研究，因此需要构建基准并验证其攻击可行性。

Method: 1) 构建SceneSafetyBench：375个图文问答对，覆盖初始、中间、最终三类场景状态及五种视觉呈现方式；2) 在8个VLM上评测场景状态输入的攻击成功率，并比较三类VLM与QR相关图像在逐层安全信号剖面、后层读出值及生成期动态上的差异；3) 提出S3TA：将有害目标转化为场景状态，独立查询各图文对，再拼接响应以形成系统化攻击。在MM-SafetyBench七个类别、8个开源与商用VLM上评测。

Result: 8个VLM在场景状态输入下ASR为45.6%–72.3%；S3TA在MM-SafetyBench七类上模型平均ASR达71.2%，超过最强基线6.1个百分点；文本侧过滤与视觉审核防护效果有限。

Conclusion: VLM的安全风险不止于输入内容筛查，还存在于模型基于场景的推理所推断出的协助上，需针对场景化推理过程建立防护机制。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can provide harmful assistance by interpreting visual events, even without an explicit request for the underlying harmful objective. We identify this \textbf{scene-state safety vulnerability} and introduce \textbf{SceneSafetyBench}, comprising 375 image--question pairs spanning Initial, Medium, and Ultimate scene states and five visual presentations. Across eight VLMs, scene-state inputs elicit harmful assistance with attack success rates (ASRs) of 45.6--72.3\%. Comparative analysis across three VLMs shows that scene-state inputs most closely resemble QR-related images in their layer-wise safety-related signal profiles, while exhibiting lower average late-layer readouts and distinct signal dynamics during generation. To turn this vulnerability into a systematic attack, we propose \textbf{S3TA}, which translates harmful objectives into scene states, queries image--question pairs independently, and assembles the responses. On seven MM-SafetyBench categories across eight open-weight and commercial VLMs, S3TA achieves a model-average ASR of \textbf{71.2\%, exceeding the strongest baseline by 6.1 percentage points}. Text-side filters and visual moderation provide limited protection. These findings highlight the need to safeguard the assistance models infer through scene-grounded reasoning, beyond screening input content.

</details>


### [28] [CueClash: Frontier Vision-Language Models Judge 3-D Relations Close to the Picture Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22180)
*Soham Dan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CueClash通过将2-D线索与3-D地面布局解耦，证明VLM的三维关系判断主要依赖图像捷径而非布局推理，其瓶颈在"从图像到布局"的映射步骤，而非关系判定本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 照片中2-D线索与地面布局通常一致，导致无法区分VLM究竟是依据图像特征捷径还是真正的三维布局推理来判断3-D关系。作者需要一个能将二者分离的实验设计。

Method: 构建CueClash渲染场景，对五种关系分别使2-D线索恰在一半陈述上正确；定义"捷径差距"=线索正确时的准确率−线索错误时的准确率。测试Claude Opus 5.5、GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.6 Flash（低推理强度）。进一步做对照实验：直接给定地面坐标与关系定义；用相机深度做像素测量；让模型绘制俯视布局图并分析其深度压缩；在CV-Bench照片上验证；并检查两个开源模型中布局信息是否到达语言模型。

Result: 捷径差距为+18~+37个百分点；剔除两个线索错误项与边界情形/遮挡混淆的关系后为+7~+29。给定地面坐标与定义时准确率99~100%。对可穿过开口的物体，像素测量配合相机深度可消除大部分差距。俯视图分析：模型基本保留深度顺序但压缩深度；GPT-5.5的图近似复制原图，Opus 5.5与Gemini恢复约0.6的压缩量，Gemini随后对所有深度施加额外均匀缩放；Opus 5.5在"契合"类关系上最依赖图像线索，其深度校正量与人类观察真实场景时相当。三模型在2-D线索误导的CV-Bench照片上准确率下降。两个开源模型中布局信息确实传入语言模型，但其布局图丢失了大部分信息。

Conclusion: VLM的3-D关系失败并非源于关系判定或布局缺失，而是"图像→布局"这一中间步骤：模型将深度压缩的2-D图像近似复制为俯视布局，从而继承图像捷径。给定布局即可正确推理，说明改进方向应指向布局重建的深度校正能力。

Abstract: A vision-language model (VLM) can judge 3-D relations from the picture or from the ground layout. In photographs the two usually agree. We separate them with CueClash, rendered scenes in which the 2-D cue for each of five relations is right on exactly half the statements. The shortcut gap is accuracy when the cue is right minus accuracy when it is wrong. For Claude Opus 5.5, GPT-5.5 and Gemini 3.6 Flash at low reasoning effort it is +18 to +37 percentage points. It is +7 to +29 without two relations whose cue-wrong items are confounded with borderline cases or occlusion. Given ground coordinates and each relation's definition, they answer 99 to 100% correctly. Pixel measurements with camera depths remove most of the gap for objects fitting through openings. The failure lies in the step from the picture to the layout. The maps that the models draw of the scene from above mostly keep the depth order but, like the picture, shorten depth. GPT-5.5's maps stay close to a copy of the picture. Opus 5.5's and Gemini's maps undo about 0.6 of this shortening. Gemini's then shrink all depths by a further uniform factor. Opus 5.5 follows the picture mainly on fits, and its maps correct depth about as much as human observers of real scenes do. The three models also lose accuracy on CV-Bench photographs where a reliable 2-D cue misleads. In two open models the layout information reaches the language model, but their maps lose much of it.

</details>


### [29] [Pathology Randomization for Synthetic Priors and Zero-Shot Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21975)
*Alexandra G. Roberts,Tonmoy Hossain,Mark Olchanyi,Stefan Brandstätter,Jonathan Williams Ramirez,Ana Lawry Aguila,Matthew Rosen,Annabel Sorby-Adams,W. Taylor Kimberly,Juan Eugenio Iglesias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PathoDraw：一个基于健康解剖与病灶条件分布的合成MRI生成模型，无需真实病理数据即可训练对比度无关的3D UNet，实现跨序列、跨分辨率的零样本病理检测，并可同时用于超分与配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院脑影像档案含大量代表性不足人群与疾病的病理样本，但缺乏标注；现有合成数据方法多针对健康解剖，临床场景的病理检测仍依赖真实患者数据、对比度专用模型或疾病专用生成器，难以规模化利用未整理的临床数据。

Method: 将病灶视为给定健康解剖的条件分布，先采样病灶并绘制入标签图，再进行对比度无关渲染；单一网络从任意输入扫描中估计健康解剖并给出精确的有符号强度偏差。对比度无关的3D UNet直接在病灶下方学习健康标签与有符号偏差，通过稳健的组织统计与学习特征识别病理，训练全程不使用真实病理。

Result: 在公开的卒中、肿瘤、白质高信号数据集及归档临床数据上完成零样本检测，覆盖T1w、FLAIR、T2w多种分辨率，并遵循最新无监督异常检测基准协议；单模型性能超过或可比于在真实扫描上训练的对比度专用异常检测器，且保持健康解剖完整。同一生成器还可训练超分辨率与含病理配准网络。

Conclusion: 该模型对采集协议与疾病类型均具鲁棒性，使未整理归档数据可用于临床分析，代码已开源。

Abstract: Hospital brain imaging archives hold a diverse array of pathology at sample sizes beyond prospective studies, particularly for underrepresented populations and diseases. To unlock uncurated clinical data and scale analysis, methods are needed to detect abnormalities across contrasts and resolutions. Synthetic data has provided robust, contrast-agnostic models for a variety of human neuroimaging tasks in healthy anatomy, but pathology detection and other tasks in clinical settings still require real patient data, contrast-specific models, or disease-specific generators. We propose PathoDraw, a generative model of brain MRI with pathology. A conditional distribution of lesions given the healthy anatomy is drawn into the label map before contrast-agnostic rendering, and a single network estimates healthy anatomy with exact signed intensity deviation of any input scan. The contrast-agnostic 3D UNet learns the healthy labels under the lesion and the signed deviation directly and identifies pathology via robust tissue statistics and learned features, despite no training on real pathology. We show zero-shot detection on public stroke, tumor, and white matter hyperintensity datasets as well as archived clinical data, spanning $T_1$w, FLAIR and $T_2$w at various resolutions under a recent unsupervised anomaly detection benchmark protocol. Our single model exceeds or performs comparably to contrast-specific anomaly detectors trained on real scans and keeps healthy anatomy intact. The same generator trains networks for super-resolution and for registration in the presence of pathology. Our model, robust across acquisition and disease, enables the use of uncurated, archived data for clinical analysis. Our code is available at https://github.com/agr78/Pathology-Randomizer.

</details>


### [30] [EgoLink: Egocentric Language-Vision Interactive Network Knowledge Challenge Summary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21690)
*Renhe Sun,Yueying Feng,Bohan Yu,Tong Niu,Yunqi Liu,Zitong Wang,Haolin He,Kexin Shi,Hualei Wang,Qiuqiang Kong,Chang Yao,Yuge Huang,Weiqiang Wang,Jingyuan Chen,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoLink Challenge（ACM MM 2026）提出第一人称多模态智能基准，包含自我中心视频社交推理与工具交互智能体两个赛道，最佳系统分别达0.9263准确率与0.844联合成功率/0.916微准确率，表明显式中间证据与过程验证是可靠性的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多聚焦于可见物体与活动识别，缺乏对情感/社交线索解读、视觉接地指代消解、以及将用户请求转化为可验证工具动作的能力评测；需考察感知—推理—决策在离线问答与状态改变式交互两类设定下是否保持耦合。

Method: 构建两个赛道：Track 1为自我中心视频社交推理（多选题问答），Track 2为通过对话、工具调用与闭环执行完成服务任务的交互智能体。Track 1采用多阶段数据流水线：MCQ生成、思维链生成、自动质量分级、视频贡献度过滤。评测结合客观答案打分与基于过程/结果的评估。

Result: Track 1最佳系统准确率0.9263；Track 2最佳系统联合成功率0.844，微准确率0.916。参赛报告显示高性能系统的共性策略：候选验证、选项鲁棒推理、场景感知规划、工具规范化、状态感知纠错。

Conclusion: 过程验证与证据接地推理对可靠的第一人称多模态智能至关重要；该基准可同时分析最终正确性与执行质量，为自我中心多模态智能体研究提供统一评测框架。

Abstract: The EgoLink Challenge at ACM Multimedia 2026 benchmarks egocentric multimodal intelligence linking first-person perception, social reasoning, and interactive action. Unlike benchmarks centered on visible objects and activities, EgoLink evaluates whether models can interpret emotional and social cues, resolve visually grounded references, and convert user requests into verified tool-mediated actions. Its two tracks cover social reasoning in egocentric video and interactive agents that complete service tasks through dialogue, tool use, and closed-loop execution. Together, they test whether perception, reasoning, and decision-making remain coupled under both offline question answering and state-changing interaction. We highlight a multi-stage Track 1 data pipeline for multiple-choice question (MCQ) generation, chain-of-thought generation, automatic quality grading, and video-contribution filtering. We summarize the task design, evaluation metrics, rules, leaderboards, and representative solutions. The benchmark combines objective answer scoring with process- and result-based evaluation, enabling analysis of both final correctness and execution quality. The best Track 1 system achieved 0.9263 accuracy, while the best Track 2 system achieved 0.844 joint success and 0.916 micro accuracy. Participant reports reveal a shared principle: strong systems make intermediate evidence explicit through candidate verification, option-robust reasoning, scenario-aware planning, tool canonicalization, and state-aware correction before answering or acting. These findings highlight the importance of process verification and evidence-grounded reasoning for reliable egocentric multimodal intelligence.

</details>


### [31] [NexSight: Verifiable Tasks for Fine-Grained Agentic Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22790)
*Tianci Xie,Li Chen,Pinxue Guo,Rui Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 NexSight 框架，用可验证任务来同时评测和训练长视频的智能体式理解：SightForge 管线自动构建证据可追溯任务集 SightLine（310 任务、108 个长视频），SightLoop 提供统一多尺度评测环境，并将验证任务的求解轨迹转为步骤级监督训练 Sight-35B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频细粒度问题（次数、顺序、状态变化时刻）需在数小时视频中定位零散短片段，现有能力难以度量也难以学习：稀疏证据只能证明事件发生而无法确定总数，无需漏检/重复计数的验证；仅答案监督不显式监督证据搜索过程。

Method: SightForge 智能体管线：仅当对草稿答案盲的验证器能从原始视频中密集重新获取的证据重建答案、且反例检查无法推翻时才接受任务；SightLoop 共享多尺度 harness 支持时间探索、局部检查、证据整合与回访并记录观测；在不相交视频上，求解轨迹转化为步骤级监督用于训练观测决策。

Result: SightLine 含 310 个证据可追溯任务、108 个长视频；最强模型仅得 54.52%，给出标注证据时达 79.35%；Sight-35B 在不足 6K 样本下交互准确率从 21.29% 提升至 42.26%，超过同族 397B 模型，后训练后开始使用基座从未使用的显式空间检查，并在两个外部长视频基准上提升 8 点以上。

Conclusion: 通过可验证的任务构建与步骤级证据监督，可显著提升智能体长视频理解中的搜索与证据整合能力，并使评测与训练形成闭环。

Abstract: Fine-grained questions about long videos ask how many times an action occurs, in what order events unfold, or exactly when a state changes. Answering them can require finding brief moments scattered across hours of footage. Agentic models that decide when and where to look promise to find these moments, yet this ability is hard both to measure and to learn. Sparse supporting evidence may establish that an action occurred without establishing its total count; verifying timeline-wide claims requires checking for missed occurrences and double counting. Answer-only supervision, in turn, does not explicitly supervise the search for evidence. We present NexSight, which builds verifiable tasks to both evaluate and train agentic long-video understanding. Its agentic pipeline, SightForge, accepts a task only if a verifier blind to the draft answer can reconstruct it from evidence densely reacquired from the raw video and counterexample checks fail to refute it. This yields SightLine, 310 evidence-traceable tasks from 108 long videos, evaluated through SightLoop, a shared multiscale harness supporting temporal exploration, local inspection, evidence integration, and revisiting while recording returned observations. On disjoint videos, solver trajectories on verified tasks become step-level supervision for observation decisions. The strongest model reaches only 54.52% on SightLine, yet 79.35% when given the annotated evidence. With under 6K examples, Sight-35B nearly doubles its base model's interactive accuracy (21.29% to 42.26%), outperforms the 397B model of its family, begins using explicit spatial inspection after post-training, which its base model never uses, and gains over 8 points on two external long-video benchmarks.

</details>


### [32] [ReTouchGS: Ultralight Render-free Aesthetic Color Enhancement and Transfer for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23054)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Boyu Wei,Chengkun Yue,Xuanhua Yin,Yiyang Li,Haodong Chen,Qiang Qu,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReTouchGS 是首个无需参考图像、可对预训练 3DGS 模型进行美学色彩增强的框架，通过从模型与相机参数预测场景级 LUT，兼顾参考引导色彩迁移，并配套提出 ReTouch3D 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS 色彩迁移与交互编辑依赖参考图像和用户指定的颜色调整，而许多场景中用户只需自动化的、跨视角一致的色调美感提升，缺乏参考自由的美学色彩增强方案。

Method: 从预训练 3DGS 模型及其相机参数预测场景级颜色查找表（LUT）；参考引导模式下，用同一场景的一张编辑视图与其原始渲染拟合场景级 LUT；采用仅 44.7K 参数的共享 blend-aware 色彩细化网络对解析色彩更新进行细化，实现无需渲染的色彩映射，保留几何并秒级输出标准 3DGS 资产；同时构建 ReTouch3D 基准以评估跨视角的美学增强与参考引导迁移。

Result: 实验表明在美学色彩增强与参考引导色彩迁移两类任务上均取得高色彩精度，并对未见场景表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 以场景级 LUT 加轻量细化网络的范式，可在保持几何与跨视角色彩一致性的前提下实现高效、可泛化的 3DGS 色彩增强，ReTouch3D 为其提供了系统评测支撑。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables photorealistic scene reconstruction. Existing 3DGS color transfer and interactive editing methods are guided by reference images and user-specified color adjustments. However, in many applications, users simply want aesthetically guided color enhancement that automatically makes the scene more visually appealing while maintaining consistent colors and tones across viewpoints. We present ReTouchGS, to our knowledge the first framework for reference-free aesthetic color enhancement of pretrained 3DGS models. It predicts a scene-level color lookup table (LUT) from the model and its associated camera parameters. ReTouchGS also supports reference-guided color transfer by fitting a scene-level LUT using one edited view of the same scene and its corresponding original render. A shared blend-aware color refinement network with only 44.7K parameters refines an analytic color update to realize either color mapping without rendering. It preserves geometry and delivers standard 3DGS assets in seconds. We also design the ReTouch3D benchmark to support the evaluation of aesthetic color enhancement and reference-guided color transfer across viewpoints. Experiments demonstrate high color accuracy and strong generalization to unseen scenes in both aesthetic color enhancement and reference-guided color transfer.

</details>


### [33] [DAP-MR: Degradation-Aware Prompt-Magnitude Routing for Real-World Ultrasound Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22331)
*Ngoc-Hoang-Lam Le,Thi-An Le,Dinh-Hung Le,Manh-Hung Nguyen,Kai-Lung Hua,Ching-Chun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出退化感知提示–幅值路由（DAP-MR），用专用退化token表征退化类型、独立路由预测连续幅值，解耦"何种退化"与"退化多强"，可即插即用于单步与多步扩散超分框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将面向自然图像的超分模型用于超声图像时，依赖语义文本提示不可靠：模型生成的标签与实际复原需求常不匹配，通用文本编码器也可能误解专业医学术语。相比之下，聚焦常见图像退化的提示对复原指导更直接。但单一提示同时描述退化类型与严重程度会使两种因素纠缠，模型难以判断退化强度。

Method: DAP-MR由两部分构成：(1) 每种退化类型由一个专属token表示，该token以退化导向提示的上下文化EOS嵌入初始化，并在超声超分训练中继续适配；(2) 独立的幅值路由器预测连续的、依赖输入的幅值，用于调制对应token。即语言提供"复原什么"的退化感知初始化，幅值路由提供"影响多强"的显式信号。

Result: 在合成数据集（域内及解剖域偏移）与无配对真实超声数据上，DAP-MR均取得更好的复原质量，且跨域泛化更稳定。

Conclusion: 通过将退化类型与退化强度解耦为token与幅值两条路径，DAP-MR无需改动核心结构即可嵌入单步与多步扩散超分框架，是提升超声超分鲁棒性与跨域一致性的有效方案。

Abstract: When super-resolution models designed for natural images are used on ultrasound scans, relying on semantic text prompts can lead to unreliable results. The tags these models generate often do not match the actual restoration needs, and specialized medical terms may be misunderstood by general-purpose text encoders. Instead, prompts focused on common image degradations are more directly useful for guiding restoration. However, using a single prompt to describe both the type and severity of image degradation can entangle these factors, making it hard for the model to know how strong the degradation is. To address this limitation, we introduce Degradation-Aware Prompt--Magnitude Routing (DAP-MR). Each degradation type is represented by a dedicated token initialized from the contextualized EOS embedding of a degradation-oriented prompt and subsequently adapted during ultrasound SR training. A separate magnitude router predicts a continuous, input-dependent magnitude that modulates the corresponding token. Thus, language provides a degradation-aware initialization for what should be restored, while magnitude routing supplies an explicit signal for how strongly it should influence restoration. DAP-MR can be added to both one-step and multi-step diffusion super-resolution frameworks without changing their core structures. Our experiments--on synthetic datasets with both in-domain and under anatomical-domain shift, as well as unpaired real-world ultrasound data--show that DAP-MR delivers better restoration quality and generalizes more consistently across different domains.

</details>


### [34] [Nunchi: Ears Help Eyes in Multi-Party Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23115)
*JunYeong Park,Shijing Wang,Seonwook Park,Michael Stengel,Shalini De Mello,Sang-Il Choi,Hyung Jin Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出SORBET基准（1,578段视频、3,340个样本，含同步语音、转写与注视对象标签）和Nunchi框架，利用对话线索（说话人、语音内容）辅助视觉语言模型判断多方对话中的注视对象，性能较最强基线提升4.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人场景中人脸增多使仅凭注视几何产生歧义，而对话中的说话人与语音内容等线索可消解歧义；但现有注视数据集缺乏面向多人的同步语音与转写信息，且已有模型难以有效利用这些异质、需与人对应、且可能与几何冲突的线索。

Method: 构建SORBET基准：覆盖比现有注视数据集更多的多人场景，样本含同步语音、转写及注视对象标签。提出Nunchi框架：由转写及注视/说话人模型输出构造文本提示，将每条线索按共享标签关联到候选人，再由VLM权衡注视几何与对话线索以判定被注视者。

Result: Nunchi比最强现有方法高4.8个百分点；在SORBET上对话线索有效而额外视觉线索无效；增益在三种VLM、自动转写及两个现有数据集上均成立。

Conclusion: 对话线索是消解多人注视歧义的关键补充，SORBET与Nunchi为多人对话注视分析提供了基准与方法，并具备跨模型、跨数据集的泛化性。
