Abstract: Identifying whom a person looks at is central to understanding multi-party conversations, yet gaze geometry alone becomes ambiguous as more people appear. Conversations offer cues that geometry lacks, such as who is speaking and what is said, but no gaze dataset provides them for many multi-party scenes. We introduce SORBET, a benchmark of 3,340 samples from 1,578 clips with synchronized speech, transcripts, and attended person labels, covering more multi-party scenes than existing gaze datasets. Prior gaze models struggle to use these cues, which come in different forms, must be linked to the right person, and can conflict with gaze geometry. We propose Nunchi, a framework that constructs a text prompt from the transcript and the outputs of gaze and speaker models, links every cue to the candidates with shared labels, and lets a vision-language model (VLM) weigh gaze geometry against conversational cues to identify the attended person. Nunchi outperforms the strongest existing method by 4.8 percentage points. On SORBET, conversational cues help whereas additional visual cues do not, and the gains hold across three VLMs, with automatic transcripts, and on two existing datasets.

</details>


### [35] [SignAligner: Dual-Granularity Span Generation for Sign Language Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22409)
*Jongbhin Woo,Youngjoon Jang,Joon Son Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SignAligner是一个提示条件化的视觉-语言片段生成器：输入手语视频与字幕句/目标词查询，输出文本形式的起止帧区间；先在字幕片段与词片段上联合训练，再用基于重叠的奖励微调，在BOBSL字幕测试集达66.68 F1@0.50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语语料需要两种时间尺度标签：字幕片段（用于字幕对齐）和词片段（用于手语定位）。词级定位已在语料规模上可行，但在字幕与词片段上联合训练以做字幕对齐仍研究不足；同时，用重叠奖励直接优化时间IoU（而非交叉熵）未被充分验证。

Method: 提出提示条件化的视觉-语言片段生成器SignAligner：以视频加字幕句或目标词为查询，回归文本形式的start/end帧。两阶段训练：(1)在字幕片段与词定位片段上联合训练；(2)用基于时间重叠的奖励（overlap-based reward）做精调，同时保留辅助词样本。并设置代码、标签与最大更新预算匹配的对照生成器以隔离联合训练效应。

Result: BOBSL字幕测试集上精调模型达66.68 F1@0.50，为无外部手语视频资源的对比系统中最优；重叠奖励精调优于同一起点的交叉熵继续训练；在匹配对照下，加入词片段联合训练可提升字幕对齐，且经重叠奖励精调后优势仍保留；对词查询，模型跟随被查询词而非固定位置——当目标词远离视频中心时，显著优于仅用位置先验的预测。

Conclusion: 单一共享片段生成器配合重叠奖励精调即可同时完成字幕对齐与生成供人工复核的词片段，词级与字幕级标签的联合训练带来互补收益，支持训练目标与评测指标（时间IoU）保持一致的设计思路。

Abstract: Large sign-language corpora benefit from temporal labels at two scales: subtitle spans for subtitle alignment and word spans for sign spotting. Sign spottings already locate words in time at corpus scale, but training subtitle alignment jointly on subtitle and word spans remains underexplored. We present SignAligner, a prompt-conditioned visual-language span generator that maps a sign-language video and a subtitle-sentence or target-word query to a textual start/end frame interval. It is first trained jointly on subtitle spans and word spans from sign spottings, then refined with overlap-based rewards while retaining auxiliary word examples. On the subtitle test set of the British Broadcasting Corporation-Oxford British Sign Language (BOBSL) corpus, the refined model reaches 66.68 F1@0.50 (F1 with a temporal intersection-over-union threshold of one half), the best among compared systems without external sign-video resources. This overlap-reward refinement outperforms cross-entropy continuation from the same jointly trained model. Joint training with word spans improves subtitle alignment in a pair of our generators matched in code, labels and maximum update budget. The advantage remains after both models of the pair are refined with overlap rewards. For word queries, the refined model follows the queried word, not a fixed position in the video: when the word's sign lies away from the video centre, it is far more accurate than predictions that use position alone. Together, these findings support one shared span generator refined with overlap rewards, aligning subtitles and producing word spans for human review.

</details>


### [36] [TextSphere: Efficient Text-to-Image Generation via Shared Hyperspherical Alignment and Uniformity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23155)
*Yixia Zhang,Girish A. Koushik,Xilu Wang,Wenwu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TextSphere，将类别条件的Sphere Encoder扩展为文本到图像生成框架，通过AdaLN注入CLIP文本嵌入并引入球面语义对齐与均匀性（SSAU）目标，仅用4/6步采样即在CUB与MS-COCO上取得低于扩散与GAN基线的gFID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成质量高但需多步推理、效率低；Sphere Encoder推理步数少但为类别条件设计，缺乏跨模态对齐，难以直接扩展到自由文本条件。

Method: 在保留原有球面采样机制的前提下，用AdaLN将预训练CLIP文本嵌入注入Sphere Encoder以引导生成；提出SSAU目标，在超球面上同时优化语义对齐与表示均匀性；并用PLS-SVD分析跨模态对齐与模态间隙。

Result: 在CUB-200-2011、MS-COCO-2014、Flickr30k上，gFID相对文本条件基线持续下降，rFID低于1.0；仅4步（CUB）和6步（MS-COCO）采样即优于从头训练的扩散与GAN模型；发现重建保真度提升不一定带来更好的生成质量。

Conclusion: 语言条件化加上球面对齐目标可高效实现高质量文本到图像生成；重建保真度与生成质量并不一致；PLS-SVD分析为方法有效性提供了跨模态对齐层面的解释。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-image generation have substantially improved image quality, particularly through diffusion-based methods that require multiple inference steps. In contrast, Sphere Encoder offers an efficient generative framework capable of producing images with fewer forward steps. However, extending it to free-form text is nontrivial, because its class-conditioned design lacks cross-modal alignment. To address this challenge, we propose TextSphere, an efficient text-to-image generative framework that extends Sphere Encoder with language conditioning and cross-modal alignment. We incorporate pretrained CLIP text embeddings through adaptive layer normalization (AdaLN) to guide image generation while retaining the original spherical sampling mechanism. To bridge the cross-modal representation gap, we introduce a Spherical Semantic Alignment and Uniformity (SSAU) objective that jointly promotes semantic alignment and representation uniformity on the hypersphere. Experiments on CUB-200-2011, MS-COCO-2014, and Flickr30k demonstrate that TextSphere consistently reduces generation FID (gFID) over the text-conditioned baseline while achieving reconstruction FID (rFID) below 1.0. With only 4 and 6 sampling steps, TextSphere achieves lower gFID than the compared diffusion- and GAN-based models trained from scratch on CUB-200-2011 and MS-COCO-2014, respectively. We further observe that improved reconstruction fidelity does not necessarily yield better generation quality. In addition, we use Partial Least Squares Singular Value Decomposition (PLS-SVD) to analyze cross-modal alignment and modality gap, offering insight into the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [37] [Breaking Visual Conventions: Spectrally Coupled Weight Perturbation for Creative Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22318)
*Fu Feng,Ruoheng Zhou,Yucheng Xie,Xu Yang,Jing Wang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CosPert是一种免训练方法，通过对注意力投影的奇异向量因子进行耦合谱扰动，在推理阶段提升文生图模型的视觉创造力，且不破坏类别身份与提示对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型倾向生成符合常见视觉惯例的图像，即便面对开放式提示也缺乏新颖性。已有增强创造力的方法依赖语义条件探索或去噪过程中修改特征与引导，而预训练权重这一潜在变异来源尚未被有效利用——无结构扰动要么视觉影响微弱，要么损害图像质量。

Method: CosPert针对经一次性敏感性分析筛选出的层中的注意力投影进行扰动：对每个投影使用共享随机变换及其转置，耦合更新奇异向量因子同时扰动谱增益；采用统一相对预算校准各投影的权重变化幅度；并用sigmoid调度使扰动影响集中于去噪早期、在细化阶段逐渐衰减。该方法为即插即用，扰动准备完成后采样无需辅助网络或额外模型评估。

Result: 在FLUX.1与Stable Diffusion 3.5上，CosPert提升了视觉创造力，产生更多样的物体结构同时保持类别身份；在50个物体类别上，其平均CREward为所评估方法中最高，并保持了良好的提示对齐。

Conclusion: CosPert表明预训练权重中的结构化谱扰动可作为一种有效的创造力增强来源，兼具免训练、即插即用、无额外推理开销的优势，且不牺牲语义忠实度。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models often produce high-quality images that follow familiar visual conventions, even in response to open-ended prompts such as ``a creative [CLS]''. Existing methods encourage novelty by exploring semantic conditions or modifying features and guidance during denoising. Pretrained weights offer a complementary source of creative variation, but unstructured perturbations can have little visual effect or degrade image quality. We introduce Coupled Spectral Perturbation (CosPert), a training-free method that targets attention projections in layers identified by a one-time sensitivity analysis. For each projection, CosPert uses a shared random transformation and its transpose to couple updates to the singular-vector factors while perturbing the spectral gains. A common relative budget calibrates the magnitude of weight changes across projections, and a sigmoid schedule concentrates their influence in early denoising and gradually attenuates them during refinement. CosPert provides plug-and-play creativity enhancement at inference time. Once the perturbations are prepared, sampling requires no auxiliary networks or additional model evaluations. On both FLUX.1 and Stable Diffusion 3.5, CosPert enhances visual creativity, producing more varied object structures while preserving category identity. Across 50 object categories, CosPert achieves the highest mean CREward among the evaluated methods while maintaining strong prompt alignment.

</details>


### [38] [Cross-Modal StateKV Injection for Communication in Multi-LLM Systems across Temporal Segments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22304)
*Xiaoqian Zhang,Zhihao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"跨模态状态KV注入"（Cross-Modal StateKV Injection）的提供者—载体框架：提供者模型观察互补视频帧，其视觉token位置的KV状态经对齐投影后以门控残差方式注入载体模型，在不延长载体KV缓存长度的前提下实现多模型间通信，并在MLVU与Video-MME上与纯文本通信基线对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型协同做视频理解时需要一个能传递互补视觉观测的通信接口。文本通信虽可解释，但引入中间生成阶段，且会丢失对后续推理有用的信息，因此需要直接在状态层面传递视觉信息。

Method: 框架由提供者与载体构成：提供者模型分别观看互补视频帧，提取视觉token位置的KV状态；这些状态经对齐与投影映射到载体的表示空间，再通过门控残差更新集成进载体的KV缓存。注入不改变载体KV序列长度，但总计算量包含提供者与通信模块的额外开销。实验在MLVU、Video-MME上比较载体单独推理、文本通信与StateKV（两种提供者架构）。

Result: StateKV相对文本通信基线取得幅度不大且依赖任务的准确率提升，报告延迟更低；部分类别持平或下降。在不延长载体缓存的情况下，增加提供者侧帧覆盖可在部分任务上带来额外增益。

Conclusion: 视觉KV状态层面的跨模型通信可行且有潜力，但收益有限。作者主张在明确控制观测预算与计算预算的条件下进一步研究视觉KV通信。

Abstract: Video understanding with multiple multimodal language models requires a communication interface that can transfer complementary visual observations. Text-based communication is interpretable, but introduces an intermediate generation stage and can omit information relevant to subsequent reasoning. We study Cross-Modal StateKV Injection, a provider--carrier framework that communicates through key--value (KV) states at visual-token positions. Provider models observe complementary video frames; their visual KV states are aligned and projected into the carrier representation space and integrated through gated residual updates. Injection preserves the carrier KV-cache sequence length, while retaining the additional computation required by the providers and the communication module. The reported experiments on MLVU and Video-MME compare carrier-only inference, text-based communication, and StateKV with two provider architectures. Results show modest, task-dependent accuracy gains and lower reported latency than the evaluated text-communication baseline, with some categories remaining unchanged or decreasing. Increasing provider-side frame coverage offers additional gains on selected tasks without extending the carrier cache. These findings motivate further study of visual KV communication under explicitly controlled observation and computation budgets.

</details>


### [39] [Multispectral-Polarization-Infrared Multi-Aperture Camouflaged Vehicle Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22246)
*Shu Wang,Xiong Lin,Yuan Song,Junjie Wang,Feng Huang,Wenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出光谱-偏振-红外三模态伪装车辆检测框架，配合多孔径成像装置，采用共识引导的决策级融合，在自建三模态数据集上取得82.7% mAP、16 ms端到端延迟，并在MID-1K数据集上验证了跨模态组合（RGB-深度-红外）的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪装会抑制各传感模态的判别性线索，单模态检测困难；现有多模态方法依赖像素级精确配准与特征级融合，导致精度与延迟之间的权衡，难以兼顾鲁棒性与实时可部署性。

Method: 构建光谱-偏振-红外三模态检测框架，并与多孔径成像装置协同设计以利用互补成像物理特性；提出共识引导的决策级融合策略，在有限配准容差范围内融合跨模态检测结果，降低对精确对齐的依赖；同时构建包含红外、偏振与光谱数据的三模态伪装车辆数据集。

Result: 在自建数据集上达到82.7% mAP，端到端延迟16 ms，优于代表性特征级融合方法；在公开MID-1K数据集上无需改动架构即可迁移至RGB-深度-红外模态组合，mAP@0.5较当前最优方法提升5.7%。

Conclusion: 结果验证了决策级共识融合与多孔径感知在实现鲁棒、可部署伪装车辆检测方面的潜力，说明以决策级融合替代像素级精确配准可同时兼顾精度与实时性。

Abstract: Camouflaged vehicle detection is challenging because camouflage suppresses discriminative cues across sensing modalities. Existing multimodal methods often require precise pixel-level registration and feature-level fusion, creating a trade-off between accuracy and latency. We propose a spectral-polarization-infrared tri-modal detection framework co-designed with a multi-aperture imaging setup to exploit complementary imaging physics. A consensus-guided decision-level fusion strategy combines cross-modal detections within bounded registration tolerances, reducing dependence on precise alignment while maintaining accuracy and real-time performance. We construct a tri-modal camouflaged vehicle dataset containing infrared, polarization, and spectral data. Experiments show that the proposed method achieves 82.7% mean Average Precision (mAP) with an end-to-end latency of 16 ms, outperforming representative feature-level fusion approaches. On the public MID-1K dataset, the framework generalizes to RGB-Depth-Infrared modalities without architectural modification and improves mAP@0.5 by 5.7% over state-of-the-art methods. These results demonstrate the potential of decision-level consensus fusion and multi-aperture sensing for robust and deployable camouflaged vehicle detection.

</details>


### [40] [PhyVGGT: Feed-Forward Articulated Object Reconstruction from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22244)
*Qi Shao,Zhihao Cao,Shuhao Zhai,Anh Nguyen,Hong Liang,Baoru Huang,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyVGGT：在冻结三维基础模型上单次前向从无标定单目视频重建铰接物体的可动部件、关节轴与枢轴并驱动未见状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从无标定单目视频重建带3D可动部件、关节参数与未观测铰接状态的铰接物体对机器人操作与交互式数字孪生至关重要。现有逐场景优化方法需数分钟至数小时且依赖外部深度或相机位姿；前馈3D基础模型可一次输出稠密点图但不分割可动部件、不推断关节；直接在单目点图上回归/拟合连续关节在随意拍摄视频上失败，原因包括深层注意力跨帧模糊运动部件边界、低估沿视线方向运动、小开合角下连续轴条件数差。

Method: 提出 PhyVGGT：基于冻结3D基础模型的单次前向框架，将可动部件匹配与闭式关节估计锚定到静止基座的未畸变几何。①部件与运动解码器：融合中间逐帧token与全局token，将部件query参照静态基座query，以分割3D部件、在静态基座上对齐点图并预测学习的Bingham先验；②运动学推断层：对静态表面法向拟合正交Manhattan坐标系，在结合成对运动散度与学习先验的Bingham后验下闭式选择关节轴，并解线性系统求铰链枢轴以驱动未见状态。

Result: 仅在合成数据上训练，PhyVGGT在五个基准上取得单目最优性能：超过FAMOS与单目ArtiPoint，中位误差优于需位姿RGB-D输入的ArtiPoint；48帧视频推理6.8秒，而Articulat3D逐场景优化需数小时。

Conclusion: 以冻结3D基础模型为几何锚、将部件分割与关节估计解耦为学习先验+闭式几何求解，可在单目随意视频上实现快速、可泛化的铰接物体重建与状态驱动，显著优于逐场景优化与前馈基线。

Abstract: Reconstructing an articulated object with its 3D movable parts, joint parameters and unseen articulation states from an uncalibrated monocular video is essential for robot manipulation and interactive digital twins. Existing per-scene optimisation methods require minutes to hours and external depth or camera poses, whereas feed-forward 3D foundation models reconstruct dense point maps in one pass without segmenting movable parts or inferring joints. Even on these monocular point maps, regressing or fitting continuous joints directly fails on casual videos, since cross-frame attention blurs moving part boundaries in deep layers, underestimates motion along the viewing ray and leaves continuous axes ill-conditioned under small opening angles. To address these challenges, we propose PhyVGGT, a single-pass framework on a frozen 3D foundation model that anchors moving-part matching and closed-form joint estimation to the undistorted geometry of the stationary base. First, our Part and Motion Decoder fuses intermediate per-frame tokens with global tokens and references part queries to a static-base query to segment 3D parts, align point maps on the static base and predict learned Bingham priors. Second, our Kinematic Inference Layer fits an orthogonal Manhattan frame to static surface normals, selects the joint axis in closed form under a Bingham posterior combining pairwise motion scatter with the learned priors, and solves a linear system for the hinge pivot to drive unseen states. Trained solely on synthetic data, PhyVGGT achieves state-of-the-art monocular performance across five benchmarks, outperforming FAMOS and monocular ArtiPoint, surpassing posed RGB-D ArtiPoint in median error, and running in 6.8 s per 48-frame video against hours of per-scene optimisation in Articulat3D. Project page: https://phyvggt.github.io.

</details>


### [41] [ExplicitIR: Explicit Spatial Policy Fields for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22222)
*Dezhi Zhang,Shangrong Yang,Yudong Liang,Wenjian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExplicitIR 将全能图像复原策略显式表示为逐像素的 Budget 场（更新位置与强度）与 Style 场（窄/宽上下文窗口选择），实现单次前向的空间自适应复原，无需任务标签、提示或测试时退化估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全能图像复原方法的适应策略隐式编码于特征表示中，复原决策既不可显式表示也无法独立控制；且真实退化具有空间非均匀性，全图均匀更新会导致干净区域过平滑、局部退化欠校正。

Method: 提出 ExplicitIR：由空间策略估计器（SPE）预测 Budget 与 Style 两个逐像素策略场；再由策略自适应解码块（PADB）通过窄窗与宽窗响应的自适应融合执行该策略，实现独立控制与精确局部调制。

Result: 在三任务与五任务基准上的大量实验表明，ExplicitIR 的性能与最强的全能基线方法相当（competitive）。

Conclusion: 将复原策略从隐式特征中解耦为显式、逐像素、可独立控制的场，是缓解过平滑与欠校正、实现空间自适应全能复原的有效途径，且无需额外标签、提示或退化估计模块。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration aims to recover clean images blindly from inputs with various degradation types and levels using a single model. However, the adaptation policy in most existing methods remains implicitly encoded in feature representations, leaving the restoration decision neither explicitly represented nor independently controllable. Since real-world degradations vary spatially, applying uniform updates across the entire image leads to over-smoothing of clean areas and under-correction of localized degradations. To address these limitations, we present ExplicitIR, which represents the restoration policy as two explicit per-pixel fields: Budget, which determines where and how strongly to update, and Style, which selects between narrow and wide context windows. Specifically, ExplicitIR incorporates a Spatial Policy Estimator (SPE) to predict these fields, explicitly representing the restoration policy and capturing spatially-varying degradations, which are then applied by Policy-Adaptive Decoder Blocks (PADB) through adaptive fusion of narrow- and wide-window responses, enabling independent control and precise local modulation. This design enables spatially-adaptive restoration in a single forward pass, without requiring task labels, prompts, or test-time degradation estimators. Extensive experiments on three-task and five-task benchmarks demonstrate that ExplicitIR achieves performance competitive with the strongest all-in-one baselines.

</details>


### [42] [Classification of Tissues on MALDI-MSI Image Modality for Pancreatic Cancer Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22680)
*Abubakar Qahir,Jenny Benois-Pineau,Victoria Bourgeais,Fabien Baldacci,Sandrine Dabernat,Nicolas Desbenoit,Mélanie Moreau,Mehdi Boubaddi,Marion Marty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一套全自动流水线，将原始MALDI-MSI数据转换为可直接用于深度学习的图像块，并训练模型用于胰腺组织（肿瘤/非肿瘤）二分类识别，效率与质量均优于交互式流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MSI预处理工具虽多，但缺乏面向深度学习的端到端自动化工作流；人工交互流程耗时且难以规模化。

Method: 构建全自动MALDI-MSI数据转换流水线，将原始谱数据转为图像块，并与参考交互式流程进行对比；基于生成数据训练独立的肿瘤区域识别模型。

Result: 自动流水线与参考流程输出图像在MSE指标上非常接近；平均计算时间约20分钟，而参考流程需一整天；高分辨率谱模型准确率达0.94，低分辨率谱平均准确率为0.59。

Conclusion: 该流水线在保证图像质量的同时大幅提升效率，可用于深度学习驱动的胰腺肿瘤MSI分类，但低分辨率谱下性能仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Mass Spectrometry Imaging (MSI) spatially maps molecular distributions in tissue sections. Despite the availability of preprocessing tools for MSI data, building end-to-end workflows suitable for deep learning remains challenging. We have developed a fully automated pipeline for transformation of raw Matrix-Assisted Laser Desorption/Ionization Mass Spectrometry imaging (MALDI-MSI) data into deep-learning-ready image patches for binary pancreatic tissue classification (tumoral vs non-tumoral) and built individual models for recognition of tumoral regions in spectral images. Images resulting from the proposed pipeline and from reference interactive workflow are very close in terms of image quality metric mean squared error (MSE). The proposed pipeline computational time is of 20 min in average compared to a full working day of the reference interactive workflow. The models achieved 0.94 accuracy on high-resolution spectra and a mean accuracy of 0.59 on lower-resolution spectra.

</details>


### [43] [Let Samples Sing in Chorus: Mitigating Spurious Correlations via Cross-view Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22668)
*Weiwei Li,Junzhuo Liu,Tong Chu,Hengfu Yu,Yinjie Lei,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CHORD：多视图跨视图一致性去偏框架，无需偏置监督即可提升最差组准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度模型依赖虚假相关，在少数群体上失效。现有方法或依赖人工指定的偏置属性（可能与模型真实使用的捷径不一致），或从单一表示空间推断未知偏置（证据不完整且含噪）。作者洞察：在某一特征空间中被遮蔽的偏置，在另一空间中可能更清晰。

Method: 提出 CHORD（Cross-view Harmonic Outlier Reversal for Debiasing）：让样本在多个表示空间中"合唱"以获得稳健的跨视图共识。在每一视图中聚合类内划分，将其作为候选偏置的代理，从而在无偏置监督下对目标表示与分类器进行去偏。

Result: 在视觉、语言、语音与医学影像四个模态的七个基准上取得一致的最差组准确率提升；代码与预训练模型已开源。

Conclusion: 跨视图共识是替代显式偏置标注进行稳健去偏的有效途径，方法具备跨模态通用性。

Abstract: Deep models often rely on spurious correlations that fail on underrepresented groups. Existing methods either correct human-specified bias attributes or infer unknown biases from the learned representation space. However, annotated attributes may differ from the shortcuts actually used by a model, while a single representation provides incomplete and potentially noisy evidence. Our key insight is that a bias obscured in one feature space may be clearer in another. In this work, we propose CHORD (Cross-view Harmonic Outlier Reversal for Debiasing), a multi-view framework that allows samples to "sing in chorus" across diverse representation spaces to achieve robust cross-view consensus. CHORD aggregates aggregates within-class partitions in each view, which is a proxy for specifying candidate biases, to debias the target representation and classifier without bias supervision. Experiments on seven benchmarks across vision, language, speech, and medical imaging show consistent improvements in worst-group accuracy. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/davelee-uestc/chord_debiasing_public .

</details>


### [44] [OmniTongue: A Comprehensive MLLM Benchmark for Automated Tongue Diagnosis in Traditional Chinese Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22652)
*Liuyi Xu,Yun Guo,Shusheng Li,Xuewen Zhang,Yining Qian,An-Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个面向中医舌诊的综合细粒度VQA基准OmniTongue，含1351张专家校验图像、21616条指令对、16个临床维度、区域级病理标注与66个细粒度标签，系统评测显示现有MLLM存在显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在西方医学影像上表现优异，但在中医舌诊中的潜力尚未被系统评估。舌诊需全局与局部协同感知，是检验MLLM视觉理解深度的严格试金石；而现有数据集维度单一、粒度粗糙、标签模糊，无法评估真实临床能力。

Method: 构建OmniTongue基准：整合1351张专家验证图像与21616条指令对；核心设计包括16个临床维度的多维评估、面向病理定位的区域级标注、66个细粒度标签以精确表征诊断结果；并在此基础上对现有MLLM开展大规模评测。

Result: 评测揭示显著性能差距：医学专用模型并未呈现领域优势；各模型Macro-F1普遍偏低，表明其依赖统计捷径而非对舌象病理的真实理解。

Conclusion: 研究凸显MLLM在区域性医学特征理解上的局限，并确立OmniTongue对发展鲁棒自动化舌诊模型的价值；代码与数据集将后续公开发布。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have excelled in Western medical imaging, yet their potential in Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) tongue diagnosis remains systematically unevaluated. Tongue diagnosis requires sophisticated global and local perception, serving as a rigorous touchstone for assessing the visual understanding depth of MLLMs. However, existing datasets are limited by single dimensions, coarse granularity, and ambiguous labels, hindering the evaluation of authentic clinical capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce OmniTongue, the first comprehensive, fine-grained Visual Question Answering benchmark for TCM tongue diagnosis. This benchmark integrates 1,351 expert-validated images and 21,616 instruction pairs. Its core design features 16 clinical dimensions for multidimensional evaluation, region-specific annotations for pathological localization, and 66 fine-grained labels for precise diagnostic representation. Extensive evaluations of existing MLLMs reveal significant performance gaps, demonstrating that medical-specific models lack domain advantages, while universally poor Macro-F1 scores expose a reliance on statistical shortcuts rather than genuine understanding of tongue pathology. These findings not only highlight the current limitations of MLLMs in interpreting regional medical features but also underscore OmniTongue's significance for developing robust automated tongue diagnosis models. The code and dataset will be made publicly available in a future release.

</details>


### [45] [Strong Fused Performance Can Mask Branch Collapse in a Dual-View Heart-Sound Classifier: A Three-Part Diagnostic Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22556)
*Safayet Khan,Hasan Zohirul Islam,Md. Tauhid Bin Iqbal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出融合模型的融合分数无法证明各分支都学到有用表示，在双视图心音分类中可能出现"分支塌缩"；作者提出三部分诊断方法，发现AudioFuse的频谱图分支实际未被使用，并归因于早期优化不平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常仅以融合分数评价融合模型，隐含假设每个分支都学到了有用信息。作者怀疑该假设可能静默失效，因此在双视图心音分类任务中检验分支是否真正被利用。

Method: 以AudioFuse（频谱图Transformer + 波形CNN）为对象，提出三部分分支塌缩诊断：(1)删除某分支是否改变融合分数；(2)对其特征做线性探针是否优于同结构未训练副本；(3)该分支单独训练时能否学会其视图。并结合更新统计与初始化实验分析成因，以及人工标签线索迁移实验。

Result: 从头联合训练时，尽管融合性能强，频谱图分支被删除后融合分数几乎无变化；其特征线性探针相对未训练副本提升甚微；但同架构单独训练AUC超过0.9，说明该视图可学，而联合训练模型在必须依赖该分支时接近随机。更新统计与初始化实验指向早期优化不平衡；将强人工标签线索从一个输入移到另一个输入可切换模型依赖的分支。该塌缩在PhysioNet上观察到，但在ICBHI和CirCor上未满足完整诊断。

Conclusion: 仅凭融合性能无法确认每个分支学到了有意义的表示。建议在报告融合指标的同时，报告分支删除实验与未训练对照探针，以识别静默的分支塌缩。

Abstract: Fusion models are usually judged by their fused score, a practice that assumes that every branch has learned something useful. We show that this assumption can fail silently in dual-view heart-sound classification. In AudioFuse, which fuses a spectrogram transformer with a waveform CNN, the spectrogram branch is unused despite strong fused performance when both branches are trained from scratch. We introduce a three-part diagnostic for branch collapse: whether deleting a branch changes the fused score, whether a linear probe on its features beats an untrained copy of the same branch, and whether the branch can learn its view when trained alone. Deleting the branch has negligible effect, and probing its features shows little improvement over an identically initialised, untrained copy. Yet the same architecture trained alone exceeds 0.9 AUC, so its view is learnable, while the jointly trained model is near chance when it must rely on this branch. Update statistics and an initialisation experiment point to an early optimisation imbalance. Moving a strong artificial label cue from one input to the other can switch which branch the model relies on, even though the branch architectures stay the same. We observe branch collapse on PhysioNet, but the full diagnostic is not met on ICBHI or CirCor. Fused performance alone therefore cannot establish that every branch learned a meaningful representation, and we recommend reporting branch deletion and an untrained-control probe alongside it.

</details>


### [46] [Fast Training-Free Low-Light Video Enhancement with a Frozen Image Diffusion Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22551)
*Hon Ming Yam,Chun Pong Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种因果、免训练的暗光视频增强流水线，在冻结的MR. Illuminate图像先验上加入时序输入融合与延迟起始偏移传播两个时序组件，实现比ZVRD快261倍且在多项指标上更优的增强效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有唯一免训练的暗光视频方法ZVRD速度过慢（256×256单帧需一分钟以上），难以实用；而预训练图像扩散模型可在无暗光训练数据、无需针对新相机重训的情况下增强暗光视频，因此需要一种高效的无训练方案。

Method: 1）时序输入融合：在扩散反演前对运动补偿后的暗光帧进行递归平均；2）延迟起始偏移传播：仅在关键帧上运行完整采样轨迹，非关键帧只反演自身轨迹的低噪声部分，并叠加从最近关键帧变换（warp）得到的增强偏移；整体采用因果结构，基于冻结的图像先验模型。

Result: 在ZVRD自身分辨率下，流水线比ZVRD快261倍，且五项报告指标全部更优；在13个留出的SDSD 1280×720片段上，相比逐帧八步处理PSNR提升0.55 dB、LPIPS提升0.034、warping误差减半，耗时还少10%。模型内部测量与线性高斯反演模型解释了原因：融合在反演放大噪声前去除帧间噪声，且光流误差对传输偏移的扰动比传输状态小十倍。但感知增益不迁移到低分辨率，346×260下LPIPS反而差0.028。

Conclusion: 该因果免训练流水线显著提升暗光视频扩散增强的效率与质量，但0.65 fps的帧率使其属于离线方法，且感知收益在高分辨率下成立、低分辨率下失效。

Abstract: Pretrained image diffusion models can enhance low-light video without low-light training data and without retraining for a new camera. ZVRD, the only published training-free method for low-light video, is too slow to use: our implementation needs over a minute per $256 \times 256$ frame. We present a causal, training-free pipeline that adds two temporal components to a frozen MR. Illuminate image prior. Temporal input fusion recursively averages motion-compensated low-light frames before diffusion inversion. Late-start offset propagation runs the full sampling trajectory only on key frames. Each non-key frame inverts the low-noise part of its own trajectory and adds an enhancement offset warped from the latest key frame. At ZVRD's own resolution the pipeline is $261\times$ faster than ZVRD and scores better on all five metrics we report. On 13 held-out SDSD clips at $1280 \times 720$, it improves on framewise eight-step processing by 0.55 dB in PSNR and 0.034 in LPIPS and halves the warping error, in 10% less time. Measurements inside the model show why, and a linear-Gaussian model of inversion explains them. Fusion removes frame-to-frame noise before inversion amplifies it. A flow error perturbs the transported offset ten times less than it perturbs a transported state. The perceptual gain does not transfer to low resolution: at $346 \times 260$, LPIPS is worse by 0.028. At 0.65 frames per second, the pipeline is an offline method.

</details>


### [47] [ATS: Anti-Aliased Triangle Splatting with Exact Per-Primitive Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22512)
*Brent Zoomers,Joni Vanherck,Leander Winters,Jeroen Put,Lode Jorissen,Nick Michiels*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将 Triangle Splatting 的像素中心点采样替换为窗口函数在像素上的精确闭式积分，并据此实现保守剔除，从而在无需重训的情况下实现抗锯齿、优于 2x2 超采样的时域稳定性和多尺度训练能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Triangle Splatting 在像素中心评估三角形并对内切圆半径小于一像素的三角形做剔除，导致严重走样；同时低分辨率下会误剔除大量三角形，丢失场景内容。

Method: 利用三角形角平分线将其划分为三个子三角形，在每个子三角形内窗口函数是到某条边距离的幂函数，因而具有闭式原函数；借助格林公式把积分及其梯度化为每个子三角形被像素裁剪后每条边上的单项贡献，得到整三角形上的闭式积分，并据此设计只剔除在任何像素上都无法达到 alpha 阈值的三角形的保守剔除策略。

Result: 精确积分可直接对已有模型做抗锯齿而无需重训练；时域走样少于 2x2 超采样；支持多尺度训练，在降低分辨率下达到或超过超采样训练的效果。

Conclusion: 像素级精确解析积分是 Triangle Splatting 采样与剔除问题的有效统一解法，兼顾画质、时域稳定性与多尺度效率。

Abstract: Triangle Splatting represents scenes using semi-transparent triangles, but it evaluates each triangle at the pixel center and culls any triangle with an inradius smaller than one pixel. The former causes severe aliasing, while the latter culls most of the scene at lower resolutions. We show that the integral of the Triangle Splatting window over a pixel admits an exact closed-form solution. The angle bisectors split the triangle into three sub-triangles, in each of which the window is a power of the distance to one edge. Each piece therefore has a closed-form antiderivative, and Green's theorem reduces the integral and its gradients to one term per side of the sub-triangle clipped against the pixel. The integral over the whole triangle, which we show has a closed form, then allows conservative culling, removing only triangles that cannot meet the renderer's alpha cutoff at any pixel. This exact formulation anti-aliases existing models without retraining, exhibits less temporal aliasing than 2x2 supersampling, and enables multi-scale training, matching or outperforming supersampled training at reduced resolutions.

</details>


### [48] [Joint-ID: Transformer-based Joint Image Enhancement and Depth Estimation for Underwater Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22212)
*Geonmo Yang,Gilhwan Kang,Juhui Lee,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Joint-ID，一种基于Transformer的神经网络，通过联合学习同时实现水下图像增强与深度估计，在共享分层特征空间中端到端训练，并构建多任务合成数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下成像受散射、吸收与湍流影响，导致图像质量下降、可见度受限，需同时提升图像质量并获取深度信息以支撑水下机器人视觉感知。

Method: 构建Transformer骨干网络Joint-ID，从原始水下图像同时输出高感知质量图像与深度；设计考虑无效深度、清晰度不足及基于颜色与局部纹理的图像退化的多模态目标函数；采用共享分层特征空间的端到端联合修复与深度估计训练流程；并提出含多种失真与场景深度的合成数据集用于多任务学习。

Result: 在合成数据集、标准数据集及具有多样光谱和强浑浊度的真实水下图像上评估，验证了图像增强与深度估计的有效性；并展示Joint-ID可完成特征匹配与显著性检测。

Conclusion: 联合学习策略可有效提升水下视觉感知，适用于海洋学与水下机器人等应用场景。

Abstract: Underwater imaging is a challenging task due to factors such as scattering, absorption, and turbulence, which degrade image quality and limit visibility. In this paper, we propose a novel approach for enhancing underwater images that leverages the benefits of joint learning for simultaneous image enhancement and depth estimation. We introduce Joint-ID, a transformer-based neural network that can obtain high-perceptual image quality and depth information from raw underwater images. Our approach formulates a multi-modal objective function that addresses invalid depth, lack of sharpness, and image degradation based on color and local texture. We design an end-to-end training pipeline that enables joint restoration and depth estimation in a shared hierarchical feature space. Additionally, we propose a synthetic dataset with various distortions and scene depths for multi-task learning. We evaluate Joint-ID on synthetic and standard datasets, as well as real underwater images with diverse spectra and harsh turbidity, demonstrating its effectiveness for underwater image enhancement and depth estimation. Furthermore, we demonstrate the ability of Joint-ID to perform feature matching and saliency detection for visually guided underwater robots. Our proposed method has the potential to improve the visual perception of underwater environments and benefit applications such as oceanography and underwater robotics. Supplements are available at: https://sites.google.com/view/joint-id/home

</details>


### [49] [Centroid Spectral Coupling: Implicit Alignment and Representation Diversity in Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22158)
*M. Hadi Sepanj,Benyamin Ghojogh,Saed Moradi,Paul Fieguth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出单项目标函数 CSC，通过中心化质心谱耦合统一实现对视图对齐、表示多样性和防坍缩的隐式约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非对比 SSL 方法依赖多个加权损失项分别处理视图对齐、表示多样性与防坍缩，导致超参调优复杂。作者希望用单一目标隐式耦合这三项需求。

Method: 从独立归一化的增强视图对构造质心，对去中心化二阶矩矩阵施加 ridge 谱目标；保留质心模长编码视图一致性，并通过谱方向上的能量均衡促进多样性。理论上给出非负对齐项与谱失衡项的精确分解、全局最优几何刻画、对齐与多样性的有限损失保证，并证明常数输出坍缩在归一化表示空间中是严格鞍点。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Imagenette 上，CSC 在非对比方法中线性 Top-1 最高，在两个 CIFAR 数据集上 kNN Top-1 也最高。ridge 参数敏感性实验显示 κ 增大增强视图对齐但降低有效秩，呈经验性对齐—多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 统一谱目标无需显式负样本或分别加权的对齐/多样性惩罚即可获得有竞争力的视觉表示，验证了单项目标实现多目标的可行性。

Abstract: Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) aims to align augmented views while preserving representation diversity and preventing collapse. Existing non-contrastive methods often address these goals using multiple weighted loss terms. We introduce Centroid Spectral Coupling (CSC), a single-term SSL objective that implicitly couples view alignment, representation diversity, and collapse resistance. CSC constructs centroids from independently normalized augmented-view pairs and applies a ridge-based spectral objective to their uncentered second-moment matrix. Preserving centroid magnitudes encodes view agreement while encouraging balanced representation energy across spectral directions. We establish an exact decomposition into nonnegative alignment and spectral-imbalance components, characterize the geometry of global optima, and derive finite-loss guarantees for alignment and diversity. We also show that complete constant-output collapse is a strict saddle in normalized representation space. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Imagenette show that CSC achieves the highest linear Top-1 accuracy among the evaluated non-contrastive methods on all three benchmarks, and the highest kNN Top-1 accuracy on both CIFAR datasets. A ridge-parameter sensitivity study reveals an empirical alignment--diversity trade-off: increasing $κ$ strengthens view alignment but reduces representation effective rank. These results show that a unified spectral objective yields competitive visual representations without explicit contrastive negatives or separately weighted alignment and diversity penalties.

</details>


### [50] [Text-Guided Modality Correction for Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22126)
*Yu Quan,Dong Zhang,Ping Gong,Peng Huang,JinHui Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出文本引导模态校正网络TMCNet，将文本引导融入区域特征提取、跨模态对齐与多尺度解码，提升开放词汇目标检测的未见过类别识别与多尺度检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OVD方法存在三点不足：对目标区域缺乏充分的文本引导；跨模态对齐策略有限；未充分利用跨层级互补特征，从而限制了未见过类别及多尺度目标的检测性能。

Method: TMCNet包含三个模块：(1) 文本引导模态编码器，将文本语义注入查询以引导区域特征聚合，降低表示语义歧义；(2) 跨模态对齐模块，通过视觉与文本特征交互强化区域表示与类别语义的对应；(3) 渐进式文本校正解码器，结合自顶向下通路与横向连接，按尺度逐步融合文本引导的深层语义特征与浅层视觉特征。

Result: 在COCO-OVD与LVIS-OVD两个公开数据集上，仅以简单文本查询为引导即取得较强的开放词汇检测性能；扩展至开放词汇分割任务亦有性能提升。

Conclusion: TMCNet通过多阶段文本引导有效增强区域-类别语义对应与跨层级特征融合，具备跨任务适用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) is essential for detecting objects beyond the categories seen during training. Establishing vision-language associations is crucial for recognizing unseen categories, while integrating complementary visual features across levels supports detection at different scales. However, existing methods often provide insufficient textual guidance for object regions, rely on limited cross-modal alignment strategies, and underuse complementary features across levels, limiting their ability to detect unseen categories and objects of varying scales. To address these limitations, we propose a Text-Guided Modality Correction Network (TMCNet) that incorporates textual guidance into region feature extraction, cross-modal alignment, and multi-scale decoding to strengthen region-category semantic correspondence and promote complementary feature fusion across levels. First, a text-guided modality encoder incorporates textual semantics into queries to guide regional feature aggregation, reducing semantic ambiguity in object representations. Second, a cross-modal alignment module strengthens the correspondence between region representations and category semantics through interactions between visual and textual features. Finally, a progressive text correction decoder combines a top-down pathway with lateral connections to progressively fuse text-guided deep semantic features with shallow visual features at the corresponding scales, preserving both semantic information and spatial detail for objects of different sizes. Experiments on two public datasets, COCO-OVD and LVIS-OVD, demonstrate that TMCNet achieves strong performance in open-vocabulary object detection using simple text queries as guidance. Extending TMCNet to open-vocabulary segmentation also yields performance gains, demonstrating its cross-task applicability and extensibility.

</details>


### [51] [Where Should the Lamp Go? Ambient-Aware Lamp Placement for Portraits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22005)
*Yuyan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LightMatch 偏好驱动的逆向布光框架，分离可控光源与环境光并优化光源位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光照估计与肖像重打光均为拍摄后编辑，缺乏"拍摄前光应放在哪里"的指导。

Method: 先用成对人工判断学习 Bradley-Terry 质量模型；感知头通过交叉注意力从冻结自监督特征回归球谐光照；再用球谐线性性分离出可控光源并求解其可达位置。

Result: 留出环境下达到 oracle 改进的 87.8%，相对优于忽略环境光的传统求解器 28.4%、相对优于使用 DPR 估计器的同流程 70.5%；未见身份真实 OLAT 人脸达 73.2%；实拍中灯方向误差降低 37.7%。

Conclusion: 偏好建模与球谐分解可有效指导拍摄前的布光位置优化，并在真实场景中验证有效性。

Abstract: Portraits shape first impressions, and lighting is an important factor. Prior work, such as lighting estimation and portrait relighting, edits lighting after capture but does not guide where to place a light before the shot. This leaves an open question: where should a light go to best light a face? Therefore, we propose LightMatch, a preference-grounded inverse lighting framework that disentangles the controllable light from ambient illumination and optimizes its placement. We first learn a Bradley-Terry quality model from pairwise human judgments. A perception head then regresses spherical-harmonic lighting from frozen self-supervised features via cross-attention. Finally, a placement solver isolates the light via spherical-harmonic linearity and optimizes its reachable position. On held-out environments, LightMatch attains 87.8% of the oracle improvement, outperforming the conventional solver that ignores ambient light by a relative 28.4% and the same pipeline with the widely used DPR estimator by a relative 70.5%. It reaches 73.2% on real OLAT faces of unseen identities and, on our real captures, cuts lamp-direction error by 37.7%.

</details>


### [52] [DEFTS: Data-Efficient, Training-Free Few-Shot Learning for CLIP in the Text Subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21993)
*Yijin Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DEFTS 是一种仅在文本子空间内操作的数据高效、免训练的小样本分类方法：将估计的图像原型投影到文本嵌入张成的线性子空间，用贝叶斯后验均值求解少量线性组合系数，显著优于现有免训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CLIP 小样本方法主要依赖图像嵌入，未充分利用文本嵌入的语义结构；而原始文本嵌入作为直接分类器又往往次优。

Method: 先实证表明原始文本嵌入虽不适合作分类器，但其张成的线性子空间仍具强判别性；据此将估计的图像原型投影入该子空间，把高维估计问题化为少数系数的求解，系数通过以原始文本嵌入坐标为先验中心的贝叶斯后验均值计算，从而缩小图文模态差距。

Result: 在 12 个图像分类基准上，DEFTS 显著优于现有免训练方法。

Conclusion: 将小样本适配限制在文本子空间内并借助贝叶斯系数估计，是一种有效且免训练、数据高效的 CLIP 小样本分类范式。

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) achieves impressive zero-shot image classification performance by comparing image embeddings against class-specific text embeddings. While recent few-shot methods leverage labeled training samples to improve upon this zero-shot baseline, they often predominantly rely on image embeddings and underutilize the semantic structure of the text embeddings. In this paper, we introduce DEFTS, a data-efficient, training-free few-shot classification method strictly in the text subspace. We first demonstrate empirically that while raw text embeddings can be suboptimal as direct classifiers, the linear subspace they span remains highly discriminative. DEFTS therefore proposes to reduce the image-text modality gap by projecting estimated image prototypes directly into this subspace. Importantly, by expressing these prototypes as linear combinations of text embeddings, DEFTS reduces a high-dimensional estimation problem to the derivation of just a few coefficients. These coefficients are then computed via a Bayesian posterior mean, using a prior naturally centered on the original text embedding coordinates. Extensive experiments across 12 image classification benchmarks demonstrate that DEFTS significantly improves upon existing training-free methods.

</details>


### [53] [Wrong Input, Wrong Threshold: Re-evaluating Adversarial Facial Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21923)
*Tunahan Parlayıcı,Erchan Aptoula,Yücel Saygın*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出，人脸隐私保护方法宣称的"90%以上"防护率源于存在双重缺陷的评测协议：输入端未复现部署系统的检测-对齐-裁剪流程，度量端使用饱和的成功率差且阈值失配；作者改用成对余弦位移指标并引入可微对齐算子重新评测，发现原方法防护被严重高估，而可微对齐可显著恢复防护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发表的人脸隐私（对抗扰动）方法普遍报告对黑盒识别模型90%以上的防护成功率，但这些数字来自一个具有两个独立缺陷的评测协议，且所用度量本身无法量化任一缺陷的规模。因此需要诊断缺陷来源、提出不受标定影响的度量，并给出修复方案。

Method: (1) 输入缺陷：2022年以来的方法将整张照片或检测框直接送入网络，而实际部署系统会先检测、对齐到关键点模板再裁剪；作者将同一批图像经真实前端重打分。(2) 度量缺陷：成功率差会饱和，故提出"成对余弦位移"指标，使两个阈值在代数上相互抵消，无需依赖具体标定。(3) 阈值缺陷：检验沿用自2021年的四个常数阈值。(4) 归因与修复：分析失配成因为重采样不匹配而非裁剪外扰动丢失，提出通过可微对齐算子进行优化，并恢复图库打分。

Result: 经真实前端重打分后，标准迁移攻击损失56%的规避防护，预算分配攻击损失7%（未重新生成任何图像）。成功率差指标在预算扫描中变动8.2倍，而其下真实效应仅变动1.34倍。改用成对余弦位移后，在三类未见识别器上，输入缺陷导致的效应分别放大2.2倍与10.7倍；在匹配预算下，L∞迁移排序被反转——唯一在评测时做对齐的调查方法增益0.18，而MI-FGSM损失0.05。可微对齐优化在未见识别器与一个商用系统上带来+24至+58个百分点的提升，但在其中一个系统上仍有残差。四个继承阈值在对齐裁剪上使误接受率接近名义1%，而在整图上放宽至2.3–5.4%，且在规避与冒充两个任务上存在符号相反的偏差。

Conclusion: 人脸隐私保护领域报告的90%以上防护率是评测协议缺陷造成的虚高；输入前端、度量指标与阈值三者均需修正。可微对齐与图库打分并非新思想，而是第一代文献已采用、后被丢弃的组件，本文量化了丢弃它们的代价。作者呼吁以成对余弦位移作为不受标定影响的稳健度量，并在优化与评测中统一采用可微对齐前端。

Abstract: Published face-privacy methods report protection above 90% against black-box recognition models. That number comes from an evaluation protocol with two independent defects, reported in a measure that cannot size either one. The input is wrong: methods published since 2022 feed the network the whole photograph, or a detector's box, where deployed systems detect, align to a landmark template and crop. Re-scoring the same files through a realistic front end costs a standard transfer attack 56% of its dodging protection and a budget-allocating attack 7%, with no image regenerated. The measure is wrong: that loss is a difference of success rates, so it saturates, moving by 8.2x over a budget sweep while the effect beneath it moves by 1.34x. We therefore report the paired cosine shift, in which both thresholds cancel algebraically, so the input defect holds without committing to a calibration. Measured that way it is 2.2x and 10.7x larger on three unseen recognizers, and at matched budget it reverses the L-infinity transfer ranking: the one surveyed method that aligns at evaluation gains 0.18 where MI-FGSM loses 0.05. The cause is a resampling mismatch, not perturbation lost outside the crop; optimising through a differentiable alignment operator largely removes it, adding +24 to +58 points on unseen recognizers and a commercial system, with a residual on one of them. The thresholds are wrong: the four constants inherited since 2021 hold the false-accept rate near its nominal 1% on aligned crops but admit 2.3-5.4% on whole images, with an opposite-signed bias across dodging and impersonation, the two tasks reported side by side. Differentiable alignment and gallery scoring are not new: the first generation of this literature used both, and we show what dropping them cost.

</details>


### [54] [Sketch, Don't Paint: Allocating Test-Time Compute in Generative Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21812)
*Sheng Lu,Guangpin Tao,Tong Lu,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 多模态推理模型将视觉思维纳入思维链，但每个视觉思维都沿用完整扩散去噪流程，推理代价高昂；本文研究测试时算力应如何沿“何时渲染视觉思维”与“每个思维需多少去噪步数”两轴分配，并提出按需绘制与 sketch-and-vote 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错式多模态推理中，视觉思维（像素合成、迷宫路径草图、逐帧视频推演）均继承自逼真图像生成的全套扩散调度，迭代去噪主导了推理开销；同时“总是绘制”未必最优，需要弄清视觉思维何时有益、需要多少去噪步数。

Method: 通过仅改变思维渲染方式的受控干预，识别出三种机制（因果性思维、装饰性思维、有害性思维）；在 3 个交错推理器、14 个模型-基准组合上比较直接作答、纯文本、视觉三档；并为“绘制有收益”的情形提出 sketch-and-vote：从独立噪声采样 8 步草图（步数无需标签选定），直到某答案获得三票。

Result: 8 步草图在准确率上近乎匹配 50 步完整渲染，且计划一致性与两次独立完整渲染间的一致性相当；视频模型中失败多表现为停滞未达目标，故推演弃权。14 个组合中直接作答在 9 个组合胜出；用每组合 10 个标注样例选择档位，平均准确率从 60.7%（总是绘制）升至 65.1%。sketch-and-vote 在图像推理器与两个视频推理器上以更少去噪步数超越完整渲染：VSP 空间规划 +8.6 点，迷宫任务 +9.6 与 +22.1 点。

Conclusion: 视觉思维所需去噪步数远低于逼真合成，且其效用依模型与任务而异，故统一绘制次优；应按需分配测试时算力，并在绘制有效处采用少步草图加投票的推断策略。

Abstract: Multimodal models increasingly reason by synthesizing pixels, sketching maze paths within interleaved chains of thought or rolling out video solutions frame by frame. Each visual thought runs the full diffusion schedule inherited from photorealistic synthesis, so iterative denoising dominates inference cost. We study how to allocate test-time compute along two axes: when to render visual thoughts rather than thinking in text or answering directly, and how many denoising steps each thought requires. Controlled interventions that vary only how the thought is rendered uncover three regimes. Causal thoughts carry the plan and become legible early: an 8-step sketch nearly matches a 50-step render in accuracy, commits to the same plan as often as two independent full renders agree, and in video models mostly fails by stalling short of the goal, so the rollout abstains. Decorative thoughts can be omitted without loss, whereas harmful thoughts lower accuracy. Because the regime depends on both model and task, always drawing is suboptimal. Across 14 model-benchmark pairs of three interleaved reasoners, answering directly, without any thinking, wins 9 pairs, and choosing among direct, text-only, and visual tiers from ten labeled examples per pair raises mean accuracy from 60.7% (always drawing) to 65.1%. Where drawing pays, we propose sketch-and-vote, which samples 8-step sketches (a step count chosen without labels) from independent noises until one answer reaches three votes. With this one setting, sketch-and-vote beats full rendering with fewer denoising steps on an image reasoner and two video reasoners, by 8.6 points on VSP spatial planning and by 9.6 and 22.1 points on mazes.

</details>


### [55] [Beyond Co-Generation: Rethinking Jointness in Joint Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22857)
*Jihoo Jung,Ji-Hoon Kim,Suyeon Lee,Gia Thinh Pham,Joon Son Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"内在联合性"(intrinsic jointness)概念，用于甄别T2AV生成中真正的跨模态协同与单纯文本跟随，发现现有模型该能力有限，并提出JointREPA后训练方法与UNSAIDBench基准以改善语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2AV生成的音视频一致性评估存在混淆：语义详细的提示词无法判断一致性究竟源于真实的跨模态协同，还是音视频两模态各自独立地遵循文本。需要一个能隔离出真正联合性的评估视角。

Method: 1) 提出intrinsic jointness定义：在提示词未指明细节时，音频与视频能否产生相同的具体实现；2) 使用语义欠指定提示（如"演奏乐器"而非"弹电吉他"）评测四个近期模型；3) 提出JointREPA，一种基于表征对齐的简洁后训练方法以促进跨模态协同；4) 构建UNSAIDBench，专门评测语义欠指定下的内在联合性。

Result: 对四个近期模型的评测显示其内在联合性普遍有限；在两个不同模型与三个基准（含UNSAIDBench）上的实验表明，JointREPA能持续提升语义一致性。

Conclusion: 内在联合性为评估T2AV真实跨模态协同提供了可行视角，JointREPA作为通用后训练手段可稳定改善语义一致性，UNSAIDBench填补了欠指定场景下的评测空白。

Abstract: Our aim is to advance the understanding of genuine audio-visual coordination in joint text-to-audio-video (T2AV) generation, with particular emphasis on semantic consistency. Evaluating audio-visual consistency in T2AV generation is nontrivial, as detailed text prompts obscure whether such consistency stems from genuine cross-modal coordination or merely from independent text following. To isolate the former, we introduce intrinsic jointness: the ability of audio and video to produce the same realization for details left underspecified by the prompt. Using semantically underspecified prompts (e.g., "musical instrument playing" rather than "electric guitar playing"), we investigate four recent models and reveal their limited intrinsic jointness. To address this limitation, we propose JointREPA, a simple representation alignment-based post-training method to facilitate cross-modal coordination. Since existing benchmarks use semantically detailed prompts, we introduce UNSAIDBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate intrinsic jointness under semantic underspecification. Experiments across two distinct models and three benchmarks, including UNSAIDBench, demonstrate that JointREPA consistently improves semantic consistency.

</details>


### [56] [Propose & Verify: Fast Reflection Symmetry Detection via Harmonic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22856)
*Yishai Lavi,Shai Avidan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的 FAST 框架，将几何成对投票与球谐域谱计算结合，可高效检测 3D 形状的多个反射对称平面，速度超次优 GPU 基线 100 倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测 3D 形状的多重反射对称需要在大量候选平面上聚合几何证据，传统投票方法计算代价高昂，难以在噪声下快速、可靠地同时找出多个对称面。

Method: 利用全局反射平面必过形状质心这一事实，把搜索降为平面朝向的搜索；用几何一致性过滤与独立见证机制剔除不可靠的成对假设；将投票视为定义在平面朝向（球面）上的信号，借助 Funk–Hecke 定理（旋转对称核下傅里叶卷积定理的球面对应）把高斯平滑转化为谐波系数相乘，从而在球谐域高效计算连续平滑的对称场，再从中提取、细化并几何验证对称候选。

Result: 在两个基准（含一个高度对称形状的新基准）上取得所评估方法中最高的干净多平面检测 F1（0.861 与 0.947），每形状 GPU 推理低于 4 ms，比次快的多平面 GPU 基线快 100 倍以上；对几何噪声鲁棒，单平面检测成功率 94.9%，具竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过将平面朝向投票映射到球谐域并用 Funk–Hecke 定理加速平滑，FAST 以训练无关方式实现了高精度、毫秒级的多反射对称检测，兼顾速度、鲁棒性与单/多平面性能。

Abstract: Detecting multiple reflection symmetries in 3D shapes requires aggregating geometric evidence over a large space of candidate planes, making traditional voting approaches computationally expensive. We introduce the Fourier Accelerated Symmetry Transform (FAST), a training-free framework that combines geometric pair voting with efficient spectral computation. Exploiting the fact that global reflection planes pass through the shape centroid, we reduce the search to plane orientations, while geometric consistency filtering and independent witnesses suppress unreliable pair hypotheses. Our key insight is to represent these votes as a signal over plane orientations and efficiently compute a continuous, smoothed symmetry field in the spherical-harmonic domain. Specifically, we exploit the Funk-Hecke theorem, the spherical counterpart of the Fourier convolution theorem for rotationally symmetric kernels, to transform Gaussian smoothing into multiplication of harmonic coefficients. This enables efficient field evaluation, from which symmetry candidates are extracted, refined, and geometrically verified. Across two benchmarks, including a new benchmark of highly symmetric shapes, FAST achieves the highest clean multi-plane detection $F_1$ scores among evaluated methods (0.861 and 0.947), with GPU inference below 4 ms per shape: over $100\times$ faster than the next-fastest evaluated multi-plane GPU baseline. FAST also demonstrates strong robustness to geometric noise and competitive single-plane detection, achieving 94.9% success on clean single-plane shapes.

</details>


### [57] [RefCloth: Transferable Adversarial Tablecloths for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22736)
*Shaozhen Liu,Zhiwei Li,Jiayang Sun,Shuangjun Du,Yi Zhong,Shenrui Ma,Yaozu Liu,Qi Li,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefCloth 通过在冻结源策略上优化一块持久的对抗性桌布纹理，实现无需改动任务物体或相机的零样本迁移攻击，在原生仿真中跨任务、跨模型显著降低 VLA 策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对闭环 VLA 策略的视觉攻击依赖 2D 补丁劫持传感器流，或修改目标物体网格，后者在操作对象变化时需要重新训练，缺乏持久性与泛化性。作者希望用单一、与场景绑定的物理纹理实现跨任务、跨模型的零样本攻击。

Method: 在冻结的源策略上优化一块世界绑定的对抗桌布纹理；该纹理在静态第三人称视角与移动腕部视角下经历动态遮挡。为弥合可微渲染器与原生渲染器之间的差距并泛化到变化的表面视角，方法采用有界纹理参数化、共享表面 UV 变换，以及初始化时消除静态渲染偏移的同路径干净参考。部署时不修改任务物体或相机，直接零样本迁移。

Result: 在原生仿真的四个 LIBERO 套件上，同一块冻结纹理从 OpenVLA-OFT 迁移至 π0 与 π0.5，相较未优化桌布平均降低成功率 62.50 与 43.75 个百分点，显著超过幅度与变化匹配的随机对照。双视角审计与目标闭环相机干预显示，移动腕部视角暴露带来的单独下降超过主视角的两倍，且二者具有强协同效应；消融实验验证了 UV 多样化与路径校准的作用。

Conclusion: 单一持久的世界绑定对抗纹理可作为一种可迁移、无需重训的物理层攻击面，对闭环 VLA 策略构成严重威胁；移动腕部视角是关键暴露通道，多视角协同显著放大攻击效果。

Abstract: Closed-loop vision-language-action (VLA) policies rely continuously on multi-view cameras, yet existing visual attacks typically hijack sensor streams with 2D patches or alter target object meshes that require retraining when manipulation objects change. We introduce RefCloth, a framework that optimizes one persistent adversarial tablecloth on a frozen source policy and deploys it zero-shot across unseen tasks and target models without modifying task objects or cameras. Throughout manipulation, static third-person and moving wrist views observe this shared world-bound texture under evolving occlusions. To generalize across shifting surface views while bridging the gap between differentiable and native renderers, RefCloth combines bounded texture parameterization with shared surface UV transformations and a same-path clean reference that removes static rendering offsets at initialization. Across four LIBERO suites in native simulation, one frozen texture transfers from OpenVLA-OFT to $π_0$ and $π_{0.5}$, reducing success relative to an unoptimized cloth by 62.50 and 43.75 percentage points on average and far exceeding amplitude- and variation-matched random controls. Dual-view audits and target closed-loop camera interventions show that moving wrist exposure drives over twice the standalone drop of the main view with strong synergy, while ablations confirm the roles of UV diversification and path calibration.

</details>


### [58] [TANGO: Real-World Unregistered RGB-Guided Hyperspectral Super-Resolution with Two-Camera Acquisitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22699)
*Matteo Kolyszko,Marco Buzzelli,Simone Bianco,Raimondo Schettini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个大规模未配准RGB引导高光谱超分公开数据集DUET（403个室内外双相机场景），并提出TANGO方法（全局对齐+由粗到细局部对齐+门控遮挡机制），在DUET与REAL上各尺度均达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有未配准高光谱超分方法虽已处理两模态几何失配，但评测受限于缺乏基于真实双相机RGB–HSI采集、且具备高分辨率HSI参考的大规模公开基准。

Method: 构建DUET数据集（403个场景，RGB与高光谱相机分体采集）；TANGO先通过端到端全局对齐补偿主导的跨相机位移，再用由粗到细的局部特征对齐精修残余不一致，并引入学习型门控机制在无法一致匹配处削弱RGB引导。

Result: 在DUET与REAL上所有评估尺度因子均取得SOTA；在DUET上PSNR最高超最强对手2.29 dB，同时FLOPs减少2.5倍。

Conclusion: DUET填补了真实双相机未配准HSI超分基准的空白，TANGO以更少算力实现更优配准与重建性能，代码与数据集将公开。

Abstract: Unregistered hyperspectral image super-resolution aims to recover a high-resolution HSI from a low-resolution hyperspectral observation and an unregistered high-resolution RGB reference. Although recent methods have begun to address the geometric misalignment between the two modalities, their evaluation is still constrained by the lack of a large-scale public benchmark based on real two-camera RGB--HSI acquisitions with a high-resolution HSI reference. To address this gap, we introduce DUET, the first large-scale public dataset for unregistered RGB-guided hyperspectral super-resolution, comprising 403 indoor and outdoor scenes captured with two separate RGB and hyperspectral cameras. We further propose TANGO (Two-camera AligNment with Gated Occlusions), which first compensates for the dominant cross-camera displacement through end-to-end global alignment and then refines the remaining inconsistencies with a coarse-to-fine local feature alignment. A learned gating mechanism further attenuates the RGB guidance where the two views cannot be consistently matched. Experimental results show that TANGO achieves state-of-the-art performance on both DUET and REAL across all evaluated scale factors, outperforming the strongest competitor on DUET by up to 2.29 dB in PSNR while requiring 2.5$\times$ fewer FLOPs. The code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/FagiolottoConSombrero/TANGO-DUET

</details>


### [59] [Video recordings and play behaviour annotations of group-housed dairy calves on two Dutch farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21706)
*Haiyu Yang,Heidi Lesscher,Enhong Liu,Miel Hostens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文发布了一个公开的奶牛犊牛群体饲养视频与个体行为标注数据集，涵盖651小时视频、104小时连续行为编码及110万解码帧与逐帧游玩标签，用于犊牛行为福利研究与自动行为识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玩耍行为被视为犊牛正向福利的指标，但公开可获取的、带个体行为标注的群体饲养犊牛视频资源稀缺，制约了行为学与自动识别研究。

Method: 在荷兰两家牧场（教研牧场2024、2025年；商业牧场2024年）的犊牛群养栏进行四次摄像部署，各约一周，共采集1,173个视频文件、651小时。由受训观察者使用Noldus The Observer XT，依据含18项玩耍及其他行为的ethogram对8个观察日进行连续编码（104小时，其中90小时覆盖栏内每头犊牛）；另发布7个标注会话的110万解码帧、基于画面烧录时钟的OCR逐帧时间戳、逐犊牛分割轨迹及逐帧玩耍标签。

Result: 数据集包含651小时原始视频、104小时连续行为编码（90小时全个体覆盖）、110万解码帧、逐帧时间戳、个体分割轨迹与逐帧玩耍标签，形成视频—标注—轨迹多层结构。

Conclusion: 该数据集可支撑犊牛行为与福利研究，并为自动行为识别方法的开发与评估提供基准资源。

Abstract: Play is used as an indicator of positive welfare in dairy calves, but openly available video of group-housed calves with individual behaviour annotations is scarce. We describe video from four camera deployments of about one week each in group pens of dairy calves on two farms in the Netherlands: a research and teaching dairy farm (2024 and 2025) and a commercial dairy farm (2024). The recordings comprise 1,173 video files spanning 651 h. For eight observation days, trained observers coded calf behaviour continuously in Noldus The Observer XT with an 18-item ethogram of play and other behaviours, covering 104 h of video, 90 h of which have every calf in the pen coded. The release also contains 1.1 million decoded frames from seven annotated sessions, a per-frame timestamp read by optical character recognition from the camera clock burned into each frame, per-calf segmentation tracks and per-calf frame-level play labels. The data support studies of calf behaviour and welfare and the development and evaluation of automated behaviour recognition.

</details>


### [60] [Unified Residual Translation for Paired Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22655)
*Joyanta Jyoti Mondal,Mridul Banik,Ibne Farabi Shihab,Md. Shifatul Ahsan Apurba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出URT，将通道/尺度对齐与残差预测解耦，用统一框架处理跨对比度、跨模态、跨分辨率的配对图像恢复任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配对图像恢复涵盖对比度变化（如MRI对比度合成）、传感器模态差异（可见光-红外、SAR-光学）和空间分辨率变化（超分），这些任务的输入通道与目标网格各异，因此需要回答"预测流程中有多少必须针对每个任务专门设计"这一问题。

Method: URT将固定的通道与尺度映射与可学习的残差预测分离：先用确定性参考（通道平均、复制或双三次插值）将输入匹配到目标维度，再由NAFNet编解码器基于完整输入预测加性修正。各任务分别训练实例，但共享同一预测规则、ℓ1目标与优化器。另研究四种频域变体，考察拟合参考与分带残差增益的作用。

Result: 在六个任务与尺度设定中，URT在IXI、SEN1-2及RealSR ×3/×4上取得所评估基线中最高的PSNR与SSIM；IXI达30.774dB，SEN1-2达13.478dB，RealSR三个尺度亮度PSNR分别为33.596、28.742、27.342dB；在LLVIP上PSNR与SSIM排名第二。

Conclusion: 结果支持将显式维度对齐与学习式残差修正作为异构配对成像任务的统一表述。

Abstract: Paired image restoration spans changes in imaging contrast, sensor modality, and spatial resolution. These settings differ in their input channels and target grids, raising the question of how much of the prediction pipeline must be specialized to each task. We present the Unified Residual Translator (URT), which separates fixed channel and scale mapping from learned residual prediction. A deterministic reference uses channel averaging, replication, or bicubic interpolation to match the target dimensions, while a NAFNet encoder-decoder predicts an additive correction from the complete input. Separately trained instances share the prediction rule, $\ell_1$ objective, and optimizer across MRI contrast synthesis, visible-to-infrared translation, SAR-to-optical translation, and real-camera super-resolution. Across six task and scale settings, URT achieves the highest PSNR and SSIM among the evaluated baselines on IXI, SEN1-2, and RealSR $\times3$ and $\times4$. It reaches 30.774dB on IXI and 13.478dB on SEN1-2, with luminance PSNR of 33.596, 28.742, and 27.342dB at the three RealSR scales. On LLVIP, it ranks second in PSNR and SSIM. A study of four spectral alternatives identifies complementary benefits of fitted references and bandwise residual gains. Together, these results support explicit dimensional alignment and learned residual correction as a common formulation for heterogeneous paired imaging tasks.

</details>


### [61] [Sub-Meter Canopy Height Synthesis from Sentinel-2 via Anchored Residual Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22636)
*Kiarie Ndegwa,Tony Chang,Andreas Gros,Vincent A. Landau,David D. Diaz,Luke Zachmann,Nathan E. Rutenbeck,Guy C Bayes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出从免费10 m Sentinel-2影像生成0.5 m冠层高度模型（CHM）的20×跨模态超分辨框架，基于感知-失真权衡给出两个共享条件的操作点：确定性回归器Anchor（低失真）与在学得CHM隐空间中学习Anchor残差的条件流匹配模型AR-flow（高感知保真）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚米级CHM对森林碳核算、火险燃料制图和单木生态学至关重要，但依赖航空LiDAR并按多年国家周期更新，时效与成本受限。核心困难在于光学反射率并不直接观测几何高度，需在20×跨模态尺度上"预测不可见信息"，且需在失真与感知质量之间做显式取舍。

Method: 以感知-失真权衡为指导设计两个共享条件的操作点：(1) Anchor为确定性回归器，最小化失真；(2) AR-flow为条件流匹配生成模型，在学得的CHM潜空间中拟合Anchor的残差，追求感知保真。训练集为覆盖美国本土69个生态区的197k个LiDAR配对瓦片，测试集为2,530个留出瓦片，对比8个基线。

Result: 仅用免费卫星输入时Anchor达到2.83 m MAE；AR-flow将切片Wasserstein距离从1.13降至0.208，但MAE升至3.68 m。冠尺度统计指标改善，而严格单木检测仍较弱——生成的亚像元细节为"合理结构"而非实测单木。

Conclusion: 跨模态超分辨可提供可用的区域级0.5 m CHM，并使季度大陆尺度刷新成为可能，从而用卫星监测为更昂贵的数据采集提供时机与范围依据；但生成细节不应被误读为单木级别的测量值，需在应用中明确其统计性而非个体性含义。

Abstract: Canopy height models (CHMs) - per-pixel maps of vegetation top height - are essential for forest carbon accounting, wildfire fuel mapping, and tree-level ecology, yet sub-meter CHMs require airborne LiDAR refreshed on multi-year national cycles. We produce 0.5 m CHMs from free 10 m Sentinel-2 imagery, a 20$\times$ cross-modal super-resolution task where optical reflectance must predict geometric height it does not directly observe. Motivated by the perception-distortion trade-off, we derive two operating points with shared conditioning. A deterministic regressor (Anchor) targets low distortion; a conditional flow matching model, trained on the Anchor's residual in a learned CHM latent space (AR-flow), targets perceptual fidelity. Trained on 197k LiDAR-paired tiles spanning 69 ecoregions across the contiguous United States and evaluated on 2,530 held-out tiles against eight baselines, the Anchor achieves 2.83 m MAE using only free satellite input. The AR-flow reduces sliced Wasserstein distance from the Anchor's 1.13 to 0.208, at a higher MAE of 3.68 m. Crown-scale statistics improve, but strict individual-tree detection remains weak: generated sub-pixel detail represents plausible structure rather than measured individual trees. Quarterly continental refresh becomes tractable, letting satellite monitoring inform timing and extent for more expensive data acquisitions.

</details>


### [62] [Lost in Read-out: Locating the Image Quality Gap in Vision-Language Models with a Closed-Form Edit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22564)
*Zhuangzi Li,Shilv Cai,Jian Jin,Zhiyi Pan,Haihong Xiao,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLM 的零样本图像质量评估中，质量信息在视觉编码器与语言层中线性可解码，且远优于最终答案所体现的水平；主要损失发生在最后的读出环节，该环节还会偏好色彩鲜艳度、低估清晰度。对单一末层神经元的闭式编辑可在未见提示下恢复 86–100% 的读出差距，且不破坏感知问答能力，但迁移性受限于提示形式与上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 作为零样本图像质量评估器时，其“好/差”判断与人类意见仅中等一致。作者要区分两种可能：模型是“看不见”质量，还是“说不出”质量，并定位失效环节。

Method: 在五个 VLM 上做逐层线性探针，检测质量信息在各层的可解码性；分析标签效率（约一百个标签即可恢复大部分信息）；比较读出与人类意见的偏差（过度奖励色彩鲜艳度、低估清晰度）；进而用干预检验：对最后一层单个神经元做闭式编辑，用多提示一次性拟合，并在未参与拟合的提示上评估，与同提示下末层状态上的 ridge head 对比；同时检验对感知问答的影响、对其他题型（是/否、评分）的迁移及情感语句后的对比反转。

Result: 质量信息在视觉编码器和最后语言层中均可线性解码，显著优于答案所揭示的水平；约 100 个标签即可恢复大部分信息。闭式单神经元编辑在未见提示下恢复 86–100% 的 ridge head 相对答案的增益（KonIQ）。当差距小（两个最大模型）时增益不足，未达迁移判据；当 head 本身低于答案时得分下降；编辑仅在从语言模型中段及之后写入时有效。该编辑不损害感知问答，但不迁移到是/否或评分题，且在情感色彩语句后会反转好—差对比。

Conclusion: VLM 的质量评估失效主要源于最后的读出而非感知本身；单神经元闭式编辑是一种精准、约束性强的修正手段，能针对性弥合读出差距且基本不损及其他能力，但其有效性依赖于读出环节确为瓶颈、提示形式与上下文，说明读出层的结构性偏差是零样本质量评估的关键限制。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are widely used as zero-shot image quality assessors, yet their good-vs-poor answers agree only moderately with human opinion. We ask whether these models fail to see quality or fail to say it. Layer-wise probes on five VLMs point to the second: quality is linearly decodable from the vision encoder and still from the last language layer, far better than the answer reveals, and about a hundred labels recover most of it. The loss lies mainly in the final read-out, which partly reads something else: relative to human opinion, it over-rewards colorfulness and under-rewards sharpness on all five models. We test this by intervention: if the read-out is at fault, a closed-form edit of a single last-layer neuron, fitted to many prompts at once, should close the gap and no more. Both hold. Under prompts never used for fitting, the edit recovers 86--100\% of what a ridge head on the last-layer state, fitted under the same prompts, finds beyond the answer on KonIQ. Where that gap is small, it gains little, too little for our transfer criterion on the two largest models; where the head itself is below the answer, it lowers the score; and it works only when written from the middle of the language model on. Unlike unconstrained fine-tuning, the edit leaves perception question answering intact. It does not carry over to yes-or-no or rating questions, and it reverses the good--poor contrast after sentiment-laden sentences. Code will be made available.

</details>


### [63] [Reliability-Driven Feature Correction for Hyperspectral and LiDAR Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22508)
*Lianming Zhang,Xiaolu Wang,Qiya Song,Jinyang Liu,Renwei Dian,Xudong Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReDF可靠性驱动框架，用类别证据强度量化各模态在每处空间位置的特征可靠性，并据此引导特征校正与融合，在三个HSI-LiDAR数据集上取得更高的OA与Kappa。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSI与LiDAR提供互补的光谱与高程信息，但模态特征中"类别判别可靠性"的空间变化很少被显式建模，弱或模糊特征会损害跨模态交互与融合。

Method: 1) 可靠性引导特征校正模块：为每个模态构建类原型库，将特征—原型相似度转换为类别证据以估计可靠性；基于估计可靠性构建可靠性加权共享表示，并以邻域一致性引导双向跨模态特征校正，用另一模态信息修正类别证据弱的特征。2) 可靠性引导特征重加权模块：以可靠性估计与跨模态分歧为条件生成低秩调制，作用于校正后的模态描述子，自适应缩放各模态对融合表示的贡献。

Result: 在三个公开HSI-LiDAR数据集上，平均总体精度（OA）与Kappa系数均高于对比方法。

Conclusion: 显式建模模态特征的逐位置类别可靠性可有效抑制弱特征干扰，提升跨模态交互与融合质量，从而改善地物分类性能。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image (HSI) and LiDAR data provide complementary spectral and elevation information for land-cover classification. However, the spatially varying class-discriminative reliability of modality features is rarely modeled explicitly, allowing weak or ambiguous features to adversely affect cross-modal interaction and fusion. To address this issue, we propose ReDF, a reliability-driven framework that quantifies the reliability of each modality's features at each spatial location through the strength of class-related evidence they provide, and uses it to guide feature correction and fusion. Specifically, a Reliability-Guided Feature Correction module introduces a class prototype bank for each modality and converts feature-prototype similarities into class evidence for reliability estimation. The estimated reliabilities are used to construct a reliability-weighted shared representation and to guide bidirectional cross-modal feature correction based on neighborhood agreement, refining modality features with weak class evidence through information from the other modality. Building on these outputs, a Reliability-Guided Feature Reweighting module conditions a low-rank modulation on the estimated reliabilities and the cross-modal disagreement, and applies it to the corrected modality-specific descriptors to adaptively rescale each modality's contribution to the fused representation used for final classification. Experiments on three public HSI-LiDAR datasets show higher mean overall accuracy and Kappa than the compared methods.

</details>


### [64] [PURE: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Multi-Granularity Multimodal Identity Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22343)
*Hengyu You,Xintong Wang,Jin Cao,Liang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PURE，一个面向多粒度多模态身份匹配的诊断型基准，覆盖感知、理解、推理、基于证据的判断四个能力维度，共14项任务、1457道选择题，用于揭示聚合匹配准确率所掩盖的能力短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身份匹配的答案依赖于目标粒度：两条记录可能在基础层级匹配而在变体层级不匹配。传统最终匹配准确率无法区分模型在各个子能力上的表现，因此需要一个细粒度诊断工具来刻画模型"错在哪里"。

Method: 以电商为真实场景，构建PURE基准：从真实匹配对派生1,457道多选题、14项任务，由两位领域专家校验；按感知、理解、推理、证据支撑判断四个维度组织；在十个近期MLLM上进行评测，并开展模态消融与直接匹配分析。

Result: 十个MLLM总体得分47.1%–86.0%；"理解"维度平均分最高，"判断"最低，主要弱在视觉差异定位与变体层级规则判断；模态消融显示模型能从多模态证据中获益，但会错误识别匹配记录间的冲突；直接匹配中，基础层级下多数模型识别匹配对优于非匹配对，变体层级则相反。

Conclusion: PURE揭示了聚合准确率之外的诊断信息：不仅反映模型判断对错，还定位其在证据解读与匹配标准应用上的薄弱环节，为多粒度身份匹配的细粒度评估提供基准。

Abstract: Identity matching asks whether two records denote the same identity, yet the answer depends on the target granularity. Two records may match at the base level but not at the variant level: they share an underlying design while differing in attributes such as color or size. Final matching accuracy does not separately characterize the capabilities involved. To study this problem, we use e-commerce as a challenging real-world domain. We introduce PURE, a diagnostic benchmark for multi-granularity multimodal identity matching covering four capability dimensions: Perception, Understanding, Reasoning, and Evidence-grounded Judgment. The benchmark comprises 1,457 multiple-choice questions across 14 tasks, derived from real-world matching pairs and verified by two domain experts. Across ten recent MLLMs, overall scores range from 47.1% to 86.0%. Understanding receives the highest mean score and Judgment the lowest, with weaknesses in visual difference localization and variant-level rule judgment. Modality ablations show that models benefit from multimodal evidence but can incorrectly identify conflicts between matching records. In direct matching, most models recognize matching pairs more reliably than non-matching pairs at the base level, whereas the reverse holds at the variant level. PURE therefore reveals what aggregate matching accuracy hides: not only whether models judge a pair correctly, but where they struggle to interpret evidence and apply matching criteria.

</details>


### [65] [SIDUMN: Self-Supervised Image Denoising Under Symmetric Multiplicative Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22317)
*Nyan Htet Naing,Gyeongha Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIDUMN——面向均值一对称乘性噪声的对称加噪自监督去噪框架，用反射对偶噪声构造互补加噪图像互预测，推理时对K对对偶重构取平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘性且信号相关的噪声使自监督去噪困难：现有方法多针对加性噪声，缺乏对均值一对称乘性噪声的适用框架。

Method: 从已知噪声分布采样独立乘性因子及其关于1的反射，构造两张互补"更噪"图像；网络以一张预测另一张；推理时平均K对对偶重构（对偶平均）。理论分析给出训练损失的总体目标为干净条件均值减去一个乘性交叉项，并证明对任意已训练网络，对偶平均不增加单张更噪输入重构的期望平方误差。

Result: 理论推导成立（总体目标刻画、对偶平均的误差非增性），数值实验验证了理论结论与所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 对称乘性噪声下的对偶加噪+对偶平均是自监督去噪的有效且具理论保障的策略。

Abstract: Self-supervised denoising is challenging when the corruption is multiplicative and signal-dependent. In this letter, we propose SIDUMN, a symmetric noisier framework for self-supervised denoising under mean-one symmetric multiplicative noise. Given noisy observations and the noise law, the method samples an independent synthetic noise factor from the same law and constructs two complementary noisier images by applying the factor and its reflection about one. The denoising network is trained to predict one noisier image from the other, and at inference the reconstructions of $K$ such antithetic pairs are averaged. We show that the population target of the training loss is the clean conditional mean minus a multiplicative cross-term. We further prove that, for any trained network, antithetic averaging never increases the expected squared error of a single noisier-input reconstruction. Numerical results validate the theoretical findings and the proposed method.

</details>


### [66] [SARVIA: Fast-Adapting the Strong SAR Foundation Models That Are Already in Your MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22273)
*Yuxuan Li,Zichen Tian,Qianru Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SARVIA：一种针对现代多模态大模型（MLLM）的自适应后预训练方法，利用可计算的自监督视觉问答对MLLM进行领域特化，仅需600步、2 GPU时即可导出的SAR视觉编码器，在6个SAR检测与分割基准上超越专门的SAR基础模型，将领域训练成本降低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAR基础模型发展依赖昂贵的大规模SAR预训练，作者质疑这种大规模领域特定预训练的必要性。研究发现现代MLLM的视觉编码器作为下游初始化已可超越专门的SAR基础模型，因此提出"复用通用视觉能力、仅做简短领域特化"的新范式。

Method: 构建由无标注观测生成、答案可计算的自监督视觉问题：通用图像任务捕捉空间关系，SAR专用任务利用原始测量与SAR-光学对应关系。以共享的答案预测目标联合训练视觉与语言部分的低秩适配器（LoRA），并采用损失驱动的任务采样；随后将适配后的视觉编码器导出为独立骨干网络。

Result: 仅用600个适配步、2 GPU小时，Sarvia在全部6个SAR检测与分割基准上优于所对比的基础模型；相比专门SAR预训练，领域训练成本降低数个数量级，并在不同架构与规模上提升视觉迁移效果。

Conclusion: 该范式将重点从重复、昂贵的预训练转向可复用的特化流程，可持续受益于开源MLLM的进步。数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) foundation models provide transferable representations for diverse perception tasks in all-weather Earth observation. However, their development often relies on costly large-scale SAR pretraining, raising the question of whether such extensive domain-specific pretraining is necessary. We find that modern multimodal large language models (MLLMs) already provide visual encoders that can outperform dedicated SAR foundation models as downstream initializations. This motivates a new paradigm: reuse general visual capabilities and devote domain training to brief specialization. We therefore introduce SARVIA, an adaptive post-pretraining method that specializes modern MLLMs using self-supervised visual questions with computable answers. These questions are constructed from unlabeled observations through general image tasks that capture spatial relations and SAR-specific tasks that exploit native measurements and SAR--optical correspondence. A shared answer-prediction objective jointly trains low-rank adapters across visual and language components, with loss-driven task sampling. We then export the adapted visual encoder as a standalone backbone. With 600 adaptation steps and 2 GPU-hours, SARVIA outperforms the compared foundation models on all six SAR detection and segmentation benchmarks. Compared with dedicated SAR pretraining, SARVIA reduces domain-training costs by orders of magnitude while improving visual transfer across architectures and scales. This new paradigm shifts the emphasis from repeated, costly pretraining to a reusable specialization process that can benefit from advances in open MLLMs. Dataset and code: https://github.com/zcablii/SARVIA.

</details>


### [67] [Temporal Pseudo-RGB Encoding for Label-Free Detection of VEEV Infection in Human Brain Endothelial Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21892)
*Zhengjie Zhu,Stephanie Valdez Trefry,Hao Lu,Abbas Alili,Yongxin Guo,Aarthi Narayanan,Metin Nafi Gurcan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出"时序伪RGB编码"：把 t-6h、t-3h、t 三个时刻的明场图像分别作为 RGB 三通道堆叠成单张图，使形态学变化"变成颜色"，从而可直接用 ImageNet 预训练的 CNN 建模感染时间进程。在 VEEV TC-83 感染的 HBMVEC 上，多任务网络一次前向同时预测感染/对照状态与感染后时间，准确率 91.6%（单帧 87.4%），晚期阶段提升更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无标记病毒检测对抗病毒筛选与病原监测很重要，但现有深度学习方法多对单帧图像分类，忽略时间维度上的形态演化；而引入循环或 3D 卷积代价高。作者希望在不改动网络结构的前提下，让标准 2D 预训练 CNN 直接利用时间信息。

Method: 提出时序伪 RGB 编码：将 t-6h、t-3h、t（红、绿、蓝）三帧明场图像堆叠为单张三通道图像，交由 ImageNet 预训练 CNN 处理，无需结构修改。在 VEEV TC-83 感染的 HBMVEC 上训练多任务网络，一次前向同时输出感染/模拟状态与感染后时间；采用留一行交叉验证，三个实验批次、全部 36 孔均做折外评分，并与单帧模型、循环模型、3D 卷积模型及 ViT-B/16 骨干对比。

Result: 感染判别准确率 91.6% vs 单帧 87.4%（配对差 +4.2 个百分点，95% CI [+2.5,+6.1]，符号翻转检验 p=3×10⁻⁵）。在晚期（40–48 h，漂浮细胞与碎片干扰严重）优势扩大到 +23.9 个百分点（95% CI [+19.2,+28.9]）。固定行划分下感染后时间回归 MAE=1.31 h（R²=0.975）。在测试的时序模型中该方法平均准确率最高，且计算成本远低于循环与 3D 卷积方案；但仅其相对单帧模型的优势具有统计显著分辨度。

Conclusion: 时序伪 RGB 编码是一种低成本、无需改结构即可为 2D CNN 注入时间信息的有效手段，在晚期图像质量退化的场景收益最大；但结论限于卷积骨干与 VEEV-HBMVEC 体系——ViT-B/16 骨干未观察到相应增益，其相对其他时序模型的优势也尚未被统计确认。

Abstract: Label-free detection of viral infection is central to antiviral screening and pathogen surveillance. Most deep-learning methods analyze individual frames and do not model temporal progression. We evaluate temporal pseudo-RGB encoding, which stacks three time-offset brightfield frames ($t{-}6$ h, $t{-}3$ h, $t$) as the red, green, and blue channels of a single image so that morphological change becomes color and an ImageNet-pretrained convolutional neural network (CNN) can be trained on infection trajectories without architectural modification. In human brain microvascular endothelial cells (HBMVEC) infected with Venezuelan equine encephalitis virus (VEEV) TC-83, a multi-task network predicts infected-versus-mock status and time post-infection in one forward pass. Across three experimental runs under leave-one-row-out cross-validation, with all 36 wells scored out of fold, the encoding reaches 91.6% accuracy against 87.4% for the single-frame model (paired difference $+4.2$ percentage points, 95% CI $[+2.5, +6.1]$; sign-flip $p = 3\times10^{-5}$; well-level inference, conditional on three runs). At the late-phase endpoint (40-48 h), where floating cells and debris can make conventional brightfield images difficult to interpret, the advantage widens to $+23.9$ points (95% CI $[+19.2, +28.9]$). Time post-infection is recovered on the fixed row split with MAE = 1.31 h ($R^2 = 0.975$). Among the temporal models tested the encoding has the highest mean accuracy, at a fraction of the cost of the recurrent and 3D convolutional alternatives, though only its margin over the single-frame model is resolved; the tested ViT-B/16 backbone showed no corresponding gain, so the finding is limited to convolutional backbones and this virus-cell system.

</details>


### [68] [Data-Driven Variable-Length Compression of 3D Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21802)
*Yvette Y. Lin,Sai Karthikey Pentapati,Sarvesh Sundaram,Guandao Yang,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种学习式变长3D形状压缩方法，利用训练数据集层面的结构统计，并通过快速前馈推理按单个形状复杂度自适应调整压缩表示长度，在网格与符号距离场上均改善率失真性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D形状压缩方法存在两点不足：一是无法利用数据集级别的统计结构，二是对所有形状使用固定的压缩率，忽视了不同形状复杂度差异。

Method: 学习式变长压缩框架，作用于通用潜表示；借助训练集跨样本结构建模，并仅用快速前馈推理即可为每个形状预测合适的压缩表示长度。

Result: 在网格（meshes）与符号距离场（SDF）两类表示上均取得率失真（rate-distortion）性能提升。

Conclusion: 数据集级结构利用与逐形状自适应码长可同时实现，且不牺牲推理效率，方法对多种形状表示具有通用性。

Abstract: Compressing 3D shapes is important for the efficiency of many applications, yet existing methods either cannot leverage dataset-level statistics or use a fixed compression ratio for all shapes. We propose a learned variable-length compression method that exploits structure across a training dataset while adapting compressed representation size to individual shape complexity using only fast feed-forward inference. Our method operates on general latent representations and improves rate-distortion for both meshes and signed distance fields. Code is available at https://github.com/voilalab/varlen-3D.

</details>


### [69] [Every Camera Grows Up: Camera-Height Regression in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21782)
*Sheng Lu,Guangpin Tao,Tong Lu,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示交互式视频世界模型存在"相机高度回归"失败现象：生成的视角会从给定的起始相机高度漂移到人类眼高，并给出基准EyeHeight、现象刻画与成因分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型被用作具身智能体的模拟器，其起始图像固定了相机高度（如地面机器人0.15 m、俯视相机2.2 m），忠实模拟器应在运动中保持该高度。作者发现现有模型无法保持，故系统性地定义、量化并溯源这一失败。

Method: 构建基准EyeHeight：6种渲染相机高度（0.15–2.2 m）+ 4种实拍标定机位高度，配合无尺度多视角高度估计；在180°转身行走rollout上评估6个近期开源权重模型；用单应性跟踪地板运动、地面视差图、文本高度提示、无分类器文本引导与高度均衡微调等消融实验定位成因。

Result: 六个模型均丢失初始高度：从0.15 m初始化的片段中仅0–20%在1.3倍起始高度内结束，推断视角收敛至1.1–1.5 m的人类眼高，而真值渲染保持在数个百分点内。漂移随旋转角度增大，在起始帧仍在上下文时即已出现，并在SANA-WM中经360°转回后大幅逆转。成因指向以人类为中心的视频先验（地板视角几乎缺席）；文本高度提示与密集地面视差图无效，而前半段去噪的无分类器文本引导会加剧漂移；无引导采样+高度均衡微调可提升保持率但损失5 MUSIQ分。仅视角漂移即可改变视觉导航策略预测的路径。

Conclusion: 相机高度回归是当前开源视频世界模型的普遍缺陷，源于人类中心视频先验而非缺乏显式高度线索；该问题会污染具身智能体的下游策略评估，需在数据与采样/微调层面系统性修正。

Abstract: Interactive video world models serve as simulators for embodied agents: from a starting image and an action trajectory, they generate the views the agent would see. The starting image fixes how high the camera is, for example 0.15 m for a floor robot or 2.2 m for an overhead camera, and a faithful simulator must keep this height as the camera moves.
