We show that six recent open-weight models fail to do so: as motion replaces the starting view with generated content, they move the camera toward human eye level, a failure we call camera-height regression. Our benchmark, EyeHeight, pairs six rendered elevations (0.15 m to 2.2 m) and four calibrated real-world rig heights with scale-free multi-view height estimation. All six models lose their initial height during a $180^\circ$ turn-and-walk rollout: initialized at 0.15 m, only 0-20% of clips end within $1.3\times$ of the starting height as inferred viewpoints converge to human heights of 1.1 m to 1.5 m, whereas ground-truth renders remain within a few percent. In Matrix-Game models, homography-tracked floor motion stays consistent with constant elevation during rotation even as newly outpainted room geometry yields a higher reconstructed viewpoint. Regression grows with rotation angle, begins while the start frame remains in context, and largely reverses in SANA-WM when a $360^\circ$ turn restores the initial view. We trace this pull to human-centric video priors in which floor-level views are nearly absent: in SANA-WM, textual height cues and dense floor-disparity maps do not prevent it, whereas classifier-free text guidance during the first half of denoising strengthens it. Combining unguided sampling with height-balanced fine-tuning recovers more retention but costs 5 MUSIQ points, a loss fixed during those same early steps. Viewpoint drift alone shifts the paths predicted by visual navigation policies.

</details>


### [70] [MIRAGE: Motion Identity Reduction via Autoregressive Generative Encoding for Privacy-Preserving Skeleton-based Motion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22200)
*Thomas Carr,Xujun Che,Depeng Xu,Shuhan Yuan,Aidong Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIRAGE：一种无需目标骨架、因果的骨架序列匿名器，逐帧在原始坐标空间加入由历史帧注意力计算的身份削弱残差，在自适应攻击下显著降低再识别率同时保持较高动作识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨架流常被送入第三方分析系统，骨架本身可通过体型比例与运动风格泄露身份。现有对抗匿名器逐帧独立处理、缺乏时序信息；而运动重定向方法需目标骨架和完整序列，无法在线使用。

Method: 提出MIRAGE：因果、无目标的匿名器，对每个输入帧在原始坐标空间加入身份削弱残差，残差由对过去帧的注意力计算；在旋转位置编码的编码器-解码器中用统一的因果（可选窗化）掩码约束所有注意力，并通过KV缓存实现逐帧流式推理。实验采用NTU RGB+D 60、5个生成器种子，评估被动对手与在匿名输出上重训的自适应对手，并与Moon等人的逐帧残差匿名器在相同训练条件下对比。

Result: 批处理与流式输出最大差异7.2e-6 m（全部56,578条序列）。消费者重训后：MIRAGE动作识别83.9%（原始89.1%，Moon等88.7%），重训身份分类器再识别率43.4%（Moon等69.4%，原始75.4%）；最强自适应攻击者达49.3%（随机2.5%）。对冻结的原始训练分类器，MIRAGE再识别率10.36%（最强重定向基线22.09%），但冻结动作识别降至37.04%（对比75.82%）。

Conclusion: MIRAGE通过因果注意力残差有效降低身份泄露，且在自适应场景下优于逐帧基线，但并不实现完全匿名；在满残差尺度下会破坏冻结模型的动作识别性能，不能直接替代现有冻结模型的原始输入。此外，无界窗口使每流状态随序列长度增长。

Abstract: Skeleton motion identifies people through body proportions and movement style, and skeleton streams are increasingly passed to third-party analytics that run action classifiers trained on raw data. A prior adversarial anonymizer adds a per-frame residual in the raw coordinate space but sees one frame at a time; motion retargeting needs a target skeleton and complete sequences, which rules out online use. We present MIRAGE, a target-free, causal anonymizer that adds an identity-reducing residual to each incoming frame in the raw coordinate space, computed by attention over past frames. One causal, optionally windowed mask governs every attention in a rotary-position encoder-decoder, and key/value caches let the trained function run frame by frame; on all 56,578 NTU 60 sequences, batched and streaming outputs differ by at most 7.2e-6 m. Reported results use an unbounded window, so per-stream state grows with stream length. We evaluate against a passive adversary that applies raw-trained identity models and an adaptive adversary that trains on the anonymizer's output, treating the adaptive adversary as the headline, on NTU RGB+D 60 over five generator seeds, alongside the per-frame residual anonymizer of Moon et al. trained identically. When consumers retrain on the released data, MIRAGE keeps 83.9% action recognition (raw 89.1%; Moon et al. 88.7%) while a retrained identity classifier reaches 43.4% re-identification (RI) over 40 subjects, against 69.4% for Moon et al. and 75.4% on raw data. The strongest adaptive attacker still reaches 49.3% (chance 2.5%), so MIRAGE reduces identity leakage; it does not anonymize. Against raw-trained (frozen) classifiers, MIRAGE lowers RI below the strongest retargeting baseline (10.36% vs. 22.09%), but frozen action recognition falls to 37.04% (vs. 75.82%), so at full residual scale it is not a drop-in replacement for raw input to existing frozen models.

</details>


### [71] [HandRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement for Hand Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22164)
*Zhekai Wang,Luqing Luo,Wenhao Yao,Junjie Lu,Huanan Zhu,Feifei Gao,Yuhua Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HandRSI：一个由编码智能体自动改进推理代码（harness）并与视频手部姿态估计器通过选择性蒸馏交替迭代的递归自改进框架，在统一协议下显著降低误差与抖动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称3D手部姿态估计器仍存在图像边缘漏检、遮挡手位置错误和抖动等问题；通常依赖手写推理后处理与微调两步来修复，但二者相互依赖却分别调参，难以协同优化。

Method: 系统由视频估计器与可执行推理harness组成，递归自改进：(1) 每轮由编码智能体改写harness，仅当在留出数据上十项指标复合得分提升且无显著单项退化时采纳；(2) 采纳后的harness指导估计器的选择性蒸馏——在无真值帧上监督手存在性与时序一致性，有真值帧监督姿态；(3) 因更新后的估计器可能改变哪些refinement仍有效，harness需在其上重新验证。最终在精度-速度前沿上用超体积选择得到Lite/Medium/Heavy三档系统。

Result: 从ACE-Ego-Hand出发，五轮迭代使HOT3D上MPJPE-p降低35%、ARCTIC上降低11%，抖动约减半；HOT3D上MPJPE-p比同真值微调的ACE-Ego-Hand低16%；统一协议下在两基准全部十项指标上优于九个复跑的已发表方法；三档系统运行速度为20.7–41.1 FPS。

Conclusion: 将推理harness的自动搜索与估计器的选择性蒸馏耦合、并交替重验证，可有效修复边缘漏检、遮挡与抖动问题，性能超越手工refinement与单纯微调，且代码、评测器、harness与权重均公开。

Abstract: Egocentric 3D hand pose estimators still miss hands at the image border, misplace occluded hands and jitter. Such failures are usually fixed by hand-written inference refinements and by fine-tuning, two interdependent steps that are tuned separately. We present HandRSI, a recursive self-improvement framework for a hand pose system that consists of a video estimator and its executable inference harness. In each round, coding agents refine the harness, and a change is accepted only if it improves a composite of all ten benchmark metrics on held-out data without significantly regressing any of them. The accepted harness then guides the estimator through selective distillation, which supervises hand presence and temporal consistency on frames without ground truth, while ground truth supervises pose. Since the updated estimator may change which refinements remain useful, the harness is re-validated on it. Starting from the released ACE-Ego-Hand model, five rounds reduce MPJPE-p by 35% on HOT3D and 11% on ARCTIC and roughly halve jitter. On HOT3D, HandRSI's MPJPE-p is 16% lower than that of ACE-Ego-Hand fine-tuned on the same ground truth. Under a unified protocol, it outperforms nine re-run published methods on all ten metrics of both benchmarks. Hypervolume selection on the accuracy-speed front yields Lite, Medium and Heavy systems running at 20.7 to 41.1 FPS. This report gives the full method, every stage of every round and the complete protocol. Code, evaluator, harnesses and weights are public.

</details>


### [72] [LoopZip: Visual Token Compression for Efficient Looped Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22728)
*Weizhong Huang,Zhicheng Liu,Yan Zhang,Fei Chao,Xiawu Zheng,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练视觉token压缩算法LoopZip，针对循环视觉语言模型：先用视觉聚合构建紧凑候选池，再用跨调用选择（平均同一共享注意力层前两次调用的指令注意力）与权重共享的值校正，联合减小不同循环状态下的输出偏差。在LoopVL-1B上50%剪枝保留97.16%性能，87.5%剪枝超最强基线5.36%；显存峰值降约58.4%-59.0%，端到端加速2.01-2.47倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环VLM通过复用共享层加深计算，但反复处理长视觉序列仍带来显著计算与显存开销。现有视觉token压缩方法中，直接选择原始token会忽视token重要性随循环调用的变化并丢弃后续所需信息；选择与聚合结合的方法又缺乏对跨调用输出变化的联合考量，信息保留受限。

Method: 免训练视觉token压缩：1）视觉聚合在循环主干前构建紧凑候选池；2）跨调用选择对同一共享注意力层前两次已存在调用的指令注意力取平均，以保留两种状态均需要的token；3）权重共享的值校正对共享视觉value调整同时拟合两状态的预选层输出，降低跨状态输出偏差。

Result: 覆盖2个开源循环VLM、11个基准、3种剪枝比。LoopVL-1B在50%剪枝保留原模型97.16%性能；87.5%剪枝超最强基线5.36%。在Hugging Face Transformers、vLLM、SGLang上，LoopVL-1B+LoopZip使GPU显存峰值分配降低58.36%-58.98%，端到端加速2.01-2.47倍。

Conclusion: LoopZip以免训练方式解决了循环VLM中跨调用token重要性变化与输出偏差问题，在高剪枝比下显著优于基线，并带来可观的显存节省与推理加速，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Looped vision-language models (VLMs) deepen computation by reusing shared layers, approaching larger models' capabilities with fewer parameters and easing the storage and deployment burden of stacking independent layers. However, repeatedly processing long visual sequences still incurs substantial compute and GPU memory costs. Visual token compression reduces these costs by shortening sequences. Existing methods mainly select original tokens directly or combine selection with aggregation. In looped models, the former may overlook changing token importance across calls and discard information needed later; the latter lack joint consideration of output changes across calls, limiting information preservation. We propose LoopZip, a training-free visual token compression algorithm addressing both issues. Visual aggregation builds a compact candidate pool before the looped backbone, and cross-invocation selection averages instruction attention from the first two existing calls of the same shared attention layer to retain tokens needed by both states. Weight-tied value correction then jointly fits shared visual-value adjustments to both pre-selection layer outputs, reducing output deviations across states. To validate LoopZip's effectiveness, we conduct extensive experiments covering two open-source looped VLMs, eleven benchmarks, and three pruning ratios. On LoopVL-1B, LoopZip retains 97.16% of the original model's performance at 50% pruning ratio and exceeds the strongest baseline by 5.36% at 87.5% pruning ratio. Across Hugging Face Transformers, vLLM, and SGLang, LoopVL-1B with LoopZip reduces peak allocated GPU memory by 58.36%-58.98% and achieves 2.01-2.47x end-to-end speedups over the original model.

</details>


### [73] [Visual Harness Scales General VLMs to Medical Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22815)
*Shaowen Wan,Hanqi Jiang,Zihao Wu,Junhao Chen,Weihang You,Ruiyu Yan,Bowen Chen,Tianming Liu,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出医学视觉工具架（visual harness）\ours，为通用视觉语言模型配备交互式视觉工具与系统化诊断证据管理，在11项任务、4种影像模态上全面提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用VLM在医学影像任务上表现有限，单次静态推理难以充分挖掘图像证据；作者认为通过合适的视觉工具架可显著释放其潜力，探索"视觉工具架"作为将通用VLM扩展到医学影像的新范式。

Method: 构建\ours：为通用VLM提供交互式视觉工具与无损证据记忆库。模型交互式探索影像并逐步积累诊断证据，证据存入无损记忆以便检索复用；给出初始答案后，获得关于潜在遗漏与已识别发现可靠性的评估，据此引导进一步的图像检查与测量，并在迭代式智能体循环中修订答案，直至证据充分。

Result: 在11项任务、4种模态上以Qwen3.8-27B实现全部任务提升：脾脏测量63.3%→98.3%，肾脏搜查57.5%→71.2%，肺结节检测0.0%→28.2%。高能力API模型收益更大，如GPT-6.1 Sol肺结节检测10.8%→69.2%。

Conclusion: 强模型能力与有效医学视觉工具架具有互补性；视觉工具架可作为将通用VLM扩展至医学影像任务的新范式。

Abstract: We show that an appropriate visual harness can substantially improve the performance of general vision-language models (VLMs) across diverse medical imaging tasks. We introduce \ours, a medical visual harness that equips general VLMs with interactive visual tools and systematic diagnostic evidence management. With \ours, general VLMs interactively explore medical images and progressively acquire diagnostic evidence, which is preserved in a lossless memory for subsequent retrieval and use. After submitting an initial answer, the VLM receives an assessment of potential omissions and the reliability of identified findings. This assessment guides further image inspection and measurement, followed by answer revision in an iterative agentic loop until sufficient evidence is obtained. Across 11 tasks and four imaging modalities, \ours improves performance on all tasks with Qwen3.8-27B, with notable gains in spleen measurement (63.3\% to 98.3\%), renal search (57.5\% to 71.2\%), and pulmonary nodule detection (0.0\% to 28.2\%). High-capability API-based VLMs achieve even higher performance with \ours. For example, GPT-6.1 Sol improves pulmonary nodule detection from 10.8\% to 69.2\% with \ours, demonstrating the complementary benefits of stronger model capabilities and an effective medical visual harness. Our results highlight the potential of visual harnesses as a new paradigm for scaling general VLMs to medical imaging tasks.

</details>


### [74] [ConserveEdit: Source-Anchored Transactions for Drift-Bounded Iterative Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22724)
*Shuyang Jiang,Fucheng Deng,Yuchuan Luo,Zhenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ConserveEdit 是一个与编辑器无关的控制器，将逐步精修视为对"契约"的事务提交，保证授权区域外像素逐位不变并使漂移与迭代次数无关；在 MagicBrush 上显著降低受保护内容违例（48%→2%）并提升约束成功率（23%→53%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令驱动的图像编辑器单次编辑效果可信，但在迭代精修循环中每次被接受的输出会作为下一次输入，导致请求区域外的不期望改动不断累积。现有固定标量权衡无法同时约束多个目标，也无法显式保护特定内容。

Method: 提出 editor-agnostic 控制器 ConserveEdit，把每步精修视为一次针对源图像一次性编译出的契约的事务。契约将"可变区域"（每个目标授权区域，加有界 halo 以容纳阴影/反射）与"必须保留"（带明确容差的受保护内容）分离。候选结果经由 source-anchored mask 合成，并分别通过三项测试——目标进展、受保护内容漂移、图像质量——才被提交，否则回滚修复。漂移测试会指出被违反的实体，从而可收缩支撑域而非直接放弃。

Result: 在全部 1053 条 MagicBrush 测试编辑（冻结 Qwen-Image-Edit）上：直接调用会改变掩码外内容（12.1 dB PSNR）；经 source-anchored mask 合成后掩码外逐位精确，与 GT 的 DINO 相似度由 0.67 升至 0.85，与 ConserveEdit 持平，说明该增益来自掩码。相比自动掩码，掩码直接编辑在 48% 的轮次改变受保护人脸或文字；掩码+标量选择主要靠放弃来回避（23% 约束成功率）；ConserveEdit 收缩支撑域，违例降至 2%、约束成功率 53%；在已知真值的模拟器中，约束成功率由 55.0% 提升至 61.8%。

Conclusion: 掩码是必要条件但不充分；分离式契约测试优于固定标量权衡。自动 grounding 仍是主要瓶颈。代码与日志将公开。

Abstract: Instruction-driven image editors produce convincing local edits in one pass, but inside an iterative refinement loop every accepted output becomes the next input, so unintended changes outside the requested region accumulate. We present ConserveEdit, an editor-agnostic controller that treats each refinement step as a transaction against a contract compiled once from the source image. The contract separates what may change (the authorized region of each target, with a bounded halo for shadows and reflections) from what must survive (protected content with explicit tolerances). Candidates from a frozen editor are composed through a source-anchored mask and committed only if target progress, drift on protected content, and image quality pass as separate tests; otherwise the step is rolled back and repaired. For any editor, this keeps every pixel outside the authorized region bit-exact and bounds drift independently of the number of iterations, whereas no fixed scalar trade-off reproduces the separate tests. On all 1053 MagicBrush test edits with a frozen Qwen-Image-Edit, masking is necessary but not sufficient. A direct call already alters the rest of the image (12.1 dB PSNR outside the mask); composed through the source-anchored mask, it stays bit-exact there and its DINO similarity to the ground truth rises from 0.67 to 0.85, matching ConserveEdit, so this gain comes from the mask. Next to automatic masks, however, masked direct editing changes a protected face or text on 48% of turns, and masked scalar selection avoids this mostly by abstaining (23% constrained success). Because its drift test names the violated entity, ConserveEdit shrinks the support instead (2% violations, 53% constrained success); in a simulator with known ground truth it raises constrained success from 55.0% to 61.8%. Automatic grounding remains the main bottleneck. Code and logs will be released.

</details>


### [75] [Beyond the Camera: A Tri-Modal Driving World Model for Future RGB, Thermal, and LiDAR Forecasting in Adverse Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22669)
*Faysal Mehrab Chowdhury,Md. Mahfuzur Rahman,Ahmed Rafi Hasan,Kishor Datta Gupta,Mohd Ariful Haque,Savannah Shannon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究RGB、热红外与LiDAR三传感器联合驾驶世界模型，在DSERT-RoLL真实恶劣天气序列上验证了后训练与联合训练的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型多预测相机视频，少数加入LiDAR，热红外相机（暗光与恶劣天气下仍可成像）几乎不作为预测目标，且尚无同时预测三种传感器的工作。

Method: 在DSERT-RoLL恶劣天气序列上，对每个传感器后训练预训练视频世界模型；LiDAR表示为与RGB相机对齐的深度视频，致密化与编码方式依据模型视频tokenizer的重建能力选择；构建将三传感器视为单一视频的三传感器模型；LiDAR以米为单位对实测回波评分，并与经同一tokenizer处理的持续性基线对比。

Result: 压缩匹配后，热红外模型在雾天相对持续性基线的表观劣势消失；在六种天气、全部58条验证序列上，后训练的RGB与热红外预测均优于压缩匹配持续性基线；联合训练使LiDAR在2s处误差降低2.1m，相机保真度仅小幅下降；仅三传感器模型的LiDAR预测在平均误差上优于持续性基线；遮挡单一传感器时，雪天对其它传感器预测的退化大于雾天。

Conclusion: 公平的tokenizer匹配对比是评估多传感器世界模型的前提；联合三传感器训练可在相机保真度小幅让步下显著改善LiDAR预测，且跨传感器信息互补在雪天更为关键。

Abstract: Driving world models forecast what a vehicle's sensors will record next. Most forecast camera video and some add LiDAR, but thermal cameras, which still see in darkness and some adverse weather, are rarely a forecast target, and to our knowledge no world model forecasts all three sensors. We study joint forecasting of RGB, thermal, and LiDAR on real adverse-weather sequences of the DSERT-RoLL dataset. We post-train a pretrained video world model for each sensor and represent LiDAR as a depth video aligned with the RGB camera, with densification and encoding chosen by what the model's video tokenizer can reconstruct. A tri-sensor model sees all three sensors as one video. We score LiDAR in meters against measured returns and compare against persistence baselines passed through the same tokenizer as the model. With this matching, an apparent loss of the thermal model to persistence in fog disappears. On all 58 validation sequences in six weather conditions, post-trained RGB and thermal forecasts beat compression-matched persistence. Joint training lowers the LiDAR error at 2s by 2.1m at a small cost in camera fidelity, and only the tri-sensor model's LiDAR forecasts beat persistence in mean error. Blanking one sensor degrades the forecasts of the others more in snow than in fog.

</details>


### [76] [OVET-Consensus: Rank-Preserving Counterfactual Event Verification for Video Moment Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22637)
*Alibay Osmanli,Shaogang Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文针对视频时刻检索中的"评分器瓶颈"提出 OVET-Consensus：一个保序的反事实事件校验层，仅允许高置信度的 rank-1 覆盖，在 QVHighlights 上将 R1@0.5 从 64.71 提升至 65.35。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时刻检索系统常提出与目标事件有重叠的候选窗口，却仍选错窗口，说明瓶颈在评分/选择阶段而非候选生成阶段。作者用诊断性 oracle 量化这一差距：同一候选族在父级重排池上可达 75.68 R1@0.5，在完整 100 候选证据池上可达 94.97 R1@0.5，远超时序专家 64.71 的表现，表明候选池中存在正确答案但未被选出。

Method: 以 QD-DETR 衍生的父级感知时序专家（parent-aware temporal expert）为固定骨干，构建 OVET-Consensus 校验层。该层采用保序（rank-preserving）设计，保留时序专家的排序列表为默认输出，仅允许由在留出训练视频上校准的策略触发的、高置信度的 rank-1 覆盖。校验信号包括：父级感知区间证据、Qwen3-VL 的结构化事件判断、VideoLLaMA3 的成对时序信号以及稠密时序证据；但这些信号不得自由重排 top-K 列表。

Result: 在 QVHighlights 验证集上，R1@0.5 由 64.71 提升至 65.35，配对 bootstrap 区间为正；mAP 基本不变（42.67→42.74）。系统共做出 20 次覆盖，其中 15 次为有益修正、3 次为有害修正（其余中性）。

Conclusion: 结果表明校验器设计面临的实际约束：语义兼容性是有用证据，但时序定位仍需判定事件在视觉兼容场景中的起始、变化与结束位置；强行让语义信号自由重排反而风险高，受限的保守覆盖策略更稳健。

Abstract: Modern video moment retrieval systems often propose windows that overlap the target event, yet still select the wrong one. We study this scorer bottleneck on QVHighlights with a fixed QD-DETR-derived temporal expert: the parent-aware temporal expert reaches 64.71 R1@0.5, while diagnostic oracles over the same candidate family reach 75.68 R1@0.5 within the parent-reranked pool and 94.97 R1@0.5 over the full 100-candidate evidence pool. We introduce OVET-Consensus, a rank-preserving counterfactual event verification layer that keeps the temporal expert's ranked list as the default and permits only high-confidence rank-1 overrides from a policy calibrated on held-out training videos. The verifier uses parent-aware interval evidence, structured Qwen3-VL event judgments, VideoLLaMA3 pairwise temporal signals, and dense temporal evidence, but never lets those signals freely reorder the top-K list. On QVHighlights validation, OVET-Consensus improves the parent-aware temporal expert from 64.71 to 65.35 R1@0.5 with a positive paired-bootstrap interval, while mAP is largely unchanged, from 42.67 to 42.74; it makes 20 overrides, with 15 helpful corrections and 3 harmful corrections. The results point to a practical constraint on verifier design: semantic compatibility is useful evidence, but temporal grounding still requires deciding where the event starts, changes, and ends inside a visually compatible scene.

</details>


### [77] [EfficientVPR: Toward Efficient Visual Place Recognition via Scene-Aware Prompt Tuning and Adaptive Feature Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22506)
*Wenjing Tang,Chuanguang Yang,Zhulin An,Libo Huang,Yongjun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级单阶段VPR框架EfficientVPR，通过场景感知视觉提示微调与实例相关关键局部特征增强，在DINOv2-small规模下实现SOTA并大幅提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VPR方法主要靠扩大模型容量或引入计算密集的重排序阶段来应对极端环境变化，形成显著效率瓶颈，难以兼顾精度与实用计算约束。

Method: 两项创新：i) 场景感知视觉提示微调，以较少参数适配预训练特征并动态适应样本特定特性；ii) 实例相关关键局部特征增强模块，进一步强化判别性区域。整体为轻量单阶段框架。

Result: 在Pitts250k、MSLS、Eynsham、AmsterTime、SVOX上全面评测：DINOv2-small规模下超越所有同尺度方法，刷新SOTA；相比SOTA的DINOv2-large两阶段方法提速73倍、特征维度降低60%，平均R@1差距保持在2.5%以内。

Conclusion: EfficientVPR证明无需扩大容量或重排序即可获得良好速度-精度权衡，为实用化VPR提供高效方案。

Abstract: Visual place recognition (VPR) faces critical challenges in handling extreme environmental variations while meeting the computational constraints of practical applications. Current methods predominantly address these challenges by either scaling up model capacity or employing computationally intensive reranking stages, creating a significant efficiency bottleneck. To overcome this limitation, we propose EfficientVPR, a lightweight one-stage framework that achieves speed-accuracy trade-offs through two key innovations: i) a scene-aware visual prompt tuning method which adapts pretrained features with less parameters while dynamically adjusting to sample-specific characteristics, and ii) an instance-dependent key local feature enhancement module that further reinforces discriminative regions. Comprehensive evaluations on Pitts250k, MSLS, Eynsham, AmsterTime and SVOX demonstrate that our method establishes a new SOTA for DINOv2-small models by outperforming all same-scale competitors, and delivers a $73\times$ speedup with 60\% lower-dimensional features while maintaining competitive (within 2.5% average R@1 gap) against the SOTA DINOv2-large-based two-stage method. Our code will be available at https://github.com/WiniTang/EfficientVPR.

</details>


### [78] [Efficient Self-Supervised Adaptation of 3D Abdominal Vision-Language Model for Institution-Specific HCC Classification via Full Fine-Tuning and PEFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22504)
*Febryan Putra Kartika,Cheng-Yu Ma,Ying-Jia Lin,Chi-Tung Cheng,Kuan-Fu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究将预训练3D医学视觉-语言模型跨机构适配到肝细胞癌（HCC）分类任务，通过CT-报告自监督对比学习比较全量微调与参数高效微调（PEFT），证明无需病灶级或分类标签即可有效适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VLM虽能建模图像-文本跨模态关系，但其向特定机构临床任务的适配尚不充分；且在数据有限、存在跨机构域偏移的约束下，如何高效微调缺乏实践证据。

Method: 使用另一机构的配对腹部CT与放射报告，对预训练3D医学VLM进行CT-报告对比学习的自监督微调，用于HCC分类；对比全量微调与PEFT（图像编码器用ConvLoRA、文本编码器用LoRA），并分析对比温度初始化的影响。

Result: 两种策略在跨机构域偏移下均取得较强分类性能：PEFT的AUC为0.79、F1为0.70；全量微调为AUC 0.82、F1 0.71，均低于有监督HCC分类方法的报告值。全量微调收敛快但数轮内即过拟合；PEFT仅更新约1%参数即达可比性能，但需更多训练步。对比温度初始化显著影响分类性能。

Conclusion: 3D医学VLM可借助自监督CT-报告对比学习高效适配机构特异的HCC分类任务，无需病灶级标注；实际应用中需权衡全量微调与PEFT在过拟合、训练成本与步数上的取舍。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) capture cross-modal relationships between image and text, yet their adaptation to institution-specific clinical tasks remains underexplored. In this study, we fine-tuned a pretrained 3D medical VLM for hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) classification using paired abdominal CT scans and radiology reports from a different institution with acquisition characteristics that differ from the model's original pretraining corpus. We compared two adaptation strategies: full fine-tuning and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT), motivated by the common use of PEFT to reduce computational cost and enable adaptation under limited-data constraints. Both approaches achieve strong downstream HCC classification performance despite the cross-institutional domain shift, with PEFT reaching an AUC of 0.79 and F1 of 0.70, and full fine-tuning achieving an AUC of 0.82 and F1 of 0.71. Although these results remain below the performance reported by supervised HCC classification approaches, our method achieves improvements over the pretrained model through CT-report contrastive learning without requiring lesion-level annotations or HCC classification labels as training targets. Full fine-tuning converges rapidly but overfits within a few epochs, whereas PEFT (ConvLoRA for the image encoder and LoRA for the text encoder) attains comparable performance while updating approximately 1% of the model parameters, although requiring more training steps. We also examine the role of contrastive temperature, observing that temperature initialization significantly affects classification performance. This study demonstrates that a 3D medical VLM can be efficiently adapted to institution-specific HCC classification using self-supervised CT-report contrastive learning, while highlighting the practical trade-offs between full fine-tuning and parameter-efficient fine-tuning.

</details>


### [79] [Fast and Strong Image Protection against Diffusion Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22494)
*Jiwon Park,Jihye Kang,Namhyuk Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出保护性扩散模型(PDM)，一次训练即可通过多步去噪为新图像采样对抗扰动，避免逐图优化；以TRP损失作为奖励进行后训练，在面部与艺术作品上优于9个基线，成本仅为教师模型的十分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化文生图扩散模型可凭少量照片复现个人形象，现有图像保护方法对每张图从零优化扰动，成本随图像数量线性增长，难以规模化应用。

Method: 训练一个保护性扩散模型(PDM)：先蒸馏一个迭代式教师模型，使其能在多步去噪过程中直接采样新图像的扰动；再用目标残差投影(TRP)损失作为奖励进行后训练，该目标强于既有目标函数。

Result: 在面部与艺术作品数据上，PDM优于九个基线方法，并以约教师模型十分之一的成本接近其保护效果。

Conclusion: 将扰动生成建模为可学习的扩散采样过程，可显著降低图像保护的计算开销并提升保护强度，为可扩展的个性化生成防护提供可行方案。

Abstract: Personalized text-to-image diffusion models can reproduce a person from a handful of photographs. Image protection perturbs each image before publication so that fine-tuning on it fails, but it optimizes every perturbation from scratch, so its cost grows with the number of images. We present the protective diffusion model (PDM), which is trained once and samples the perturbation of a new image over many denoising steps. PDM distills an iterative teacher and is then post-trained with the target residual projection (TRP) loss as a reward, a stronger objective than existing ones. PDM outperforms nine baselines on faces and artworks and nearly matches its teacher at a tenth of the cost.

</details>


### [80] [Sample-Aligned Decoders for Latent Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22458)
*Yu Lei,Yi Li,Hongcheng Gao,Yipeng Wei,Liang Li,Li Su,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Sample-Aligned Decoders (SAD) 后训练框架，仅微调解码器（冻结生成器），通过"生成样本分布对齐"与"编码器锚定保持"两项机制，使解码器适配生成潜变量的分布，在无推理开销下提升多种SOTA生成器的FID等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜生成模型由解码器将生成的潜变量还原为图像，但解码器仅在编码器潜变量上训练，从未见过测试时的生成潜变量，导致生成器残差误差被直接渲染进最终图像。现有方案（重训生成器、每步采样加开销、让解码器对扰动的真实潜变量鲁棒）均假设生成误差类似扰动，但作者发现生成潜变量虽同样落在编码器潜流形上，却服从不同分布，故应让解码器适配生成潜变量的分布。

Method: SAD为事后（post-training）框架，只微调冻结生成器中的解码器，推理零开销。核心含两部分：(1) Generated-Sample Distribution Alignment：在冻结基础模型特征空间中，将解码后的生成潜变量与无配对真实图像对齐，解决无配对监督问题；(2) Encoder Anchor Preservation：约束解码器在编码器潜变量上保持接近原解码器，缓解潜空间漂移（decoder脱离配对目标后遗忘原潜空间）。理论分析表明该对齐仅在生成潜变量上有效，且校正幅度与生成器相对于编码器产生该潜变量的频率成正比；在编码器潜变量上则会与锚定项相冲突。

Result: 在三种tokenizer下的四个SOTA ImageNet生成器上均有提升，如REPA-E的FID-50K从1.15降至0.98；可与discriminator guidance互补，并可扩展到文生图生成。

Conclusion: 解码器失配的根源是生成潜变量与编码器潜变量的分布差异而非单纯扰动，针对生成分布对齐并辅以编码器锚定的SAD可作为一种通用、无推理成本的后训练插件，显著改善潜生成模型的解码质量。

Abstract: Latent generative models synthesize images by decoding generated latents, yet their decoder is trained only to reconstruct encoder latents and never sees the latents it decodes at test time, so the residual errors of the generator are rendered directly into the final image. Existing remedies retrain the generator, add cost at every sampling step, or make the decoder robust to perturbed real latents, assuming that generator errors resemble such perturbations. We find that they do not. Generated latents lie on the encoder latent manifold as closely as real ones, yet follow a different distribution. The decoder should therefore be adapted to the distribution of generated latents, which raises two challenges: (1) unpaired supervision, since a generated latent has no ground-truth image, and (2) latent-space drift, since a decoder freed from paired targets forgets the latent space it must decode. We propose Sample-Aligned Decoders (SAD), a post-training framework that fine-tunes only the decoder of a frozen generator at no inference cost. Generated-Sample Distribution Alignment matches decoded generated latents to unpaired real images in a frozen foundation-model feature space, and Encoder Anchor Preservation keeps the decoder close to the original one on encoder latents. Our analysis shows that the alignment is effective only on generated latents, where it corrects each sample in proportion to how much more often the generator produces such latents than the encoder; on encoder latents it can only work against the anchor. SAD improves four state-of-the-art ImageNet generators across three tokenizers, e.g., FID-50K from 1.15 to 0.98 for REPA-E, complements discriminator guidance, and extends to text-to-image generation. Our code and models are available at https://torpedo2003.github.io/SAD/.

</details>


### [81] [Do Diffusion Models Learn to Generalize Basic Visual Skills?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22427)
*Amish Sethi,Boya Zeng,Wenhao Chai,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出受控实验框架Visual Generative Lab (VGL)，用显式规则合成各基础视觉技能（大小、位置、旋转、计数、颜色、形状及组合）的训练数据，以规则化指标精确测量扩散模型的泛化能力；发现：外推能力有限、组合泛化更依赖技能组合多样性而非数据规模、技能多独立失效（位置除外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估依赖人类偏好与提示跟随基准（文生图），或FID、IS等分布级指标（类条件模型），无法区分模型是真正习得基础视觉技能还是仅匹配训练分布/模式，缺乏对泛化能力的可控诊断手段。

Method: 构建VGL受控实验框架：按显式规则为每项基础视觉技能（size、position、rotation、count、color、shape及其组合）生成合成训练数据，并设计任务特定、基于规则的精确度量指标；在该数据上从零训练扩散模型并系统评估其（含组合）泛化行为。

Result: (1) 默认设置下外推受限：size与position在超出训练范围数个像素即性能下降，count在范围外仅约50%准确率，rotation外推精度随随机种子波动大；(2) 组合泛化中，不同基础技能组合的数量比数据集规模更重要；(3) 各技能大多独立失效，position为例外。

Conclusion: 分布级指标与人类偏好评测不足以揭示扩散模型的基础视觉技能习得情况；受控、规则化基准可精确定位泛化短板，并提示提升组合多样性比扩大数据量更有利于组合泛化。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate high-quality images, but how well they generalize is hard to measure. Current evaluations rely on human preference and prompt-following benchmarks for text-to-image models and on distribution-level metrics such as Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) and Inception Score (IS) for class-conditional models, but these cannot tell whether models learn basic visual skills or largely match training patterns. To address this gap, we introduce the Visual Generative Lab (VGL), a controlled experimental framework for studying how diffusion models learn and generalize basic visual skills, including size, position, rotation, count, color, shape, and their compositions. By generating synthetic training data for each skill from explicit rules, VGL allows precise measurement of generalization with task-specific, rule-based metrics. Training models from scratch on these data reveals three patterns in this controlled setting: (1) with our default design, extrapolation is limited: size and position degrade within a few pixels of the training range, count reaches only about 50% accuracy on out-of-range counts, and rotation extrapolation accuracy varies widely across seeds; (2) for compositional generalization, the number of distinct basic visual skill combinations matters more than dataset size; (3) skills mostly fail independently, except position. Code and data-generation scripts: https://github.com/AmishSethi/visual-generative-lab.

</details>


### [82] [Can Vision-Language Models Truly Understand Images? A Benchmark Beyond Commonsense Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22360)
*Ling Lin,Heng Su,Xiangzeng Liu,Yang Bai,Congcong Zhu,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出ContraSense基准，用反直觉图文对削弱统计先验，检验MLLM是否真正基于视觉内容推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言基准多为符合人类常识的样本，模型可依赖数据集先验与统计共现而非真实视觉理解即取得高分，难以判断其预测是否"忠实"于图像内容。

Method: 系统构建ContraSense基准：精心筛选刻意违背常识预期的反直觉图文对，显著削弱统计偏差的有效性，迫使模型必须依赖视觉证据进行推理；并在多种代表性开源与闭源MLLM上开展全面评测。

Result: 当前MLLM与人类存在显著性能差距：最强闭源模型准确率仅约60%，而人类被试达96.04%。

Conclusion: 现有MLLM在忠实视觉理解上仍存在明显不足，需要发展更鲁棒、真正以图像内容为依据的多模态推理方法；基准已公开。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive performance on a wide range of vision-language tasks. However, it remains unclear whether their predictions are grounded in the visual content itself or are largely driven by statistical co-occurrence patterns and commonsense correlations learned from large-scale image-text corpora. Existing vision-language benchmarks predominantly consist of samples that conform to human commonsense, allowing models to achieve strong performance by exploiting dataset priors rather than genuinely understanding image content. To address this limitation, we present ContraSense, a systematically constructed benchmark for evaluating faithful vision-language understanding. ContraSense consists of carefully curated counter-intuitive image-text pairs that deliberately violate common expectations, substantially reducing the effectiveness of statistical biases and requiring models to rely on visual evidence for reasoning. We conduct comprehensive evaluations on a diverse set of representative open- and closed-source MLLMs. The results reveal a substantial performance gap between current MLLMs and humans: even the strongest closed-source model achieves only about 60\% accuracy, compared with 96.04\% for human participants. These findings demonstrate that current MLLMs still struggle with faithful visual understanding and highlight the need for more robust multimodal reasoning grounded in image content. The ContraSense benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/LingLin00/ContraSense.

</details>


### [83] [SandBAR: A Band-Asymmetric Architecture for Underwater Image Restoration with Removable Frequency Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22355)
*Yanzi Liu,Haoran Hou,Jiachi Tong,Shen Chen,Shuang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SandBAR——一种面向水下图像复原的频带非对称统一架构，并配套SPARK模块、可学习低频引导路径、三尺度空频目标以及可移除的Post-attention Scaffold Training（PaST）训练方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下散射、吸收与浑浊造成互补的频带退化：低频承载大尺度、通道相关的颜色/外观失真，高频承载细节丢失。这种频带非对称性要求对全局颜色与局部结构采用不同的复原形式，而现有方法多将频率处理作为孤立模块，未在架构层面围绕该非对称性组织流程。

Method: 提出SandBAR统一架构，在block、encoder、objective三个层级组织频带非对称：设计带非对称三明治block并预留post-attention修复槽位；提出SPARK模块，对低频带施加通道感知的大感受野校正、对高频细节带施加轻量局部细化；引入可学习低频引导路径，将输入的低通视图注入深层encoder；采用三尺度空频目标在decoder全程监督同一频带轴；并基于预留槽位提出PaST——可移除的非线性脚手架训练方案，训练时赋予频带特定容量，推理时不保留该容量。

Result: 在EUVP / LSUI / UIEB上分别达到29.89 / 28.92 / 22.77 dB，在该基准族上刷新三域平均SOTA，超出最强已发表结果约1.0 dB；PaST在推理阶段不增加参数与FLOPs，并保持匹配的延迟。

Conclusion: 频带非对称性可作为贯穿网络结构与优化目标的组织原则，有效统一全局颜色与局部结构复原；脚手架式训练可在零推理开销下提升性能。

Abstract: Light scattering, absorption and turbidity degrade underwater imagery through complementary frequency tendencies: large-scale, channel-dependent appearance distortions and local high-frequency detail loss. This band asymmetry motivates different forms of restoration for global colour and local structure. Our primary contribution is SandBAR, a novel unified band-asymmetric architecture for underwater image restoration. Rather than treating frequency processing as an isolated module, SandBAR organises the restoration pipeline around band asymmetry at the block, encoder and objective levels. Within this architecture, we introduce a novel band-asymmetric sandwich block with a reserved post-attention repair slot and the Split-band Parallel Asymmetric Repair Kernel (SPARK), a novel module that applies channel-aware large-support correction to the low band, which predominantly carries large-scale appearance distortions, and lightweight local refinement to the detail-bearing high band, thereby restoring global colour while preserving fine structure. To deliver the global cast to the depth at which the network represents global structure, a learnable low-frequency guidance path injects a low-pass view of the input into the deep encoder, and a three-scale spatial-frequency objective supervises the same axis throughout the decoder. Building on the reserved post-attention slot, we further introduce Post-attention Scaffold Training (PaST), a novel removable nonlinear scaffolding scheme that trains the permanent SandBAR graph under band-specific capacity and runs it at inference without that capacity. SandBAR reaches 29.89 / 28.92 / 22.77 dB on EUVP / LSUI / UIEB and establishes a new three-domain mean state of the art on the benchmark family, exceeding the strongest published result by approximately 1.0 dB. PaST adds no parameters or FLOPs at inference and preserves matched latency.

</details>


### [84] [TRIDENT: Efficient Triple-Task Learning of Dehazing, Depth, and Uncertainty Estimation for Underwater 3-D Robot Visual Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22224)
*Geonmo Yang,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量高效的水下视觉感知系统TRIDENT，用单骨干+三并行解码器联合完成增强、深度估计与不确定性建模，性能与效率均优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下成像受光传播影响导致纹理模糊、色彩对比度低，削弱机器人稳定作业的感知能力；同时现有方法难以用轻量模型同时应对多维水下视觉任务。

Method: 构建基于学习的水下感知系统，提出TRIDENT模型：一个共享特征的backbone接三个并行解码器，分别处理图像增强、深度估计与不确定性估计，并以轻量快速方式设计，支持复杂参数化表达与联合学习。

Result: 在多个标准数据集上，TRIDENT在图像增强与深度估计任务上显著优于现有方法；同时完成三项任务时，其内存占用与推理时间效率仍优于对比方法；联合学习在特征匹配上表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: 该工作表明多任务共享特征的水下感知框架可兼顾精度与效率，并可由2-D视觉平滑扩展至3-D视觉任务。

Abstract: Underwater visual systems often suffer from blurry textures and low color contrast due to the inevitable light propagation. These issues can significantly degrade the perception of stable robotic operations. In this paper, we introduce a novel learning-based sensing system that tackles the multidimensional vision tasks in underwater; concretely, we deal with image enhancement, depth estimation, and uncertainty for 3-D visual systems. Also, we propose a TRIDENT model in a fast and lightweight manner; TRIDENT consists of three parallelized decoders and one backbone structure for efficient feature sharing. In addition, it is designed to be trained to express complex parameterization. In experimental evaluation on several standard datasets, we demonstrate that TRIDENT significantly outperforms other existing methods on image enhancement and depth estimation. Despite performing three tasks, our model has better efficiency than the others for both memory size and inference time. Finally, our joint learning approach demonstrates robustness in feature matching and seamlessly extends from 2-D to 3-D vision tasks. Supplements are available at https://sites.google.com/view/underwater-trident/home

</details>


### [85] [ARG-Avatar: Animation-Rendering Guidance for Efficient Feedforward Avatar Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22207)
*Jianjin Xu,Soham Dasgupta,Fernando De la Torre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅68M参数的高效前馈框架ARG-Avatar，通过显式利用3D动画与渲染的几何对应关系（FACRoPE注意力与ITR管线），在无需大规模扩展的情况下达到SOTA人体化身重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式人体化身重建使推理时间降至数秒，但近期方法严重依赖扩大模型容量、训练数据和算力。作者质疑这种扩展是否必要，认为3D动画与渲染本身已提供强几何对应，现有方法却仍试图从数据中学习这些对应。

Method: 设计Foreground Avatar Coordinate RoPE attention（FACRoPE），将坐标先验编码进旋转位置编码，引导化身token关注其对应的图像patch；并提出Intermediate Token Rendering（ITR）管线，在中间token层面进行渲染监督，从而将几何对应关系显式注入网络，减少对参数与数据规模的依赖。

Result: 在多数据集实验中，使用两张及以上输入图像时持续优于先前方法。相较LHM++，可训练参数约少9倍、训练序列少4.8倍、训练算力少14倍，推理速度快6倍，并在分布外NeuMan基准上取得2.7 dB的PSNR提升。

Conclusion: 显式利用3D动画与渲染的几何对应，比单纯扩展模型容量、数据和算力更有效；紧凑的前馈框架即可实现SOTA精度与显著效率优势。

Abstract: Reconstructing animatable 3D human avatars from images is important for applications such as gaming and augmented reality. Feedforward methods make avatar reconstruction accessible by reducing inference time to a few seconds, but recent approaches rely heavily on scaling model capacity, training data, and computation. We ask whether such scaling is necessary. To this end, we introduce ARG-Avatar, an efficient feedforward framework with only 68M trainable parameters that achieves SOTA reconstruction quality. Our key insight is that 3D animation and rendering already provide strong geometric correspondences that existing methods largely attempt to learn from data. In light of this, we design the Foreground Avatar Coordinate RoPE attention (FACRoPE) and Intermediate Token Rendering (ITR) pipeline to guide avatar tokens to attend to their corresponding patches. Experiments across multiple datasets show that ARG-Avatar consistently outperforms prior methods when using two or more input images. Compared with LHM++, ARG-Avatar uses approximately 9$\times$ fewer trainable parameters, 4.8$\times$ fewer training sequences, and 14$\times$ less training compute, while running 6$\times$ faster and achieving a 2.7 dB PSNR improvement on the out-of-distribution NeuMan benchmark.

</details>


### [86] [SRED: Sparse Regional Evidence Distillation Across Magnifications in Whole-Slide Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22189)
*Zhiyuan Yang,Jiahao Cheng,Vincent Quoc-Huy Trinh,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出稀疏区域证据蒸馏（SRED），通过稀疏自编码器事件字典与逻辑OR池化，将20x教师特征的区域级事件出现性迁移至5x编码器，在无切片级标签下提升分类/生存预测性能并保持5x推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高倍镜WSI保留细节但需编码大量patch，低倍镜降低计算却损失细节。现有跨倍率蒸馏匹配的特征常包含低倍输入不可达的细节，且部分方法依赖切片级标签，限制可扩展性。

Method: 用稀疏自编码器(SAE)从20x教师特征学习事件字典；对空间对齐的patch做逻辑OR池化，记录每个事件是否在对应5x区域内任一patch被检测到，从而保留区域内局部与共享证据；再用这些区域级目标微调预训练5x编码器，无需切片级标签。

Result: 相比原生5x特征，在7个分类与分级队列上平均macro-F1提升1.1个百分点，在5个生存队列上平均C-index提升1.7个百分点；运行时间与原生5x相当，相对20x平均加速11.58倍。可视化显示部分字典分量对应可识别的组织学模式。

Conclusion: 以“可达的区域级事件出现性”作为蒸馏目标，可避免匹配低倍输入不可达的细节，实现无标签、高效率的跨倍率知识迁移，兼顾性能与计算开销。

Abstract: In computational pathology, high-magnification whole-slide image analysis preserves tissue detail but requires encoding many patches, whereas lower magnification reduces computation by covering a higher field of view per patch while sacrificing detail. Existing cross-magnification distillation matches features that often encode detail unreachable by low-magnification input, sometimes requiring slide-level labels that limit scalability. To address those issues, we introduce Sparse Regional Evidence Distillation (SRED), which transfers regional event occurrences that encode reachable high-level interactions between details without slide-level labels. We use a sparse autoencoder (SAE) to learn an event dictionary from 20x teacher features. Logical OR pooling records whether each event is detected in any spatially aligned patch, retaining both localized and shared evidence within the corresponding 5x region. A pretrained 5x encoder is fine-tuned to predict these regional targets without slide-level labels. Visual analysis identifies dictionary components responsive to recognizable histological patterns, supporting target interpretation. Compared with native 5x features, SRED improves average macro-F1 by 1.1 points across seven classification and grading cohorts and average C-index by 1.7 points across five survival cohorts. Runtime matches native 5x, with an average 11.58x speedup over 20x.

</details>


### [87] [Unsupervised Multi-modal Medical Image Registration via Invertible Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22152)
*Mengjie Guo,Jinming Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无监督可逆神经网络配准方法INNReg，通过图像翻译+配准两阶段实现多模态医学图像对齐，并用NMI障碍损失提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态医学图像配准为图像引导治疗提供全面信息，但不同模态间空间关系复杂且未知，直接配准困难。基于翻译的配准要求翻译后保持几何一致性，现有方法在此方面不足。

Method: INNReg包含图像翻译网络（多模态→单模态）与配准网络（用翻译后单模态图像对齐多模态图像）。为保证翻译后几何一致性，引入可逆神经网络（INN），并结合动态深度可分离卷积的局部注意力机制；同时设计基于归一化互信息（NMI）的新型障碍损失（barrier loss）约束配准网络。

Result: 在两个公开多模态医学数据集（MRI T1/T2、MRI/CT）上实验，INNReg表现出优越的配准性能。

Conclusion: 可逆结构与几何一致性约束结合NMI障碍损失，能有效提升无监督多模态配准精度；代码已开源。

Abstract: In medical imaging, the alignment of multi-modal images plays a critical role in providing comprehensive information for image-guided therapies. Despite its importance, multi-modal image registration poses significant challenges due to the complex and often unknown spatial relationships between different image modalities. To address this, we introduce a novel unsupervised translation-based multi-modal registration method, termed Invertible Neural Network-based Registration (INNReg). INNReg consists of an image-to-image translation network that converts multi-modal images into mono-modal counterparts and a registration network that uses the translated mono-modal images to align the multi-modal images. Specifically, to ensure the preservation of geometric consistency after image translation, we introduce an Invertible Neural Network (INN) that leverages a dynamic depthwise convolution-based local attention mechanism. Additionally, we design a novel barrier loss function based on Normalized Mutual Information to impose constraints on the registration network, which enhances the registration accuracy. The superior performance of INNReg is demonstrated through experiments on two public multi-modal medical image datasets, including MRI T1/T2 and MRI/CT pairs. The code is available at https://github.com/MeggieGuo/INNReg

</details>


### [88] [BATMoS: Budget-Aware Guidance Allocation for Efficient Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22028)
*Zhixu Du,Kuo Yang,Jingwei Sun,Hai "Helen" Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视频扩散模型推理预算的效果取决于去噪轨迹上的计算位置（八种等预算配置的VBench总分相差4.80分）。本文提出BATMoS，用单一预训练去噪器在新鲜CFG与缓存引导之间路由，以幅度阈值规则在低幅度块复用引导、其余块保留新鲜引导，由一个整数控制预算，无需额外路由训练或逐预算阈值搜索。在CogVideoX-2B上（每种配置4,720视频、16个VBench维度）取得八种分配策略中最高总分，比完整CFG少20%分支评估而总分仅低0.10分；与特征复用组合可将有效分支评估从80降至62，总分仅下降0.11分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型推理中，计算预算的效果高度依赖其在去噪轨迹上的分配方式；引导评估在不同扩散时刻的价值并不均等，而现有做法缺乏显式、可调且无需额外训练的预算分配设计。

Method: 提出BATMoS（Budget Aware Temporal Mixture of Strategies），在单个预训练去噪器上于"新鲜无分类器引导（CFG）"与"缓存引导"两种策略间按时间路由：采用基于幅度的时间规则，对低幅度块分配引导复用，其余块保留新鲜引导；预算由单一整数控制，无需训练路由网络，也无需逐预算阈值搜索。可与特征复用叠加。

Result: 在CogVideoX-2B上，跨全部16个官方VBench维度、每种配置4,720个视频评估，BATMoS在八种分配策略中总分最高；相比完整CFG减少20%分支评估，总分仅下降0.10分。与特征复用组合时，有效分支评估从80降至62，总分下降0.11分。

Conclusion: 时间维度的引导分配是从固定推理预算中提取更多生成质量的实用机制，BATMoS提供了一种免训练、单参数可控且可与其他加速技术组合的预算分配方案。

Abstract: The effectiveness of a video diffusion model's inference budget depends on where computation is spent along the denoising trajectory. Across eight equal-budget inference configurations, VBench total scores span 4.80 points, highlighting the importance of explicit allocation design. Our key insight is that guidance evaluations are not equally valuable across diffusion time: retaining the sampling updates while selectively reusing guidance can preserve generation quality with fewer denoiser evaluations. We introduce BATMoS (Budget Aware Temporal Mixture of Strategies), which routes between fresh classifier-free guidance and cached guidance using a single pretrained denoiser. A magnitude-based temporal rule assigns guidance reuse to low-magnitude blocks and reserves fresh guidance for the remaining blocks, with a single integer controlling the computation budget. The resulting schedule requires no additional router training or per-budget threshold search. Evaluated on CogVideoX-2B with 4,720 videos per configuration across all sixteen official VBench dimensions, BATMoS achieves the highest total score among the eight allocation policies and comes within 0.10 points of full classifier-free guidance using 20\% fewer branch evaluations. BATMoS also composes with feature reuse, reducing computation from 80 to 62 effective branch evaluations with a 0.11-point decrease in total score. These findings make temporal guidance allocation a practical mechanism for extracting more generation quality from a fixed inference budget.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond Appearance: Where Forest Visual Place Recognition Actually Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21996)
*Blake Matheson,Ben McShanag,Isabella Moffat,Michael Milford,Peyman Moghadam,Dana Kulić,Somayeh Hussaini,Kavindie Katuwandeniya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在WildCross基准上以MegaLoc为外观基线，系统评测时间序列、语言、场景图和深度几何四类上下文对森林环境视觉位置识别（VPR）的作用。同向重访中，分数空间序列匹配将100%精度下的召回率从50.9%提升至71.6%，而描述子空间序列匹配降至36.5%；其他上下文表示无一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林环境中重复植被以及光照、季节、视角变化导致不同地点外观相似、同一地点外观差异大，仅依赖外观的描述子在VPR中易失效。作者希望检验时间、语言、结构与几何上下文能否补充外观描述子，并厘清各表示失效的边界条件。

Method: 以MegaLoc作为外观基线，在WildCross基准上评测四类上下文表示：分数空间与描述子空间的序列匹配、视觉-语言描述与重排序、场景图表示、基于深度的树干图；实验覆盖同向重访、扩展路线与反向穿越三种场景，指标包含Recall@1及100%精度下的召回率。

Result: 同向重访中两种序列匹配的Recall@1相近（94.1% vs. 91.5%），但100%精度下召回率由单帧MegaLoc的50.9%升至分数空间的71.6%，描述子空间则降至36.5%。其余上下文表示相对外观基线无一致提升：同一地点描述缺乏地点特异性区分力，重排序受初始候选短名单约束，树干图匹配即便引入LiDAR深度仍然较弱。

Conclusion: 序列级时间上下文（尤其是分数空间融合）是森林VPR中最有效的补充信号，显著提升高精度要求下的召回；语言、场景图与深度几何上下文在当前形式下难以稳定超越外观基线，其瓶颈分别在于判别力不足、候选截断与匹配鲁棒性，提示未来需针对这些限制设计上下文表示。

Abstract: Visual place recognition (VPR) in forest environments remains challenging because repetitive vegetation, and changes in illumination, season, and viewpoint can make distinct places appear similar and revisited places appear substantially different. In this paper, we investigate whether temporal, linguistic, structural, and geometric context can improve on an appearance-only descriptor, and what limits each representation when it does not. Using MegaLoc as the appearance-based baseline on the WildCross benchmark, we evaluate score-space and descriptor-space sequence matching, vision-language captions and reranking, scene-graph representations, and depth-derived stem graphs across same-direction revisits, extended routes, and reverse traversals. On a same-direction revisit, both sequence matching strategies reach similar Recall@1 (94.1% vs. 91.5%), but recall at 100% precision rises from 50.9% (single-frame MegaLoc) to 71.6% in score space and falls to 36.5% in descriptor space. The remaining context representations provide no consistent improvement over the appearance-based baseline. Same-place captions show limited place-specific discrimination, reranking is constrained by the initial candidate shortlist, and stem-graph matching remains weak even with LiDAR depth.

</details>


### [90] [Deciding Without Pointing: A Case Study of Whether a Decision Model Localizes the Anomalies It Detects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21995)
*Hsiang-Hung Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究决策式(非生成式)多模态模型Clef-flash在工业检测中"检测到异常是否能定位异常",发现其决策头路由注意力几乎不提供定位信息,但可通过区域提问、重训头部或读取骨干状态部分恢复定位,仍不及专用小样本定位器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型能以逐选项概率回答图像问题而不生成文本,但其检测结果是否具备空间定位能力尚不清楚,本文以工业异常检测为案例检验"检测—定位"一致性。

Method: 以Clef-flash(后训练Qwen3.5-9B骨干)为对象,在MVTec AD与MVTec AD 2上做零样本检测,评估类别级图像AUROC与逐图像素AUROC;分析决策头路由注意力、遮蔽图像token、改动/移除问题文本上下文、与中心先验对比;对骨干注意力与状态做探针;并测试更大模型Clef(Qwen3.8-27B骨干),以及区域提问、重训头部、读取骨干三种定位恢复方案。

Result: 零样本检测表现不均:MVTec AD类别级图像AUROC 0.924,MVTec AD 2仅0.668。AD上决策头路由注意力平均像素AUROC 0.391,遮蔽图像token后检测不变;LLM骨干注意力确实指向缺陷,但改换问题后低于中心先验,去除文本上下文后低于随机(骨干注意力脱离物体,而头部热图几乎不变)。探针表明骨干状态在正常图像上携带位置信息,但在测试划分图像上决策依赖缺陷区域。更大模型Clef的决策头同样无可用的定位图。三种恢复手段有效但均不及专用小样本定位器。

Conclusion: 决策式MLLM的异常检测与定位能力显著解耦:决策头不承担定位,骨干注意力虽隐含定位线索但高度依赖问题文本且不稳定;定位需借助区域级提问、头部重训或骨干表征读取等外接手段,性能仍落后于专用小样本定位方法。

Abstract: Decision models can answer a typed question about an image with a probability per option, without generating text. In our case study of Clef-flash (post-trained Qwen3.5-9B backbone) on industrial inspection benchmarks, we ask whether it localizes the anomalies it detects. We obtained uneven results with zero-shot detection: per-category image AUROC 0.924 on MVTec AD and 0.668 on MVTec AD 2. On the former, the decision head's routing attention produces 0.391 mean per-image pixel AUROC, and hiding the image tokens from it leaves detection unchanged. The multimodal LLM's backbone attention does point at the defect, however it falls below a center prior when we change question. It falls below chance if we remove textual context in the question, partly due to backbone attention leaving the object, while the head's map hardly changes. The backbone's states carry the location when probed with normal images, on the test-split images, however, the decision depends on the defect region. A larger model, Clef, that comes with a post-trained Qwen3.8-27B backbone, gives no useful map in the decision head either. We found that it's possible to recover anomaly location by asking question specifically on an image region, retraining the head or reading the backbone, but none matches dedicated few-shot localizers.

</details>


### [91] [How Many Persistent Features Does a Biometric Need? A Law Linking Equal Error Rate to Feature Count and Persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21973)
*Oleg Komogortsev,Mehedi Hasan Raju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文推导出生物特征EER与特征数N、测试-重测相关ρ之间的解析规律：简单律 EER=Φ(-½√N·atanhρ)，并扩展至持久性存在跨比较波动的情形，给出 EER=Φ(-m/(√(τ²+v)+√v)) 及无法被特征数降低的误差下界 Φ(-m/τ)，在101种条件下经验验证并导出设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有实证工作发现EER与特征时间持久性、所需特征数之间呈直线关系，但缺乏解释这些规律的理论法则。作者希望从原理上推导出这一关系，并进一步处理持久性在比较间波动（而非固定）的现实情形，为生物特征系统的特征数量与持久性设计提供定量依据。

Method: 假设N个独立标准化高斯特征、测试-重测相关为ρ、以余弦相似度打分；利用Fisher z变换将真实与 impostor 分数转为两个正态分布，推导EER闭式表达并反解N；用有效特征数 N_eff 处理特征间相关。中心扩展在Fisher尺度上引入持久性波动τ，得到一般形式 EER=Φ(-m/(√(τ²+v)+√v))，v=1/(N_eff-2)，无拟合参数。用已发表仿真、EER-ICC关系线、GazeBase/GazeBaseVR眼动嵌入，以及眼动、击键、ECG、人脸嵌入共101种条件进行验证。

Result: 无拟合参数的简单律可复现已发表仿真与EER-ICC直线关系，并较好预测GazeBase上KCC-EER关系（R²=0.88，GazeBaseVR为0.63）。一般形式在101种条件上从真实/impostor分数读取，达到R²=0.98、中位误差0.6个百分点，优于简单律的0.87和1.9个百分点；61种眼动/击键/ECG条件在同一分数上检验一致性，40种人脸条件采用留出受试者且精度相同。τ√(N_eff-2) 给出设计规则：该量远小于1时增加或去相关特征收益充分，超过约3后收益甚微，仅更高或更均匀的持久性有效。

Conclusion: EER与特征数、持久性之间的直线经验规律可由Fisher z变换下的高斯模型精确解释；引入持久性波动后得到含误差下界的一般定律，且无自由参数即具高预测精度。τ√(N_eff-2) 作为设计规则，界定了增加/去相关特征的有效区间，指出在高持久性波动情形下唯一有效的改进途径是提升持久性水平或其均匀性。

Abstract: A biometric designer controls the number of features and their temporal persistence. Earlier empirical work linked the equal error rate (EER) to persistence and to the required number of features by straight lines. We derive the law behind these regularities. For $N$ independent standardized Gaussian features with test-retest correlation $ρ$, scored by cosine similarity, Fisher's $z$-transform turns the genuine and impostor scores into two normals, giving $\mathrm{EER}=Φ(-\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{N}\,\operatorname{atanh}ρ)$ and, inversely, $N=(2q/\operatorname{atanh}ρ)^2$ with $q=Φ^{-1}(1-\mathrm{EER})$; intercorrelated features enter through an effective count $N_{\mathrm{eff}}$. The central extension lets persistence vary across comparisons with spread $τ$ on Fisher's scale: $\mathrm{EER}=Φ(-m/(\sqrt{τ^2+v}+\sqrt{v}))$ with $v=1/(N_{\mathrm{eff}}-2)$, which for a normal spread has a floor $Φ(-m/τ)$ that no number of features can lower. With no fitted parameter the simple law reproduces published simulations and the EER-ICC line, and predicts the KCC-EER relation of eye-movement embeddings on GazeBase ($R^2=0.88$), though less well on GazeBaseVR ($0.63$). Read from the genuine and impostor scores, the general form matches the EER of eye movements, keystroke dynamics, the ECG and face embeddings over 101 conditions with $R^2=0.98$ and a median error of $0.6$ points, against $0.87$ and $1.9$ points for the simple law. The 61 eye-movement, keystroke and ECG conditions check consistency on the same scores; the 40 face conditions use held-out subjects, with the same accuracy. The product $τ\sqrt{N_{\mathrm{eff}}-2}$ gives a design rule: adding or decorrelating features pays fully while it is well below one and little once it exceeds about three, where only higher or more uniform persistence helps.

</details>


### [92] [Rethinking Perceptual Similarity for Remote Sensing: Multi-Layer Attention Scoring and Human Preference Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21816)
*Eli Underwood,Marc Bosch,Neil Joshi,Jeffrey Wen,Myron Brown*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向遥感生成图像的人对齐感知质量度量 RSPD，基于 RemoteCLIP 多层对齐注意力响应构造，并在多类遥感生成任务的 2AFC 基准上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像的感知相似度度量难以迁移到遥感（RS）领域：严重的几何与语义错误可能在视觉上仍显合理，却对下游任务决定性错误。因此需要一个与人类感知对齐、面向生成遥感图像的专用质量度量。

Method: 首先开展受控扰动研究，逐步加大常见合成扰动的严重程度，基于 RemoteCLIP 中逐层的对齐注意力分数（AAS）响应发现：不同遥感伪影在不同深度被最佳捕获。据此提出 RSPD——一种成对度量，对七个独立校准的层距离取平均，且无需在人类偏好标签上调参。为在真实任务上评估，构建盲测、参考条件约束的 2AFC 基准，含五个各 300 三元组子集（合成伪影、机载与星载超分、SAR 到光学转换、时序对照），并构建 300 三元组的有序错配基准（确定性标签）。

Result: 在相同 RemoteCLIP 检查点下，RSPD 显著优于使用最终全局嵌入的简单余弦距离，并在各类真实遥感生成任务上与最强通用基线具有竞争力。

Conclusion: RSPD 为生成遥感图像提供了与人类感知对齐、免偏好标签调参的感知距离度量，证明多层特征融合优于单一全局嵌入；配套基准为遥感生成质量评估提供了可复现的评测协议。

Abstract: Perceptual similarity metrics for natural images transfer poorly to remote sensing (RS), where severe geospatial and semantic failures can remain visually plausible while still being decisively wrong for downstream use. To address this, we develop a novel human-aligned perceptual quality metric for generated RS imagery and evaluate it across several generation tasks. We first conduct a controlled perturbation study where we progressively increase the severity of common synthetic perturbations. Using the layer-wise Aligned Attention Score (AAS) responses in RemoteCLIP, we find that different RS artifacts are best captured at different depths. Based on these findings, we introduce Remote Sensing Perceptual Distance (RSPD), a pairwise metric that averages seven independently calibrated layer distances without tuning on human-preference labels. To evaluate our metric on realistic RS generation tasks, we construct a blind, reference-conditioned two-alternative forced-choice (2AFC) benchmark with five 300-triplet subsets spanning synthetic artifacts, airborne and spaceborne super-resolution, SAR-to-optical translation, and timeseries controls. We also construct a 300-triplet ordered misregistration benchmark with deterministic labels. Using the same RemoteCLIP checkpoint, RSPD substantially outperforms simple cosine distance with the final global embedding and is competitive with the strongest generic baselines across the realistic RS generation tasks.

</details>


### [93] [Keeping Parts Honest: Isolated Part Graphs for Faithful Vision Transformer Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22853)
*Bernard Tomczyk,Gail Collyer-Hoar,Bryan Williams*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于"孤立部件图"的可解释ViT分类器，通过让每个部件仅从其自身patch编码、并用部件对计算边，使预测可直接分解为节点与边相似度之和，从而在构造上保证解释忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于部件/原型的ViT解释方法把预测归因于单个图像区域，但自注意力在patch间混合信息，导致部件级特征吸收全图上下文，产生"证据泄漏"，无法忠实反映决策依据。作者实证显示：将各被解释区域隔离编码时，现有可解释ViT准确率崩塌；即便在隔离条件下重训练也只能恢复部分精度。

Method: 1) 将图像表示为孤立部件构成的图，每个部件只由其自身patch经ViT编码，且对最后若干block在隔离条件下微调，阻断跨区域证据泄漏；2) 无标注地发现部件，并通过子部件进行层次化细化，提升跨图像的一致性与稳定性；3) 边由部件对计算，回收此前被注意力在物体内共享、却不影响部件本身的证据；4) 将图像图与每类学习到的原型图匹配，预测即为节点与边相似度的直接组合，且每项只由其归属patch计算，解释在构造上即忠实。

Result: 在11个细粒度与通用物体基准上，该方法准确率超过依赖泄漏的现有可解释方法，同时提供的解释真实反映模型实际使用的证据。

Conclusion: 通过隔离部件编码与图级原型匹配，可在不牺牲（甚至提升）精度的前提下，实现构造性忠实的ViT解释，为可解释分类器设计提供"部件独立+关系建模"的新范式。

Abstract: Part and prototype-based explanations for Vision Transformers (ViTs) present a decision as evidence from individual image regions. However, self-attention mixes information across patches, so part-level features absorb context from the whole image and consequently fail to faithfully reflect the decision. We show that existing interpretable ViT classifiers depend heavily on this leakage: when each explained region is encoded in isolation, their accuracy collapses, and retraining them under isolation recovers only part of the lost accuracy. We propose a classifier that represents each image as a graph of isolated parts. Each part is encoded only from its own patches by a ViT whose last blocks are fine-tuned under isolation, preventing evidence leakage from elsewhere in the image. Parts are discovered without annotation and refined hierarchically through their sub-parts, making them more consistent and stable across images than those of competing methods. Each edge is computed from part pairs, and recovers evidence that attention previously shared across the object without affecting the parts. Matching the image graph to a learned prototype graph per class makes the prediction a direct combination of node and edge similarities, each computed only from the patches it is credited to, so every explanation is faithful by construction. Across 11 fine-grained and general-object benchmarks, our method exceeds the accuracy of existing explainable methods that rely on leakage, while providing explanations that reflect what the model actually uses.

</details>


### [94] [Evolve in Panorama, Render in View: Panoramic World State Evolution for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22733)
*Longtao Jiang,Mingfei Han,Jianmin Bao,Tingjun Dai,Jinjie Wei,Zhiran Zhang,Xin Tao,Zhihui Li,Salman Khan,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoEvo 用全景视频扩散模型的隐空间作为持续演化的世界状态，并用透视渲染器生成高质量视角，解决视角离开再返回时场景冻结、物体丢失与外观漂移的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机转开再转回时，现有视频世界模型常把未观测区域"冻结"、丢失物体或改变外观，根源在于视野外内容缺乏持续变化的表示。

Method: 以 Panoramic Evolver 在全景视频扩散模型隐空间中演化世界状态（每个时刻都包含所有方向）；构建事件中心的 360° 真实视频数据集 PanoEvo-Data，并将 Evolver 由粗到细训练为图像到全景视频模型；再用基于透视视频扩散模型的 Perspective Renderer，通过隐空间投影与方向受限交叉注意力渲染清晰视角。

Result: 在 WRBench 上于 23 个公开评测模型中取得最佳综合得分，在 STEVO-Bench 上取得最高总分。

Conclusion: 以全景隐空间作为持续演化的世界表示、配合透视渲染的两阶段范式，可有效维持视角变化下的场景一致性；代码、模型与数据将开源。

Abstract: Video world models should maintain a world that keeps evolving as the viewpoint changes. Yet when the camera turns away and returns, current models often freeze the unobserved scene, lose objects, or change their appearance, because content outside the view has no representation that keeps changing. We present PanoEvo, which uses a Panoramic Evolver to evolve the world state in the latent space of a panoramic video diffusion model, where every viewing direction is present at every time step, so the scene evolves whether or not it is observed. Existing panoramic video models are not trained to evolve a scene from a single image or the objects within it, so we build PanoEvo-Data, an event-centric dataset from real 360° videos, and train the Panoramic Evolver coarse-to-fine as an image-to-panoramic-video model. Since each view covers only a small part of the low-resolution panorama, directly projected views are blurry and often show low-quality details or structural errors. A Perspective Renderer, built on a perspective video diffusion model, reads this state via latent-space projection and direction-restricted cross-attention to render sharp, high-quality views. PanoEvo achieves the best overall score among 23 publicly evaluated models on WRBench and the highest total score on STEVO-Bench. Code, models, and data will be released.

</details>


### [95] [PCRG: Training-Free Pruning and Caching of Reference Tokens for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22717)
*Siyuan Wang,Haoran Li,Yi Yue,Tak L. Fung,Quan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无训练、即插即用的参考token剪枝与缓存方法PCRG，将参考token分为剪枝/缓存/保留三级以加速参考到视频的扩散生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考到视频扩散Transformer将参考与生成token打包进单一全注意力序列，参考token约占一半；注意力代价随序列长度平方增长，且多数参考token静态、平滑或与输出无关，造成大量冗余计算。

Method: 利用模型已有两种信号对参考token分级：来自VAE latent的低层冗余度r（时间或邻域上变化小则信息少），以及来自VLM条件器的高层重要性g（提示词与首尾帧的注意力）；非冗余token保留，冗余且重要者缓存（KV只算一次复用），冗余且不重要者剪枝（首步前移出序列并保持RoPE位置不变）。

Result: 在已蒸馏至8步并int8量化的MiniMax-H3上，V2V-Bench端到端加速1.57x、VACE-Benchmark 1.49x，VBench质量仅降0.24%/0.17%；与步缓存/稀疏注意力叠加后，TeaCache 1.86x、Spark 1.82x、Veda 2.15x，保真度几乎不变。

Conclusion: 通过缩短序列而非改变注意力计算方式或步数实现加速，具正交可叠加性，兼顾效率与质量。

Abstract: Reference-to-video diffusion transformers pack the variational autoencoder (VAE) latents of all references and the generated tokens into one full-attention sequence, in which reference tokens make up about half. Because attention cost grows quadratically with sequence length, these tokens take up an even larger share of the attention computation, yet most of them are static, smooth or irrelevant to the output. To accelerate reference-conditioned generation, we propose pruning and caching of reference tokens for video generation (PCRG), a training-free, plug-and-play method that uses two signals the model already computes to assign each reference token to one of three tiers, namely pruned, cached or kept. The first is a low-level redundancy r from VAE latents, since a token whose latent barely changes over time or across its neighbors adds little new content. The second is a high-level importance g from the vision-language-model (VLM) conditioner, where the attention that the prompt and any first or last frames pay to a token tells whether the output needs to read it. Tokens that are not redundant are kept, and redundant tokens are cached if important and pruned otherwise. Pruned tokens leave the sequence before the first step with all rotary position embedding positions unchanged. Cached tokens have their keys and values computed once and reused, and kept tokens are processed as usual. On MiniMax-H3, already distilled to 8 timesteps and quantized to int8, PCRG is 1.57x faster end to end on our V2V-Bench and 1.49x faster on the public VACE-Benchmark, while the quality of VBench drops by only 0.24% and 0.17%, respectively. PCRG shortens the sequence rather than changing how attention is computed or which steps are run, so it also stacks on step caching and sparse attention. On V2V-Bench it reaches 1.86x with TeaCache, 1.82x with Spark at nearly unchanged fidelity and 2.15x with Veda.

</details>


### [96] [MedImgAnnotator: batch wise medical image annotation and preprocessing tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22450)
*Ewunate Assaye Kassaw,Amit Mehndiratta,Peush Sahni,Anup Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedImgAnnotator是一个简单、快速、半自动的批量标注工具，可将单例主动标注时间从约206.5秒降至59秒，并显著提升易用性，适用于大规模MRI/CT队列的AI预测标签编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建大规模队列数据集训练深度学习模型需要大量标注或修正AI预测标签，但现有开源标注工具导航步骤复杂、耗时长且不支持批处理，缺乏高效方案。

Method: 集成编辑工具、图像-掩膜配对逻辑、预处理函数、目标检测工具与插件管理系统，开发半自动批量标注工具MedImgAnnotator；以开源MR与CT扫描为数据，通过开发者与12名用户的主动标注时间和易用性评分进行评估，并由MICCAI 2026 CARE Challenge肝脏分割优胜者用于6412例MRI体数据的肝脏掩膜标注。

Result: 主动标注时间由206.5±32.87秒降至59秒；12名用户评估的Wilcoxon符号秩检验在标注时间与易用性评分上P值分别为0.032和0.008，差异显著；工具在多种模态与身体区域的不同感兴趣区标注中均表现可用。

Conclusion: MedImgAnnotator显著降低大规模影像队列的主动标注时间并提升易用性，以可执行文件和Python脚本形式开放获取。

Abstract: The curation of large cohort datasets for developing deep learning models demands annotating or editing over the AI-predicted labels. However, openly available annotation tools are time-consuming, as they involve complex navigational steps and do not support batch processing. This study aimed to develop a simple, fast, semiautomated batch-annotation tool named MedImgAnnotator. The tool was created by integrating editing tools, image mask pair matching logic, preprocessing functions, object detection tool, and a plugin management system. Its performance was evaluated in terms of active annotation time and ease of use by the developer and 12 users using open-source MR and CT scans. Additionally, the tool had been used by the MICCAI 2026 CARE Challenge liver segmentation best performance winner to annotate liver masks from 6412 MRI volumes. The evaluation results showed that MedImgAnnotator reduced active annotation time from 206.5+/-32.87 (average active annotation time of openly available tools) seconds to 59 seconds. The Wilcoxon signed-rank test of 12 users evaluation results recorded P values of 0.032 and 0.008 for the active annotation time and higher ease of use rating metrics, respectively, indicating that the current tool significantly reduces annotation time and enhances ease of use. Moreover, the tool utilization for annotating different regions of interest from different modality scans and body regions shows its usability across modalities and areas of interest. MedImgAnnotator, which significantly reduces active annotation time for large imaging cohorts, is available as an executable file and as a Python script. Access link: https://ewunateassaye.github.io/developed-tools/medimgannotator.html

</details>


### [97] [Where Demand Meets Supply: Spatial Elastic Consensus for Low-Bandwidth Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22439)
*Linshan Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Spatial Elastic Consensus (SEC)，一种带宽自适应协作感知框架，以双边供需共识分配token预算，并通过"先细化后掩码"的反膨胀流水线保证严格64KiB预算合规，在OPV2V、V2XSet与DAIR-V2X上取得最优AP@0.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单车3D感知受遮挡与传感器视距限制，难以支撑安全驾驶；协作感知可共享互补观测，但受限于紧张的车载通信带宽。现有空间选择器多为发送端驱动，把稀缺带宽浪费在自车已可见的目标上；且在掩码后做卷积会使孤立token向邻域扩散，隐性违反低带宽约束。

Method: 1) 双边需求—供给共识：自车构建内容感知的需求先验，各协作者评估本地供给，单轮协商将token预算分配给自车遗漏的关键证据，且无需传输稠密图；2) anti-dilation refine-before-mask流水线：先细化特征再离散选token，杜绝感受野泄漏，保证预算精确合规；3) 轻量可变形采样器：以学习到的局部偏移聚合稀疏消息，补偿token选择造成的空间上下文损失并缓解跨车姿态漂移。

Result: 在严格64KiB预算下，SEC在仿真数据集OPV2V、V2XSet及真实数据集DAIR-V2X上均稳定取得最佳AP@0.7。

Conclusion: 双边共识与预算精确传输相结合，是实现高效低带宽协作感知的关键。

Abstract: Occlusions and limited sensor range make ego-only 3D perception insufficient for safe driving. Collaborative perception addresses this by enabling connected vehicles to share complementary observations, yet it remains constrained by tight vehicular communication budgets. While spatial selection is more flexible than fixed-rate feature compression, existing selectors remain largely sender-driven and waste scarce bandwidth on ego-visible targets. Furthermore, applying convolutions after masking spreads isolated tokens into their neighborhoods, silently violating low-bandwidth constraints. To address this, we propose Spatial Elastic Consensus (SEC), a bandwidth-adaptive framework centered on bilateral demand-supply consensus. Instead of unilateral token selection, the ego vehicle formulates a content-aware demand prior while each collaborator evaluates its local supply. This single-round consensus allocates the token budget to critical ego-missed evidence rather than ego-visible redundancy, without transmitting dense maps. To guarantee exact budget compliance, an anti-dilation refine-before-mask pipeline refines features prior to discrete token selection, preventing receptive-field leakage. A lightweight deformable sampler then aggregates these sparse messages with learned local offsets, compensating for the spatial context lost to token selection and mitigating inter-agent pose drift. Evaluated at a stringent 64KiB budget, SEC consistently achieves the best AP@0.7 on the simulated OPV2V and V2XSet datasets and the real-world DAIR-V2X dataset. These results demonstrate that combining bilateral consensus with budget-exact transmission is key to efficient low-bandwidth collaborative perception.

</details>


### [98] [When Neural Collapse Fails to Route: Routing Drift in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22429)
*Nicolas Larue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"路由漂移"(routing drift)概念，刻画渐进式神经坍缩分类器在类增量学习(Class-IL)中局部可分但全局误路由的失败模式，并给出后处理式可靠性校准路由(RCR)方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐进式神经坍缩分类器能学出强的任务内决策边界，但全局Class-IL决策失败：样本在真实任务块内可分，却从另一块获得最大全局分数，导致Task-IL高、Class-IL低；小回放缓冲区因样本被反复优化而掩盖该问题。

Method: 直接从全局类别argmax定义route accuracy，将任务内判别性与跨任务分数可比性解耦；据此提出RCR——一种轻量后处理分类器，保留全局ETF框架，将标准化ETF对齐与围绕传输后、NC收缩的类代表的径向合理性证据组合，作为类局部可靠性证据，无需重训练表示。

Result: 在3个数据集、2种固定回放预算共6个设置中，5个取得最佳Class-IL FAA，6个均降低Class-IL遗忘；最难路由场景下较参考提升至多+6.75 FAA，配对评估中至多+8.03 FAA与+8.62最终路由准确率，同时保持Task-IL性能。

Conclusion: 路由漂移是基于ETF的持续学习可度量的失败模式；可靠性校准的类证据可在不重训练表示的前提下修复部分丢失的全局路由。

Abstract: Progressive neural-collapse classifiers can learn strong task-local decision boundaries while failing the global Class-IL decision. We identify the failure as routing drift: under an expanding equiangular tight frame (ETF) classifier, a held-out sample can remain separable within its true task block, yet receive its largest global score from another block. This produces high Task-IL accuracy but low Class-IL accuracy, and small replay buffers can mask the problem because stored samples are optimized repeatedly and often remain well routed. We formalize route accuracy directly from the global class argmax and use it to separate within-task discrimination from cross-task score comparability. This decomposition suggests a classifier-level correction: preserve the global ETF frame, but add class-local reliability evidence when transported class measures deviate from the neural-collapse ideal. We develop Reliability-Calibrated Routing (RCR), a lightweight post-hoc classifier that composes standardized ETF alignment with radial plausibility around transported, NC-shrunk class representatives. Across three datasets and two fixed replay-buffer budgets, this NC-consistent correction gives the best Class-IL FAA on five of six settings and reduces Class-IL forgetting on all six. The largest gains occur in harder routing regimes: up to $+6.75$ FAA over the reported reference, and up to $+8.03$ FAA and $+8.62$ final route accuracy in paired evaluations, while preserving Task-IL performance. These results position routing drift as a measurable failure mode of ETF-based continual learning and show that reliability-calibrated class evidence can repair part of the lost global routing without retraining the representation. Code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://towzeur.github.io/RCR/.

</details>


### [99] [CoBoVLA: Decoupling Language from Action with Discrete Codes for Robust and Interpretable Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22395)
*Mehul Arora,Dhruv Agarwal,Ambuj Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoBoVLA是一个250M参数的VLA模型，通过学习到的离散可解释码将语言理解、规划与控制解耦，显著提升对指令改写的鲁棒性并便于失败溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA在LIBERO上表现优异但对指令改写敏感（0.6B–7.5B模型在LIBERO-Para下降22–52点，SmolVLA从83%降至8%）；且因语言理解与动作生成联合学习，难以判断失败源于误解指令还是执行错误。

Method: 提出CoBoVLA：文本编码器将指令映射为共享步骤码（step codes），视觉规划器将其转换为动作码序列（action codes），由语言无关的执行器（actor）执行；指令仅编码一次，采用紧凑离散表示以支持资源受限硬件上的实时控制。

Result: LIBERO-Goal达91.4%（SmolVLA为82.8%），单控制步延迟17ms；LIBERO-Para上4,092条未见改写达81.1%、匹配episode 78.5%（段落改写的SmolVLA LoRA为36.5%）；无两步示教下完成48.8%的160个自由形式两步任务；指令编码正确时成功率91%，否则降至27.2%。

Conclusion: 解耦语言理解与控制可提升指令鲁棒性并使VLA失败更易诊断；剩余失败主要源于语言理解阶段。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve high success rates on the LIBERO benchmark but remain sensitive to paraphrasing. Existing VLAs ranging from 0.6B to 7.5B parameters experience a 22-52 point performance drop on LIBERO-Para while SmolVLA exhibits an even larger decline, from 83\% to 8\%. Because most VLAs jointly learn language understanding and action generation, it is difficult to determine whether a failure originates from misunderstanding an instruction or executing it incorrectly. To address these challenges, we introduce CoBoVLA, or CodeBookVLA, a 250M-parameter VLA that decouples language understanding, planning, and control through learned discrete interpretable codes. A text encoder maps the instructions to shared step codes, which a visual planner then converts into action code sequences executed by a language-independent actor. This design improves robustness to instruction paraphrasing while allowing failures to be traced to individual stages. It also enables efficient real-time control on resource-constrained hardware by encoding instructions only once and using compact discrete representations to keep each component lightweight. CoBoVLA achieves 91.4\% success on LIBERO-Goal, compared with 82.8\% for SmolVLA, under a matched protocol, with 17 ms per control step latency. On LIBERO-Para, it achieves 81.1\% across 4,092 unseen paraphrases and 78.5\% on matched episodes, compared with 36.5\% for SmolVLA LoRA fine-tuned on paraphrases. Without two-step demonstrations, CoBoVLA completes 48.8\% of 160 free-form two-step tasks. Finally, success reaches 91\% when instructions are correctly encoded but falls to 27.2\% otherwise, identifying language understanding as the main source of remaining failures. Together, these results demonstrate that decoupling language understanding and control can improve instruction robustness while making VLA failures easier to diagnose.

</details>


### [100] [Inertial Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22314)
*Yuan Gao,Fan Xu,Yu Guo,Yuxu Lu,Zhengru Fang,Hao Wu,Xiaomeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 InertialWorld，将惯性作为自回归视频世界模型的结构性先验，通过 Inertial Memory（欧拉稠密网格 + 拉格朗日持久载体）跨时间携带场景证据而非每步重建，从而缓解误差累积并提升长时预测稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界具有惯性，状态从既有状态演化而非每时刻重建；但现有视频世界模型缺乏显式的惯性状态演化机制，运动与结构的连续性仅隐含在视频预测中。自回归推演时反复重建会累积误差，导致预测动力学不稳定。

Method: 以惯性作为自回归预测的结构性先验，构建 Inertial Memory 作为可演化的视频状态，包含两种互补表示：稠密欧拉网格保存场景对齐证据，持久拉格朗日载体将局部内容绑定到学习的规范坐标。每一步依据动作条件运动传输载体内容，由传输后的载体与稠密网格预测完整下一帧视频状态，并用学习门控将传输内容与新预测内容融合为下一步记忆。

Result: 在机器人操作、3D 游戏仿真与开放世界导航等多个领域的实验中，InertialWorld 的自回归预测性能与强视频世界模型基线相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 显式的惯性状态演化机制可有效替代逐步重建，缓解自回归误差累积，为视频世界模型提供更稳定的长时动力学建模范式。

Abstract: The physical world has inertia: it evolves from what already exists rather than being recreated at every instant. However, many existing video world models lack an explicit mechanism for inertial state evolution, leaving the continuity of motion and structure implicit in video prediction. During autoregressive rollouts, repeated reconstruction can accumulate errors and destabilize the predicted dynamics. To address this limitation, we introduce InertialWorld, an Inertial Video World Model that makes inertia a structural prior for autoregressive prediction: existing scene evidence is carried across time rather than reconstructed at every step. Central to this formulation is Inertial Memory, which turns memory from passive predictive context into an evolving video state with two complementary views: a dense Eulerian grid preserves scene-aligned evidence, while persistent Lagrangian carriers bind local content to learned canonical coordinates. At each step, the model transports the carrier content according to action-conditioned motion, predicts the complete next video state from the transported carriers and dense grid, and uses a learned gate to combine transported and newly predicted content into the memory for the next step. Extensive experiments across multiple domains, including robot manipulation, 3D game simulation, and open-world navigation, demonstrate that InertialWorld achieves competitive autoregressive prediction performance against strong video world model baselines. Codes link: https://github.com/YuanGao-YG/InertialWorld

</details>


### [101] [Cross3DGS: Unified Vulkan-Based 3D Gaussian Splatting Training from Workstations to Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22240)
*Hiroyuki Sato,Yusuke Takimoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Cross3DGS：跨厂商、跨平台的Vulkan版3DGS训练器，通过按设备选择执行调度（规则+偏好序），在NVIDIA、Intel、Arm、Qualcomm、AMD、Apple GPU及Android/iPhone上完成标准训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手持/可穿戴设备采集的场景目前仍需送往CUDA工作站做3DGS训练；在采集设备或其配套手机、笔记本上训练不仅是移植kernel的问题——不同GPU与驱动在并行特性、内存限制和命令提交上存在差异，这些差异决定训练调度能否正确执行并返回正确更新。

Method: 提出Cross3DGS，保留单一优化器，两步式为每台设备选择执行调度：先用基于设备限制、负载规模和已观测驱动失败的规则剔除已知无法正确运行的调度，再按固定偏好序在剩余可行调度中选取；实现为跨厂商、跨平台的Vulkan训练器。

Result: 在所测全部配置（NVIDIA、Intel、Arm、Qualcomm、AMD、Apple GPU，含Android设备与一部iPhone）上完成标准质量训练（30k次更新、100万高斯）；现有可移植训练器VkSplat与Brush在部分移动GPU上无法运行。工作站11个场景平均：质量高于VkSplat且训练时间无显著差异，Brush在相当质量下训练时间约为其3倍；各设备最终质量几乎一致。并有移动应用实现端上完成从采集到可查看模型。

Conclusion: 通过调度选择而非kernel移植，可实现跨厂商跨平台的3DGS训练，质量与效率具竞争力，并支持手持设备端到端建模。

Abstract: Scenes captured with handheld and wearable cameras are still shipped to a CUDA workstation for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) training. Training on the device that captured them, or on its companion phone or laptop, requires more than translating kernels: GPUs and drivers differ in parallel features, memory limits, and command submission, and these differences decide whether a training schedule executes and returns the correct update. We present Cross3DGS, a cross-vendor, cross-platform Vulkan trainer that keeps one optimizer and chooses its execution schedule per device in two steps: rules on device limits, workload size, and observed driver failures exclude the schedules known not to run correctly, and a fixed preference order selects one of the remaining feasible schedules. The same core completes standard-quality training (30k updates, one million Gaussians) on every configuration we measured, spanning NVIDIA, Intel, Arm, Qualcomm, AMD, and Apple GPUs and including Android devices and an iPhone, whereas the existing portable trainers VkSplat and Brush do not run on some of the mobile GPUs. Averaged over 11 scenes on a workstation, Cross3DGS has higher quality than VkSplat with no significant difference in training time, and Brush reports about three times the training time at comparable quality. The final quality of Cross3DGS is nearly identical across devices. A mobile application turns a handheld capture into a viewable model entirely on the device.

</details>
