### [102] [CoSphere-SLAM: Multi-Agent Panoramic Gaussian Splatting SLAM with Spherical Loop Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22235)
*Qi Shao,Zhihao Cao,Shuhao Zhai,Taoyu Wu,Zhuoxiao Li,Anh Nguyen,Long Hu,Xianzhi Li,Hong Liang,Baoru Huang,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoSphere-SLAM 是首个面向全景相机的多智能体高斯泼溅 SLAM 框架，通过 SO(3) 球面回环检测、Sim(3) 共识剔除对称误闭环、方向性全局优化与全向高斯融合，实现高精度轨迹与高质量建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集协同建图在搜救、建筑巡检与仓储自动化中至关重要，全景相机可单帧覆盖全场景。但全景智能体与针孔智能体的差异带来新问题：全景可在任意 3D 朝向观测到重叠场景，而全旋转搜索又会在对称房间间产生互相一致的错误闭环；连续单目尺度漂移会在重访表面处撕裂刚性合并的地图；短基线下的深度噪声会扭曲回环平移方向。

Method: 1) 球面回环检测：利用快速傅里叶相关在 SO(3) 上对齐任意朝向的球面特征，并通过全局 Sim(3) 一致性共识拒绝对称误闭环；2) 方向性全局优化：基于上述闭环求解逐关键帧 Sim(3) 位姿图，引入对数尺度正则以校正尺度漂移，并将光流平移方向与基于深度的平移幅值解耦；3) 全向高斯融合：以关键帧相似度变换高斯，并在最大射线响应处渲染高斯，联合优化位姿与地图。

Result: 在室内与跨会话多智能体全景基准上，CoSphere-SLAM 在单智能体与多智能体方法中取得最优的轨迹精度与渲染质量。

Conclusion: CoSphere-SLAM 首次将全景相机、多智能体协同与高斯泼溅 SLAM 统一，系统性地解决了 SO(3) 回环对齐、对称误闭环、尺度漂移与深度噪声等关键难题，验证了全景多智能体密集建图的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Dense collaborative mapping is essential for search and rescue, building inspection, and warehouse automation, where panoramic cameras capture the entire surroundings in a single frame. Unlike pinhole agents that share views only at similar headings, panoramic agents observe overlapping scenes at any 3D orientation, yet searching full rotations also produces mutually consistent false loop closures across distinct symmetric rooms. Moreover, continuous monocular scale drift splits rigidly merged maps at revisited surfaces, while depth noise at short baselines distorts loop translation directions. To address these challenges, we propose CoSphere-SLAM, the first multi-agent Gaussian splatting SLAM framework for panoramic cameras, whose spherical loop closure aligns spherical features over $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ via fast Fourier correlation across arbitrary orientations and rejects symmetric false closures through global $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ consensus. Building on these closures, directional global optimization solves a per-keyframe $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ pose graph with log-scale regularization to correct scale drift while separating optical-flow translation directions from depth-based magnitudes. Finally, omnidirectional Gaussian fusion transforms each Gaussian with its keyframe similarity and renders Gaussians at their maximum ray response to jointly refine poses and maps. Experiments on indoor and cross-session multi-agent panoramic benchmarks demonstrate that CoSphere-SLAM achieves state-of-the-art trajectory accuracy and rendering quality among single-agent and multi-agent methods. Project website: https://cosphere-slam.github.io.

</details>


### [103] [Social Signal Bench: Evidence-Weighted Evaluation of Co-occurring Social Signals in Video and Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22704)
*Siddharth Ravi,Anders R. Bargum,Hugo Anselme,Patrizio Cugia di Sant'Orsola,Alberto Cardaci,Flora Kincso Parragi,Vincent Borowiec,Line H. Clemmensen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Social Signal Bench（47小时密集标注基准，含视频25小时/音频22小时）及Evidence-Weighted F1（EW-F1）指标，用于评估共现社交信号识别与参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 兴趣、犹豫等社交信号依赖语境且常共现，现有情感计算基准很少评测共现信号；传统多标签F1忽略语义关联与标注者分歧。

Method: 构建两个数据集：视频集覆盖10类社交信号（25小时），音频集覆盖6类信号（22小时）；每个数据集含公开训练集（线上会话视频，10小时专家标注、其余伪标注）与跨四个会话领域的私有专家标注测试集。提出EW-F1：以标注者证据加权参考标签，并通过逐片段最优匹配对语义相关预测给予分级得分。

Result: 受控扰动实验表明EW-F1能区分语义相关错误与无关错误，而传统F1不能；在统一评估协议下，基于该基准后训练的7B开源模型在视频上优于通用前沿模型，在音频上与之持平。

Conclusion: 该工作为共现社交信号识别提供了更贴近语义与标注不确定性的基准与评价指标，并显示领域后训练可使中小规模开源模型达到甚至超越通用大模型表现。

Abstract: Recognizing social signals such as interest and hesitation requires interpreting context-dependent behaviors that may co-occur in conversation. Existing affective computing benchmarks rarely evaluate such co-occurring signals, and conventional multi-label F1 overlooks semantic relationships and annotator disagreement. We introduce Social Signal Bench, a 47-hour densely annotated benchmark for the evaluation of social signal recognition and engagement, comprising two datasets: Social Signal Bench Video (25 hours), covering ten social signals in audiovisual recordings, and Social Signal Bench Audio (22 hours), covering six signals in audio-only recordings. Each dataset pairs a public training set of online conversational video, of which 10 hours are expert-annotated and the remainder pseudo-labeled, with a private expert-labeled test set spanning four conversational domains. We also propose Evidence-Weighted F1 (EW-F1), which weights reference labels by annotator evidence and assigns graded credit to semantically related predictions through optimal per-clip matching. Controlled perturbations demonstrate that EW-F1 distinguishes semantically related errors from unrelated ones, unlike conventional F1. Under a shared evaluation protocol, 7B open models post-trained on Social Signal Bench outperform general-purpose frontier models on video and match them on audio.

</details>


### [104] [CascadeJEPA: Enhancing 3D Geometric Representations via Spatially Coupled Cascaded Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22686)
*Kailai Zhou,Ziqi Chen,Xiaoning Xu,Yuefeng Wu,Zixuan Fu,Zijun Lin,Zhihao Li,Youwei Pang,Xiaoqi Zhao,Xun Cao,Bihan Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CascadeJEPA通过全网格上下文编码与跨阶段交叉注意力的级联预测，缓解I-JEPA中语义与几何表征的失衡，在保持语义识别性能的同时显著提升3D几何任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到I-JEPA预训练存在语义与几何学习日益背离的问题：语义识别能力提升的同时3D几何表征退化。推测原因在于以不完整的空间上下文预测高度抽象的隐表征，使模型偏向语义规律而牺牲细粒度几何信息。

Method: 提出CascadeJEPA，一种空间耦合的级联预测框架：采用全网格（full-grid）上下文编码以增强patch级交互，并在预测阶段之间引入交叉注意力；同时基本保留原始I-JEPA架构。

Result: 在多个3D几何任务上相较I-JEPA取得显著提升，同时维持较强的语义识别性能；优势还延伸至未来特征预测与世界模型规划任务。

Conclusion: 增强几何表征可有效支撑预测式世界建模，CascadeJEPA为兼顾语义与几何的视觉预训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for predictive visual representation learning. However, our analysis of I-JEPA reveals a growing discrepancy between semantic and geometric learning: improved semantic recognition during pretraining is accompanied by degraded 3D geometric representations. We hypothesize that this partly stems from predicting highly abstract latent representations with incomplete spatial context, favoring semantic regularities over fine-grained geometric information. To address this issue, we introduce CascadeJEPA, a spatially coupled cascaded prediction framework that promotes richer patch-level interactions through full-grid context encoding and cross-attention between prediction stages, while largely preserving the original I-JEPA architecture. Extensive experiments demonstrate substantial improvements over I-JEPA across multiple 3D geometric tasks while maintaining strong semantic recognition performance. Furthermore, its advantages extend to future feature forecasting and world model planning, highlighting the potential of enhanced geometric representations to support predictive world modeling.

</details>


### [105] [SpatialCast: Reading the 3D World via Cast Shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22657)
*Enhan Li,Jinghao Zhou,Jiaze Zhang,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpatialCast 基准，利用投影阴影来系统评估多模态大模型将物理场景条件与视觉观测相联系的空间推理能力，含 21,000 道合成题（分场景/几何两个测试分区）与 200 道真实场景题，并验证其训练数据可用于 GRPO 后训练以提升能力并迁移至外部基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态空间基准已较好覆盖空间关系、记忆与视角推理，但较少系统评估模型如何将物理场景条件与视觉观测联系起来（即场景—投影一致性、干预效应预测与逆推物理变化）。投影阴影恰是可观测的三维场景证据，其外观由物体几何与姿态、光照、视点共同决定，因此适合作为该能力的诊断载体。

Method: 构建 SpatialCast 基准：以投影阴影为观测信号，设计三个互补任务——评估场景—投影一致性、预测干预引起的变化、由视觉变化逆推背后的物理改变；数据规模为 21,000 道合成题，并划分场景分区与几何分区分别测试，另附来自 20 个实拍场景的 200 道 SpatialCast-Real 题；同时对开源 MLLM 使用 SpatialCast 训练数据进行 GRPO 后训练。

Result: 评测显示闭源 MLLM 优于通用开源权重模型及面向空间推理的专用 MLLM（后者在合成基准上接近随机水平）；即便强闭源模型在阴影长度与光源高度判断上仍显吃力。GRPO 后训练可提升开源 MLLM 在该基准上的表现，并在多个外部空间基准上取得改进，提示所学能力具有迁移性。

Conclusion: SpatialCast 可作为受控资源，用于诊断 MLLM 的空间推理能力并研究其学习与泛化；实验结果支持其作为连接物理场景条件与视觉观测的评测与训练工具的有效性。

Abstract: As multimodal large language models (MLLMs) advance toward understanding and interacting with the physical world, spatial reasoning becomes essential for understanding scene structure and anticipating the effects of physical changes. Existing spatial benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating spatial relations, memory, and viewpoint reasoning, but less systematically assess how models connect physical scene conditions with visual observations. We introduce SpatialCast, a benchmark that uses cast shadows to evaluate this ability. Cast shadows provide observable evidence of a 3D scene. Their image appearance depends on object geometry and pose, illumination, and viewpoint. We examine this relationship through three complementary tasks that assess scene-projection consistency, predict intervention-induced changes, and infer the physical changes behind visual transitions. The benchmark contains 21,000 synthetic questions with separate scene and geometry test partitions, complemented by 200 SpatialCast-Real questions from 20 photographed scenes. Evaluations show that closed-source MLLMs outperform general-purpose open-weight MLLMs and MLLMs designed for spatial reasoning, which remain near chance on the synthetic benchmark, while even strong closed-source models struggle with shadow-length and light-height judgments. We also use SpatialCast training data for GRPO post-training, improving open-weight MLLM performance on the benchmark. Further evaluations show improvements on several external spatial benchmarks, suggesting transfer of the learned capabilities. These experiments support SpatialCast as a controlled resource for diagnosing spatial reasoning capabilities and studying their learning and generalization.

</details>


### [106] [REVEAL: Reconstructing Video Amodal Content via Language and Flow Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22645)
*Minh Tran,Winston Bounsavy,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REVEAL：融合光流与文本先验的扩散式视频非模态补全框架，并提出长视频基准LAVAT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的视频非模态补全缺少辅助先验，难以重建严重遮挡区域，且长序列时序一致性差。

Method: 统一扩散框架：用户文本查询一方面驱动开放词表视频分割得到可见掩码，另一方面为纹理重建提供语义引导；非模态分割引入光流引导，将前帧可见掩码 warp 到当前帧以传播可见内容并近似当前形状先验；纹理补全用文本引导约束被遮挡外观重建。另构建LAVAT长视频+文本描述基准。

Result: 在现有基准上取得SOTA，并在长序列上保持高质量时序一致性；LAVAT支持重度遮挡下文本引导的视频非模态补全评测。

Conclusion: 运动（光流）与语义（文本）互补先验可有效提升视频非模态补全的形状推断与纹理一致性，长视频评测仍需专门基准。

Abstract: Amodal perception enables humans to perceive entire objects even when parts are occluded, a remarkable cognitive skill that artificial intelligence struggles to replicate. Recent diffusion-based methods have extended amodal completion from images to videos, yet they lack auxiliary priors to guide the reconstruction of heavily occluded content and to maintain temporal consistency over long sequences. We present REVEAL (REconstructing VidEo Amodal content via Language and flow guidance), a unified diffusion-based framework that integrates complementary motion and semantic priors for video amodal completion. A user-provided text query serves a dual role: it drives open-vocabulary video segmentation to obtain visible masks, and it provides semantic guidance for texture reconstruction. For amodal segmentation, we introduce optical flow guidance: by warping visible masks from previous frames to the current frame, the flow-warped mask propagates visible content into occluded regions and approximates the object's current shape, providing a strong shape prior even under simultaneous deformation and occlusion. For texture completion, text guidance constrains the reconstruction of occluded appearance while providing a stable semantic reference that helps maintain visual coherence throughout the reconstruction. We also contribute LAVAT, a new benchmark featuring long video sequences paired with text descriptions, enabling evaluation of text-guided video amodal completion under heavy occlusion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that REVEAL achieves state-of-the-art performance on existing benchmarks while maintaining high-quality temporal consistency over extended sequences. The project page is available at: https://uark-aicv.github.io/REVEAL/.

</details>


### [107] [PlaceTally: A Test-Time Memory Model for Constructing Place Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22608)
*Alibay Osmanli,Shaogang Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PlaceTally 是一个"问题盲"（question-blind）的测试时记忆模型：为每个视频构建地点图，使相机重访同一地点时同一物体只被计数一次。在 RevisitCount 基准上，测试房间平均相对准确率 62.5%、留出房间 61.2%，显著优于整段视频读取的 51.2%/44.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中物体计数在相机返回已见地点时很困难：整段读取的 VLM 只给出几个小数字；分段读取的模型会在每个片段中重复计数同一物体；目标跟踪器则需预知类别或固定词表。作者观察到"计数记忆无需知道找什么，只需知道去过哪里"——物体很少在两次访问间离开房间，因此按地点分组的视频片段几乎不共享物体，且地点可在提问前被识别。

Method: 提出 PlaceTally：为每个视频构建地点图。每个视频片段与历史最佳匹配的地点合并，或新建一个地点（强匹配极少出错，而真正重访往往只弱匹配）。冻结的 VLM 对每个地点仅从不超过 128 帧中计数一次，从而每次读取输入有界，记忆规模随地点数增长；建图速度比实时快 15 倍。

Result: 在 RevisitCount（真实房间重录基准）上，测试房间平均相对准确率 62.5%，留出房间 61.2%；同一模型整段读取仅为 51.2% 与 44.3%。性能在房间重访频率变化时保持稳定，而对访问求和做固定校正的方法则从 38.5% 波动到 61.4%。

Conclusion: 基于地点的测试时记忆可有效解决视频重访下的重复计数问题，无需问题或类别先验，兼具准确性与效率，且对重访频率变化具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Counting distinct objects in a video is hard when the camera returns to places it has seen. A vision-language model that reads the whole video answers with a few small numbers, and one that reads it in parts counts an object again in every part that shows it. Object trackers need the category before the video is read, or a fixed vocabulary. We observe that a memory for counting does not need to know what it is looking for, only where it has been: objects rarely leave a room between visits, so parts of a video grouped by place share almost no objects, and a place can be recognised before any question is asked. Building on this, we introduce PlaceTally, a question-blind test-time memory model that constructs a place graph for each video. Each video fragment joins the place of its best earlier match or opens a new place, because a strong match is rarely wrong while a true return often matches weakly. A frozen vision-language model counts each place once from at most 128 of its frames, so every read has a bounded input and the memory grows with the number of places; the graph is built 15 times faster than real time. On RevisitCount, our benchmark of real room re-recordings, it scores 62.5% mean relative accuracy on test rooms and 61.2% on held-out rooms, against 51.2% and 44.3% for the same model reading the whole video. It stays level as rooms return more or less often, while a fixed correction of summed visits moves from 38.5% to 61.4%.

</details>


### [108] [Propose with the Gradient, Accept with the Loss: One Hedged Attack for Pixel-Sparse Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22596)
*Hon Ming Yan,Chun Pong Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究像素稀疏对抗攻击，提出统一框架分析sPGD与Sparse-RS均为"提议分布+接受规则"的局部搜索，并据此设计单次运行、每步仅一次前向与反向传播的梯度引导随机搜索（GRS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素稀疏攻击的鲁棒性目前依赖sAA集成评估，但其成员各有缺陷：sPGD依赖易被破坏的一阶模型，Sparse-RS即便在梯度有效处也忽略梯度。作者希望厘清局部搜索究竟能从梯度中学到什么，从而设计更高效、更强的单一攻击。

Method: 将sPGD与Sparse-RS统一为"提议分布+接受规则"的局部搜索；借助Taylor界确定应度量的量，并在搜索访问的状态上测量，归纳出五条规则：单像素处梯度的符号最可靠并用于设定新像素值；候选像素排序应宽松跟随；待移除像素排序更可靠可更严格跟随；在一次移动被接受前不应重复提议同一像素；接受与否仅由损失决定。由此构成GRS算法。

Result: 在13种CIFAR-10配置和5个ImageNet模型上，GRS匹配或超过sAA最强单一成员，在18个设置中的17个上与sAA差距在1个百分点内或更强，其中8个设置显著强于sAA；计算开销为3个（而非最多7个）前向传播单位，CIFAR-10上比sAA成员完整运行快3.7–5.0倍。

Conclusion: GRS表明，恰当利用梯度的局部随机搜索可在单次无需集成的前提下达到或超越sAA的评估强度，同时显著降低计算成本，为像素稀疏鲁棒性评估提供更高效基准。

Abstract: A pixel-sparse adversary may change a few pixels of an image to any value. Robustness to such attacks is currently measured with an ensemble, sparse-AutoAttack (sAA), because its members fail in different places: sparse projected gradient descent (sPGD) trusts a first-order model that a full pixel change breaks, and sparse random search (Sparse-RS) ignores the gradient even where it is informative. We show that both attacks are local searches, each a proposal distribution plus an acceptance rule, and ask what such a search can learn from the gradient. A Taylor bound says what to measure, and measurements at the states the search visits give five rules. The sign of the gradient is its most reliable use at a single pixel and sets a new pixel's value. Its ranking of candidate pixels should be followed loosely, and its ranking of the pixels to remove, which is often more reliable, can be followed more closely. A pixel should not be proposed twice before a move is accepted, and acceptance should be decided by the loss alone. These rules define Gradient-guided Random Search (GRS), which runs once and costs one forward and one backward pass per step. On 13 CIFAR-10 configurations and 5 ImageNet models, GRS matches or beats the best single member of sAA and is within one point of sAA itself or stronger on 17 of 18 settings, and it is significantly stronger than sAA on 8. It uses 3 instead of up to 7 forward-pass units, and on CIFAR-10 it runs 3.7 to 5.0 times faster than the members of sAA run in full.

</details>


### [109] [Disagree to Prune: Reallocating Visual Tokens Where a Small VLM Dissents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22472)
*Yanchao Li,Jiaqing Xie,Ben Gao,Yusu Zhang,Beilun Wang,Yanbo Wang,Xiaohua Xu,Zhongyu Huang,Tianfan Fu,Nanqing Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PRUDENT：先用约一半视觉token预算作答，再用节省的token对与小VLM（看完整图像）答案不一致的问题以双倍预算重答，在相同平均预算下优于八种剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法主要改进token排序，并对所有问题使用同一预算一次性作答。高质量排序下仅少数问题受损，且被保留token上的信号难以反映被删除的信息；作者发现这些受损问题上，看完整图像的小VLM会与剪枝答案不一致。

Method: 两阶段预算分配：(1) 以约50%预算完成首轮作答；(2) 用节省的token对"小VLM判定与首轮答案不一致"的问题以约2倍预算重答。论文给出重答何时收益的理论/分析，并论证小VLM应读取完整图像的原因。

Result: 在18个基准、相同平均视觉token预算下超过八种剪枝方法；预算越紧优势越大；在五种InternVL规模及另外两个模型家族上均成立。小VLM的不一致可标记八种方法的大部分剪枝失败；将重答策略用于两种最强方法（沿用其自身排序）也带来提升；其他检查器亦有效。

Conclusion: 识别哪些剪枝答案是可信的，为视觉token剪枝提供了超越"token排序"的第二条杠杆；额外token被自适应地分配到首轮出错之处。

Abstract: Visual token pruning speeds up large vision-language models (VLMs) by keeping only a part of their visual tokens. Most existing methods improve the ranking of the tokens and then answer every question once with the same budget. With strong rankings, pruning hurts only a few questions, and signals computed on the kept tokens see little of what it removed. We find that, on most of these questions, a small VLM given the full image disagrees with the pruned answer, since it still sees the missing evidence. We propose PRUDENT, which first answers with about half the budget and spends the saved tokens on re-answering the disagreeing questions at twice the budget. We show when such re-answering pays and why the small VLM should read the full image. On 18 benchmarks, PRUDENT outperforms eight existing pruning methods at the same average budget of visual tokens. Its lead widens as the budget tightens, and holds across five InternVL sizes. PRUDENT also has the highest average in two more model families. The disagreement of the small VLM flags most pruning failures of all eight methods, and re-answering on it also improves the two strongest with their own rankings. Other checkers give similar gains, and the extra tokens go where the first pass hurts the answer. These results suggest that identifying which pruned answers are trustworthy provides an additional lever for visual token pruning beyond the ranking of tokens. Code and data are available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/PRUDENT.

</details>


### [110] [SlopeGuard: Towards Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection via Radial Spectral Slope Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22727)
*Cheng Fu,Yiheng Li,Yang Yang,Wenzheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SlopeGuard，将径向频谱斜率（Radial Spectral Slope）这一紧凑的全局频谱衰减描述子与语义特征结合，以提升AI生成图像检测器对未见生成器的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器易过拟合于特定生成器的伪影，泛化到未见合成流程时性能下降；已有频域方法学习高维频谱模式，仍保留生成器相关变化，难以迁移。

Method: 采用径向频谱斜率作为抑制细粒度变化的全局频谱衰减紧凑描述子；分析发现真实与生成图像存在系统性但依赖生成器的斜率差异，故仅凭斜率不足以决策。据此提出SlopeGuard：斜率感知的数据处理 + 斜率引导的特征学习，构建信息量高的块级视图，融合径向频谱斜率与语义特征，并按频谱非典型性自适应调整先验保持。

Result: 在AIGCDetectBench上平均准确率97.5%，GenImage上97.8%，AIGIBench上85.1%，达到SOTA；在模糊、裁剪、JPEG压缩与加性噪声下保持有竞争力性能。

Conclusion: 频谱斜率提供有用取证证据但不宜单独决策，将其与语义表示耦合可显著增强跨生成器泛化与鲁棒性。

Abstract: AI-generated image detectors often overfit to generator-specific artifacts, hindering generalization to unseen synthesis pipelines. Existing frequency-based methods usually learn high-dimensional spectral patterns that may preserve generator-dependent variations. We explore Radial Spectral Slope, a compact descriptor of global spectral decay that captures transferable spectral trends while suppressing fine-grained variations. Our analysis shows that real and generated images exhibit systematic but generator-dependent slope differences, indicating that slope provides useful forensic evidence but is insufficient as a standalone decision criterion. This motivates combining spectral slope with semantic representations for more robust detection. Based on this insight, we propose SlopeGuard, which couples slope-aware data processing with slope guided feature learning to construct informative patch-based views, integrate Radial Spectral Slope with semantic features, and adapt prior preservation according to spectral atypicality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SlopeGuard achieves state-of-the-art mean accuracies of 97.5% on AIGCDetectBench, 97.8% on GenImage, and 85.1% on AIGIBench, while maintaining competitive performance under blur, cropping, JPEG compression, and additive noise.

</details>


### [111] [Practice in Text, Act in Pixels: Correcting Where Perception Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22722)
*Zhongling Xu,Hairong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究在有限真实截图预算下如何最高效地缩小GUI智能体的文本-视觉差距，发现读取器的误读具有系统性，并据此提出"读取器对齐"方案：微调读取器而非策略，使全任务成功率从55.6%提升至80.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体可以低成本地从界面文本视图学习，再借助视觉-语言"屏幕读取器"在截图上执行。但在低成本读取器的模拟移动平台上，在文本上能完成大多数任务的策略，在相同任务的截图上却大量失败。作者追问：少量真实截图的预算应投在哪里才能最有效地弥合这一差距。

Method: 首先分析发现读取器的误读是系统性的，会重复出现在相同的界面元素上；而按误读错误率匹配噪声这一自然的sim-to-real手段忽略了这种结构，在预设对比中并不优于直接在截图上训练策略。为此提出"读取器对齐"：用截图与其文本视图配对数据微调读取器，使其学会输出每个屏幕的文本视图（与文本训练时一致），策略本身保持不变。

Result: 将截图预算用于读取器时，在新任务实例上获得80.9%的全任务成功率，而用于训练策略时为55.6%。在相同标注截图条件下，适配读取器也优于适配策略；且单个对齐后的读取器无需重新训练即可提升所有尝试过的文本训练策略。

Conclusion: 应在误读的源头（读取器）而非策略端投入少量真实截图预算。读取器对齐是一种高效、可复用的sim-to-real方法，可即插即用地增强各类文本训练的GUI策略。

Abstract: GUI agents can learn cheaply from a text view of an interface and act on screenshots through a vision-language screen reader. Yet on a simulated mobile platform with a low-cost reader, policies that complete most tasks in text fail on many of the same tasks on screenshots. We ask where a small budget of real screenshots is best spent to close this gap. We find that the reader's misreads are systematic and recur on the same interface elements. Noise matched to the reader's error rates, a natural sim-to-real remedy, misses this structure, and in a pre-specified comparison it did not beat training the policy directly on screenshots. We therefore correct the misreads at their source with reader alignment, which fine-tunes the reader on screenshots paired with their text views. The reader learns to output each screen's text view, as in text practice, and the policy stays unchanged. Spent on the reader, the screenshot budget gives 80.9% full-task success on new task instances, against 55.6% when spent on training the policy. Given the same labeled screenshots, adapting the reader also beats adapting the policy, and a single aligned reader improves every text-trained policy we tried without retraining any of them.

</details>


### [112] [Teaching What to Look For: Privileged Perception Questions for Agentic On-Policy Distillation in Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22755)
*Yifan Xu,Ruopeng Gao,Zhixiao Wang,Jiafu Tang,Yichun Yang,Zihao Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PQ-OPD：用仅训练期可见的“感知问题”引导教师智能体更全面探索视频，再通过 on-policy 蒸馏把该探索策略迁移到学生模型，使推理阶段无需 PQ 即可提升细粒度视频描述能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 详细视频描述需忠实覆盖物体、动作与时间事件，但固定帧采样易遗漏短暂动作、小目标等细粒度证据，因此需要能主动定位相关时刻并检查局部区域的描述智能体。然而训练此类主动感知行为困难：与视频问答不同，通用描述指令并不指明“应获取哪些视觉证据”，缺乏监督信号。

Method: 提出 Perception-Question On-Policy Distillation（PQ-OPD）。训练时引入仅训练期使用的 Perception Questions（PQs），只规定“需要检查什么”而不泄露答案，从而引导教师模型更全面地探索视频并聚焦相关视觉证据；在学生模型生成的轨迹上进行 on-policy 蒸馏，将这种引导迁移到学生的推理过程、工具调用与最终描述中。推理阶段不需要任何 PQ。

Result: 在三个详细描述基准与两个 caption-to-QA 基准上，PQ-OPD 在不同教师-学生规模与输入帧预算下均稳定优于对应的未训练智能体；在既定评测协议下，于 DREAM-1K、CaReBench、ShortVidBench、MotionBench 与 VidCapBench-AE 上，其主指标结果在对比的开源模型中表现最强。

Conclusion: 消融实验以及对时间覆盖度与答案泄露的分析表明，学生模型确实将训练期引导内化为更有效的视频探索策略，而非依赖 PQ 或答案信息。

Abstract: Detailed video captioning requires comprehensive and faithful descriptions of objects, actions, and temporal events. Fixed-frame sampling, however, can miss brief motions, small objects, and other fine-grained evidence, motivating captioning agents that actively seek relevant moments and inspect local regions. Training such behavior is challenging because, unlike video question answering, generic captioning instructions do not specify what visual evidence should be acquired. We propose Perception-Question On-Policy Distillation (PQ-OPD), which uses training-only Perception Questions (PQs) to guide active video perception. PQs specify what should be inspected without revealing the corresponding answers, encouraging the teacher to explore the video more comprehensively and focus on relevant visual evidence. Along trajectories generated by the student, on-policy distillation transfers this guidance to the student's reasoning, tool calls, and captions; no PQs are required at inference. Across three detailed-captioning and two caption-to-QA benchmarks, PQ-OPD consistently improves the corresponding untrained agents across teacher-student scales and input-frame budgets. Under our evaluation protocol, PQ-OPD achieves the strongest primary-metric results among the compared open-source models on DREAM-1K, CaReBench, ShortVidBench, MotionBench, and VidCapBench-AE. Our ablations, together with analysis of temporal coverage and answer leakage, further indicates that the student internalizes the training-time guidance as a more effective video exploration policy.

</details>


### [113] [GaussianMamba: Geometric and Relational State Space Scanning of 3D Gaussian Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22742)
*A. Enes Doruk,Ozgur Erkent*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GaussianMamba双分支框架，将高斯基元的各向异性几何与特征-语义关系显式注入互补扫描策略，用于3D语义占据预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D语义占据预测需表征复杂空间结构并建模场景上下文。3D高斯基元提供稀疏自适应表示，Mamba擅长高效序列建模，但高斯基元不规则且无序，序列构建困难，常规空间排序会忽略其各向异性几何与特征-语义关系。

Method: 双分支Mamba框架：第一分支利用由基元尺度与朝向导出的各向异性高斯支撑，构建保局域遍历序列；第二分支按特征-语义亲和度重排空间分组，并以几何加权语义差异调整局部特征。两分支序列分别送入独立Mamba模块，最后通过考虑不同扫描路径所产生局域特征差异的支撑感知机制进行特征融合。

Result: 在nuScenes-SurroundOcc与SSCBench-KITTI-360上，IoU与mIoU均较GaussianFormer和GaussianFormer-2取得稳定提升。

Conclusion: 将几何与语义先验融入Mamba扫描顺序及融合机制，可有效适配高斯基元的不规则性，提升3D语义占据预测性能，验证了互补扫描策略的有效性。

Abstract: 3D semantic occupancy prediction requires representing complex spatial structures and modeling contextual relationships across a scene. 3D Gaussian primitives provide a sparse and adaptive representation of scene geometry and semantics, while Mamba offers efficient sequence modeling for capturing dependencies among primitives. However, Gaussian primitives are irregular and unordered, making sequence construction challenging, as conventional spatial ordering may overlook their anisotropic geometry and feature-semantic relationships. In this paper, we present GaussianMamba, a dual-branch Mamba framework that explicitly incorporates Gaussian-specific geometric and feature-semantic relationships into complementary scanning strategies. The first branch refines a locality-preserving traversal using anisotropic Gaussian support derived from primitive scale and orientation. The second branch reorders spatially organized groups according to feature-semantic affinity and adjusts local features using geometry-weighted semantic differences. The resulting sequences are processed by separate Mamba modules, and their features are fused through a support-aware mechanism that incorporates local feature discrepancies arising from the distinct scanning paths. Experiments on nuScenes-SurroundOcc and SSCBench-KITTI-360 demonstrate consistent improvements over GaussianFormer and GaussianFormer-2 in both IoU and mIoU.

</details>


### [114] [Edge-Adaptive Hiera-Seg Model for Building-Integrated Photovoltaic Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21680)
*Na Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出边缘自适应HieraSeg模型，融合多朝向视觉-辐照度信息实现建筑光伏(BIPV)短期功率预测，并输出可追溯的预测解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIPV发电受立面朝向、屋顶几何、邻近建筑遮挡、植被、云移动、组件温度、逆变器状态与边缘设备可靠性等多因素耦合影响，预测困难且需在边缘端低延迟运行。

Method: 将RGB立面图像、可选热成像、辐照度、组件温度、立面朝向、屋顶掩膜、阴影标签、天空状况、历史功率与边缘设备状态对齐为同步预测窗口；HieraSeg进行层次化区域提取（立面/屋顶/天空/阴影/背景），边缘自适应模块在丢帧、时延漂移与局部阴影变化下动态调整特征权重；分割分支识别遮挡组件区域后再由预测头输出功率。

Result: 在模拟多建筑BIPV数据集上设置未见建筑与未见朝向测试；模型输出预测功率、置信区间、主导证据、遮挡贡献、朝向贡献及能量管理复核优先级，用以判别预测由辐照变化、可见阴影、朝向响应、陈旧边缘信息或近期功率历史驱动。

Conclusion: 框架可在通信时延与算力受限的边缘控制器上本地预测，将视觉遮挡、辐照动态与功率历史关联为可追溯结果，服务于建筑能量管理、微网调度与BIPV运行规划。

Abstract: Building integrated photovoltaic power forecasting is challenging because facade orientation, rooftop geometry, nearby buildings, vegetation, cloud movement, module temperature, inverter status and edge device reliability jointly affect power generation. This paper proposes an edge-adaptive HieraSeg model for short term BIPV power forecasting with multi-orientation visual-irradiance fusion. RGB facade images, optional thermal cues, irradiance measurements, module temperature, facade orientation, rooftop masks, shading labels, sky condition, historical power and edge device state are aligned into synchronous forecasting windows. HieraSeg extracts hierarchical regions from facade, rooftop, sky, shadow and background images, while the edge-adaptive module changes feature weights under missing frames, latency shifts and local shading changes. The segmentation branch identifies occluded module areas and separates facade, rooftop, sky and background evidence before the forecasting head estimates future BIPV output. Experiments are designed on a simulated multibuilding BIPV dataset with unseen building and unseen orientation tests. The model reports predicted power, confidence interval, dominant evidence, occlusion contribution, orientation contribution and energy-management review priority. This output is intended to show whether the forecast is driven by irradiance change, visible shading, orientation response, stale edge information or recent power history. The framework supports local forecasting at edge controllers with limited communication delay and computing resources, linking visual occlusion, irradiance dynamics and power history to traceable forecasting results for building energy management, microgrid scheduling and BIPV operation planning.

</details>


### [115] [REEL: Brain-Guided Video Generation and Selection by Reranking Encoder Embeddings with Linear Decoding from fMRI data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21907)
*Fabrizio Spera,Tommaso Boccato,Moein Khajehnejad,Nicola Toschi,Matteo Ferrante*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出REEL框架：用线性语义解码器+预训练视频生成器+三路神经编码流，以"生成-重排序"（proposal-and-rerank）策略缓解视频刺激与BOLD慢动力学的时序失配，在CC2017与BMD上提升Video 50-way准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态图像fMRI解码已取得显著进展，但视频重建仍受困于快速变化的视觉刺激与缓慢BOLD响应之间的时序失配；作者希望验证一条概念更简单、可作为复杂流程互补方案的路线。

Method: 受贝叶斯思想启发的候选生成-重排序策略：线性语义解码器、预训练视频生成器，以及三条神经编码流（ridge回归读出，其中两条流使用可学习池化）；在CC2017与BOLD Moments Dataset (BMD)上评估，指标为Video 50-way识别准确率。

Result: 相比仅生成单一候选，重排序将CC2017三名被试的平均50-way准确率由0.2359提升至0.2434，BMD十名被试由0.3653提升至0.3920。

Conclusion: 重排序式候选评估可有效改善视频脑解码，说明该策略是相对日益复杂解码流程的一条互补且更简洁的方向；但提升幅度有限，受限于线性解码器与生成器的先验能力。

Abstract: Visual brain decoding from functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data has made remarkable progress in recent years, driven by increasingly powerful artificial intelligence (AI) models and the growing availability of large-scale public datasets. However, despite the impressive advances achieved in static image decoding, the reconstruction of videos from fMRI signals remains challenging due to the temporal mismatch between rapidly changing video stimuli and the slow dynamics of the blood-oxygen-level-dependent (BOLD) response. We evaluate a Bayesian-inspired proposal-and-rerank strategy on CC2017 and the BOLD Moments Dataset (BMD), using a linear semantic decoder, a pretrained video generator, and three neural encoding streams with ridge readouts and learned pooling in two streams. We refer to this framework as REEL (Reranking Encoder Embeddings with Linear decoding). Compared with a single generated candidate, reranking increases mean Video 50-way accuracy from 0.2359 to 0.2434 across three CC2017 participants and from 0.3653 to 0.3920 across ten BMD participants. These findings suggest an alternative and conceptually simpler strategy for improving video brain decoding, highlighting the potential of our approach as a complementary direction to increasingly complex pipelines.

</details>


### [116] [FrameSeg3D: Learned Local Frames for Segmenting Small Parts on High-Resolution 3D Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22124)
*Xinwen Zhang,Tongyan Wang,Huawei Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FrameSeg3D，通过为每个小部件预测局部坐标系（中心与朝向），再在其法向视角下以扫描仪原始分辨率分割，实现高分辨率3D扫描中小部件的精确分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率3D扫描中分割小部件（如指甲、牙齿）面临三大挑战：部件占扫描比例极小、边界仅为微弱的曲率/高度变化、朝向各异。既有方法分两类且均有缺陷：整扫描方法（点/网格网络需降采样、多视图方法部件仅占少数像素且侧面视角差）分辨率不足；先定位后放大分割的方法依赖特定部件先验（如牙弓形状），无法跨部件迁移。作者指出精度瓶颈在于"视角"（方向与尺度）而非点数。

Method: FrameSeg3D：首先从低分辨率整体视图中为每个部件预测局部坐标系（中心与朝向法线）；再在围绕该法线的视角下、以扫描仪原始分辨率对部件进行特写分割。由于每个部件都居中、正对相机、尺度归一，单一分割网络即可服务不同类别部件，并能从少量标注掩码中学习。

Result: 在手部扫描与 Teeth3DS+ 测试折上验证：指甲 mean Dice 0.970，逐牙 IoU 96.5，边界 IoU 70.5（最准确的牙齿边界），均优于现有SOTA。

Conclusion: 分割精度受"部件如何被观测（方向与尺度）"而非覆盖点数所限；通过坐标系预测+法向特写分割，FrameSeg3D 以统一网络跨部件类型实现高精度小部件分割，具备良好的可迁移性与少样本学习能力。

Abstract: Segmenting small parts on high-resolution 3D scans, such as nails on hand scans and teeth on intraoral scans, poses three challenges: 1) each part covers a tiny fraction of the scan, 2) its boundary is only a subtle change of height and curvature, and 3) it faces its own direction. Existing methods fall into two groups, and both fall short. The first group processes the whole scan at once and sees each part too coarsely. Point and mesh networks must shrink the scan to fit in memory, so a small part keeps only a few of its points. Multi-view methods render images of the whole object, so each part fills only a few pixels and a tilted part is seen from the side. The second group first finds each part and then segments it up close. But these methods find the parts with knowledge about one kind of part, for example the shape of the dental arch for teeth. Such knowledge does not carry over to other kinds of parts. We find that accuracy is limited by how a part is seen, from which direction and at what scale, not by how many points land on it. We therefore present FrameSeg3D. It first predicts a local frame for every part, its center and the direction it faces, from a low-resolution view of the whole scan. It then segments the part in views around its frame normal at the scanner's resolution, so covering the whole scan no longer costs resolution. Because every part arrives centered, facing the camera, and at a fixed scale, one segmentation network can serve different kinds of parts and learn from few labeled masks. We test FrameSeg3D on two kinds of parts, nails and teeth. It outperforms state-of-the-art methods on hand scans (mean Dice 0.970 on nails) and on the Teeth3DS+ test fold (per-tooth IoU 96.5), with the most accurate tooth boundaries (boundary IoU 70.5).

</details>


### [117] [RetiDual-Select: Reliability-Aware Specialist-Generalist Routing for Retinal OCT under Image-Quality Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22117)
*Zihao Han,Philippe De Wilde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RetiDual-Select 框架：静态 PoE 融合为参考，小门控提出候选，验证集冻结的伤害守卫决定是否替换，独立评分决定转人工复核；自适应收益仅在严重对比度压缩条件下出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应融合既能修复静态集成的错误，也会推翻原本正确的决策，因此需要将候选择、路由安全性与预测复核分离，明确自适应路由何时真正有益。

Method: 将视网膜专科模型 RETFound 与医学视觉语言模型 MedSigLIP 的图像编码器组合；以校准的 PoE 融合为静态参考，小门控逐图提出候选，验证集冻结的 harm guard 裁决候选可否替代参考，另设独立分数排序复核优先级；在三个数据集与 15 个预设合成扰动条件下评估，并做百分位重标定与事后仅验证集分析。

Result: 静态 PoE 在三个数据集上均取得最高或并列最高的干净准确率；2% 接受错误目标下，Kermany-4 接受 94.3% 图像、选择性准确率 99.89%，OCT-C8 接受 92.4%、98.65%；两分支均在干净图像上正确的比例为 95.0% 与 91.8%，仅单分支正确的为 4.4% 与 6.2%；自适应增益集中于严重对比度降低，路由机会扩大约九倍，百分位重标定基本消除该效应；门控跟随更可靠分支时增益更高，冻结守卫将最差条件下 OCT-C8 损失从 1.39 降至 0.36 个百分点。

Conclusion: 当图像质量造成可靠性机会且门控能捕捉该机会时自适应路由有效，否则系统回退到静态 PoE 或转人工复核；结论受限于合成扰动，未证明对自然采集或扫描仪漂移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Adaptive fusion can repair a static ensemble's errors, but can also overturn correct decisions. RetiDual-Select combines retinal specialist RETFound with the image encoder of the medical vision-language model MedSigLIP, separating candidate fusion, routing safety and predictive review. Calibrated product-of-experts (PoE) fusion is the static reference, a small gate proposes an alternative for each image, a validation-frozen harm guard decides whether that candidate may replace the reference, and a separate score prioritizes review. Static PoE reached the highest or joint-highest clean accuracy on all three datasets. At a clean 2% accepted-error target the review policy accepted 94.3% of Kermany-4 images at 99.89% selective accuracy, and 92.4% of OCT-C8 at 98.65%. Both branches were correct on 95.0% and 91.8% of clean images; only 4.4% and 6.2% had exactly one correct branch. Across 15 prespecified synthetic conditions, adaptive gains were concentrated in severe contrast reduction, where this routing opportunity widened roughly ninefold; percentile rescaling largely removed the effect, linking it to compressed dynamic range. In post-hoc validation-only analyses, gain was higher when the gate followed the more reliable branch, and a frozen guard cut the worst-condition OCT-C8 loss from 1.39 to 0.36 percentage points. Adaptive routing was useful when image quality created a reliability opportunity and the gate followed it; otherwise the system retained Static PoE or deferred for review. The findings are bounded by the synthetic perturbations evaluated and do not establish robustness to natural acquisition or scanner shift.

</details>


### [118] [CueSplat: Learning to Schedule Sparse Deformation for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22095)
*Qingyuan Tang,He Zhu,Shixuan Sun,Anbang Wu,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CueSplat：冻结4DGS骨干，利用高斯轨迹局部可预测性与轻量路由器按预算选择精确形变更新，在边缘设备实现最高8.15倍加速且PSNR仅降0.16 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐帧对所有高斯评估形变网络计算开销大，难以在边缘设备部署；观察到高斯轨迹具有局部可预测性，但预测可靠性随高斯与时间变化。

Method: 训练后框架：保持预训练骨干与高斯集合不变；用近期历史预测高斯属性；轻量学习路由器在离线校准的场景专属预算内选择需精确形变更新的高斯（兼顾预测可靠性与渲染贡献）；选中索引在邻近帧复用以降路由开销，选中高斯每帧仍获精确更新；以漂移信号调度连续三帧全形变以重建预测历史、控制累积误差。

Result: 在四种主流4DGS算法上评测，Jetson AGX Orin与Orin NX分别最高6.74倍与8.15倍加速，数据集平均PSNR最多下降0.16 dB。

Conclusion: 通过预测+稀疏精确更新的混合策略，可在近乎无损画质下大幅降低边缘端4DGS渲染成本。

Abstract: Deformation-based 4D Gaussian splatting (4DGS) enables dynamic scene rendering. However, evaluating deformation networks for all Gaussians at every frame is compute-intensive on edge devices. We find that Gaussian trajectories are often locally predictable, yet prediction reliability varies across Gaussians and time. Thus, we propose CueSplat, a post-training framework that exploits local temporal regularity while keeping the pretrained backbone frozen and retaining its Gaussian set. Specifically, CueSplat predicts Gaussian attributes from recent history and uses a lightweight learned router to select exact deformation updates within a scene-specific budget calibrated offline. Selection accounts for prediction reliability and rendering contribution. Selected indices are reused across nearby frames to reduce routing overhead, while selected Gaussians still receive exact updates each frame. To control accumulated prediction error, we use drift signals to schedule three consecutive full-deformation frames that rebuild the prediction history. Evaluated across four mainstream 4DGS algorithms, CueSplat achieves up to $6.74\times$ and $8.15\times$ speedups on Jetson AGX Orin and Orin NX, respectively, with a negligible quality drop (at most $0.16$ dB in dataset-average PSNR).

</details>


### [119] [SCAN: Site-Aware Collaborative Privacy-Preserving Adaptation of Vision-Language Models for Neurological Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21955)
*Nikhil J. Dhinagar,Rishab Pendam,Harmony S. Cen,Sophia I. Thomopoulos,John P. John,Mahir H. Khan,Sook-Lei Liew,Erik Kaestner,Carrie McDonald,Paul M. Thompson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCAN：一种站点感知的协作自适应框架，使新医院无需重训、无需共享脑MRI扫描即可适配3D医学视觉-语言模型，并通过带差分隐私的类原型摘要实现跨站点协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球神经影像数据可强化诊断基础模型，但隐私法规通常禁止集中脑MRI扫描，因此需要在不交换患者数据、不重新训练的前提下实现多站点协作适配。

Method: 提出SCAN站点感知协作适配框架。各站点保留精确类原型用于本地预测，并可共享隐私保护的类摘要（范数有界的和与计数，经校准噪声单次扰动）。在67个站点、12个国家、7,641例扫描（覆盖AD、卒中、癫痫与对照）上评估本地适配、跨站点收益及对既有站点的影响。

Result: 7个未见站点上冻结VLM零样本AUC：AD与卒中为0.833–0.945。免训练本地原型使三站点癫痫AUC由0.598/0.625/0.508升至0.684/0.729/0.762，某站点AD AUC由0.833升至0.863，接近本地训练分类器（平均AUC 0.757 vs 0.759）。差分隐私(ε=4)下新站点参与未显著降低既有站点精度（AUC变化≤0.2%），共享增益有限，成员推断攻击接近随机水平。

Conclusion: SCAN为医院提供了全球范围的框架，可在不交换扫描、不重训的条件下适配并共享脑MRI视觉-语言模型。

Abstract: Worldwide neuroimaging data could strengthen diagnostic foundation models, but privacy rules often prevent centralizing brain MRI scans. We present SCAN, a site-aware collaborative adaptation framework for Neurological Disease Classification that lets new sites adapt a 3D medical vision-language model (VLM) without retraining or sharing patient scans. Each site keeps exact class prototypes for local prediction and can share privacy-protected class summaries: norm-bounded sums and counts perturbed once with calibrated noise. Using 7,641 scans from 67 sites across 12 countries, covering Alzheimer's disease (AD), stroke, epilepsy and controls, we assess local adaptation, cross-site benefits, and effects on existing sites. At seven unseen sites, the frozen VLM's zero-shot AUCs were 0.833-0.945 for AD and stroke. Training-free local prototypes improved epilepsy AUC at three sites from 0.598/0.625/0.508 to 0.684/0.729/0.762 and AD AUC at one site from 0.833 to 0.863, nearly matching a classifier trained at each site (mean AUC 0.757 vs. 0.759). With differential privacy ($ε$ = 4), new sites contributed without materially reducing accuracy at existing sites (AUC change $\leq$0.2%), although gains from sharing were limited, and membership attacks performed near chance. SCAN offers hospitals a worldwide framework to adapt and share brain MRI VLMs without exchanging scans or retraining.

</details>


### [120] [GridDriver: On-Board BEV-Based Language Planning and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21924)
*Katharina Winter,William Engel,Zain A. Alishammat,Fabian B. Flohr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GridDriver，一种高效BEV条件化大语言模型框架，集成轨迹预测、结构化驾驶指标、自然语言导航、驾驶解释与场景问答，兼顾规划与生成效率并实车部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV条件化LLM可实现语言交互驾驶，但车载部署需同时满足高效感知、连续规划与自回归语言生成，现有Q-Former式压缩存在几何信息损失与效率瓶颈。

Method: 采用轻量级时空BEV感知与空间结构化token向共享LLM提供场景上下文；以逐帧调度机制将轨迹预测与异步语言生成交错执行；用空间结构化表示替代Q-Former压缩。

Result: NAVSIM达87.9% PDMS，nuScenes-QA达57.6%准确率（规划与QA共享检查点）；空间表示分析显示几何信息保留与轨迹预测优于Q-Former；规划时延32.7 ms，车载ROS 2流水线9.4 plans/s；并在自采数据与研究车开环实车验证。

Conclusion: GridDriver在公共基准与真实场景中验证了BEV条件化LLM方案的可行性，同时明确了该范式的优势与局限。

Abstract: Bird's-Eye-View (BEV)-conditioned large language models (LLMs) enable language-interactive driving, but onboard deployment requires efficient perception and continuous planning alongside autoregressive language generation. We present GridDriver, an efficient BEV-conditioned LLM framework combining trajectory prediction, structured driving indicators, natural-language navigation, driving explanations, and scene question answering. Lightweight spatio-temporal BEV perception and spatially structured tokens provide scene context to a shared LLM, while frame-wise scheduling interleaves trajectory prediction with asynchronous language generation. GridDriver achieves competitive performance on public benchmarks, reaching 87.9% PDMS on NAVSIM and 57.6% accuracy on nuScenes-QA with a shared planning and QA checkpoint. Spatial representation analyses demonstrate improved geometric information retention and trajectory prediction compared with Q-Former compression. The system achieves 32.7 ms standalone planning latency and 9.4 plans/s in an onboard ROS 2 pipeline. We further evaluate planning on our own data recordings and demonstrate open-loop onboard operation on a research vehicle. Across public benchmarks and real-world evaluations, we identify strengths and limitations of the BEV-based approach.

</details>


### [121] [Can a World Model Predict a Deepfake Video? A Consistency Margin Prior for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21846)
*Pradip Gupta,Shubham Mandloi,Ravi Kumar Satzoda,Sujeeth Bharadwaj,Yassine Benajiba,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用预测式视频模型V-JEPA 2.1构建"预测残差"作为一致性先验，通过轻量Latent Consistency Head分离残差幅值与方向，在仅训练8.25M参数、仅用FF++训练的情况下实现跨数据集深度伪造检测的强泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在训练集上表现良好，但跨数据集测试时性能显著下降，原因在于其依赖生成器特有的伪造伪影。作者希望探索预测式视频模型能否提供一个跨数据集泛化性更强的信号。

Method: 冻结V-JEPA 2.1编码器，仅用rank-16 LoRA适配预测器；将预测器输出与冻结编码器目标之差定义为预测残差，假设伪造片段残差平均更大，作为一致性边际先验；用轻量Latent Consistency Head（LCH）将残差幅值与方向分离用于训练与分类；保持其他设置固定，对比标准与反向边际目标以检验边际方向对跨数据集泛化的影响，并在FF++验证集上匹配两者性能后再评估未见数据。

Result: 反向边际设置平均呈现更大的跨数据集泛化差距，说明一致性边际方向影响跨域迁移。完整模型仅增加8.25M可训练参数（占334M总参数的2.47%），仅用FF++训练即在Celeb-DFv2上取得90.5 AUC，并在多个其他跨数据集基准上表现良好。

Conclusion: 消融表明：分离残差幅值与方向、降低数据集特定变化、采用低秩投影，均能提升跨数据集泛化；预测残差作为一致性信号是有效且高效的深度伪造检测先验。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors often perform well on the dataset they are trained on, but their performance drops when tested on videos from different datasets. In this paper, we study whether a predictive video model can provide a signal that generalizes better across datasets. Instead of relying only on generator-specific artifacts, as many existing detectors do, we use a model trained to predict natural video and measure the difference between what it predicts and what it observes in a video. Using V-JEPA 2.1, we keep the encoder frozen and adapt the predictor using rank-16 LoRA. We define the difference between the predictor output and the frozen encoder target as the prediction residual. We hypothesize that manipulated clips produce, on average, larger prediction residuals than authentic clips, and use this as our consistency-margin prior. We process these residuals using a lightweight Latent Consistency Head (LCH), which separates residual magnitude from direction for training and classification. To test whether the direction of the consistency margin affects cross-dataset generalization, we compare standard and reversed margin objectives while keeping the remaining training setup fixed. We match the two settings on FF++ validation performance before evaluating them on unseen data. The reversed-margin setting exhibits a larger cross dataset generalization gap on average, providing evidence that the direction of the consistency margin affects cross-dataset transfer. Our complete model adds only 8.25M trainable parameters, which is 2.47% of the total 334M parameters. Trained only on FF++, it achieves an AUC of 90.5 on Celeb-DFv2 and performs well across several other cross-dataset benchmarks. Our ablations show that separating residual magnitude from direction, reducing dataset-specific variation, and using a low-rank projection improve cross-dataset generalization in our experiments.

</details>


### [122] [VSpark: Speculative Decoding for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22666)
*Suho Yoo,Youngjoon Jang,Hyebin Cho,Taeyeop Lee,Jaewook Jeong,Joon Son Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VSpark通过校准动作噪声的采样rollout、特征对齐训练草稿模型、以及复用被拒草稿之后的延续预测，提升自回归视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）的投机解码加速比，在四类VLA与四个操作基准上实现1.28×–2.10×无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归VLA逐token生成机器人动作，推理延迟高。投机解码可无损加速，但在机器人控制场景中，多个不同token可编码近似相同的动作，导致"接近正确"的草稿token仍被验证拒绝，接受率低，加速受限。

Method: 三项设计：(i) 使用带校准动作噪声的rollout采集数据，使草稿模型暴露于更广泛的机器人状态分布；(ii) 特征对齐，令草稿模型拟合VLA的隐藏层特征而非仅输出token；(iii) continuation reuse，保留VLA在拒绝草稿上已计算的预测作为下一步验证的候选，而非丢弃。

Result: 在采用不同动作分词器的四种VLA与四个机器人操作基准上，取得1.28×至2.10×的加速，且保持VLA解码无损（lossless）。

Conclusion: 针对机器人控制中"多token同动作"导致的投机解码接受率瓶颈，通过数据、表示与计算复用三个层面的改进可显著提升VLA推理加速，且不牺牲生成正确性。

Abstract: Our aim is to accelerate autoregressive vision-language-action models (VLAs), which generate robot actions one token at a time. Speculative decoding makes this possible: a lightweight draft model proposes several tokens at once, and the VLA verifies them in a single pass, accepting only those that match its own predictions, so decoding remains lossless. The speedup, however, is limited in robot control, where many different tokens encode nearly the same motion and a draft close to the correct action is still rejected. To address this, our approach, VSpark, is built upon three design choices: (i) rollouts collected with calibrated action noise, which expose the draft to a wider range of robot states; (ii) feature alignment, which trains the draft to match the VLA's hidden features rather than its output tokens alone; and (iii) continuation reuse, which keeps the predictions the VLA has already computed along rejected drafts as candidates for the next verification step, instead of discarding them. On four VLAs with different action tokenizers and four manipulation benchmarks, VSpark achieves 1.28x to 2.10x speedup while keeping the VLA's decoding lossless.

</details>


### [123] [Must You See Fakes to Catch Fakes? Language Says What to Look For, Real Images What to Ignore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22650)
*Tianchi Xu,Yuxuan Zhou,Yuheng Zhang,Yanan Guan,Tao Dai,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练检测器SaySee，不在生成图像上训练，仅用文本构建"生成方向"并用真实图像校正偏移，24个评测集上平均AUC达98.2%，超出最佳现有方法4.2个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器存在两大问题：一是从训练集中少数生成器学到的线索带有生成器专属痕迹，跨生成器迁移性差，限制泛化；二是训练用真实/生成图像在格式与内容上存在差异，检测器把这些差异当作真实性证据，导致决策受与真实性无关的因素影响，损害鲁棒性与泛化性。二者根源都在于"在生成图像上训练"。

Method: SaySee为免训练检测器，利用预训练视觉基础模型已习得的"AI生成"概念：Say阶段用仅在真实性上有差异的成对描述集合构建"生成方向"，以图像嵌入在该方向上的投影打分；See阶段用真实图像校正由格式、图像类型和内容引起的分数偏移。

Result: 在覆盖标准基准、真实场景（in-the-wild）数据及最新生成器的24个评测集上，配合MoE-ViE取得平均AUC 98.2%，较最佳现有检测器高4.2个百分点；在将格式与真实性解耦的条件下，仍为所有方法中最优。

Conclusion: 检测器无需见过生成图像即可有效识别AI生成图像；通过文本定义"生成方向"并用真实图像校正偏差，可从源头规避生成器专属线索与格式/内容混杂偏差，实现更强的泛化性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Must a detector see generated images in order to catch them? Detection of AI-generated images has long faced two problems. The cues a detector learns from the few generators in its training set carry traces specific to those generators and transfer poorly to new ones, which limits generalization. The real and generated training images also differ in format and content, so the detector takes these differences as evidence of authenticity, and its decisions change with factors that have nothing to do with authenticity, which harms robustness as well as generalization. Both problems stem from training on generated images. A detector never trained on them can largely avoid both at the source. Vision foundation models have already learned the concept of "AI-generated" during pretraining, and text can point to it directly. Building on this, we propose SaySee, a training-free detector: Say builds a generation direction from sets of paired descriptions that differ only in authenticity and scores an image by the projection of its embedding onto it, and See uses real images to correct the score offsets caused by format, image type and content. On 24 evaluation sets that span standard benchmarks, in-the-wild data and the latest generators, SaySee reaches a mean AUC of 98.2% with MoE-ViE, 4.2 points above the best existing detector. When format is decoupled from authenticity, it remains the best of all methods. Code will be released.

</details>


### [124] [Distilling World Action Models for Efficient Bimanual Manipulation via Anchored Path Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22592)
*Jialong Liu,Junjie He,Yandu Chen,Bin Zhou,Heng Su,Peijun Tang,Jianan Wang,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Anchored Path Matching (APM)，以学生实际到达的状态为锚点，用教师端点与首中间状态作监督目标，并用锚点优先梯度消解冲突，实现单检查点支持 1/2/4 步联合视频-动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models 通过迭代去噪联合生成未来视频与机器人动作，推理开销大；少步蒸馏可减少模型评估次数，但粗粒度更新会使学生偏离教师轨迹，需要更稳定的路径级监督。

Method: 从学生到达的每个状态出发，将教师续推到零噪声，取其端点与第一个中间状态作为双目标；用模态路由适配器（modality-routed adapters）从同一初始状态同时匹配两个目标；当两个梯度冲突时，采用锚点优先更新，保留端点梯度并在每个模态内去除局部梯度的相反分量。

Result: RoboTwin 2.0 上仅在 Clean 演示训练、直接评测 Random 域随机化设置：单步时闭环成功率较同推理预算的基线蒸馏提升 2.0 个百分点（Clean）与 1.4（Random）；视频 PSNR 由 30.169 升至 32.317 dB，SSIM 与 LPIPS 亦改善。两个真机任务上成功率优于一步教师，控制周期延迟较八步教师降低 21.3×。

Conclusion: APM 通过锚点式双目标监督与梯度冲突处理提升了少步联合生成的稳定性与保真度，单检查点即可在部署时按预算选择采样步数，无需重训或查询教师，兼顾成功率与推理效率。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly generate future video and robot actions through iterative denoising. Few-step distillation reduces the number of model evaluations, but its coarse updates can move the student away from the teacher's trajectory. We propose Anchored Path Matching (APM), which supervises endpoint and short-transition predictions from states reached by the student. From each state, we continue the teacher to zero noise and use its endpoint and first intermediate state as targets. Modality-routed adapters learn to match both targets from the same starting state. When the two gradients conflict, anchor-priority updates preserve the endpoint gradient and remove the opposing component of the local gradient within each modality. One checkpoint supports 1-, 2-, and 4-step joint generation. The sampling budget can be chosen at deployment without retraining or teacher queries. On RoboTwin 2.0, we train only on Clean demonstrations and evaluate directly on the domain-randomized Random setting. At one step, APM improves closed-loop success over continued base distillation at matched inference budgets by 2.0 percentage points on Clean and 1.4 on Random. Video peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) rises from 30.169 to 32.317 dB, with gains in structural similarity (SSIM) and learned perceptual image patch similarity (LPIPS). On two real-robot tasks, APM improves success over the one-step teacher and reduces control-cycle latency by $21.3\times$ relative to the eight-step teacher.Project page: https://jialonglong.github.io/Anchored-Path-Matching/.

</details>


### [125] [Semantic-Guided Geometric Decoding for Token-Efficient Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22577)
*Zhaojie Chen,Junyan Yang,Le Yang,Qi Dang,Yucheng Huang,Qianyi Che,Zhaoqiang Chen,Xinqi Zhu,Qiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SemGeo 提出一种 token 高效的视觉定位框架：用单个 grid token 表示每个实例做粗定位，再由几何解码器并行恢复精确离散框坐标，并通过高斯软标签与强化后训练缓解训练-推理解耦问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素到序列的自回归坐标生成需对每个框执行多步顺序解码，推理成本随实例数增长；像素到嵌入方法虽降低开销，但在共享特殊 token 下缺乏显式空间编码。

Method: 以单 grid token 表示实例实现粗定位；几何解码器复用视觉特征与隐状态并行输出离散框坐标；训练上引入编码坐标距离的高斯软标签联合优化 proposal 生成与框定位（SFT），并加入结合 box-set 奖励与坐标监督的强化后训练，使语言模型与几何解码器基于自身预测训练。

Result: 在涵盖目标检测、指代目标检测与文档版面定位的八个基准上，六个基准取得对比 MLLM 中最高的 F1@mIoU；在 vLLM 与 Transformers 下平均框生成吞吐分别为代表性定位基线的 2.07× 与 1.25×，且准确率同时更高。

Conclusion: 以单 token 粗定位加并行几何解码的范式可同时提升视觉定位的精度与推理效率，软标签与强化后训练有效缓解 teacher-forcing 与推理解码间的失配。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide a unified interface for language-conditioned visual perception. In pixel-to-sequence grounding, autoregressive coordinate generation requires multiple sequential decoding steps per box, increasing inference cost with the number of instances. Pixel-to-embedding methods reduce this overhead but lack explicit spatial encoding when using a shared special token. We introduce SemGeo, a token-efficient visual grounding framework that represents each instance with a single grid token for coarse localization. A geometric decoder reuses visual features and hidden states to recover precise discrete box coordinates in parallel. To train proposal generation and box localization together, we introduce Gaussian soft labels that encode coordinate distances during supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Reinforcement post-training further combines box-set rewards with coordinate supervision on model-generated proposals. This stage trains the language model and geometric decoder on their own predictions to mitigate the mismatch between teacher-forced training and inference. Across eight benchmarks spanning object detection, referring object detection, and document layout grounding, SemGeo achieves the highest F1@mIou among compared MLLMs on six benchmarks. It also delivers $2.07\times$ and $1.25\times$ the mean box-generation throughput of representative grounding baselines under vLLM and Transformers, respectively, while achieving higher accuracy in both comparisons.

</details>


### [126] [Plausible Videos Can Lead to Poor Decisions: Diagnosing and CURE-ing World Models for Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22561)
*Raphael C. Kim,Michele Santacatterina,Jingsen Zhu,Ramin Zabih*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CURE（Certify-Upweight-Rollout-Explore）框架，用可验证的证书评估动作条件世界模型能否被信任用于规划，并据此决定加权去偏、测试时增加采样或探索补数据，从而以"决策质量"而非"感知质量"指导规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型常被用于规划，但能生成逼真且与训练数据一致的视频并不代表能做出好的决策。论文聚焦两个问题：(1) 何时可以信任世界模型进行规划；(2) 不可信任时该怎么办。

Method: 提出 CURE：先"Certify"——通过证书判断世界模型对规划器的决策是否可信（核心是训练数据对规划器决策的 support 覆盖程度）；再据证书结果采取行动：Upweight 提升训练数据权重以去偏模型；Rollout 在测试时增加采样；Explore 在 support 缺失处采集新数据。在 PhyWorld 模拟物理、MuJoCo 机械臂控制、Language-Table 推物（仿真与真实视频）等多域部署验证。

Result: 证书所信任的决策遗憾显著更低（Language-Table 仿真 0.06 vs 0.38；MuJoCo 接触 0.07 vs 0.38），而视频感知质量无法区分决策好坏。在相同数据下，CURE 相较最优悲观决策规则降低遗憾 10–25%（各 support 水平），相较世界-动作模型、搜索与策略学习降低 41–64%。

Conclusion: 规划中应关注决策质量而非感知质量；基于 support 的可验证证书能有效判定世界模型可信度并指导后续数据/采样策略，显著降低遗憾。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models are increasingly used as world models for planning, yet a world model that makes plausible, realistic-looking videos that are consistent with the training data can produce poor decisions. We address two key questions: (1) when can we trust a world model for planning, and (2) what should we do when we cannot? We introduce CURE (Certify, Upweight, Rollout, Explore), a certificate-guided technique that assesses whether a world model can be trusted for planning and determines what to do next: whether to upweight the training data to debias the model; roll out more samples at test time; or explore by collecting new data where support (how well the training data cover the planner's decisions) is missing. We deploy CURE across multiple domains, spanning simulated physics based on PhyWorld, MuJoCo arm control, and Language-Table pushing in simulation and on real videos. The decisions our certificate trusts have lower regret than those it does not (e.g., 0.06 vs. 0.38 on Language-Table sim and 0.07 vs. 0.38 on MuJoCo contact), whereas video perceptual quality does not identify good decisions. By focusing on decision quality rather than perceptual quality, CURE lowers regret over the best pessimistic decision rule by 10-25% across support levels, and over world-action models, search and policy learning by 41-64%, given the same data.

</details>


### [127] [CutAgent: Creative-Gene-Driven Planning and Experience-Guided Self-Refinement for High-CTR E-commerce Advertising Video Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22418)
*Xuyue Huang,Guanbo Huang,Jiasong Feng,Run Ling,Ao Ma,Xi Zhao,Yaoyu Li,Jie Gao,Ke Cao,Wei Feng,Zhanjie Zhang,Hao Wang,Jinghe Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双智能体框架CutAgent，将电商广告视频创作建模为Plan–Execute–Self-Evolve流程，通过结构化创意基因连接产品理解与视频生产，在线CTR达2.21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将产品属性直接映射为脚本或剪辑决策，缺乏对广告策略、叙事结构与制作风格的显式建模，且无法复用过往生产失败经验。

Method: GeneAgent将多模态产品输入（文本属性+产品图像）映射为创意基因（叙事单元与制作规则）；构建CutAgent-70K数据集（约70K样本，含属性/图像、反提取创意基因、清洗片段池、真实CTR），采用高CTR监督微调+CTR引导的GRPO训练。VideoAgent以基因为指导，从长期记忆中检索"类别→基因"的失败–修正经验作为约束，完成分镜脚本规划并协调素材检索、旁白、字幕、音乐与时间线渲染；独立反思回路诊断缺陷、修正决策并将验证后的修正写回记忆。

Result: 在CutAgent-Eval上优于代表性基线，取得最高在线CTR 2.21%。

Conclusion: 显式建模创意策略并以创意基因为中介、结合记忆化自我进化机制，可有效提升电商广告视频的创作质量与商业效果。

Abstract: E-commerce advertising video creation requires both high-level creative planning and reliable multi-modal production. However, existing methods often map product attributes directly to scripts or editing decisions without explicitly modeling advertising strategies, narrative structures, and production styles. They also have limited ability to reuse past production failures. To address these limitations, we propose $\textbf{CutAgent}$, a dual-agent framework that formulates advertising video creation as a Plan--Execute--Self-Evolve process. CutAgent comprises two collaborative agents, $\textbf{GeneAgent}$ and $\textbf{VideoAgent}$, connected by a structured creative gene that bridges product understanding and video production. GeneAgent maps multi-modal product inputs---textual attributes together with product images---to creative genes that specify narrative units and production rules. To train GeneAgent, we construct $\textbf{CutAgent-70K}$ with approximately 70K samples of product attributes/images, reverse-extracted creative genes, cleaned clip pools, and real-world CTR, and apply high-CTR supervised fine-tuning followed by CTR-guided GRPO. Guided by the generated gene, VideoAgent retrieves category-then-gene failure--correction experiences from a long-term memory as constraints for shot-script planning, then coordinates material retrieval, narration, subtitles, music, and timeline rendering. Independently, a reflection loop diagnoses quality defects, revises deficient production decisions, and writes verified corrections back to memory for later retrieval. Experiments on $\textbf{CutAgent-Eval}$ show that CutAgent outperforms representative baselines in e-commerce advertising video creation, achieving the highest online CTR of $\textbf{2.21\%}$.

</details>


### [128] [SpatialMind: Adaptive Multi-Level Geometric Fusion for Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22415)
*Shibin Zhao,Hao Tan,Fan Yang,Haixin Wang,Feilong Chen,Yidong Ma,Chaoyang Zhao,Ming Tang,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出任务自适应框架SpatialMind，通过门控网络动态融合多粒度几何表征，解决MLLM中固定几何融合难以适配不同空间推理任务的问题，在VSI-Bench取得72.3的SOTA成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将3D重建模型融入MLLM的方法要么对所有任务使用单一几何粒度，要么采用固定的多层级融合方案，忽视了任务特定的推理需求。作者通过系统实验发现，不同认知层级（如距离估计 vs 路线规划）需要不同粒度的几何表征，固定融合无法满足多样化空间推理需求。

Method: 提出SpatialMind任务自适应框架，核心是GeoFus多层几何融合模块：构建一个门控网络，同时以文本指令、视觉语义和几何表征为条件，自适应调节浅层、中层、深层几何特征在LLM解码器各层的贡献；并引入负熵正则化损失以缓解门控坍缩。

Result: 门控可视化表明GeoFus能根据任务需求自适应调整几何粒度；在多个空间基准上表现有竞争力，VSI-Bench达到72.3的SOTA分数。

Conclusion: 任务自适应的多层级几何融合优于固定融合方案，能有效提升MLLM的3D空间推理能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate promising capabilities in 2D visual understanding, yet remain limited in 3D spatial reasoning. Recent studies explore integrating 3D reconstruction models into MLLMs. However, these approaches either use a single geometric granularity across all tasks or adopt a fixed multi-level fusion scheme, regardless of task-specific reasoning requirements. Through systematic experiments on multi-level geometric fusion, we find that spatial tasks at different cognitive levels (e.g., distance estimation vs. route planning) require geometric representations of different granularities, revealing that fixed fusion cannot accommodate diverse spatial reasoning requirements. To bridge this gap, we propose SpatialMind, a task-adaptive framework for dynamically integrating multi-level geometric representations. SpatialMind employs GeoFus, a multi-layer geometric fusion module built around a gating network jointly conditioned on textual instructions, visual semantics, and geometric representations. This network adaptively modulates the contributions of shallow, intermediate, and deep geometric features across the layers of the LLM decoder. Furthermore, we introduce a negative-entropy regularization loss to mitigate gate collapse. Gate visualizations indicate that GeoFus adapts geometric granularity to task requirements. Experiments demonstrate that SpatialMind achieves competitive performance across spatial benchmarks, attaining a state-of-the-art score of 72.3 on VSI-Bench. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/zhaosb-star/SpatialMind.

</details>


### [129] [M$^3$-Test: Coverage Gaps in Joint-Perturbation Testing of Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22329)
*Enhui Wan,Xiaowan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 M3-Test 覆盖审计框架，将每个干净输入与其单模态变体及完整的 5×5 联合扰动族对齐，把"通过鲁棒性测试"转化为请求级（request-level）的残余风险清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQA 模型能通过所有纯图像和纯问题的单独测试，但在两类变化同时发生时失败。标准鲁棒性报告只做条件平均，无法指出哪些"已被接受的请求"仍然脆弱，因此需要细粒度的覆盖审计。

Method: M3-Test 将干净输入与单模态变体、完整 5×5 族及 4×4 强度联合库对齐，使任意缩减测试集可在同一请求集上重放；对四个视觉语言模型共 260 万条响应进行审计，并通过重执行失败记录、匹配的通过对照、脆弱失败记录复核，排除了执行噪声与评分误差的解释；同时从其他模型学习风险排序，用于测试分配。

Result: 在通过全部 40 项单模态测试的 Qwen 问题中，26–29% 存在联合失败，而联合记录中失败比例不足 0.6%；仅通过 25 个最大强度角点仍留下 11–14% 的失败。失败可复现率约 90%，匹配通过对照的翻转率仅 0.3–0.6%，最脆弱记录的复核支持率 80–89%。学习到的风险排序在 25 次额外查询内发现约 80% 受影响问题，优于最大优先（65–68%）和成对覆盖数组（53–57%）。

Conclusion: M3-Test 把"通过的鲁棒性套件"转变为请求级账本，明确哪些请求仍未被测试充分覆盖，可支持验收、回归评审与测试资源分配，并使既有评测结果可复用。

Abstract: A visual question answering (VQA) model can pass every image-only and question-only test yet fail when the same changes occur together. Standard robustness reports average over conditions and therefore cannot say which accepted requests remain vulnerable. We introduce M3-Test, a coverage audit that aligns each clean input with its single-modality variants and a complete 5x5 family, 4x4 strength joint library, so that any reduced test suite can be replayed on the same requests. Across 2.6 million responses from four vision-language models, joint failures affect 26-29% of the Qwen questions that pass all 40 single-modality tests, although fewer than 0.6% of their joint records fail. Passing the 25 maximum-strength corners does not close this gap: 11-14% of the accepted questions still fail elsewhere in the library. Re-executing the archived failures reproduces about 90% of them, against a 0.3-0.6% flip rate among matched passing controls, and re-checking the most fragile failure records supports 80-89% of them. On the evidence examined, neither execution noise nor grading error can fully account for the gap. The same aligned grids make previous evaluations reusable. On held-out images, a risk order learned from other models discovers about 80% of affected Qwen questions within 25 additional queries, compared with 65-68% for maximum-first and 53-57% for pairwise covering arrays. M3-Test thus turns a passing robustness suite into a request-level account of what remains untested, supporting acceptance, regression review and test allocation.

</details>


### [130] [CEVA: Complementary Evidence Verification via Frame-Level Reorganization for Short-Video Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22141)
*Wenshen Yang,Junfeng Hao,Jing Li,Decheng Liu,Yangkunfei,Shuyu Jiang,Haizhou Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CEVA框架，首次在融合前按"证据角色"重组短视频帧（显式对齐帧 vs. 候选互补语境帧），在FakeSV/FakeTT上取得最佳整体效果，并证明该预融合证据组织模块可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频已成为公众追踪事件的常见渠道，其快速、视觉说服力强的形式也助长假新闻传播。与图文帖不同，短视频新闻多模态交织且场景不断变化，难以判断哪些帧能验证主张。现有检测器要么对采样帧同质化池化，要么按文本-帧相似度选帧；二者都忽视了"对齐帧可能只是在重复误导性主张，而非主导的上下文反而可能反驳该主张"这一关键问题。

Method: 提出CEVA（互补证据验证方法）。核心为双分支互补重组模块（DBCR）：将帧划分为显式对齐帧与候选互补语境帧两类证据角色；随后在重组后的证据上执行跨模态融合，并引入双向对比正则化。

Result: 在FakeSV与FakeTT数据集上，CEVA整体优于所比较的方法；在固定帧预算与骨干网络条件下，DBCR优于均匀、随机与基于相似度的选帧策略，并能提升两种结构不同的检测器。

Conclusion: 融合前的证据组织是短视频假新闻检测中有效且可迁移的组件。

Abstract: Short-video platforms are now a common way for the public to follow unfolding events. Their rapid, visually persuasive format also makes fake news easier to spread. Unlike text-image posts, short-video news interleaves multiple modalities and changing scenes, making it difficult to identify which frames verify a claim. Existing detectors either pool sampled frames homogeneously or select them by text-frame similarity. Both overlook that aligned frames may repeat a misleading claim, whereas less dominant context may challenge it. We present CEVA (Complementary Evidence Verification Approach), to our knowledge the first short-video fake news detection framework to explicitly organize frames by their evidential roles before multimodal fusion. Its Dual-Branch Complementary Reorganization (DBCR) module separates explicit-alignment frames from candidate complementary-context frames, and cross-modal fusion with bidirectional contrastive regularization operates on the reorganized evidence. On FakeSV and FakeTT, CEVA achieves the best overall results among the compared methods. Under fixed frame budgets and backbones, DBCR outperforms uniform, random, and similarity-based selection and improves two structurally different detectors. Together, these results establish pre-fusion evidence organization as an effective and transferable component of short-video fake news detection.

</details>


### [131] [Diagnosing and Correcting Skin-Tone Color Casts in Ultra-Lightweight Photo Retouching Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22106)
*Choulhyouc Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 超轻量修图网络在人像上产生"绿偏"（皮肤变绿）色偏，整帧PSNR/SSIM无法察觉；作者用零参数、皮肤掩膜的逐像素非对称绿过量损失，将偏色人像从52/70降到1/70。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 色偏本应来自场景或相机并由修图模型去除，但作者发现模型反而引入色偏：合成自监督（退化高质量图再学习恢复）这一常用的无配对数据配方会"静默"地让网络欠恢复皮肤色度并额外加入色相偏移。而整帧PSNR/SSIM几乎不变，图像级目标（如全局均值绿损失）无法捕捉该问题，甚至在无真值、无白平衡锚定协议下被掩盖。

Method: 以6,825参数的直方图条件网络为代表，在真值锚定、免白平衡的评估协议下量化皮肤绿偏；对比合成自监督与MIT-Adobe FiveK专家配对训练的差异；提出零新增参数的逐像素、皮肤掩膜、非对称绿过量损失（仅约束带符号绿-品红轴），并在三种超轻架构上验证；分析整帧绿色指标与皮肤区CIEDE2000在方法层面的相关性（Spearman rho=0.89）。

Result: 合成自监督下70张人像中52张出现绿偏；对比全局均值绿损失反而恶化（52/70→57/70）。新损失在分层肤色上降到1/70：FFHQ 70,000张由68.1%降至2.2%，留出CelebA-HQ由64.4%降至0.8%。域内FiveK PSNR/SSIM基本不变（+0.13 dB、+0.004），代价是整帧CIEDE2000小幅上升（8.77→9.69），近中性皮肤出现过度的品红矫正（skin Δa* −1.5→+4.4，CIEDE2000 5.6→6.4，干净输入）。最终网络导出为35 KB TFLite。

Conclusion: 合成自监督会引入整帧指标不可见的皮肤绿偏，图像级目标无效；沿带符号绿-品红轴的皮肤掩膜非对称损失可低成本（零参数、35 KB可部署）消除该偏色，但不保证整体肤色保真度，需权衡近中性皮肤的品红过矫正。

Abstract: Color casts are expected to come from the scene or camera, and a retouching model removes them. We report a case where the model leaves one: an ultra-lightweight retouching network renders human skin greener than the original because it under-restores the skin chroma that a degradation removes and, even on clean inputs, adds a small hue shift of its own. A common paired-data-free recipe, synthetic self-supervision (degrade high-quality images, learn to restore them), silently produces this cast while whole-frame PSNR/SSIM stay essentially unchanged. In a representative 6,825-parameter histogram-conditioned network, the cast appears in 52 of 70 portraits under a ground-truth-anchored, white-balance-free protocol. Both a histogram-conditioned and a CNN-conditioned network develop the cast under synthetic self-supervision, yet both are near-neutral in-domain when trained on MIT-Adobe FiveK expert pairs. Image-level objectives miss it: a global-mean green loss worsens the cast (52/70 to 57/70). With zero added parameters, our per-pixel, skin-masked, asymmetric green-excess loss reduces the cast to 1/70 across stratified skin tones (68.1% to 2.2% over the 70,000-image FFHQ corpus; 64.4% to 0.8% on held-out CelebA-HQ photos). In-domain FiveK PSNR/SSIM are unchanged (+0.13 dB, +0.004), at the cost of a small whole-frame CIEDE2000 increase (8.77 to 9.69) and a magenta over-correction where skin is nearly neutral (skin Delta a* -1.5 to +4.4 and CIEDE2000 5.6 to 6.4 on clean inputs): the loss targets the signed green-magenta axis, not total skin-color fidelity. The same loss suppresses the cast across three ultra-light architectures. A whole-frame green metric correlates with skin-region CIEDE2000 at the method level (Spearman rho = 0.89). The final network exports to a 35 KB TFLite model.

</details>


### [132] [Data-Efficient Algal Bloom Segmentation in Freshwater Bodies Using a Three-Stage Transfer Learning Pipeline with Attention-Enhanced Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22064)
*Albert Ansah Benson,Mingqiang Han,Rahul Harsha Cheppally,Treman Singh Kaloya,Menard Soni,Sudan Baral*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出迁移学习流水线：以ImageNet-1K预训练的Swin Transformer U-Net（SCSE解码器注意力）依次适配水体分割与藻华分割，并配合模型辅助标注将藻类数据从145扩至655张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有害藻华威胁水质与公共健康，但淡水影像中藻华区域的自动分割受限于像素级标注稀缺。

Method: Swin Transformer U-Net + SCSE解码器注意力；ImageNet-1K→水体分割（RIWA、LaRS）→藻华的三阶段迁移；模型辅助标注协议扩充语料（145→655）；在41张验证集上进行单种子消融（S2-A4及其变体），并与9个基线在256张测试集上对比；另做近重复审计。

Result: 语料扩充为主要增益（+11 mIoU）；水体预训练仅在扩充语料上有效（+1.1）；SCSE注意力+0.3；可学习位置编码-0.6（弃用）。新增476张（含188张无藻）使非水体场景误检面积占比由22.6%降至4.9%。独立训练的S2-A13取得mIoU 0.919、藻类IoU 0.902，但近重复审计显示训练/测试存在重叠，分数偏高。

Conclusion: 数据扩充与领域预训练是藻华分割性能的关键，架构微调贡献有限；测试集存在数据泄漏，需构建无重叠划分以给出可靠评估。

Abstract: Harmful algal blooms threaten water quality and public health, yet automated segmentation of bloom regions in freshwater imagery is limited by scarce pixel-level annotations. We present a transfer-learning pipeline that adapts a Swin Transformer U-Net with SCSE decoder attention from ImageNet-1K to water-body segmentation (RIWA, LaRS) and then to algal blooms, together with a model-assisted annotation protocol that expands the labelled algae corpus from 145 to 655 images. In a single-seed ablation on a 41-image validation split (checkpoint S2-A4 and its variants), corpus expansion is the dominant factor (+11 mIoU points); water-body pretraining helps only on the expanded corpus (+1.1 points over ImageNet initialization); SCSE attention adds 0.3 points; and a learnable positional encoding lowers mIoU by 0.6 points and is omitted. Adding 476 images, 188 of them algae-free, reduces false-positive area on non-water scenes from 22.6% to 4.9%. Against nine baselines on a 256-image test split, a separately trained checkpoint of the same architecture (S2-A13) reaches mIoU 0.919 and the highest algae IoU (0.902); a near-duplicate audit indicates train/test overlap in this split, so these scores are likely optimistic.

</details>


### [133] [Kolibri: Color Finds the Place, Refinement Sets the Pose for RGB-D Localization in Colored Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22829)
*Leonel Aguilar,Bartu Tuncay,Christoph Hoelscher*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Kolibri 用仅55,712参数的点云图网络在未见过房间中定位相机，在相同几何精化下匹配GeDi精度，单帧仅82ms，并支持无房间标签的全局搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描建筑中的智能体/机器人需在训练时未见过的房间中定位相机；点云配准方法虽准但代价高。作者观察到几何精化能把相近初始位姿收敛到同一最终位姿，因此学习部分只需负责"找位置"（place recognition），从而大幅降低模型复杂度与计算量。

Method: 将点云划分为10cm立方体单元(cells)，用图网络描述其颜色、局部形状与邻域关系（55,712参数）预测初始位姿，再交由几何精化得到最终位姿；由于速度快，可进一步做无房间标签的全场景搜索。

Result: 在96个未见ScanNet房间中与GeDi持平（5cm/5°内81.4% vs 80.3%），另10个留出测试房间同样持平，RTX 2080 Ti 上82ms/帧；100房间的单点云搜索较已知房间仅差1.3分，而先检索房间再定位差56.0分；真实医院急诊场景中384ms/帧定位86.5%的渲染帧，而GeDi需91分钟/帧达到相近召回。

Conclusion: 轻量学习+几何精化的分解可在未见环境中实现高效准确定位，并支持无标签大场景搜索；但方法依赖帧与点云颜色一致，在灰度帧或跨相机颜色响应不一致时，点云配准方法仍占优。

Abstract: An agent or robot in a scanned building must locate its camera in rooms that no model saw during training. Point cloud registration methods can align each RGB-D frame with the building's colored point cloud, accurately but at a high cost. We observe that geometric refinement brings nearby initial poses to the same final pose, so the learned component only needs to find the place. Kolibri does this with a graph network of 55,712 parameters that describes 10 cm cubes of points, called cells, by their color, local shape and neighbors. With the same refinement, it matches GeDi on 96 unseen ScanNet rooms (81.4% against 80.3% within 5 cm and 5°) and on a held-out test of 10 further rooms, in 82 ms per frame on an RTX 2080 Ti. The saved time allows for search without a room label. In one point cloud of 100 rooms, Kolibri loses 1.3 points against knowing the room, while retrieving the room first loses 56.0. In a hospital emergency department scene from a real scan, it localizes 86.5% of the frames rendered from the scan in 384 ms each, where GeDi needs 91 minutes per frame on cubes of the scan for a similar recall. The method needs colors that agree between the frame and the point cloud. On gray frames and across cameras with different color responses, point cloud registration methods keep the lead.

</details>


### [134] [Pixel is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22820)
*Jie Chu,Haoting Shi,Fucheng Zhang,Jiawei Liu,Junwei Liao,Ying Wen,Muning Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究发现现有统一多模态生成器的视觉生成模块无法在画布上对像素文本进行符号推理，仅相当于渲染器；为此作者从零训练纯像素空间生成模型，证明该能力缺失源于训练范式而非像素本身，并观察到缩放律、逐帧书写中间步骤的链式思考效应及强化学习后训练带来的推理提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本、图像、音频、触觉乃至机器人动作原则上都可渲染到同一画布上，作者提出将全部模态统一到视觉中的宏大目标，并以语言作为首个检验对象：要纳入语言，视觉模型必须直接在像素层面推理文本。然而现有图像、视频及统一多模态生成器在视觉生成模块中无法进行文本符号推理，只有在底层文本模块或提示重写器已给出答案时才成功，生成器退化为渲染器，且后训练几乎无法解决该局限。作者因此要辨析失败根源究竟是像素本身还是当前训练范式。

Method: 作者不带任何文本通路、从零训练一族纯像素空间生成模型，输入输出均仅为图像；模型直接在画布上读取问题、书写中间步骤并给出答案。同时考察其随规模的性能变化、单帧与逐帧书写中间步骤的对比，以及强化学习后训练的影响，并验证同一画布跨模态推理。

Result: 纯像素模型表现出大语言模型的标志性行为：性能随规模按幂律可预测提升；逐帧书写中间步骤显著优于单帧直接作答，呼应思维链；强化学习后训练进一步增强推理；共享同一画布使同一模型可跨模态推理，这是纯文本语言模型所不具备的能力。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个在无文本token的纯像素空间生成模型上对这些现象的系统性研究，表明视觉端到端符号推理的瓶颈在于训练范式而非像素表征本身。

Abstract: Text, images, audio, touch, and even robot actions can, in principle, all be rendered onto a single canvas, suggesting an ambitious goal of unifying all modalities within vision. Language provides the natural first test. To subsume it, a visual model must reason directly over text in pixels. Across a wide range of image, video, and unified multimodal generators, however, we find that their visual generation modules cannot perform symbolic reasoning over text on the canvas. They succeed only when an underlying text module or prompt rewriter already supplies the answer, effectively reducing the generator to a mere renderer, a limitation that post-training does little to resolve. To test whether this failure stems from pixels themselves or from current training paradigms, we train a family of purely pixel-space generative models from scratch without any text pathway. With images as their sole input and output, these models read questions, write intermediate steps, and produce answers directly on the canvas. Remarkably, they exhibit hallmark behaviors of large language models. Performance improves predictably with scale following a power law. Writing intermediate steps frame by frame substantially outperforms single-frame answering, echoing chain-of-thought, while reinforcement-learning post-training further strengthens reasoning. Moreover, sharing one canvas allows the same model to reason across modalities, a capability that text-only language models lack. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of these phenomena in purely pixel-space generative models free of text tokens.

</details>


### [135] [Decoding Without a Decoder: Direct Pixel Prediction from Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22287)
*Hansheng Chen,Shengqu Cai,Jan Ackermann,Rayhan Zirvi,Gordon Wetzstein,Xinchen Yan,Leonidas Guibas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DWD通过线性头在最后一步直接输出像素，去除潜扩散模型的解码器，降低推理成本并保持或提升像素质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜扩散模型依赖解码器将去噪后的潜变量映射到像素，增加了系统复杂度与推理成本；且以往方法中最终像素由去噪输出直接得到，未对两个角色加以区分。

Method: 将去噪与像素输出解耦：保留原去噪头驱动采样，新增DWD头仅在最终步预测像素。通过仅微调最后几个transformer块扩展预训练潜模型，DWD头用像素质量损失训练，同时将去噪头锚定到预训练模型以保持采样轨迹。

Result: 在潜图像与视频生成器上移除解码器并降低推理成本，像素质量持平或提升；Wan视频生成中细节畸变与闪烁明显减少，warping error降低23%；在无解码器的像素扩散模型上亦提升纹理细节，ImageNet FID由1.55降至1.43，为像素流模型的新SOTA。

Conclusion: 分离去噪与像素输出两种角色是一种通用且高效的机制，既能消除解码器开销，又能改善像素级质量，并适用于潜模型与像素扩散模型。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models rely on a decoder to map denoised latents to pixels, adding complexity and inference cost. We introduce Decoding Without a Decoder (DWD), which lets a finetuned diffusion transformer produce final pixels directly through a linear head.
