Unlike previous methods, where the denoising output leads to final pixels, DWD separates the two roles: the original denoising head continues to drive sampling, then the new DWD head predicts the pixels at the final step. We extend pretrained latent models with DWD efficiently by finetuning only the last few transformer blocks, training the DWD head with pixel quality losses while anchoring the denoising head to the pretrained model to preserve the sample trajectory. Across latent image and video generators, DWD removes the decoder and reduces inference cost while maintaining or even improving pixel quality. On Wan video generation, it visibly reduces detail distortion and flickering, lowering warping error by 23%. Remarkably, the same separation also improves pixel diffusion models that do not even use a decoder: a final-step DWD head produces substantially better texture detail in text-to-image generation, and improves ImageNet FID from 1.55 to 1.43, a new state of the art among pixel flow models.

</details>


### [136] [Spool: Low-Latency Streaming Inference for Hybrid Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23228)
*Rui Ning,Om Chabra,Hari Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spool 是一个免训练框架，利用线性递归层状态更新对状态为仿射的特性，缓存并关联组合逐帧状态变换，实现精确滑窗淘汰，在5个混合VLM、4个视频基准上取得最低 p95 TTFT（较两种vLLM基线约6.6倍）并提升2.3个百分点准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉无障碍代理、机器人等应用需在有限历史帧窗口（如最近两分钟）内快速回答视频问题。混合VLM（注意力与GDN等线性递归层交替）适合该场景，但维护滑动窗口代价高：递归层将所有历史帧压缩进共享状态，淘汰旧帧通常需重放整个窗口。

Method: 观察到递归更新对状态是仿射的、系数依赖输入，因此每帧的效应可表示为可关联组合的变换。Spool 缓存逐帧状态变换，随窗口推进高效组合，将过期帧从各递归层状态中移除而无需重放骨干网络。层内移除是精确的；跨层则因缓存输入计算而可能残留影响（类似注意力KV淘汰）。另设计专用数据结构与GPU kernel降低缓存与组合开销。

Result: 在5个开源混合VLM与4个视频推理基准的20组测试中，Spool 均取得最低 p95 首token延迟（TTFT），相比两种近期vLLM基线中较快者几何平均降低6.6倍，同时平均提升2.3个百分点准确率。

Conclusion: 通过仿射变换的关联缓存实现免训练、层内精确的滑窗状态移除，兼具显著延迟与精度优势，适用于实时长视频流式推理。

Abstract: Applications such as visual accessibility agents and robotics require answering questions about recent video content promptly, over a bounded window of past frames (e.g., the last two minutes). Hybrid vision-language models (VLMs), which interleave attention with linear recurrent layers such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN), are attractive for this setting, but maintaining a sliding window is expensive. The reason is that recurrent layers combine all past frames into a shared state, so expiring old frames normally requires re-running the entire window through the model. We observe that the recurrent update is affine in the state with input-dependent coefficients, so the effect of each frame can be captured as a transformation that composes associatively. We present Spool, a training-free framework that caches per-frame state transformations and composes them efficiently as the window advances, removing expired frames from each recurrent layer's state without replaying frames through the backbone. Removal is exact within each layer; as with key-value eviction in attention, expired frames can retain residual influence through the cached inputs computed by earlier layers. We design a dedicated data structure and GPU kernels to achieve low caching and composition overhead. Across five open-source hybrid VLMs and four video reasoning benchmarks, Spool achieves the lowest p95 time-to-first-token in all 20 model-benchmark tests, reducing it by a geometric mean of $6.6\times$ over the faster of two recent vLLM baselines, while improving accuracy by 2.3 percentage points on average.

</details>


### [137] [EviSlide-MIL: Multi-Query Pooling and Shared Prediction for Class-Incremental Whole-Slide Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23212)
*Zhouhan Qian,Sicheng Chen,Chad Wong,Tianyi Zhang,Enhui Chai,Zeyu Liu,Fei Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EviSlide-MIL，用每类多个可学习query聚合冻结patch特征并共享预测模块，实现WSI的类增量学习，减少遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSI弱监督MIL需随新肿瘤亚型引入而增量扩展类别，但历史数据受限且推理时无任务标识；特征聚合与预测模块更新会导致旧类遗忘。

Method: 为每个类别配置多个可学习query，将冻结的patch特征聚合为类特定表示；各类共享单一预测模块以抑制增量阶段的参数膨胀；冻结旧类对应query以保持其注意力模式不变；辅以经验回放和知识蒸馏缓解共享参数更新带来的遗忘。

Result: 在TCGA八个肿瘤亚型上，多数学习设置下性能优于对比方法且遗忘更低。

Conclusion: EviSlide-MIL为可扩展、保留知识的WSI分类提供了有效方案，代码与预训练模型将开源。

Abstract: Histopathological analysis of whole-slide images (WSIs) plays a critical role in cancer diagnosis, and multiple instance learning (MIL) is widely adopted for WSI analysis under weak supervision. As new tumor subtypes are introduced, extending existing MIL models requires learning new classes while retaining knowledge of previously learned ones. This presents a class-incremental learning challenge, where models must acquire new classes with limited access to historical training data and recognize all learned classes without task identifiers at inference. A major obstacle is forgetting, as updates to feature aggregation and prediction modules can degrade performance on previously learned classes. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{EviSlide-MIL}, a class-incremental WSI framework that integrates multiple learnable queries per class with a shared prediction module. The queries aggregate frozen patch features into class-specific representations, while the shared prediction module limits parameter growth across incremental stages. By freezing queries associated with previously learned classes, EviSlide-MIL preserves their attention patterns for identical inputs, while experience replay and knowledge distillation further mitigate forgetting as shared parameters are updated. Experiments on eight tumor subtypes from The Cancer Genome Atlas demonstrate that EviSlide-MIL achieves improved performance and reduced forgetting compared with competing methods across most learning settings, demonstrating its potential for scalable and knowledge-preserving WSI classification. Code and pretrained models will be released.

</details>


### [138] [VIVID: Beyond Static Multi-Image Reasoning with Localized Visual Differences and Textual Entity Dynamics in Video MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23153)
*Chengqian Ma,Yuanze Zeng,Hongyu Wang,Mingfeng Lin,YiCheng Xiao,Yu Chen,Pengyu Li,WenHao Wang,Yuexiao Ma,Xiawu Zheng,Fei Chao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIVID 通过关键帧间的 Patch Diff 构建动态 Token，在紧帧预算下保留局部运动与事件顺序，仅增 0.22% 可训练参数即超越全部十一个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频问答在帧预算紧张时需保留局部运动与事件顺序，但现有多模态大模型丢弃未选帧、独立编码关键帧，退化为静态多图推理：空间上全帧池化将小运动目标稀释进静态背景；时间上丢帧或 token 平均抹去帧间运动，混淆方向、速度与顺序。

Method: 提出 VIVID：关键帧保留核心状态，帧间 Patch Diff 在 K_f 个关键帧内保留邻帧变化。自治 Delta 驱动关键帧规划（ADKP）从 3 fps 帧池选锚点，将周边 patch 差分送入双路变化记忆（DRCM），在零额外锚点 token 下形成 Dynamic Tokens；并配合相机稳定的主动运动 ROI 与按时间排序的实体状态链。冻结 27B 主干，仅添加 0.22% 可训练参数。

Result: VIVID-Small（50 个示例，约 1.5 GPU 小时）在 8,083 道题的总体准确率上超越全部十一个基线，仅落后于 VIVID-Large（3,600 个示例）；VIVID-Large 在 MotionBench、Video-MME 与 TempCompass 上领先。

Conclusion: 以帧间差分而非静态快照建模视频，可在紧帧预算和极小参数开销下显著提升视频 MLLM 的时空理解能力，且性能随示例规模从 Small 到 Large 进一步提升。

Abstract: Video question answering under tight frame budgets requires preserving localized motion and event order, yet current video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) reduce comprehension to static multi-image reasoning by discarding unselected frames and encoding keyframes independently: (1) Spatially, whole-frame pooling dilutes small moving targets into static backgrounds; (2) Temporally, dropping intermediate frames or averaging tokens erases inter-frame motion, confusing direction, speed, and order. We propose VIVID (Visual Inter-frame Variations and Individual Dynamics), which moves beyond static snapshots like human vision: keyframes capture core states while inter-frame Patch Diffs preserve neighboring changes within $K_f$ keyframes. Autonomous Delta-Driven Keyframe Planning (ADKP) selects anchors from a $3\text{ fps}$ pool and feeds surrounding patch differences into Dual-Road Change Memory (DRCM), forming Dynamic Tokens at zero extra anchor tokens alongside a camera-stabilized Active Motion Region of Interest (ROI) and chronological Entity State Chains. Adding $0.22\%$ trainable parameters to a frozen $27\text{B}$ backbone, VIVID-Small ($50$ examples, $\sim 1.5$ GPU hours) beats all eleven baselines in overall accuracy across $8,083$ questions and trails only VIVID-Large ($3,600$ examples), which leads on MotionBench, Video-MME, and TempCompass.

</details>


### [139] [GameRealm: Grounding Visual Learning in Interactive Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23140)
*Weiguang Zhao,Yifan Wang,Ni Yang,Yating Wang,Xingyu Miao,Yankai Jiang,Mulin Yu,Tao Lu,Haoyu Guo,Junting Dong,Weinan Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GameRealm：面向视觉基础模型的大规模游戏原生多模态数据集，含1.2亿+RGB帧、1000小时游戏画面及度量深度、相机参数、音频、控制输入、3D点轨迹等丰富标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型对物理智能至关重要，但受限于训练数据碎片化、标注质量不一致、多模态监督不足。作者希望用游戏环境提供同步、精确、可扩展的原生监督信号来弥补这一缺口。

Method: 构建GameRealm数据集：覆盖单目、立体、多视角、全景等多种视角配置，提供度量深度、相机内参/外参、同步音频、控制输入和3D点轨迹等多模态标注；并设计统一的数据采集流水线，融合人工操作与智能体自动探索，实现跨多样游戏环境的可扩展采集。

Result: 在多种视觉学习任务上的大量实验表明，GameRealm在视觉生成与几何学习两方面均能持续提升视觉基础模型性能。

Conclusion: GameRealm是一个通用且高价值的数据资源，为推进视觉智能（尤其是物理智能场景下的视觉基础模型）提供新的机会。

Abstract: Visual foundation models are central to physical intelligence, yet their development remains constrained by fragmented training datasets, inconsistent annotation quality, and limited multimodal supervision. To address these challenges, we introduce GameRealm, a large-scale, richly annotated dataset that grounds visual observations in synchronized, game-native supervision. GameRealm encompasses over 120 million RGB frames and 1,000 hours of gameplay footage across all views. Beyond its unprecedented scale, GameRealm provides rich multimodal supervision, including metric depth, camera parameters, synchronized audio, control inputs, and 3D point trajectories. Its diversity further extends to flexible view configurations, spanning single-view, stereo, multi-view, and panoramic recordings. To enable scalable acquisition across diverse game environments, we develop a unified data collection pipeline that combines human-operated interaction with agentic exploration. Extensive experiments across diverse visual learning tasks demonstrate that GameRealm consistently strengthens visual foundation models in both visual generation and geometric learning. These results establish GameRealm as a versatile and valuable data resource, unlocking new opportunities for advancing visual intelligence.

</details>


### [140] [Event-Based Person Detection for Maritime UAV Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23088)
*Zijie Ning,Patrick Vandewalle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究事件相机在海事人员落水检测中的应用：用v2e从MOBDrone救援视频合成事件数据，比较RVT-T（循环视觉Transformer）与YOLOv8n、RT-DETR-R18在曝光匹配图像上的检测性能，并在Jetson Orin Nano上验证机载实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上搜救需快速发现落水人员，UAV巡检是可行手段；但飞行中相机运动与低光下较长曝光会使小目标产生运动模糊，降低帧图像上的人员检测精度。事件相机以异步方式记录亮度变化，可规避长曝光带来的运动模糊，因此值得评估其用于海事人员检测的潜力。

Method: 利用v2e事件模拟器从公开MOBDrone救援视频生成合成事件流，并保留原有标注以构建事件域数据集；将事件模型RVT-T与在曝光匹配图像上训练的YOLOv8n、RT-DETR-R18进行对比；设置50/100/200 ms不同合成曝光以考察曝光时长影响；并将检测器部署于Jetson Orin Nano评估机载可行性与能效。

Result: 在来自留出录像的843个测试样本上，100 ms合成曝光下RVT-T取得15.63% person AP50:95，显著高于YOLOv8n的6.89%与RT-DETR-R18的9.44%。曝光由50 ms增至200 ms时，帧基方法精度逐步下降，与事件模型的差距进一步扩大。部署于Jetson Orin Nano后，RVT-T保持精度，单样本约需136 ms、2.02 J，可满足UAV实时处理。

Conclusion: 事件相机配合RVT-T可在长曝光、运动模糊场景下显著优于帧基检测器，且能在嵌入式平台实现实时、低能耗的机载处理，为海事人员搜索提供可行方案。当前结果基于v2e合成事件而非真实事件相机采集，后续需在真实事件数据上验证。

Abstract: Maritime search and rescue requires locating people in the water quickly, motivating the use of UAVs to survey the search area. During flight, camera motion and the longer exposures used in low light can blur small targets and hinder person detection. Event cameras record brightness changes as they occur, reducing the motion blur associated with longer exposure times. We investigate their use for maritime person detection by generating synthetic events using the v2e event simulator from public MOBDrone rescue videos and retaining the existing annotations. We compare the recurrent vision transformer model RVT-T with YOLOv8n and RT-DETR-R18 trained on exposure-matched images. On 843 test samples from held-out recordings, RVT-T achieves 15.63% person AP50:95, compared with 6.89% for YOLOv8n and 9.44% for RT-DETR-R18 under 100 ms synthetic exposure. Increasing exposure times from 50 to 200 ms progressively reduces frame-based detection accuracy, widening the gap to the event model. To evaluate the feasibility of on-board processing, we deploy our detector on a Jetson Orin Nano platform. The RVT-T model retains its accuracy and requires approximately 136 ms and 2.02 J per sample, allowing real-time processing on the UAV.

</details>


### [141] [Transfer Once, Ask Locally: Compact Audio-Visual Memory for On-Device Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23085)
*Qitong Wang,Jihui Jin,Divya Kothandaraman,Vishnu Raj,Christopher Rasmussen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练、查询无关的音视频token剪枝框架，在传输前构建紧凑可复用记忆，实现设备端高效音视频问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量设备上的音视频问答需兼顾速度、内存、隐私与通信。将音视频编码放在服务器、轻量语言解码器放在本地可保护问题隐私，但传输与处理完整多模态表示代价高昂。

Method: 以视频时空块与对齐音频窗口为"player"，用蒙特卡洛Shapley估值衡量重要性；采用嵌入覆盖目标，将查询无关效用与跨模态冗余校准结合，并通过边际覆盖增益奖励单模态多样性；在有限token预算下保留高分player的原始token嵌入，形成可跨后续本地问题复用的记忆。

Result: 在Video-MME、Daily-Omni、Video-Holmes上，使用video-SALMONN 2+（3B）与2 Pro（4B）测试，各设置下QA准确率最优或并列最优；Daily-Omni（3B，80%剪枝）较对比方法提升12.86–14.62个百分点，设备端LLM预填充与解码延迟最低，相当嵌入内存下解码加速最高1.92倍。

Conclusion: 基于Shapley估值与覆盖目标的选择性token保留可在免训练条件下实现高质量、低延迟、可复用的音视频记忆压缩，适合端云协同的隐私保护问答场景。

Abstract: Audio-visual question answering on lightweight user devices requires balancing speed, memory usage, privacy, and communication. One approach is to run audio-visual encoders on a server and a lightweight language decoder on the user device, keeping questions local while offloading media encoding. However, transferring and processing the full multimodal representation remains costly. We propose a training-free, query-agnostic token pruning framework that constructs a compact, reusable memory before transmission. We define video spatio-temporal blocks and aligned audio windows as players and estimate their importance through Monte Carlo Shapley valuation. We use an embedding coverage objective that combines query-independent utility with cross-modal redundancy calibration and rewards unimodal diversity through marginal coverage gains. We retain the original token embeddings of high-scoring players under a limited token budget and transfer the resulting memory once for reuse across subsequent local questions. Experiments on Video-MME, Daily-Omni, and Video-Holmes using video-SALMONN 2+ (3B) and video-SALMONN 2 Pro (4B) show that our method achieves the best or tied-best QA accuracy among the compared compression methods across all evaluated settings. On Daily-Omni with video-SALMONN 2+ (3B) at 80\% pruning, our method improves accuracy by 12.86--14.62 percentage points over these methods while achieving the lowest device-side LLM prefill and decoding latency, with up to $1.92\times$ decoding speedup at comparable embedding memory usage.

</details>


### [142] [When a Larger Guidance Scale Repairs Text-to-Video Failures and When It Does Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23025)
*Taewon Kang,Matthias Zwicker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文测量研究指出，提高无分类器引导（CFG）尺度仅在提示词不含否定、不含与特定时间绑定的事件、且"需求"分数足够高时才有效修复文本到视频模型的提示词忽略失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界与用户遇到扩散模型忽略提示词时惯常做法是提高 CFG 尺度，但该修复何时有效、何时无效缺乏系统度量与可预判依据。

Method: 在 Mochi 模型上对三类失败（罕见组合、否定、晚期事件）对比"提高 CFG 尺度"与"重新生成"的修复效果；提出"需求"（demand）分数，从去噪前四分之一、视频尚未生成时读取，量化去噪器对被遗漏提示部分的响应强度；并以已发表的针对三类失败的修正方法作为固定探针进行验证，拟合阈值判定。

Result: 提高尺度对"雪地海滩"等罕见组合的修复超过重新生成；对否定"没有车辆的高速公路"无效，修复失败数少于重新生成；对晚期事件"被浪冲平的沙堡"完全无效。上述结果可由提示词与 demand 分数事先预判，阈值以上恢复率明显高于阈值以下；三种固定探针行为与预测一致。

Conclusion: 提出一个可判定的条件：提高 CFG 尺度仅在提示词无否定、无特定时间事件且 demand 足够高时有效；据此给出简短决策规则，指导用户判断何时值得尝试该常见修复、何时改用其他手段。

Abstract: When a text-to-video diffusion model ignores part of a prompt, the usual first fix is to raise the classifier-free guidance scale. This paper is a measurement study of when that fix works. On Mochi, the same increase repairs failures on rare compositions ("a snowy beach") beyond what generating again does, does not repair negations ("a highway with no vehicles"), where it fixes fewer failures than generating again does, and changes nothing for events that must happen late in a clip ("a sandcastle that a wave washes flat"). Which of these happens can be known in advance, from the prompt and one inexpensive score. The score, which we call demand, measures how strongly the denoiser reacts to the part of the prompt that the failed video leaves out, and it is read from the first quarter of denoising, before any video exists. Our main finding is a condition: raising the scale is effective only when the prompt contains no negation and no event tied to a specific time, and when demand is sufficiently high, with recovery clearly higher above a fitted threshold than below it. Published corrections for the three failure types, used as fixed probes, behave as this account predicts. A short decision rule follows: before generating again, a user can tell whether the most common fix is worth trying, and what to use when it is not.

</details>


### [143] [Tracking Ethological Behaviors of Broiler Chickens Through Computer Vision (YoloV11) Using an Android Mobile Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23007)
*Ryan Davi Oliveira de Meneses,Glauber da Rocha Balthazar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究构建并评估了一个用于肉鸡行为自动分类的计算机视觉模型，采用YOLOv11并部署到Android端，对坐、站、采食三类行为进行分类，验证了其在真实农场环境中的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确家禽养殖需要对鸡只行为进行连续监测以支持动物福利评估与决策，而人工观察效率低、难以规模化，因此需要自动化的行为识别手段并落地移动端应用。

Method: 使用公开视频，经预处理与人工标注为三类（坐、站、采食）；通过探索性分析核查数据一致性与视觉质量；训练YOLOv11模型，并经TFLite转换部署到Android应用；在未见图像与真实农场环境中进行测试。

Result: 模型性能随类别而异：静态姿势较稳健（坐姿AP=0.531，精确率=100%），动态行为较差（采食AP=0.292，召回率=0.20）；在未见图像上准确率显著提升，坐98.04%、站83.33%、采食67.65%。

Conclusion: 模型在真实农场环境中表现出有效泛化能力，支持持续行为监测、早期福利评估与精准养殖决策，为商业化规模的移动端应用奠定技术基础。

Abstract: This study developed and evaluated a computer vision model for automated ethological behavior classification of broiler chickens in climate-controlled poultry houses. Using publicly available videos preprocessed and manually annotated into three classes (sitting, standing, and feeding), the YOLOv11 model was trained and deployed on an Android application via TFLite conversion. Exploratory analysis ensured dataset consistency and visual quality, while evaluation showed class-dependent performance: higher robustness for static postures (sitting, AP=0.531; precision=100%) and lower accuracy for dynamic behaviors (feeding, AP=0.292; recall=0.20). Tests with unseen images demonstrated significant improvements, achieving accuracies of 98.04% for sitting, 83.33% for standing, and 67.65% for feeding. The model showed effective generalization in real farm environments, supporting its potential for continuous behavioral monitoring, early welfare assessments, and decision-making in precision poultry farming. These findings establish a technical foundation for future mobile-based applications at commercial scale, reinforcing the role of computer vision in automated animal welfare evaluation.

</details>


### [144] [CAST: Calibrating Correspondences for Training-Free Biomedical In-Context Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22995)
*Mingze Jiang,Jiashu Han,Haonan Chen,Zhiyi Li,Yilun Du,Kunzan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 CAST，基于冻结的 DINOv3 主干，通过稳定性测试与一致性测试校准细粒度对应关系，用于生物医学图像的上下文分割（ICS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学图像分割需持续适应新解剖结构、受试者、模态与采集协议，但密集专家标注稀缺，跨队列迁移困难。上下文分割（ICS）可在推理时用少量标注示例指定新结构，然而生物医学图像中相邻结构外观相似、受试者与成像条件差异进一步削弱区分性，可靠对应仍具挑战。

Method: CAST 为免训练框架，构建于冻结的 DINOv3 主干之上，用于校准细粒度对应：稳定性测试在全局与图像块（patch）特定层面提升特征可靠性；一致性测试评估候选对应是否得到一致的局部结构上下文支持。二者共同作用，实现跨多种解剖结构与成像条件的更可靠对应。

Result: 在覆盖 2D 与 3D 分割、跨受试者与跨模态迁移的八个生物医学基准上，CAST 在 Dice 上较最强整体基线提升 12.34 个百分点，并在全部六个体数据（3D）设置中排名第一。

Conclusion: CAST 的通用性为具有挑战性的生物医学应用开辟机会，包括 2D 到 3D 分割与图谱标签迁移。

Abstract: Biomedical image segmentation must keep up with new anatomical structures, subjects, imaging modalities, and acquisition protocols, yet dense expert annotations are scarce and models trained on one cohort often struggle to transfer. In-context segmentation (ICS) offers a way out: a new structure is specified by a few annotated exemplars at inference time. However, reliable correspondence remains challenging in biomedical images, where adjacent structures often share similar appearances, and variations across subjects and imaging conditions further obscure their distinctions. We introduce CAST, a training-free framework built on a frozen DINOv3 backbone to calibrate fine-grained correspondences. Its stability test improves feature reliability at both global and patch-specific levels, while its coherence test assesses whether candidate correspondences are supported by consistent local structural context. Together, these tests enable more reliable correspondence across diverse anatomical structures and imaging conditions. Across eight biomedical benchmarks spanning 2D and 3D segmentation, cross-subject, and cross-modality transfer, CAST outperforms the strongest overall baseline by 12.34 percentage points in Dice and ranks first across all six volumetric settings. The versatility of CAST further opens up opportunities for challenging biomedical applications, including 2D-to-3D segmentation and atlas label transfer.

</details>


### [145] [Is model self-assessment enough for diverse LVLM ensembles?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22956)
*Michael Smith,Frank P. Ferrie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建统一的无训练集成流水线，通过32种置信度估计、24种集成配置、20个数据集和2种提示规格，系统评估候选响应选择式集成，发现仅依赖各模型自评置信度即可超越单个模型，但数据集、集成组成与提示措辞是显著影响集成效果的关键混杂变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型集成虽在多任务上超越单模型，但聚合过程通常依赖训练、参数拟合或复杂任务特定流水线；且现有评估不充分，难以进行公平比较。作者希望建立统一可比的评估框架，并检验无需训练的简单自评估式集成能否胜任。

Method: 构建统一流水线进行候选响应选择：由不同模型提供候选答案，从中选出最佳答案构成集成。在20个数据集、2种提示规格下，系统评估34种置信度估计方法与24种集成配置（含不同架构模型间的集成），并与需训练或参数拟合的方案对比。

Result: 简单、无训练、无参数的方案已足够：仅依赖各构成模型自身置信度、无需显式校准，即可优于各构成模型，并在准确率等标准指标（含开放式问题）上与更复杂方案相当。同时发现数据集、集成组成与提示措辞是被先前工作忽视的关键混杂变量，会显著影响集成表现，甚至使集成劣于其最佳单模型。

Conclusion: 获得最佳集成效果需要针对这些混杂变量进行优化，但这会限制泛化性。作者建议实践者仅在部署条件可被精确刻画时采用无训练集成，并以强模型的Best-of-N配置作为良好起点。

Abstract: Ensembles of diverse Large Vision Language Models have demonstrated performance beyond that of single models across multiple datasets and tasks. However, the necessary aggregation process usually relies on training, parameter fitting, or complex task-specific pipelines. Furthermore, evaluation is limited, making a fair comparison difficult. We create a unified pipeline where we perform candidate response selection: we build an ensemble by selecting the best answer from a set provided by different models. We comprehensively evaluate across 34 different means of confidence estimation, 24 ensemble configurations, 20 datasets, and two prompt specifications. We show that simple training and parameter-free approaches can be sufficient, even for ensembles of different architectures. These simple self-assessment approaches rely solely on each constituent model's confidence, without explicit calibration, yet are able to perform better than said constituent models. They are also competitive with more involved alternatives on standard metrics such as accuracy, including on open-ended problems. However, we also discover that dataset, ensemble composition, and prompt wording are key confounding variables insufficiently explored by prior work that significantly affect ensemble performance, such that the ensemble can perform worse than its best constituent model. These must be optimized for to get the most out of ensembles, at the cost of limiting their generalizability. We recommend that practitioners consider training-free ensembles only if deployment conditions can be precisely characterized, with Best-of-N configurations of strong models being a good starting point.

</details>


### [146] [Code World Model 2.0: Towards Infinite Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22925)
*Ruoyu Wang,Hao Zhong,Yanming Yang,Jiahao Tian,Lin Liu,Biao Gong,Guoshan Lu,Yiding Tian,Chunhua Shen,Zhenzhong Lan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Invoker-Render 渲染器，将双向创作模型与因果四步流式模型统一在共享的结构代理条件接口下，并引入 Progressive Forcing（ProForcing）训练策略，在约200小时多粒度对齐语料上训练，实现多粒度代理可控、无累积漂移的实时交互式视频渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉代理（visual proxy）将显式世界状态模拟与照片级视频合成解耦，为离线片段创作与实时交互流式生成提供可控接口。但实用渲染器面临两个挑战：(1) 上游代理粒度差异大（从粗布局到精细关节几何），需运行时自适应；(2) 流式推理需在极少因果步内完成，且不能在自生成历史中累积漂移。

Method: 1) 统一条件接口：以结构代理、外观参考与语言为共享条件，配对双向创作模型与因果四步流式模型；2) 多粒度训练：构建约200小时对齐的多粒度语料，使模型在细粒度代理下遵循几何约束、在粗代理下合成合理局部运动；3) ProForcing 训练：先训练因果预测器，用训练视频派生的历史将其蒸馏为四步渲染器，随后转向连续自生成 rollouts 训练，以缓解步数压缩与 rollout 漂移的叠加；4) 流水线化服务栈支持高效流式推理。

Result: 实验显示模型在多种代理族、粒度与相机轨迹上均具备通用可控性；相较近期流式基线取得一致提升；支持长达30分钟的赛车场景 rollouts 且保持稳定，场景与主体外观不漂移。

Conclusion: Invoker-Render 通过统一条件接口与 ProForcing 训练，成功衔接双向创作与实时流式渲染，兼顾细粒度几何遵循、粗代理下的运动合理性、少步因果推理效率与长时稳定性，为可控交互式视频生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Visual proxies decouple explicit world-state simulation from photorealistic video synthesis, offering a controllable interface for both offline clip creation and real-time interactive streaming. Yet a practical renderer must address two challenges. Upstream proxies vary from coarse layouts to fine articulated geometry depending on the interaction, and streaming inference must operate in a few causal steps without accumulating drift across self-generated history. We present Invoker-Render, pairing a bidirectional creation model with a causal four-step streaming model under a shared conditioning interface over structural proxies, an appearance reference, and language. Trained on an aligned approximately 200-hour multi-granularity corpus, Invoker-Render honors fine geometric constraints when supplied while synthesizing plausible local motion under coarse proxies, enabling runtime selection of input detail. To bridge bidirectional creation and real-time streaming without compounding step compression and rollout drift, we introduce Progressive Forcing (ProForcing). ProForcing first trains a causal predictor and distills it into a four-step renderer using histories derived from training videos. Training then shifts to continuous self-generated rollouts. A pipelined serving stack supports efficient streaming inference. Experiments show versatile control across proxy families, granularities, and camera paths, consistent gains over recent streaming baselines, and stable 30-minute racing rollouts that preserve scene and subject appearance.

</details>


### [147] [Déjà View: A Shared Working-Memory Model for Visual Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23177)
*Mranmay Shetty,Gabriel Kreiman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合动态活动与图像特征间临时关联的工作记忆模型，用同一套训练权重在五个任务上按显式线索灵活切换，并系统考察延迟、干扰与图像历史对记忆表现的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作记忆模型不仅要记住刺激，还要能灵活调用记忆以完成不同任务；现有模型多针对单一任务，缺乏对"同一记忆系统如何灵活支持多任务"以及"错误如何随图像历史与任务需求变化"的统一刻画。

Method: 构建面向图像的工作记忆模型，将神经动态活动与图像特征之间的临时关联（temporary associations）相结合；在五个任务上联合训练，包括样例保持同时呈现其他图像、记忆样例的同时对干扰图像分类、判断图像新旧；同一训练模型由显式任务线索引导完成全部任务，并据此考察延迟、干扰和图像历史的影响。

Result: 在测试的延迟范围内，延迟对样例记忆表现影响很小；即使目标图像在各条件下呈现的新近程度相同，看到更多图像仍导致更多虚报；在"样例记忆+分类"任务中，随着提问数量增加，所有问题的正确概率均下降，尽管样例记忆本身表现稳健；模型在各任务40个短试次中保持稳定表现。

Conclusion: 单一工作记忆系统可被灵活地用于执行多种任务，其错误同时取决于先前呈现的图像与任务需求，说明灵活性与干扰敏感性可源于同一记忆机制。

Abstract: A working-memory model must remember stimuli and use its memory flexibly to perform different tasks. Here we introduce a model of working memory for images that combines dynamic activity with temporary associations between image features. We train the model on five tasks, including remembering a sample while other images are presented, categorizing distractor images while remembering the sample, and determining whether an image is new or has been presented before. The same trained model performs all tasks, guided by explicit cues about which response is required. We use this flexible memory model to ask how delay, distraction, and image history affect performance. Delays have little effect on sample-memory performance for the delays we tested, while seeing more images leads to more false alarms even when the target image appeared equally recently in all cases. On the sample-memory-plus-categorization task, the probability of correct responses on all questions decreases as more questions are asked, despite robust sample-memory performance. Finally, the model maintains stable performance across 40 brief trials of each task under the tested conditions. Together, these results demonstrate how a single working-memory system can be flexibly used to perform different tasks, and how its errors depend on both previous images and task demands.

</details>


### [148] [Automated Annotation-Free Synthetic Data Generation for Underwater Litter Detection: A Comparison of Generative Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23037)
*Amala Mary Vincent,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种零标注、全自动的数据增广流水线：以采样框生成插入掩码，在真实水下帧中按类别条件插入垃圾目标，并自动将框坐标作为标注，比较copy-paste、Stable Diffusion inpainting与DALL-E图像条件生成三种策略；在SeaClear数据集上DALL-E在5个域中4个提升检测性能，相对增益最高96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下目标检测受训练数据稀缺、类别不平衡以及浊度、光照和传感器差异导致的外观变化限制。现有增广手段（几何/颜色增广、GAN全合成）存在缺乏语义真实性、不遵循场景物理规律或需要额外标注的问题；在低数据水下监测场景中，额外标注是核心瓶颈，采集新素材困难而标注更耗时昂贵。

Method: 构建统一的零标注自动化流水线：从采样边界框出发，将每个框转换为插入掩码，在保留原始背景上下文的前提下，通过粘贴或生成方式将类别条件化的垃圾目标插入掩码区域，并自动将同一框坐标写入为对应标注，从而无需人工标注即获得全标注训练数据。在同一流水线内比较三种策略：copy-paste、Stable Diffusion inpainting、DALL-E图像条件生成。

Result: 在SeaClear水下垃圾数据集上，从边界框保真度、视觉真实感以及生成样本对下游检测器性能的影响三方面评估；DALL-E在5个域中的4个提升检测性能，相对仅真实数据基线最高增益达96%。

Conclusion: 基于生成模型的零标注目标插入式增广可在无人工标注条件下有效缓解水下检测的低数据瓶颈，其中DALL-E图像条件生成在多数域上带来显著性能提升，为低数据领域的标注高效增广提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Underwater object detection is often limited by scarce and imbalanced training data and by strong appearance variations caused by turbidity, lighting, and differences between sensors. Existing data augmentation strategies, such as geometric or color augmentation or fully synthetic image generation with Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), either lack semantic realism, fail to respect scene physics, or require additional annotations. In low-data domains like underwater environmental monitoring, the need for additional annotations is often the core bottleneck. Collecting new footage is hard, but labeling this dataset is even more time-consuming and costly, which limits how quickly a usable dataset can be constructed. We propose a unified fully automated zero-annotation pipeline that inserts class-conditioned trash objects into real underwater frames while preserving the original background context. Starting from sampled bounding boxes, each box is converted into an insertion mask and the object is pasted or generated within it, while the same box coordinates are automatically written as the corresponding annotation. Because labels are derived directly from the sampled boxes, the pipeline generates fully labeled training data for low-data settings without any manual annotation. Within this single pipeline we compare three strategies: copy-paste, Stable Diffusion inpainting, and DALL-E image-conditioned generation. We evaluate bounding-box faithfulness, visual realism, and the impact of the generated samples on downstream detector performance on the SeaClear underwater debris dataset. Results show that DALL-E improves detection performance in four of five domains, with relative gains of up to 96 percent over the real-only baseline.

</details>


### [149] [Wear and Go: Calibration Free Sparse Inertial Motion Capture via Online Sensor Setup Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22894)
*Yuanshuo Tan,Licheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOSAIC：无需标定姿势，仅凭普通运动即可恢复稀疏惯性动捕中每个传感器的身体部位身份与佩戴朝向，并将其作为可复用接口适配冻结的预训练姿态估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏惯性动捕通常假设每个传感器的解剖身份与佩戴朝向已知，实际部署中该假设难以成立；现有方法依赖专门标定姿势，限制可用性。

Method: 核心洞察是身体姿态连续变化而传感器配置保持不变，单个时刻歧义的运动在时间上可提供互补证据。提出setup network，对无序传感器流使用时间注意力与跨传感器注意力建模，并将分配与旋转证据累积为会话级估计；恢复的配置用于对测量做规范化，从而接入冻结的预训练姿态估计器，解耦传感器配置与运动重建。

Result: 在随机化配置的真实惯性记录上、传感器佩戴于规定身体部位时，有界偏移下取得接近已标定的姿态精度；任意朝向下累积带来显著收益；跨不同姿态网络迁移表明该方法可作为可复用接口。

Conclusion: MOSAIC将传感器setup恢复抽象为与姿态估计模型解耦的通用接口，支持传感器可互换与预训练模型复用，免标定姿势部署稀疏惯性动捕。

Abstract: Sparse inertial motion capture typically assumes that each sensor's anatomical identity and mounting orientation are known. We present MOSAIC (Motion-based Sensor Assignment and Inertial Calibration), a framework that recovers this setup from ordinary motion without a calibration pose. Our central insight is that body pose changes continuously while the sensor setup persists, allowing movements that are individually ambiguous to provide complementary evidence over time. A setup network processes unordered sensor streams with temporal and cross-sensor attention, then accumulates assignment and rotation evidence into a session-level estimate. The recovered setup canonicalizes measurements for frozen pretrained pose estimators, separating sensor configuration from motion reconstruction. For sensors worn at prescribed body sites, evaluation on real inertial recordings with randomized setups shows near-calibrated pose accuracy for bounded offsets and substantial benefits from accumulation under arbitrary orientations. Transfer across pose networks supports setup recovery as a reusable interface between interchangeable sensors and pretrained pose models.

</details>


### [150] [FastFrames: Video Generation at Interactive Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23226)
*Gene Chou,Guandao Yang,Fujun Luan,Lars Mescheder,Wei Dong,Stephan Richter,Vladlen Koltun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FastFrames 是一系列高速自回归视频扩散模型，通过替换 VAE 解码与 KV 缓存更新等"固定实现细节"为轻量替代方案，在单张 B200 上实现 36 ms/帧（28 RPS），质量与慢近 8 倍的 SOTA 模型持平，从而支持实时交互式视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型天然适合交互式视觉体验，但现有模型推理速度慢几个数量级，无法支撑流畅交互。作者通过剖析推理流水线发现，通常被视为固定实现细节的 VAE 解码与 KV 缓存更新等操作实际占端到端延迟的一半以上，因此这些组件并非不可替代。

Method: 先对 SOTA 模型推理流水线做延迟剖析（profiling），定位瓶颈；随后质疑 VAE 解码、KV 缓存更新等组件"不可或缺"的假设，用轻量替代方案替换它们，构建 FastFrames 模型族（1.3B 与 14B 两种规模），在不牺牲质量的前提下降低交互延迟。

Result: 1.3B 变体在单张 B200 上达到 36 ms/帧（28 RPS），质量与慢近 8 倍的 SOTA 模型相当；14B 变体在 8×B200 节点上达 45 ms/帧（22 RPS），视觉质量与真实感显著更优。并演示了相机条件与物理条件视频生成等实时交互应用。

Conclusion: 将推理流水线中被忽视的"实现细节"作为优化重点，能有效突破自回归视频扩散的延迟瓶颈，实现实时交互式视频生成，为交互式视觉体验提供可行路径。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models generate frames based on new observations and inputs, making them a promising medium for interactive visual experiences. However, existing models are orders of magnitude too slow for fluid interaction. This paper introduces FastFrames, a family of fast autoregressive video diffusion models that achieves interactive latency without compromising quality. We profile the inference pipeline of state-of-the-art models and find that operations often treated as fixed implementation details, such as VAE decoding and KV cache updates, in fact account for over half of end-to-end inference latency. We challenge the assumption that these components are indispensable and replace them with lightweight alternatives. Our 1.3B variant runs at 36 ms per frame, or 28 responses per second (RPS), on a single B200 GPU while matching the generation quality of a state-of-the-art model that is nearly 8x slower. Our 14B variant runs at 45 ms per frame, or 22 RPS, on an 8x B200 GPU node and yields significantly better visual quality and realism. We show that FastFrames enables real-time interaction in applications such as camera-conditioned and physics-conditioned video generation.

</details>


### [151] [Multimodal Masked Diffusion Autoencoder for Modeling Impaired Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23211)
*John Gideon,Anshul Gupta,Megan Applegate-Kenton,Kayli Battel,Todd J Rowell,Patricio Reyes Gomez,Laporsha Dees,Emily Sumner,Avinash Balachandran,Guy Rosman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态掩码扩散自编码器 ID-MAE，将损伤预测标签作为掩码 token 与注视、轨迹重建联合自监督训练，并发布 103 小时驾驶模拟器数据集 DIVERT，损伤检测配对 AUC 达 0.706。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类认知状态（分心、压力）、注视与行为同驾驶环境存在细微交互，AI 可借此辅助驾驶员；但大规模获取认知状态等级的真实标签成本高昂，需要以自监督方式缓解标签稀缺问题。

Method: ID-MAE：多模态掩码扩散自编码器，在损伤（impairment）预测之外增加自监督目标——除将损伤标签作为掩码 token 进行预测外，同时重建被掩码的注视与轨迹。另构建 DIVERT 驾驶模拟器数据集：103 小时、n=109、涵盖认知/视觉/情感三类共十种损伤诱发条件，同步采集注视、车辆状态与场景视频。并对输入模态、掩码策略、位置编码、注意力机制与解码方式进行设计空间探测。

Result: 在 DIVERT 及现有数据集上损伤推断显著提升；位置匹配样本对上损伤检测配对 AUC 0.706，而去除重建损失仅 0.590，优于现有基线；轨迹与注视预测等附加任务亦取得良好结果；训练与推断中不同损伤间存在相互作用，视觉高负荷分心可与认知分心区分。

Conclusion: 将掩码重建自监督目标与损伤预测联合可有效提升多模态驾驶员状态推断，并支持轨迹、注视等下游任务；DIVERT 数据集为多模态驾驶员状态研究提供支撑，代码与数据将于录用后公开。

Abstract: Human cognitive state (e.g., distraction, stress), gaze, and behavior interact with the driving environment in subtle ways that AI could use to assist drivers, but ground-truth labels for human cognitive state levels are costly to obtain at scale. We propose a multimodal masked-diffusion autoencoder, ID-MAE, that adds a self-supervised objective to impairment prediction: alongside predicting the impairment label as a masked token, it reconstructs masked gaze and trajectory. To probe the effects of different modalities and types of impairments on model inference, we introduce DIVERT, a driving-simulator dataset containing 103 hours of driving data (n = 109) with ten impairment inductions spanning cognitive, visual, and emotional states, recorded with gaze, vehicle state, and scene video. We demonstrate how ID-MAE affords significant improvements in impairment inference results on both the DIVERT dataset and existing datasets, and enables strong results in additional tasks, such as trajectory and gaze prediction. For location-matched pairs, our model achieves a paired AUC of 0.706 for impairment detection on DIVERT, compared to 0.590 without reconstruction loss, improving over existing baselines. We probe the design space in terms of input modalities, masking scheme, positional encoding, attention mechanism, and decoding. We also investigate the interaction between various impairments in training and inference, demonstrating that visually demanding distractions are separable from cognitive distractions. Code and data will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [152] [UnrealEvent: Synthetic Dataset Generation and Annotation for Event-Based Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23078)
*Yentel Schoovaerts,Zijie Ning,Patrick Vandewalle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UnrealEvent：基于 Unreal Engine 5.5 的合成数据集生成管线，结合 V2E 风格事件生成器与 EasySynth 语义渲染，自动产出事件表示与 YOLO 格式标注，并评估 YOLOv8 在合成、零样本真实与真实微调三种设定下的性能，揭示显著的合成-真实域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具备高时间分辨率、低功耗、高动态范围，但事件目标检测受限于带标注训练数据稀缺，人工标注成本高。

Method: 在 UE5.5 中构建合成数据生成管线：EasySynth 语义渲染通道输出渲染关卡序列，配合 V2E 启发的事件帧生成器，基于连续帧逐像素对数亮度差生成事件，并独立从正态分布采样对比度阈值以模拟真实传感器制造阈值失配；自动生成配对事件表示与 YOLO 边界框标注，无需人工标注；在 eTraM 上进行合成域、零样本真实域与真实微调评估。

Result: YOLOv8 在合成域内表现较强，但存在明显的合成-真实差距，行人类别尤为突出；少量真实子集微调可提升真实域性能，残余差距仍显著。

Conclusion: 合成管线可低成本自动生成事件检测数据，但域差距仍存，需提升资产多样性与传感器标定以缩小差距。

Abstract: Event-based cameras provide high temporal resolution, low power consumption, and a high dynamic range, but progress in event-based object detection remains constrained by the scarcity of annotated training data. To solve this issue, this paper introduces the tool UnrealEvent, a synthetic dataset-generation pipeline implemented in Unreal Engine 5.5. The pipeline combines a V2E-inspired event-frame generator with EasySynth semantic-rendering passes, allowing rendered level sequences to be converted into paired event representations and YOLO-format bounding-box annotations without manual labeling. Events are generated from per-pixel log-luminance differences between consecutive frames, with contrast thresholds sampled independently from a normal distribution to emulate the fabrication-induced threshold mismatch of real sensors. Using this pipeline, we provide a synthetic dataset and evaluate YOLOv8 detectors under synthetic, zero-shot real, and real fine-tuning training on eTraM. The results show strong in-domain synthetic performance but a substantial synthetic-to-real gap, especially for pedestrians. Fine-tuning on a small real subset improves real-domain performance, while the remaining gap highlights the importance of asset diversity and sensor calibration.

</details>


### [153] [SpikWM: Efficient End-to-End Spiking World Modeling for Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22981)
*Shuhan Ye,Hongbin Yu,Chong Wang,Hui Lu,Jiaming He,Yi Yu,Jiangbo Qian,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出脉冲潜世界模型SpikWM，一种端到端JEPA架构，用脉冲神经网络做目标条件规划，在多个视觉规划基准上超过脉冲基线并以更低能耗逼近人工神经网络水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像观测与目标的规划需要能区分视觉状态与动作后果的预测。脉冲神经网络计算稀疏，但单步脉冲会丢失运动线索、并把物理后果不同的动作混为一谈，因此需要在保持脉冲稀疏性的同时提升表征与预测能力。

Method: SpikWM为端到端联合嵌入预测架构：每帧一次仿真步、每个潜位置一次神经更新。包含三个模块：脉冲区域编码（区域池化保留空间线索）、双脉冲动作编码（正负二值响应结合）、因果膜预测（跨潜历史传递阈下证据，学习分组时间常数）。编码器与预测器在无奖励图像-动作轨迹上以next-embedding预测与SIGReg联合训练。

Result: 统一规划器下，Push-T、Two-Room、Reacher、OGBench-Cube成功率分别为88.67%、97.33%、99.33%、86.67%，优于两个脉冲基线。Push-T消融支持各模块有效性，受控回放检验对外观与物理状态变化的响应。相同规划预算下，相对LeWM估计算术能耗降低75.6%。

Conclusion: 脉冲动力学与JEPA式潜空间预测可有效结合，在视觉目标条件规划中兼顾性能与能效，为低能耗世界模型规划提供可行路径。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned planning with image observations and goals requires predictions that distinguish visual states and action consequences. Spiking neural networks offer sparse computation, but single-step spikes can obscure motion cues and merge actions with different physical outcomes. We propose the Spiking Latent World Model (SpikWM), an end-to-end joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) with one simulation step per image and one neural update per latent position. Spiking Regional Encoding retains spatial cues through regional pooling. Bi-Spike Action Encoding combines positive and negative binary responses, while Causal Membrane Prediction carries subthreshold evidence across the latent history with learned group-wise time constants. The encoder and predictor are trained jointly on reward-free image--action trajectories using next-embedding prediction and SIGReg. With a common planner, SpikWM achieves mean success rates of $88.67\%$, $97.33\%$, $99.33\%$, and $86.67\%$ on Push-T, Two-Room, Reacher, and OGBench-Cube, respectively, outperforming two spiking baselines. Push-T ablations support each module, and controlled replays examine responses to appearance and physical state changes. Under a matched planning budget, SpikWM reduces estimated arithmetic energy by $75.6\%$ relative to LeWM. Code will be released at https://github.com/ShawnYE618/SpikWM.

</details>


### [154] [MI-Hyper: Generating Multiple Instance Models for Weight Space Transfer Learning in Histopathology via Mixed Tasks Pretrained HyperNetwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22968)
*Xitong Ling,Yousef Kotp,Weiming Chen,Yining Li,Jiawen Li,Minxi Ouyang,Xinyi Guo,Mingxi Fu,Yizhi Wang,Tian Guan,Lianghui Zhu,Yonghong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MI-Hyper 用超网络在混合任务上预训练，按任务描述生成 MIL 模型的聚合与分类权重，实现无需参数更新的零样本全切片图像预测，在 308 个任务上优于 MI-Zero。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本 WSI 预测需在部署时不更新参数即泛化到新病理任务。已有方法 MI-Zero 依赖 patch-text 相似度池化，作者提出另一条路径：通过条件权重生成，从任务描述中"学习构造预测规则"，实现权重空间迁移。

Method: 提出 MI-Hyper 超网络，在混合任务上预训练，为 MIL 模型生成聚合权重与分类权重。两阶段训练：先用源标签联合训练生成器与共享 MIL 基座（basis），再冻结基座、精调生成器；生成的任务权重可在同一任务的多个样本（case）间复用。

Result: 在 52 个病理数据集共 308 个任务上，MI-Hyper 相较 MI-Zero 的平均 AUC、平衡准确率、macro-F1 分别提升 1.85、1.17、0.57 个百分点；部分分子预测任务上 AUC 与平衡准确率提升超过 20 个点。

Conclusion: 结果支持"任务条件权重生成"作为长序列建模中权重空间迁移的有效途径；超网络生成的 MIL 权重可在零样本设定下超越 patch-text 相似度池化范式。

Abstract: Zero-shot whole-slide image (WSI) prediction aims to generalize to new pathology tasks without parameter updates at deployment. MI-Zero makes these predictions by pooling patch-text similarities. Conditional weight generation, combined with supervision from diverse pathology tasks, offers an alternative: learning to construct predictive rules from task descriptions. We introduce MI-Hyper, a hypernetwork pretrained across mixed tasks to generate the aggregation and classification weights of multiple instance learning (MIL) models. We first train the generator and a shared MIL basis jointly using source labels. We then freeze the basis and refine the generator. The generated task weights are reused across cases. Across 308 tasks from 52 pathology datasets, MI-Hyper improves mean AUC, balanced accuracy and macro-F1 over MI-Zero by 1.85, 1.17 and 0.57 percentage points, respectively. On some molecular prediction tasks, both AUC and balanced accuracy improve by more than 20 points. These results support task-conditioned weight generation as a promising approach to weight-space transfer for long-sequence modeling.

</details>


### [155] [Training-Free Building Change Detection with Example-Induced Discriminants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22964)
*Zhi Lu,Bin Hong,Zhenming Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LIND 是一个无需训练的建筑物变化检测框架，利用少量变化标注构建可复用的线性建筑物分类器，在单一冻结 ViT 特征空间内统一建筑物识别与时相比对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑物变化检测（BCD）依赖密集标注，跨区域采集代价高。少量已标注样本可低成本指定目标，但变化图监督不完整：仅标注"变化"的建筑物，且未说明建筑物出现在哪个时相。作者的关键观察是：这些标注仍能揭示可用于识别其他影像（含未变化影像）中建筑物的特征。

Method: 提出 LIND（Linear INduced Discriminants）训练无关框架：将区域级特征对比转化为可复用的线性建筑物分类器；仅使用单个冻结 ViT，在同一特征空间中统一建筑物识别与时相比对；无需文本提示、无需辅助分割模型；仅在一个验证集上调一个参数，随后跨数据集与跨 shot 设置固定不变。

Result: 大量实验表明检测精度强、具备跨数据集迁移能力、推理高效；并扩展至其他语义类别与更广的土地覆盖变化。

Conclusion: 变化标注可作为一种紧凑的视觉规格，用于新场景中的检测；少样本变化标注即可支撑免训练的通用 BCD 流程。

Abstract: Building change detection (BCD) relies on dense annotations that are costly to collect across regions. A few annotated examples offer a more economical way to specify the target, but change maps provide incomplete building supervision: they label only changed buildings and leave the building-containing date unspecified. Our key observation is that these annotations nevertheless reveal features that recognize buildings in other images, including unchanged ones. We present Linear INduced Discriminants (LIND), a training-free framework that turns regional feature contrasts into a reusable linear building classifier. Using a single frozen vision transformer, it unifies building recognition and temporal comparison in the same feature space. This approach requires neither text prompts nor an auxiliary segmentation model. Only one parameter is tuned on a single validation dataset and then kept fixed across datasets and shot settings. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong detection accuracy, cross-dataset transfer, and efficient inference, with extensions to other semantic classes and broader land-cover changes. Change annotations thus provide a compact visual specification for detection in new scenes. Code: https://github.com/lzcn/lind

</details>


### [156] [You Only Encode Once: Sub-Pixel-Shifted Frames for Arbitrary-Scale Remote Sensing Super-Resolution with 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22898)
*Canjie Liu,Liuqian Wang,Qi Ming,Wei Lu,Xiaohan Hao,Yuefei Wu,Wentao Chao,Lei Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPS-GS编码一次式任意尺度遥感超分框架：先用相位相干混叠解调(PCAD)在傅里叶域融合多帧子像素信息，再经高斯投影对齐(GPA)迭代优化高斯中心与颜色，实现任意尺度连续渲染；无需真实多帧训练序列，在五个遥感数据集上优于单帧与多帧基线且推理效率有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张低分辨率遥感图像高频信息有限，任意尺度超分(ASSR)困难；多帧SR虽可利用亚像素互补信息，但现有方法依赖真实多帧训练序列并重复特征编码，数据获取成本与计算开销高。

Method: 1) 训练数据由单张高分辨率图像合成多相位LR观测，避免真实多帧序列需求；2) Phase-Coherent Aliasing Demodulation (PCAD)：基于傅里叶的对齐、相位感知残差融合与最小二乘混叠估计，利用移位相关混叠，在单次深度编码前完成多帧信息整合；3) Gaussian Projection Alignment (GPA)：通过多观测反投影迭代精化高斯中心与颜色以提升观测一致性；4) 精化后的高斯支持任意尺度的连续渲染。

Result: 在五个遥感数据集上的大量实验表明，相较单图像SR与多帧SR基线具有更强的重建性能，同时在常见尺度下保持有竞争力的推理效率。代码将公开。

Conclusion: SPS-GS以"编码一次"的范式统一多帧亚像素融合与高斯泼溅表示，兼顾训练数据可得性、重建质量与推理效率，为遥感图像任意尺度超分提供了新思路。

Abstract: Arbitrary-scale super-resolution (ASSR) of remote sensing images remains challenging due to the limited high-frequency information in a single low-resolution (LR) observation. While multi-frame SR can exploit complementary sub-pixel information, existing approaches often require real multi-frame training sequences and repeated feature encoding, increasing data acquisition demands and computational costs. To address these limitations, we propose SPS-GS (Sub-Pixel-Shifted Gaussian Splatting), an encode-once framework that learns from multi-phase LR observations synthesized from individual high-resolution images, without requiring real multi-frame training sequences. Specifically, Phase-Coherent Aliasing Demodulation (PCAD) exploits shift-dependent aliasing through Fourier-based alignment, phase-aware residual fusion, and least-squares aliasing estimation, enabling multi-frame information to be integrated before a single deep encoding pass. Gaussian Projection Alignment (GPA) then iteratively refines Gaussian centers and colors using multi-observation back-projection to promote observation consistency. The refined Gaussians support continuous rendering at arbitrary scales. Extensive experiments on five remote sensing datasets demonstrate strong reconstruction performance against single-image and multi-frame SR baselines, while maintaining competitive inference efficiency at common scales. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [157] [SlideJev: Order-Stable and Tissue-Grounded Jev Decisions for Whole-Slide Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22879)
*Lei Wu,Jiashuai Liu,Di Zhang,Chunze Yang,Jiusong Ge,Junbo Lu,Yongqi Liang,Wenjie Zhao,Ni Zhang,Chen Li,Zeyu Gao,Qidong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SlideJev框架，将候选答案独立与切片证据比对验证，并通过视觉token门控更新将判断落地到组织证据，缓解VLM的语言先验与捷径问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片病理VQA现有VLM高度依赖语言先验：答案对候选选项顺序敏感，且可绕过组织证据通过捷径得出，缺乏图像依据。

Method: 采用Jev打分：对每个候选答案用相同格式、无选项位置标签的独立提示并行计算，以其在原始语言模型头上的支持与拒绝对数似然之差作为分数，归一化后选答案；通过候选条件的读取与比较，在解码器各层对视觉token进行门控更新以实现证据接地；骨干冻结，仅用问答与配对图像监督、以同一打分训练视觉模块。

Result: 在三个全切片病理VQA基准上取得最高的平均总体准确率，并在四种选项顺序下达到100%答案一致性；图像对照实验中，正确图像相对错配与无图像输入带来的平均准确率增益在三数据集上均为最大。

Conclusion: 将候选验证与视觉证据学习解耦并通过Jev打分统一，可有效消除选项顺序敏感与语言捷径，确保模型判断真正依赖于组织证据。

Abstract: Whole-slide visual question answering (VQA) offers a natural-language interface to pathology, allowing diagnostic and phenotypic questions to be answered directly from tissue slides. However, current vision-language models (VLMs) rely heavily on linguistic priors: their answers are sensitive to the order of the candidate answers, and can be reached through shortcuts that bypass the tissue evidence. To address these challenges, we introduce SlideJev, a framework that separately verifies candidate answers against the slide and learns to ground these judgments in tissue evidence. To connect parallel candidate evaluation with visual evidence learning, we adopt Jev and score each candidate by the difference between support and rejection log-likelihoods from the original language-model head. We compute these scores in parallel using separate prompts with the same format and no option-position labels, then normalize them for answer selection. To ground these scores in tissue evidence, candidate-conditioned reading and comparison guide gated updates to visual tokens across decoder layers. With the backbone frozen, we train these visual modules through the same scores using question-answer and paired-image supervision. Across three whole-slide pathology VQA benchmarks, SlideJev achieves the highest mean overall accuracy and 100% answer consistency across four option orders. Image-control experiments further show that SlideJev achieves the largest average accuracy gains from correct images over mismatched and image-free inputs across the three datasets. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/leowu-ai/SlideJev.

</details>


### [158] [RoadAnnoter: An AI-Assisted Desktop Tool for Semantic Segmentation Annotation of Road-Scene Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22884)
*Nazmul Haque,Arnob Protim Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出开源桌面标注工具 RoadAnnoter v1.0，集成六种人在回路（human-in-the-loop）道路场景分割标注模式，并统一采用掩膜转多边形后处理与 YOLO 分割格式导出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路场景图像的精细语义分割对道路安全研究至关重要，但多边形级标注耗费大量人力，限制了带标注数据集的建设；现有工具在场景类别的先验辅助与项目特定特征适配方面不足。

Method: 开发 RoadAnnoter v1.0，集六种标注模式：手工多边形勾绘、SAM Prompt、Smart Select、Edge Fill（Canny 边缘检测结合 CIELAB 色彩空间相似度洪泛填充）、基于 Cityscapes 训练的 SegFormer 预训练语义分割，以及基于 MobileNetV3-Small 的轻量网络 TinySegNet（用累积标注迭代训练实现项目特定分割）。SAM 类模式采用 SAM 与 MobileSAM 可提示分割。六种模式共用掩膜转多边形流水线：形态学处理、高斯平滑、轮廓提取、Douglas-Peucker 简化，输出 YOLO 分割格式。

Result: 在孟加拉国事故地点的 Google 街景图像上完成标注，用于基于视觉语言模型的自由流速度估计研究。代表性场景的定性评估显示各标注模式对不同特征几何形状具有互补适用性；与 Roboflow 的视觉比较表明主要道路场景区域边界特征相似，但未开展定量精度评估。

Conclusion: RoadAnnoter 为交通工程与道路安全研究提供了一个本地运行的道路场景分割数据集构建框架，但尚缺乏定量精度验证。

Abstract: Accurate semantic segmentation of road-scene images is essential for road safety research; however, polygon-level annotation remains labor-intensive and limits labelled dataset development. This study presents RoadAnnoter v1.0, an open-source desktop application integrating six human-in-the-loop annotation modes: manual polygon delineation, SAM Prompt, Smart Select, Edge Fill, pretrained semantic segmentation using Cityscapes-trained SegFormer models, and task-specific segmentation using TinySegNet, a lightweight MobileNetV3-Small-based network trained with accumulated annotator-generated labels. SAM-based modes use promptable segmentation with SAM and MobileSAM, while Edge Fill combines Canny edge detection with colour-similarity-based flood filling in CIELAB colour space. SegFormer generates suggestions for common road-scene categories, whereas TinySegNet supports adaptation to project-specific features through iterative training. All six modes use a shared mask-to-polygon pipeline comprising morphological processing, Gaussian smoothing, contour extraction, and Douglas-Peucker simplification; annotations are exported in YOLO segmentation format. RoadAnnoter was used to annotate Google Street View images from recorded road accident locations in Bangladesh for a vision-language model-based investigation of free-flow speed estimation. Qualitative assessment across representative scenes demonstrated the complementary applicability of the annotation modes to diverse feature geometries. Visual comparison with Roboflow indicated similar qualitative boundary characteristics for principal road-scene regions, although no quantitative accuracy assessment was conducted. RoadAnnoter provides a locally operated framework for developing road-scene segmentation datasets for transportation engineering and road safety research.

</details>


### [159] [Impact of Pretraining Length, Model Size, and Annotation Budget on Downstream Clinical Tasks of Foundation Models in Radiotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22896)
*Alejandro Cortina Uribe,Sidsel Højklint Poulsen,Ivan Richter Vogelius,Jens Petersen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在约15万例放疗CBCT上开展掩码图像建模预训练ViT编码器，系统考察预训练时长、模型规模与微调策略对下游临床任务的影响，发现预文本验证损失是最稳定的下游性能预测指标，LoRA可比肩全量微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放疗期间采集的CBCT蕴含肿瘤缩小与放疗诱导组织变化信息，具备构建基础模型以监测个体治疗反应的潜力；然而目前尚无大规模放疗CBCT预训练模型，且下游任务未知，导致建模选择（预训练规模、模型尺寸、微调策略）缺乏评估依据。

Method: 在约150,000例原始3D放疗CBCT上以掩码图像建模预训练ViT编码器，通过验证损失、有效秩和线性探测追踪表征质量；下游任务为头颈癌的喂养管存在分类（51,135例CBCT、1,562例患者）与临床特征分类（T/N分期、性别、年龄、肿瘤部位；1,278例患者）；对比LoRA与全量微调及无预训练基线，并变化标注数据量。

Result: 预文本验证损失与下游性能关联最一致，有效秩与线性探测亦有相关性但随模型尺寸与微调方式不稳定；LoRA仅训练0.5–2.5%参数即达到与全量微调相当的效果；全量微调在所有规模下均优于无预训练，喂养管分类AUROC为0.91–0.97（基线0.58–0.85），性别分类为0.99–1.00（基线0.71–0.97）；模型规模影响小且不一致。

Conclusion: 放疗CBCT大规模自监督预训练可显著提升下游临床任务性能，验证损失可作为可靠的过程监控指标，且较小模型配合参数高效微调（LoRA）即可满足需求、降低计算成本。

Abstract: Cone-beam CT (CBCT) scans acquired during radiotherapy capture changes such as tumor shrinkage and radiation-induced tissue changes, offering great potential for foundation models that help monitor individual treatment responses. However, to our knowledge, no large-scale pretrained models for radiotherapy CBCT exist, and evaluating modeling choices is difficult since downstream tasks are not known in advance. We examine how pretraining length, model size, and finetuning strategy relate to downstream clinical performance, and how metrics tracked during pretraining correlate with it. We pretrain vision transformer encoders with masked image modeling on about 150,000 raw 3D radiotherapy CBCT scans, tracking representation quality via validation loss, effective rank, and linear probing. Downstream tasks are classification of feeding tube presence ($n=51,135$ CBCTs, $1,562$ patients) and clinical characteristics (T-, N-stage, sex, age, tumor site; $n=1,278$ patients) in head and neck cancer. We compare low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and full finetuning against no pretraining, varying annotated dataset size. Pretext validation loss was the most consistent predictor of downstream performance; effective rank and linear probing were also associated, but not consistently across model sizes and finetuning methods. LoRA performed comparably to full finetuning while training only 0.5-2.5% of the parameters. Fully finetuned models consistently outperformed models without pretraining across all sizes, with AUROC 0.91-0.97 vs. 0.58-0.85 for feeding tube classification and 0.99-1.00 vs. 0.71-0.97 for sex classification. Model size had only a small, inconsistent effect, suggesting smaller models suffice at lower computational cost. Code and pretrained models (non-commercial license): https://github.com/alexCortinaU/RT-CBCT_SSL

</details>


### [160] [Harnessing Image Diffusion Prior for Photo-Realistic Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22874)
*Fanghua Yu,Hongyu An,Jinfan Hu,Xinqi Lin,Zhiyuan You,Jian Wang,Hongyang Li,Chao Dong,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图像生成先验的生成式视频修复方法，通过噪声能量再平衡、运动对齐噪声扭曲、自监督去噪编码器与解耦时序注意力，实现时序一致的细节合成，兼顾图像级保真与视频级稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像生成模型能产生照片级真实、细节丰富的图像，但其随机细节合成带来时序不一致，难以将强先验迁移至视频修复；现有视频修复方法虽改善时序连贯性，却牺牲视觉保真度，形成图像级质量与视频级稳定性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 围绕"时序一致的细节合成"设计四项组件：(1) 噪声能量再平衡，抑制扰动结构的低频随机性；(2) 运动对齐噪声扭曲，沿物体运动传播细节；(3) 自监督去噪编码器，缓解编码器引入的时序不确定性；(4) 解耦时序注意力，建模跨帧依赖。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在视觉质量、细节丰富度与时序稳定性上显著优于现有视频修复方法。

Conclusion: 通过将图像生成先验以时序一致方式引入视频修复，可同时保持保真度、增强细粒度真实感并维持时序一致，弥合图像级质量与视频级稳定性之间的差距。

Abstract: Recent image generation models produce photo-realistic results with rich details, but transferring their powerful priors to video restoration remains difficult due to temporal inconsistency from stochastic detail synthesis. Existing video restoration methods improve temporal coherence but often sacrifice visual fidelity, leaving a gap between image-level quality and video-level stability. We propose a generative video restoration method that bridges this gap by enabling temporally consistent detail synthesis from image generation priors. Our method combines noise energy rebalancing to suppress structure-disturbing low-frequency stochasticity, motion-aligned noise warping to propagate details along object motion, a self-supervised denoising encoder to mitigate encoder-induced temporal uncertainty, and decoupled temporal attention to model cross-frame dependencies. Together, these components preserve fidelity, enhance fine-grained realism, and maintain temporal consistency. Extensive experiments show that our method substantially outperforms existing video restoration approaches in visual quality, detail richness, and temporal stability.

</details>


### [161] [Event-Based Dynamic Lip Region Estimation During Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23111)
*Hamze Hammami,Omar Eldardeer,Francesco Rea*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双通路连续时间循环网络（CT-RNN）的事件相机唇部区域密集热图预测方法，从全幅事件“帧”直接定位唇部空间区域，在未见说话人上达69.5%，目标切换仅2.1次/秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机语音相关应用（唇读、说话人识别、语音活动检测）多依赖手工或数据集给定的裁剪ROI，且已有事件人脸分析仅输出嘴部关键点，导致应用需求（区域）与定位输出（点）不匹配。本文旨在直接从全幅事件数据预测唇部区域的密集空间表示。

Method: 将事件编码为按极性分离的体素网格，在每个空间位置用双通路连续时间循环网络处理：一条无状态快速通路与一条有状态慢速通路，时间常数在对数空间有界、作用于每个时间箱的真实流逝时间，分别用于细化与锚定预测；并对比了加入空间编码器的变体与卷积基线。

Result: 在四名未见说话人上唇部命中率69.5%，目标切换2.1次/秒；参数量更多的卷积基线分别为8.9%和11.0%（切换次数更高）。加入空间编码器提升精度但在新的人脸尺度上失效。在真实事件传感器上（仅用模拟事件训练），模型达58.0%，CNN为43.2%，且目标切换不足其一半。

Conclusion: CT-RNN双通路结构在事件相机唇部ROI定位上兼顾精度与时间稳定性，且从模拟到真实的泛化优于CNN；空间编码器带来的精度提升不具备尺度鲁棒性。

Abstract: Event cameras offer high temporal resolution and low power consumption, making them promising for speech-related applications. These include automatic lip reading, audiovisual speech analysis, and human-robot interaction, particularly in challenging environments characterized by low illumination or high dynamic range. Event-based lip reading, speaker recognition, and voice activity detection, however, mostly operate on a cropped region of interest (ROI), by hand or by the dataset, and no event-based method supplies that ROI as a spatial region; existing event-based face analysis gives the mouth as landmarks, which presents a mismatch between application and localization. The present work predicts a dense heatmap over the lip region from the full event "frame". Events are encoded as a polarity-separated voxel grid and processed at every location by a two-pathway continuous-time recurrent network (CT-RNN) with learned, log-space bounded time constants acting on the real elapsed time of each bin: a stateless fast pathway and a stateful slow pathway, utilised for refining and anchoring the prediction. The model reaches 69.5% on lips on four unseen speakers and switches target only 2.1 times per second, against 8.9 and 11.0 for convolutional baselines with
  more parameters. Adding a spatial encoder improves precision but fails on a new face scale. On a real event sensor, trained only on simulated events, the proposed model reaches 58.0% on lips against 43.2% for the CNN, with fewer than half its target switches.

</details>


### [162] [Refin3R: Geometry-Guided Refinement for Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23051)
*Jongmin Lee,Jaeyeong Kim,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Refin3R 用「2D 图像网格分支 + 3D SfM 点分支」的双分支架构，将 SfM 几何沿图像网格传播，双向交换信息以稠密化并精化前馈点图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈三维重建模型单次前向即预测稠密点图，但缺乏显式几何约束，导致跨视图不一致且精细度不足；已有几何先验方法仅把位姿/深度当作辅助输入、逐视图独立处理，或只在先验覆盖区域精化几何，无法兼顾稠密性与精度。

Method: 提出 Refin3R：以 SfM 点为几何锚点精化前馈点图。双分支架构中，2D 分支借助几何感知的骨干特征把 SfM 几何扩展到其覆盖范围之外；3D 分支在所有视图的 SfM 点上进行推理，捕捉图像网格无法表达的多视图结构；两分支双向交换信息，联合精化每个视图。

Result: 在 ScanNet++、ETH3D、Tanks&Temples 以及域外的人体与手术场景上，点图精度一致优于前馈方法与几何引导方法，且运行速度快于最强的几何引导基线。

Conclusion: 通过将稀疏 SfM 几何以显式 3D 分支建模并与 2D 图像分支双向交互，可在保持前馈效率的同时获得稠密、精准且跨视图一致的点图，并具备良好的域外泛化能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models predict dense point maps from multiple images in a single pass. Without explicit geometric constraints, however, their predictions are often inconsistent across views and lack fine-grained precision. Recent methods incorporate geometric priors such as camera pose or depth, but use them merely as auxiliary inputs, process each view independently, or refine the geometry only where the priors are available. We present Refin3R, which refines feed-forward point maps with Structure-from-Motion (SfM) points, propagating SfM geometry over the image grid. In its dual-branch architecture, a 2D branch extends SfM geometry beyond its coverage using geometry-aware backbone features, while a 3D branch reasons over the SfM points of all views, capturing multi-view structure that the image grid alone cannot represent. The two branches exchange information bidirectionally to jointly refine each view, yielding dense and accurate point maps. Across ScanNet++, ETH3D, Tanks\&Temples, and out-of-domain human and surgical scenes, Refin3R consistently outperforms feed-forward and geometry-guided methods in point map accuracy while running faster than the strongest geometry-guided baseline.

</details>


### [163] [M3OPD-Reasoner: An Open Recipe for Post-Training Multimodal Reasoning with Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23034)
*Nguyen Quang Trung,Hoang Phan,Shuo Sun,Longyin Zhang,AiTi Aw*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出M3OPD-Reasoner：以多教师在线策略蒸馏(MOPD)为核心，将图像STEM、图像感知、视频三个领域的教师(GSPO直接训练)蒸馏进单一学生VLM，再叠加MixRL得到M3OPD-Reasoner-9B；MOPD与MixRL可作连续两阶段，对同一数据"榨取"更多收益，且全部数据、代码与各阶段权重开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM后训练中，混合数据训练(MixSFT/MixRL)难以兼顾多领域专长且易相互干扰；作者希望验证多教师在线策略蒸馏作为融合多领域能力的最佳配方，并探究其与RL阶段能否串联复用同一批数据。

Method: 构建M3OPD-SFT-1.35M(监督微调)与M3OPD-RL-193K(可验证奖励强化学习)两个数据集，并在21个基准上对训练数据去污染。以Qwen3.5-9B为起点比较：全领域混合数据训练(MixSFT、MixRL)；按领域各训一个教师(GSPO，直接或经SFT预热)；再用MOPD将教师蒸馏进单一学生。最后在MOPD学生之上以相同prompt、相同设置追加MixRL。

Result: MOPD(教师为直接用GSPO从Qwen3.5-9B训练的版本)为最佳配方：学生在各领域接近对应教师水平，同prompt下平均优于混合训练基线。在同一数据、prompt和设置下追加MixRL仍能继续提升MOPD学生且无能力崩溃，得到主模型M3OPD-Reasoner-9B。

Conclusion: 多教师在线策略蒸馏可有效融合领域专长并作为RL的良好起点；MOPD与MixRL可作为连续两阶段流程，从同一数据中获得额外收益，该组合此前尚无VLM配方研究过。

Abstract: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) enables a single student to absorb the skills of several domain teachers by learning from its own generated responses. We present Multimodal Model Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation Reasoner (M3OPD-Reasoner), a fully open recipe for post-training vision-language models (VLMs) over three reasoning domains: image STEM, image perception, and video. We curate two datasets, M3OPD-SFT-1.35M for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and M3OPD-RL-193K for reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards. On these data, we compare post-training recipes that start from Qwen3.5-9B: training one model on the mixed data of all domains (MixSFT and MixRL), and training one teacher per domain with Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), directly or after an SFT warm-up, and distilling the teachers into one student with MOPD. We evaluate on 21 image STEM, image perception, and video reasoning benchmarks after we decontaminate the training data against them. The comparison establishes MOPD of teachers trained with GSPO directly from Qwen3.5-9B as the best recipe: the student closely matches each teacher on its own domain and is on average above the mixed-training baselines on the same prompts. Since MOPD already merges the domain capabilities that MixRL would otherwise have to learn, its student is a stronger starting point for RL, and we ask whether the same data can carry the model further from there: we add MixRL on top of the MOPD student, on the same prompts and with the same settings, which, to our knowledge, no prior VLM recipe has studied. The same prompts still improve the MOPD student, with no collapse, and give our main model, M3OPD-Reasoner-9B. MOPD and MixRL can therefore work as consecutive stages that get more out of the same data. We release all data, the training code, and the checkpoints of every stage.

</details>


### [164] [Learning Grounded Reasoning from Human Gaze](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23209)
*Gabriel Sarch,Declan Campbell,Nicholas Budny,Kia Ghods,Katerina Fragkiadaki,Jonathan D. Cohen,Brenden M. Lake,Taylor W. Webb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究人类注视数据如何有效用于提升视觉语言模型（VLM）的视觉推理：将注视作为"先验"而非完整序列监督效果更好，提出 GGR 模型，兼具更高的人类对齐性与视觉定位可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉推理依赖系统地探索与提取任务相关的视觉证据，眼动注视可反映这一过程，因此可作为 VLM 视觉推理的引导信号；但人类注视含噪声与冗余，直接迁移到 VLM 可能效果不佳。作者希望厘清如何有效利用注视数据，同时保持与人类行为的对齐。

Method: 采集 7,109 条人类注视试验，覆盖多样且具挑战性的视觉推理任务；以不同形式的注视引导训练 grounded reasoning 模型（对比"完整注视序列监督"与"注视作为先验"两种方式）；据此提出 GGR（Gaze-Guided Reasoner），并进一步引入无注视监督的强化学习；在图表理解、视觉搜索与计数任务上评估，并进行行为分析。

Result: 以人类注视作为 grounded reasoning 的"先验"始终优于使用完整注视序列监督；GGR 在人类对齐性与视觉定位可靠性上更优，准确率上与主流 grounded-reasoning 基线持平或更佳；强化学习在无注视监督下进一步提升任务准确率、注视对齐度与定位质量；行为分析显示注视监督模型在给出答案前表现出更多视觉探索。

Conclusion: 人类注视的价值在于引导空间探索、塑造推理行为，而不需要完全模仿人类行为；部分/先验式的注视引导是比序列级模仿更有效的利用方式。

Abstract: Human visual reasoning is driven by systematic exploration and extraction of task-relevant visual evidence. Human gaze provides a window into this process, making gaze a useful source of guidance for visually-grounded reasoning in vision-language models (VLMs). However, human gaze also includes noise and redundancies that may transfer poorly to VLMs. In this work, we investigate how human gaze data can be effectively leveraged to improve visual reasoning in VLMs, while maintaining alignment with human behavior. We collect 7,109 human gaze trials from a diverse and challenging set of visual reasoning tasks, and train grounded reasoning models with different forms of gaze guidance. Across evaluations in chart understanding, visual search, and counting, we find that using human gaze as a prior for grounded reasoning consistently outperforms supervision using the full gaze sequence. Based on these findings, we introduce GGR (Gaze-Guided Reasoner), which achieves greater human alignment and more reliable visual grounding while matching or outperforming leading grounded-reasoning baselines in accuracy. Reinforcement learning further improves task accuracy, gaze alignment, and grounding quality despite using no gaze supervision. Behavioral analyses show that gaze supervised models exhibit more visual exploration before arriving at an answer. Our results suggest that the value of human gaze lies in guiding spatial exploration and shaping reasoning behavior without requiring complete imitation of human behavior.

</details>


### [165] [$π^4$: A Foundation Model for 4D Scene Geometry and Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23144)
*Yifan Wang,Weiguang Zhao,Ni Yang,Haoyu Guo,Junting Dong,Weinan Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出π⁴统一4D场景感知基础模型，从无标定RGB输入联合恢复稠密几何、相机位姿、度量尺度、2D/3D点轨迹与运动物体掩码，仅约0.5B参数，单次前向即可完成联合重建与跟踪并支持数千视图的大场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D感知任务（几何重建、相机位姿估计、点跟踪、运动分割）通常由独立模型分别处理，模型规模大、流程割裂，且缺乏在真实野外无标定数据上的统一方案。

Method: 将几何分支与几何条件化的运动分支耦合：运动分支预测图像空间对应关系，并借助重建的目标视图几何将其提升至3D；共享骨干网络，训练上下文最多64视图，联合输出稠密几何、位姿、度量尺度、2D/3D轨迹及运动物体掩码，并给出配套数据与训练配方及关键设计选择的实证研究。

Result: 在多类基准上取得几何评测最佳总体平均排名，单/多视图2D与3D点跟踪及运动目标分割性能具竞争力；全系统约0.5B参数，不足多数基线的一半；单次前向即可输出联合重建与跟踪，并可泛化至数千张图像的大规模场景。

Conclusion: π⁴证明了以紧凑共享骨干统一几何与运动建模的可行性，在精度、效率与可扩展性之间取得良好平衡，为4D场景感知提供统一基础模型范式。

Abstract: $π^4$ is a unified foundation model for 4D scene perception. From diverse, uncalibrated RGB observations in the wild---a single image, an unordered collection, or a video sequence---it recovers dense geometry, camera poses, and metric scale, together with 2D/3D point tracks and moving-object masks for multi-image inputs. It couples a geometry branch with a geometry-conditioned motion branch, which predicts image-space correspondences that are lifted into 3D using reconstructed target-view geometry. Across diverse benchmarks, $π^4$ achieves the best overall average rank in geometry evaluation, alongside competitive performance in single- and multiview 2D/3D point tracking and moving-object segmentation. With a shared backbone, the full geometry-and-motion system contains approximately 0.5B parameters---less than half the size of most baselines. It produces joint reconstructions and tracks in one forward pass, including large-scale scenes with thousands of input images despite training with contexts of at most 64 views. We further present the data and training recipe underlying these capabilities, together with empirical studies of key design choices. Code and models are available at https://github.com/yyfz/Pi4.

</details>


### [166] [LUTBench: Benchmarking Text-Driven Photographic Look Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23113)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Boyu Wei,Chengkun Yue,Xuanhua Yin,Yiyang Li,Chupeng Liu,Zihan Deng,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LUTBench，首个统一协议下的文本驱动照片调色（look transfer）基准：基于91个3D LUT与MIT-Adobe FiveK构建455,000组可复现图像对，用模板+训练集校验自动生成描述文本，并系统评测16种方法的目标保真度、输入转换敏感性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动色彩分级研究在数据集、文本指令、目标定义与评价指标上各不相同，导致无法公平比较各方法对同一"look"的复现忠实度，缺少共享评测协议。

Method: 收集91个3D LUT，为每个LUT定义一个带指定输入转换的摄影look；将其应用于5,000张MIT-Adobe FiveK照片，生成455,000组输入-目标图像对，目标图像可复现。文本侧：在固定色块上测量色调与色彩变化，经模板转为候选子句，再用训练图像测量验证其适用性后组合成描述。评测涵盖目标保真度、对预定义输入转换的敏感性，以及区分"未见look泛化"与"新照片上已见look复现"。

Result: 在16种方法上评测发现：更强的文本对齐度并不保证更高的目标保真度；但多数有监督变体在已见与未见look上均提升了保真度。

Conclusion: LUTBench提供了可复现、标准化的文本驱动调色评测协议，揭示文本对齐与目标保真度之间的解耦，并表明有监督训练是提升保真度与泛化的有效途径。

Abstract: Photographic color grading shapes the tone and color of a photograph to achieve a desired look. Text-driven photographic look transfer lets users specify these adjustments in natural language, with the goal of reproducing the requested look across photographs while preserving their content. However, prior research on text-driven color grading uses different datasets, text instructions, target definitions, and metrics. These differences make it difficult to compare how faithfully the methods reproduce the same look. To compare methods under a shared protocol, we introduce LUTBench. We collect 91 three-dimensional lookup tables (3D LUTs) and define one photographic look per LUT with a specified input conversion. Applying these looks to 5,000 MIT-Adobe FiveK photographs produces 455,000 input-target image pairs with reproducible target images. For each look, we use templates to turn measured tone and color changes on fixed color samples into candidate clauses. We use training image measurements to verify applicable clauses before composing the text description. The evaluation measures target fidelity, tests sensitivity to predefined input conversions, and separates generalization to unseen looks from reproduction of seen looks on new photographs. Across 16 methods, stronger text alignment does not guarantee target fidelity. However, most supervised variants improve fidelity on both seen and unseen looks.

</details>


### [167] [FMT: Lossless Image Compression in a Fixed Number of Parallel Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23108)
*Ammar Ali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FMT（固定步数多尺度树）无损图像格式，将解码顺序与熵编码器协同设计，使图像解码的串行等待不随图像尺寸增长，从而在相同或更小体积下实现远快于学习式编解码器的解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式无损编解码器压缩率优于经典编解码器，但解码一张照片需数秒，而经典编解码器仅需毫秒；快速变体又牺牲了大部分压缩增益。作者认为时间主要花在"等待"而非"计算"上：网络需等待其条件值就绪，算术解码器逐个读取符号，导致串行依赖随图像规模增长。

Method: 设计FMT格式，将图像存为粗到细的树，每步包含相互独立的值：GPU一次性预测整步并并行解码；并行rANS通道在步间传递状态，使任意尺寸图像均在固定数量的顺序步骤内解码。在该树上构建位精确（bit-exact）编解码器族，从无网络规则到大型网络。

Result: 在同一GPU上测试的编解码器中，没有一种能在体积和速度上同时优于FMT族任何成员；无网络版本在体积与速度上均超过NVIDIA HTJ2K；大网络版本在相同或更小体积下，解码Kodak照片比同一A100上运行的学习式编解码器快9–61倍。

Conclusion: 通过将解码顺序与熵编码器联合设计、以固定步数消除随图像规模增长的串行等待，FMT兼顾了学习式方法的压缩率与经典方法的解码速度，为可实用的学习式无损图像格式提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Learned lossless codecs make smaller files than common classical codecs, yet the images we archive and train on still use classical codecs. A main reason is time: a learned codec takes seconds to decode a photo that a classical codec opens in milliseconds, and their faster variants give up most of their gain in size. We argue that much of this time is spent waiting, not computing: the network waits for the values it conditions on, and the arithmetic decoder reads them one at a time. We introduce FMT (Fixed-step Multiscale Tree), a lossless image format whose decoding order and entropy coder are designed together so that neither wait grows with the image. FMT stores an image as a coarse-to-fine tree whose steps hold independent values: a GPU predicts each step at once and decodes it in parallel rANS lanes that carry their states from step to step, so an image of any size decodes in a fixed number of sequential steps. On this tree we build a family of bit-exact codecs, from a rule with no network to large networks, and no codec we ran on the same GPU beats any of them on both size and speed. Without a network, FMT beats NVIDIA's HTJ2K on size and speed; with a large one, it decodes Kodak photos 9 to 61X faster than learned codecs we ran on the same A100, at the same size or smaller.

</details>


### [168] [Wall-to-wall maps of canopy height and aboveground carbon across the Atlantic Forest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23048)
*Matheus Pinheiro Ferreira,Thiago Carmuega Rabaçal,Giulio Brossi Santoro,Giovanna de Andrade Ferreira,Rodrigo Costa Pinto,Danilo R. A. de Almeida,Paulo Guilherme Molin,Catherine Torres de Almeida,Angélica F. Resende,Vinicius Castro Souza,Marcelo Antonio Pinho Ferreira,Cássio Toledo,Eben North Broadbent,Angelica Almeyda Zambrano,Laura Simões,Juliano van Melis,Ricardo Ribeiro Rodrigues,Pedro H. S. Brancalion*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用深度学习融合多源卫星数据，生成巴西大西洋森林2024年10 m冠层高度与30 m地上碳密度图，精度显著优于全球产品，并估算现有森林碳储量为1,944 Tg C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球冠层高度图被广泛用于森林碳估算，但其区域精度鲜有检验，直接用于碳核算存在风险。

Method: 构建深度学习网络，融合Sentinel-2反射率、Sentinel-1与ALOS PALSAR后向散射及ALOS高程数据；基于圣保罗州机载LiDAR（训练7,179块、测试1,793块，覆盖58,800 km²）训练与测试，并在州外两个LiDAR站点外部验证；以124个样地拟合幂律，将冠层高度转换为地上碳密度。

Result: 留出测试集RMSE为10 m下3.05 m、30 m下2.58 m；同像元上Meta与WRI全球产品误差为其两倍以上；剩余森林在28.3 Mha上储存1,944 Tg C。

Conclusion: 本地化模型精度显著优于全球产品，全球产品用于碳核算前须先经区域验证。

Abstract: Global canopy height maps are increasingly used to estimate forest carbon, yet their regional accuracy is rarely tested. We present wall-to-wall maps of canopy height (10~m) and aboveground carbon density (30~m) for the Brazilian Atlantic Forest in 2024. Height was predicted by a deep learning network fusing Sentinel-2 reflectance, Sentinel-1 and ALOS PALSAR backscatter and ALOS elevation, trained on 7{,}179 and tested on 1{,}793 patches (58{,}800~km$^2$) from a statewide airborne LiDAR campaign over São Paulo state, Brazil. On held-out test patches, the root mean square error is 3.05~m at 10~m and 2.58~m at 30~m, and the model was validated at two LiDAR sites outside São Paulo. Over the same pixels, the Meta and World Resources Institute global product has more than twice this error. A power law fitted to 124 inventory plots converted canopy height to above ground carbon density. The remaining forest holds 1{,}944~Tg~C over 28.3~Mha. Global products therefore need local validation before use in carbon accounting.

</details>


### [169] [When Anchors Shift the Target: Advantage-Gated Adaptation for Diffusion Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23038)
*Pu Cao,Min Zhou,Ying Shu,Tiezheng Ge,Qing Song,Lu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Advantage-Gated Adaptation (AGA)，解耦前向过程强化学习中"移动锚点"的双重角色：正样本保留标准回归目标拟合，负样本改用凹目标在移动策略预测处的一阶切线梯度，唯一确定无参门控 ω(a)=max(-a,0)，并将长程行为保持交由冻结参考模型（在其工作 CFG 尺度上正则）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向过程 RL（对扩散/流匹配模型将受奖励生成重新加噪为回归目标）中，负优势会导致凹更新与无界排斥梯度，现有方法用移动策略副本的缓存预测作锚点来稳定训练，但这些锚点同时扭曲了正反馈所教模型拟合的目标。作者将此冲突形式化为"锚耦合"（anchor coupling）：在剥离冻结参考正则项后，仿射移动锚规则无法在保持全部正回归目标的同时消除负曲率。

Method: 提出 AGA 以解耦锚点的两种角色。正样本沿用标准目标拟合，其自限性更新在观测目标处自然消失，与移动锚无关；负样本采用凹目标在移动策略预测处的一阶切线，保留局部排斥方向且梯度与预测无关。在二次锚族内，上述要求唯一确定无参门控 ω(a)=max(-a,0)。由于移动副本只提供局部线性化而非保持性，AGA 将长程行为保持交给冻结预训练参考模型，并在其运行时的 classifier-free guidance 尺度上加正则，用隐式奖励视角加以解释。

Result: 在组合生成、文本渲染与偏好对齐任务上，实验显示位移正目标会降低视觉质量并增加对策略副本调度（policy-copy schedule）的敏感性；而 AGA 能一致地改善奖励—质量权衡。

Conclusion: 移动锚点是造成正负反馈目标冲突的根源；通过门控式解耦（正样本标准拟合、负样本一阶切线排斥）可得到唯一无参形式 ω(a)=max(-a,0)，并将保持性职责转交冻结参考正则，从而在多种生成任务中稳健提升奖励与质量的平衡。

Abstract: Forward-process reinforcement learning adapts diffusion and flow-matching models by re-noising rewarded generations into regression targets. Because negative advantages induce concave updates with unbounded repulsive gradients, existing methods stabilize training using cached predictions from a moving policy copy, yet these anchors also alter what positive feedback teaches the model to fit. We formalize this conflict as anchor coupling: after separating frozen-reference regularizers, affine moving-anchor rules cannot eliminate negative curvature while preserving every positive regression target. We propose Advantage-Gated Adaptation (AGA) to decouple these roles. Positive samples retain standard target fitting, whose self-limiting update vanishes at the observed target independently of the moving anchor. Negative samples instead use the first-order tangent of the concave objective at the moving-policy prediction, preserving the local repulsion direction with a prediction-independent gradient. Within the quadratic-anchor family, these requirements uniquely determine the parameter-free gate $ω(a)=\max(-a,0)$. Because the moving copy provides local linearization rather than retention, AGA assigns long-horizon behavior preservation to a frozen pretrained reference regularized at its operating classifier-free guidance scale, interpreted through an implicit-reward lens. Across compositional generation, text rendering, and preference alignment, our experiments show that displacing positive targets degrades visual quality and heightens sensitivity to policy-copy schedules, whereas AGA consistently improves the reward--quality trade-off.

</details>
