### [170] [Rescaling OLS-Merged Adapters To Improve Personalized Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22969)
*Leander Girrbach,Niklas Schweiger,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出，主流适配器合并方法（RegMean、Mix-of-Show、Subspace Routing）以OLS拟合各适配器输出，当任务激活分布重叠时会系统性收缩任务特定信号，并提出对合并更新进行重缩放以恢复信号强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将多个适配器合并进单一图像生成模型可降低部署成本并保留多任务/个性化能力，但现有OLS式重构目标在激活分布重叠时会使合并适配器"过弱"，影响下游使用。

Method: 推导一个分解，定量刻画OLS重构目标导致的信号收缩（shrinkage）现象；基于该分解，对合并后的更新量（merged update）进行重缩放（rescaling）以恢复信号强度。

Result: 在最多合并30个适配器的多主体个性化任务上，重缩放相比未缩放基线显著提升主体保真度；在风格迁移、概念擦除、图像分类及基于LLM的文本分类上同样取得改进。

Conclusion: OLS重构式适配器合并存在固有信号收缩缺陷，可通过理论分解加以量化并用简单重缩放修正，方法通用且跨模态有效。

Abstract: Merging multiple adapters into a single image generation model reduces deployment costs while preserving diverse task capabilities or personalization modes. State-of-the-art methods such as RegMean, Mix-of-Show, and Subspace Routing fit the outputs of the merged model to those of the individual adapters using ordinary least squares (OLS). We show that this reconstruction objective systematically shrinks task-specific signals whenever the activation distributions of different tasks overlap, producing a merged adapter that is too weak for downstream use. We derive a decomposition that quantifies this shrinkage and propose rescaling the merged update to restore signal strength. On multi-subject personalization with up to 30 merged adapters, rescaling substantially improves subject fidelity over unscaled baselines. Our approach also improves results on style transfer, concept erasure, image classification, and LLM-based text classification.

</details>


### [171] [BRepA: Graph-Based B-Rep Pretraining for Unified CAD Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22903)
*Dekai Zhu,Zhaiyu Chen,Stefan Gavranovic,Slobodan Ilic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BRepA——一个基于图建模、同时重建几何与拓扑的自监督预训练框架，为CAD B-Rep数据提供统一表示骨干，在分割与生成任务上显著提升数据效率与收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-Rep以图结构编码几何基元及其关系，其上的分割与形状生成需要专用网络设计；而标注B-Rep数据有限，亟需有效的预训练策略来提升性能并加速训练。

Method: BRepA采用图神经网络对B-Rep建模，通过自监督方式显式重建对象的几何与拓扑信息；仅需在无标注CAD数据上预训练一次，所得表示可泛化至多任务。在生成任务中，将扩散模型表示与预训练BRepA特征对齐。

Result: 分割任务上，仅用1%标注样本时，BRepA预训练相比从头训练mIoU提升32.52%；生成任务上，特征对齐使训练收敛加速近2倍，并在相同算力预算下提升生成质量，尽管扩散模型规模约为BRepA的20倍。

Conclusion: 几何与拓扑感知的B-Rep预训练为CAD结构的理解与生成提供了强大基础，可作为多下游任务的通用表示骨干。

Abstract: Boundary representation (B-Rep) of CAD models encodes geometric primitives and their relations as a graph structure, enabling efficient shape modeling within CAD software. Learning on B-Reps for downstream tasks, such as segmentation and novel shape generation, requires specialized neural network designs. Moreover, performance improvements and accelerated training can be achieved with effective pretraining strategies, which is particularly important for tasks with limited labeled B-Rep data. In this paper, we introduce BRepA, a unified self-supervised pretraining framework that explicitly reconstructs both the geometry and topology of B-Rep objects via graph-based modeling. By learning from large-scale unlabeled CAD data only once, BRepA produces representations that generalize across tasks and significantly enhance both discriminative and generative learning. On segmentation tasks, BRepA demonstrates remarkable data efficiency. For instance, with only 1% labeled samples, BRepA pretraining improves mIoU by 32.52% compared to models trained from scratch. On generative tasks, aligning diffusion model representations with pretrained BRepA features substantially accelerates convergence, achieving nearly 2 times faster training while improving generation quality under the same computational budget, despite the diffusion model being roughly 20 times larger than BRepA. Overall, our results show that geometry- and topology-aware B-Rep pretraining provides a powerful foundation for both understanding and generating CAD structures, serving as a versatile representation backbone for diverse downstream applications. We will release the code to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [172] [Fiberpose: state-of-the-art fiber morphometry and instance segmentation with a distance-transform neural network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22953)
*Viktor A. Balashov,Daniil S. Antonenko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Fiberpose将SEM纤维分析重构为平滑距离变换(DT)场的直接回归，并配套几何算法实现实例重建及直径、取向测量，在分割精度与直径测量上优于SAM2、Cellpose-SAM、DiameterJ等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纤维量化方法多依赖经典图像二值化加简单后处理，在弱边界、对比度不均、纤维密集的图像中结果不可靠。

Method: 提出Fiberpose框架：把纤维分析表述为平滑距离变换场的直接回归，预测场直接编码纤维几何；并设计一套适配该表示的几何算法，实现实例重建与直径、取向测量的一体化流程；以独立Python桌面应用及网页工具(https://fiberpose.org)形式发布。

Result: 在独立纤维SEM图像数据集上，实例分割精度达到SOTA，优于SAM2与Cellpose-SAM；直径测量精度优于DiameterJ、Fibar和GIFT；在密集及弱对比区域依然有效，并在训练数据未覆盖的显微模态上展现定性泛化能力。

Conclusion: Fiberpose提供了可解释的几何表示与自动化流程，可实现纤维材料可重复的定量表征。

Abstract: Analysis of fibrous structures in SEM images is a routine task in biomedical engineering and materials science. However, most available methods for fiber quantification still rely on classical image binarization followed by simple post-processing. These approaches often produce unreliable results in images with weak boundaries, heterogeneous contrast, and densely packed fibers. Here, we introduce Fiberpose, a framework that reformulates fiber analysis as direct regression of a smooth distance-transform (DT) field, together with a novel set of geometry-based algorithms specifically adapted to this representation. The predicted field directly encodes fiber geometry, while the accompanying algorithms enable instance reconstruction and measurement of diameter and orientation within a unified workflow. The framework is implemented as a standalone Python desktop application and a web tool https://fiberpose.org. On an independent dataset of fibrous SEM images, Fiberpose achieved state-of-the-art instance-segmentation accuracy in comparison with SAM2 and Cellpose-SAM, and more accurate diameter measurements than DiameterJ, Fibar and GIFT. The method remains effective in dense and weakly contrasted regions and shows qualitative generalization across microscopy modalities not represented in the training data. Fiberpose therefore provides an interpretable geometric representation and an automated pipeline for reproducible quantitative characterization of fibrous materials.

</details>


### [173] [Any2Building: Unifying Point Clouds and Images for Building Reconstruction via Shared Spatial Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23187)
*Hongye Hou,Zhuoxiao Li,Liu Xiangyu,Tengxi Wang,Jing Ou,Dongli Wu,Shuai Zhang,Tongyan Hua,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Any2Building统一框架，将部分点云与俯视图像在统一3D网格中联合表达，实现单模态或双模态输入的LoD2建筑重建，并构建百万级数据集BuildingWorld-Image。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分点云与俯视图像分别提供不完整的三维表面测量和关于建筑轮廓、屋顶几何及屋顶结构的视觉线索，二者互补，因而需要一个能在3D中统一对齐几何与视觉线索、并支持任意模态组合输入的框架。

Method: 核心为occupancy-grounded shared spatial queries表示：将可用观测组织到公共3D网格中，以完整建筑表面体素作为监督，把观测证据与目标几何关联，使共享生成器跨任务学习结构先验并为缺失结构恢复提供空间引导；随后经体素条件表面生成与低多边形重网格化流程输出紧凑LoD2模型。同时构建BuildingWorld-Image数据集（约100万对样本，覆盖18个城市）。

Result: 在多基准实验中，Any2Building在单模态与多模态点云补全、单图像建筑生成及先验辅助生成任务上的几何重建精度均优于现有方法，并在点云模态退化时保持细节与稳健恢复能力。

Conclusion: 几何与视觉线索在3D空间中的统一对齐可有效支撑多模态建筑LoD2重建，单一共享表示与生成器即可覆盖多种输入组合并取得领先性能。

Abstract: Abstracting structured buildings from limited observations is essential for 3D urban modeling and digital twins. Partial point clouds and overhead images of the same building provide incomplete 3D surface measurements and visual cues to its footprint, roof geometry, and rooftop structures, respectively. This motivates a unified framework that aligns geometric and visual cues in 3D and reconstructs buildings from either or both modalities. Building on this connection, we present Any2Building, a unified model for Level of Detail 2 (LoD2) building reconstruction from partial point clouds, overhead images, or both, with optional coarse geometric priors. At its core is an occupancy-grounded shared spatial queries representation that organizes available observations within a common 3D grid. Supervised by complete building surface voxels, these queries connect observational evidence to target geometry, enabling a shared generator to learn structural priors across tasks and providing spatial guidance for recovering missing structure. A trained voxel conditional surface-generation and low-poly remeshing pipeline then converts the predicted structure into compact LoD2 building models. To support this framework, we introduce BuildingWorld-Image, comprising approximately one million paired samples of partial point clouds, overhead images, and LoD2 building models across 18 cities. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that Any2Building outperforms existing methods in geometric reconstruction accuracy across unimodal and multimodal point-cloud completion, single-image building generation, and prior-assisted generation, while maintaining detailed and robust recovery when point cloud input modality is degraded.

</details>


### [174] [SWIR-cGAN: Synthesizing SWIR Bands Using VNIR Imagery from CBERS and AMAZONIA-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22922)
*Alisson Cleiton de Oliveira,Rennan de Freitas Bezerra Marujo,Thales Sehn Körting*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SWIR-cGAN，用注意力增强U-net生成器的条件GAN与多损失目标，从CBERS-4/4A与AMAZONIA-1的WFI可见光-近红外影像合成Sentinel-2/MSI的SWIR-1与SWIR-2波段，SSIM分别达0.900与0.917，优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西CBERS-4、CBERS-4A与AMAZONIA-1卫星通过WFI传感器提供1-3天高时间分辨率的VNIR影像，但缺少SWIR波段；现有深度学习光谱合成方法在跨卫星泛化和光谱距离较远的波段上仍存在困难。

Method: 构建条件生成对抗网络（cGAN），生成器为注意力增强的U-net，采用多损失目标函数，以WFI的VNIR输入合成Sentinel-2/MSI的SWIR-1（B11）和SWIR-2（B12）；数据划分为4,125个训练/交叉验证patch与1,026个未见测试patch，超参数在训练子集上优化，各基线模型在测试前单独调优。

Result: SWIR-1：SSIM 0.900、PSNR 31.44 dB、TOA反射率MAE 0.019；SWIR-2：SSIM 0.917、PSNR 33.35 dB、MAE 0.015。SWIR-cGAN在测试集上优于所有基线，尤其对SWIR-2；消融实验表明多损失是主要性能贡献者，注意力与残差模块带来额外提升。

Conclusion: 结果证明从WFI影像合成SWIR波段具有可行性，尤其在光谱较近的SWIR-2上表现更好；模型已开源（https://github.com/marujore/swir-cgan）。

Abstract: Spectral band synthesis is an important research topic in remote sensing. The Brazilian satellites CBERS-4, CBERS-4A, and AMAZONIA-1 provide high temporal resolution (1-3 days) visible and near-infrared (VNIR) imagery through the Wide-Field Imager (WFI), but lack short-wave infrared (SWIR) bands. Recent deep learning methods have shown promising results for spectral synthesis, but challenges remain for cross-satellite generalization and spectrally distant bands. This paper presents a conditional Generative Adversarial Network (cGAN) with an attention-enhanced U-net generator and a multi-loss objective to synthesize Sentinel-2/MSI SWIR bands 11 (SWIR-1) and 12 (SWIR-2) from WFI VNIR inputs. Experiments used 4,125 patches for training and cross-validation and 1,026 unseen patches for testing, with hyperparameters optimized using a subset of the training data. On SWIR-1, the model achieved SSIM of 0.900, PSNR of 31.44 dB, and MAE of 0.019 in TOA reflectance. On SWIR-2, it achieved SSIM of 0.917, PSNR of 33.35 dB, and MAE of 0.015. Benchmarking was performed on the test dataset, with each model optimized separately prior to evaluation. SWIR-cGAN outperformed the baselines, particularly for SWIR-2. The ablation study identified the multi-loss as the main performance contributor, while the attention and residual modules provided additional improvements. Our results demonstrate the feasibility of synthesizing SWIR bands from WFI imagery. The SWIR-cGAN is available at https://github.com/marujore/swir-cgan.

</details>


### [175] [LOCUS: Causal Support Windows for Attribute Control in Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22867)
*Zijun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出因果支持剖面分析自回归图像生成中各图像token位置对属性干预的响应差异，并据此提出LOCUS方法，将测试时潜变量优化路由到对应属性的支持窗口，显著提升多属性约束满足率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一自回归图像生成器难以在单一提示中同时满足多个属性约束。虽然属性信息可从图像token潜变量中解码，但测试时直接编辑这些潜变量困难：在prefix上叠加固定属性方向只能强化已有属性，难以覆盖与之冲突的属性（如提示为蓝色车时改为红色）。作者因此探究“干预效果如何依赖于其在图像token序列中的作用位置”。

Method: 1) 提出“因果支持剖面”（causal support profile）：对每个属性，统计在给定窗口内对图像token状态做patching时翻转渲染属性的频率，刻画位置敏感性；2) 进行预算匹配的早窗口与中窗口对比，并构建二维支持图，验证响应区域与空间内容对应；3) 基于剖面提出LOCUS（Latent Optimization in Causal Support windows）：先诊断哪个属性失败，再把测试时潜变量优化路由到该属性对应的支持窗口。

Result: 结构属性（计数、位置）在早期窄窗口最敏感，外观属性（颜色、尺寸、材质）在后期更宽窗口响应；窗口位置效应依赖目标属性。在生成器、优化器、奖励、验证器与token预算固定下，支持路由窗口在GenEval++宏平均准确率上比晚窗口高23.6个百分点。冻结生成器时，LOCUS将Janus-Pro的GenEval++准确率由24.6%提升至46.8%，多属性标签由19.5%提升至40.5%；在GenEval上单次rollout修复48.1%的失败生成（prefix优化为37.4%），平均所需轮数少14%。

Conclusion: 干预位置对自回归图像生成属性控制至关重要且具属性依赖性，依据因果支持剖面进行支持窗口路由的潜变量优化，是提升多属性约束满足的高效、即插即用策略。

Abstract: Unified autoregressive (AR) image generators struggle to satisfy multiple attribute constraints in a single prompt. Although these attributes are decodable from image-token latents, editing those latents at test time remains difficult. Adding a fixed attribute direction to the prefix can reinforce a requested attribute but rarely overrides a conflicting one, such as turning a car red when the prompt specifies blue. We investigate how the effect of an intervention depends on where it acts in the image-token sequence. We introduce the causal support profile, which measures, for each attribute, how often patching image-token states within a given window flips the rendered attribute. Structural attributes (count and position) are most responsive in early, narrow windows, while appearance attributes (color, size, and material) respond over later, broader windows. A budget-matched comparison of early and middle windows shows that the effect of window location depends on the target attribute, and two-dimensional support maps show that the responsive regions track spatial content. Based on these profiles, we propose LOCUS (Latent Optimization in Causal Support windows), which diagnoses which attribute failed and routes test-time latent optimization to its corresponding support window. With the generator, optimizer, reward, verifier, and token budget held fixed, support-routed windows outperform late windows by 23.6 percentage points in GenEval++ macro accuracy. With the generator frozen, LOCUS raises Janus-Pro's GenEval++ accuracy from 24.6% to 46.8% overall and from 19.5% to 40.5% on multi-attribute tags. On GenEval, LOCUS repairs 48.1% of failed generations in one rollout, compared with 37.4% for prefix optimization, and needs 14% fewer rounds on average to succeed.

</details>


### [176] [The Rhythm of Generated Video: Latent Chunking Leaves Periodic Traces in Compressed Bitstreams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22996)
*Chen Ye Gan,Bryan Zhen Zhang,Gene Wen,Yuxing han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出无需解码像素即可区分AI生成视频与真实视频的方法：因果视频自编码器的时间步长会在压缩比特流中留下架构特有的周期性"脉冲"（CogVideoX/Wan2.2为4帧、LTX-Video为8帧），据此用无训练的峰值比统计量实现检测与生成器溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成视频检测器主要分析解码后的像素，忽略了视频的生成与编码过程本身携带的信息；作者希望利用生成架构在码流层面留下的可测量痕迹，实现更低成本、更具泛化性的检测与溯源。

Method: 从机理出发：因果视频自编码器的时间步长将不可预测内容集中于潜变量分块（latent-chunk）边界，使率失真优化编码器周期性分配比特。作者在不解码像素的比特流上直接观测帧大小轨迹，提出无训练的峰值比（peak-ratio）统计量作为检测特征，并在多种量化设置及H.264/H.265/AV1编码下验证。

Result: 观测到CogVideoX与Wan2.2存在4帧周期脉冲、LTX-Video为8帧，且内容匹配的相机实拍视频中不存在；仅将实拍视频通过生成器自编码器即可诱发该脉冲，支持其成因解释。该统计量区分生成视频与实拍视频的AUC为0.84–0.94，并在测试的量化设置与H.264/H.265编码下保持有效；帧大小轨迹可识别4个商业生成器，在H.264、H.265、AV1下准确率分别为89.2%、84.1%、83.0%，优于基于像素的UnivFD基线（72.7–73.6%）。

Conclusion: 研究直接将生成架构与可测量的编码需求联系起来，表明码流层面的周期性比特分配痕迹可作为轻量、免训练、跨编码格式鲁棒的AI生成视频检测与溯源依据。

Abstract: Most AI-generated video detectors analyze decoded pixels and overlook how videos are produced. We show that causal video autoencoders leave an architecture-specific rhythm in compressed bitstreams: their temporal stride concentrates unpredictable content at latent-chunk boundaries, so rate-distortion optimized encoders allocate bits periodically. Without decoding pixels, we observe four-frame pulses in CogVideoX and Wan2.2 and eight-frame pulses in LTX-Video, which are absent from content-matched camera footage. Passing camera footage through a generator's autoencoder alone induces the pulse, supporting its proposed origin. A training-free peak-ratio statistic separates generated from camera video (AUC 0.84-0.94) and persists across tested quantization settings and H.264/H.265 encoding. Frame-size traces also identify four commercial generators, with 89.2%, 84.1%, and 83.0% accuracy under H.264, H.265, and AV1, beating the pixel-based UnivFD baseline (72.7-73.6%). Our results directly link generative architecture to measurable coding demand.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Cross-cultural Adaptation of Recipes for Low-Resource Languages: A Benchmark and Multilingual Culinary Lexicon for Hausa, Yorùbá, and Igbo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22708)
*Nuhu Ibrahim,Maryam Abdulazeez,Fatsuma Jauro,Salisu Aliyu,Robert Stevens,Riza Batista-Navarro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ReciLoReL基准（600条约鲁巴/豪萨/伊博语专家翻译并文化适配的食谱）与ReciLex多语烹饪词库（约1.5万实体，10类），评估主流多语LLM英→低资源语言食谱翻译，并验证词库增强可在无需目标语料微调的前提下提升文化适配性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语LLM虽提升机器翻译，但低资源语言的文化适配翻译仍欠缺研究；食谱中的食材、厨具、计量与流程需按目标文化语境改写，现有资源与评价标准不足。

Method: 构建ReciLoReL（600条专家翻译+文化适配食谱，覆盖尼日利亚三种主要语言：约鲁巴语、豪萨语、伊博语）与ReciLex（约15,000条翻译实体、10类食谱实体：食材、工具、厨师动作、工具状态、时长等）；在零样本与少样本设置下评测先进多语LLM的英→低资源语言食谱翻译；以ReciLex增强LLM（检索/词库提示式增强，不依赖大规模目标语食谱语料训练或微调）；评价上除BLEU、chrF、TER等参考指标外，提出源自跨文化适配方法的五项文化适配评价准则，并辅以人工评估。

Result: ReciLex增强使平均BLEU由13.57升至14.51，chrF由37.89升至38.74，TER由77.14降至75.12；人工评估显示五项文化适配准则在5分制上分别提升0.11–0.36分。

Conclusion: 该工作为低资源场景下的文化适配食谱翻译提供了新资源（基准与词库）、评价准则与实证结果，表明轻量词库增强即可在缺乏目标语平行语料时改善文化适配翻译质量。

Abstract: Recent multilingual large language models (LLMs) have improved machine translation, yet culturally adaptive translation for low-resource languages (LRLs) remains underexplored. This is particularly challenging for recipes, where ingredients, cooking tools, measurements, and procedures may require adaptation to the target cultural context. In this work, we introduce ReciLoReL, a benchmark of 600 expert-translated and culturally adapted recipes across Yorùbá, Hausa, and Igbo (three of the most prominent languages in Nigeria), and ReciLex, a multilingual culinary lexicon containing approximately 15,000 translated entities across ten recipe entity categories, including ingredients, tools, chef actions, state of tools, and durations. We evaluate state-of-the-art multilingual LLMs for English-to-LRL recipe translation under zero-shot and few-shot settings and investigate whether augmenting LLMs with ReciLex improves culturally adaptive recipe translation without requiring large target-language recipe corpora for training or fine-tuning. Beyond conventional reference-based translation evaluation using BLEU, chrF, and TER, we introduce five criteria for evaluating cultural adaptation, adapted from established cross-cultural adaptation methods. Across the selected LLMs and languages, ReciLex improves average BLEU from 13.57 to 14.51 and chrF from 37.89 to 38.74, while reducing TER from 77.14 to 75.12. Human evaluation further shows improvements ranging from 0.11 to 0.36 points across the five cultural adaptation evaluation criteria on a five-point scale. Overall, our work provides new resources, evaluation criteria, and empirical evaluation for studying culturally adaptive recipe translation in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [178] [RLDE: A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Interpretable Iterative Document Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22039)
*Nikhil Parkar,M. Marimuthu,Riya Joshi,Karthikeyan N*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RLDE：将长文档修订建模为无参考MDP，用Pointer Network做动态span选择、冻结BART-base生成建议、PPO优化可读性/困惑度/语法多目标奖励并加SBERT语义保持惩罚的端到端强化学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档的质量、清晰度与流畅性改进困难；人类编辑是迭代式精修而非单次重写。现有自动化方法多为黑箱生成或依赖监督学习，可解释性差，且未刻画修订的序列性。

Method: 提出RLDE框架：将文档修订表述为无参考（无需成对文档-修订目标）的MDP；动作空间限定为NOOP/INSERT/DELETE/REPLACE；用Pointer Network在token级动态选择span，生成动作交由冻结的BART-base产生上下文感知建议；PPO智能体优化基于可读性、困惑度、语法指标的多目标奖励，并引入SBERT语义保持惩罚防止删除退化。

Result: 在BEA-2019上所有指标均显著提升（p<0.01）：Flesch阅读易读度49.8→59.4，困惑度63.7→39.8，保留93%语义信息；相较GPT-4、LLaMA-2等CoT提示工程模型，以极低推理算力取得高性能；人工评估意义保持4.9/5、编辑质量4.4/5。

Conclusion: RLDE表明以度量驱动、强化学习方式对文档修订进行序列化建模，可在低推理成本下实现可解释且高质量的编辑，并在自动指标与人工评价上同时验证有效性。

Abstract: Improving the quality, clarity, and flow of long documents remains a challenging problem in NLP, as human editing typically occurs through iterative refinement rather than a single-pass rewrite. Most existing automated systems act as black-box generators or rely on supervised learning, limiting interpretability and failing to capture the sequential nature of revisions. In this paper, we propose RLDE, an automated end-to-end metric-driven RL framework that formulates document revision as a reference-free MDP problem (unsupervised with respect to paired document-revision targets). Working with a limited action space (NOOP, INSERT, DELETE, REPLACE), RLDE leverages Pointer Networks for dynamic span selection at the token level and sends generative actions to the frozen BART-base model to obtain context-aware suggestions. PPO agents learn to optimize a multi-objective reward function based on readability, perplexity, and grammar metrics with the help of a novel SBERT-based Semantic Preservation Penalty constraint to avoid deletion degeneracy. Evaluations on the BEA-2019 dataset showed statistically significant results (p < 0.01) across all metrics, where Flesch Reading Ease increased from 49.8 to 59.4, perplexity decreased from 63.7 to 39.8, while preserving 93% of semantic information. When compared to Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompt-engineered models like GPT-4 and LLaMA-2, RLDE shows high performance at a very low computational inference cost. Moreover, the quality of edits generated by RLDE has been verified via human evaluations, scoring 4.9/5 for meaning preservation and 4.4/5 for edit quality.

</details>


### [179] [RAW: Refusal Aversion in the Wild, A Causal Measurement Method for Deployed LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21641)
*Pavel Kireyev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RAW方法，利用LLM自身随机性作为自然实验，从日志中因果估计"拒绝"对用户后续参与度的代价；在15个部署模型上约半数可精确测量者，拒绝导致后续再参与度下降3–17个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM每天处理数亿对话并拒绝相当比例请求，但"拒绝"的用户侧代价（用户是否流失及幅度）在生产模型上从未被测量，构成面向消费者平台的盲区；对前沿实验室产品做拒绝的A/B测试在伦理与操作上受限，第三方平台通过固定API部署更是无法进行。

Method: RAW方法：以LLM输出的随机性作为自然实验。在任意已有日志中，近似相同的prompt下同一模型有时拒绝、有时不拒绝，该变异可识别拒绝的因果用户侧效应。应用于LMSYS-Chat-1M与WildChat中的15个部署模型，采用按用户聚类的推断；成本约每1000条对话$0.30，仅依赖日志。

Result: 在约半数可精确测量的模型中，一次拒绝导致后续再参与度下降3–17个百分点；其余模型无可分辨效应，其中一个部署的正向点估计被追溯为长期用户重试被拒请求所致。发布标签、审核分数、prompt嵌入与流水线代码。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是测量方法本身：可从日志对任意部署模型估计拒绝的因果用户成本，无需A/B测试；拒绝的用户流失代价在部分模型上显著且量级可观。

Abstract: Deployed LLMs field hundreds of millions of conversations a day and refuse a non-trivial fraction of user requests, yet the user-side cost of refusal, whether users disengage and by how much, is unmeasured on any production model: a blind spot for the consumer-facing platforms built on them. A/B-testing refusal is ethically and operationally constrained on frontier-lab products and unavailable to third-party platforms deploying these models through a fixed API. We introduce RAW, a method that uses LLM stochasticity as a natural experiment: at near-identical prompts in any existing log, the same model sometimes refuses and sometimes does not, and that variation identifies the causal user-side effect of refusal. Applying the method to 15 deployed LLMs in LMSYS-Chat-1M and WildChat, with user-clustered inference, we find that for about half of the models we can measure precisely, a refusal costs 3 to 17 pp of subsequent re-engagement; the rest show no distinguishable effect, among them one deployment whose positive point estimate we trace to persistent users retrying the refused request. Our contribution is the measurement method, which runs on any deployed model from logs alone for about \$0.30 per 1,000 conversations. We release labels, moderation scores, prompt embeddings, and pipeline code.

</details>


### [180] [Consistency Is Not Correction: Separating Confidence, Abstention, and Recovery in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21651)
*Khushal Sethi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出置信信号的"排序能力(判别)、选择性预测、纠错效用"是三个互不蕴含的目标，给出纠错效用的精确分解(门控收益−门控损害)并导出实验前可算的可达上界：仅替换提案的门控只能在基策略已错处发力，初始错误数封顶不一致对数。两个模型尺度上的实测显示该界收紧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制器(重采样、升级到大模型、弃答)通常以单一置信信号为依据，但"能排序""能选择性弃答""能改对答案"三者在逻辑上互不推出，现有文献常混用同一指标或做无统计分辨力的对比实验，导致结论不可复核。

Method: 1) 对纠错效用做精确分解：门控恢复(gated recoveries)减去门控损害(gated harms)；2) 由分解导出事前上界：正确门控能产生的不一致对数量受基策略初始错误数约束，低于配对检验所需样本量则实验不可判；3) 在两个模型尺度、小模型套件、840 回合受控研究(60 个 GSM8K 决策、六个控制器)及诊断集上分别测量判别(AUROC)、选择性预测与纠错效用；4) 报告两项预算前可执行的检查，并考察排名并列处理、并列选择阈值、不等输出长度上限对头条数字的影响。

Result: 小模型套件中扰动一致性验证器取得策略内 AUROC 0.990(95% 区间 [0.95, 1.00])，但所有回合结果与单样本解码完全一致，调用量达 4.32 倍；840 回合研究中无任何验证器在 60 个 GSM8K 决策上替换提案，比较为空；六个控制器的全部 GSM8K 增益仅来自一个任务，且该任务恰是温度零重复结果不一致、服务不可复现的样本；诊断集上门控至多触及 4 个错误而检验需 6 个，即使完美门控也无法得到显著结果。

Conclusion: 高判别力可完全伴随零纠错效用；该领域当前的瓶颈是测量分辨力而非信号选择。作者建议实验前用错误数上界与所需不一致对数做可行性核算，并统一并列处理、阈值与输出长度上限等评测细节。

Abstract: A controller that resamples an answer, escalates to a larger model, or abstains is usually justified by a confidence signal. Three claims about such a signal do not follow from one another: it ranks answers (discrimination), it supports withholding some of them (selective prediction), and it changes an answer for the better (correction utility). We give an exact decomposition of the third into gated recoveries minus gated harms, which bounds whether a comparison can succeed before it is run: a gate that only replaces a proposal can act only where the base policy was already wrong, so the initial error count caps the discordant pairs any correct gate can produce. When that cap falls below the count a paired test needs, the experiment cannot return a positive result for a working mechanism, nor distinguish one from a gate that never fired. We measure all three targets on two model scales and find the bound binds. In a small-model suite a perturbation-agreement verifier reaches within-policy AUROC 0.990 (95% interval [0.95, 1.00]) while every episode outcome matches single-sample decoding at 4.32 times the call count. In a controlled 840-episode study no verifier replaced the proposal on any of the 60 GSM8K decisions, so that comparison tested nothing; the entire GSM8K gain across six controllers is one task, and it is the task whose identical temperature-zero repeats disagreed, making it the one answer the service does not reproduce. On the diagnostic set the gate could reach four errors while the test needed six, so even a perfect gate had no significant result available. We report two checks that would have caught this before any budget was spent, and show that tie handling in rankings, tied selection cutoffs, and unequal output limits each move a headline number in the same dataset. The binding constraint on this literature is measurement resolution, not the choice of signal.

</details>


### [181] [SimAI: Mechanistic User Simulation through Psychological Network Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21808)
*Cheng Li,Adhyyan Narang,Jiayi Yuan,John F. Buss,Jina Suh,Devon Sandel-Fernandez,Yulia Tsvetkov,Adam M. Kuczynski,Tim Althoff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SimAI：一种基于心理机制的"机理式用户模拟"框架，用图重构目标监督开放式行为，并构建PSYCHE数据集（18,576个profile-network对，来自516个心理治疗案例），实现更真实、多样且具临床训练价值的用户模拟，并揭示长时交互中的安全失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中直接开展人类交互实验成本高或可能带来伤害，因此需要用户模拟器。但现有模拟器 largely 忽视驱动用户行为的心理机制，缺乏对心理因素如何交互塑造用户反应的建模。

Method: 提出SimAI框架，对心理因素间的交互及其对用户响应的影响进行机理建模；针对开放行为难以监督的问题，设计图重构目标，依据模拟对话对底层心理关系的反映程度提供图级别奖励；并构建PSYCHE数据集（516个心理治疗案例派生的18,576个profile-network对）。

Result: 自动评测显示SimAI在模拟多样性与行为多样性上优于现有患者模拟器，同时保持较强的profile对齐；30名心理健康专业人士与受训者的交互式研究中，SimAI在对话真实性、真实求助行为、临床训练实用性三项上平均评分最高；此外可支撑真实临床训练，并暴露AI与脆弱用户长时交互中的安全失效。

Conclusion: 将心理机制显式纳入用户模拟并以图级重构奖励监督，可显著提升模拟真实性与多样性；该框架既服务临床训练，也可作为探测长时交互安全隐患的诊断工具。代码已开源于github.com/behavioral-data/ANGEL-UserSim。

Abstract: Realistic user simulation enables the study of human interactions when direct experimentation is costly or potentially harmful. However, existing simulators largely overlook the psychological mechanisms that drive user behavior. We introduce SimAI, a framework for mechanistic user simulation that models how psychological factors interact to shape user responses. To overcome the challenge of supervising open-ended behavior, we propose a graph reconstruction objective that provides graph-level rewards based on how well simulated conversations reflect the underlying psychological relationships. To support this approach, we construct PSYCHE, a dataset of 18,576 profile-network pairs derived from 516 psychotherapy cases. Automatic evaluations show that SimAI achieves greater simulation and behavioral diversity than existing patient simulators while maintaining strong profile alignment. In an interactive study with 30 mental-health professionals and trainees, SimAI receives the highest average ratings for conversational authenticity, realistic help-seeking behavior, and clinical training utility. Beyond simulation quality, SimAI enables realistic clinical training and uncovers safety failures in prolonged AI interactions with vulnerable users. Code is available at https://github.com/behavioral-data/ANGEL-UserSim.

</details>


### [182] [DiagTrace: An Interactive Benchmark for Clinical Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22009)
*Yufan Wang,Rui Yang,Yi Lin,Chunhua Weng,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DiagTrace，一个基于信息源（PubMed Central 病例报告）的基准，用于评估大语言模型能否通过交互式信息寻求重建文献中记录的诊断轨迹，涵盖 8 个专科共 510 例病例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学问答与诊断推理评测通常一次性提供全部患者信息，且仅评估最终诊断或推理依据，忽略了真实诊断中需在不完整信息下逐步获取证据的过程。

Method: 每个实例由 PubMed Central 病例报告构建，包含初始表现、事件库、临床病程与诊断参考；从中选取 225 例，对 4 个 LLM 在临床病程对齐度、交互质量与效率三方面进行评估。

Result: 实验揭示了模型在证据覆盖度、与来源一致的信息获取顺序以及对话轮次使用上的权衡（trade-offs）。

Conclusion: DiagTrace 将评测从"一次性信息输入+最终诊断"转向"交互式证据获取+轨迹重建"，为评估 LLM 的增量诊断推理能力提供了可溯源、可对齐的基准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on medical question answering and diagnostic reasoning benchmarks. However, existing evaluations typically provide all patient information upfront and assess only the final diagnosis or rationale. This setup ignores the need to acquire diagnostic evidence incrementally from incomplete information in real-world diagnosis. To bridge this gap, we introduce DiagTrace, a source-grounded benchmark for evaluating whether LLMs can reconstruct the documented diagnostic trajectory through interactive information seeking. Each benchmark instance is built from a PubMed Central case report and includes an initial presentation, an event bank, a clinical course, and diagnostic references. DiagTrace contains 510 cases across eight specialties, with 225 used to evaluate four LLMs on clinical-course alignment, interaction quality, and efficiency. Results reveal trade-offs in coverage, source-consistent ordering, and turn use.

</details>


### [183] [Structure--Residual Inductive Biases for Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23197)
*Hai Huang,Bartosz Żuk,Rassul Magauin,Jiaxin Huang,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"结构—残差"原则，将表示分解为结构部分与实现细节，并以边界瓶颈概念(BBC)和连续概念(CC)两种方式实例化为显式归纳偏置，在GPT-2 Medium预训练中降低NLL并呈现互补的深度偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型仅依赖下一词预测来同时学习预测行为与内部表示，表示结构本身并非显式归纳偏置。作者受Kolmogorov复杂度的两部分编码思想启发，探索是否应将"结构—残差"分离作为显式先验。

Method: 基于两部分编码视角提出结构—残差原则，并以两种互补方式实现：BBC让token片段的信息经由边界载体路由，同时保留局部自回归计算；CC对表示几何施加正则，诱导出低频结构/高频残差的谱分解。作者推导了两者的编码界，并证明当片段内路径能量趋零时，多重性校正的边界注意力趋于精确。

Result: BBC与CC表现出显著不同的深度偏好：BBC在浅层最有效，CC在深层最有效。在300M训练token下，二者结合使GPT-2 Medium的NLL改善1.44%，且该增益在扩展至20B token的训练中依然保持。表示诊断显示BBC提升了边界状态的相对预测重要性，CC降低了谱残差能量。

Conclusion: 结构—残差归纳偏置可超越单纯的下一词预测，改进语言模型预训练，为表示设计提供新方向。

Abstract: Next-token prediction is relied upon to learn both predictive behavior and internal representations. We ask whether representation structure itself should be an explicit inductive bias. Motivated by the two-part coding view of Kolmogorov complexity, we study a structure--residual principle in which a representation separates structure from realization detail. We instantiate this principle in two complementary ways. Boundary Bottleneck Concept (BBC) routes information from token segments through boundary carriers while preserving local autoregressive computation. Continuous Concept (CC) regularizes representation geometry and induces a spectral decomposition into low-frequency structure and high-frequency residual. We derive coding bounds for both views and show that multiplicity-corrected boundary attention becomes exact as the within-segment path energy vanishes. Empirically, BBC and CC exhibit strikingly different depth preferences: BBC works best in early layers, whereas CC is most effective in later layers. At 300M training tokens, combining BBC and CC improves NLL by 1.44\% on GPT-2 Medium. These gains persist with training runs up to 20B tokens. Representation diagnostics show that BBC increases the relative predictive importance of boundary states, while CC reduces spectral residual energy. Together, these results suggest that structure--residual biases can improve language-model pretraining beyond next-token prediction. Code: https://github.com/haihuang-ml/structure-residual

</details>


### [184] [Runtime Verification of Multiple Natural Language Criteria for Agent Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23074)
*Silviu Pitis,Parand A. Alamdari,Jessica Tang,Toryn Q. Klassen,Sheila A. McIlraith*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出树注意力验证器VFM，可并行对N条自然语言准则给出满足/违反/不适用的三元判定，并在CriteriaBank（394K样本）上预训练，实现高效运行时验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体受数百条自然语言准则约束，但现有验证器无法高效地针对大规模准则集合评估输出，并报告每条准则是满足、违反还是不适用。

Method: VFM为树注意力验证器，编码共享上下文与目标文本，并行对N条准则打分并输出独立的逐准则三元裁决；在CriteriaBank语料（394K条context-target-criterion-verdict元组，覆盖321K+条不同准则字符串）上进行预训练。

Result: 微调后的VFM-4B在合成治理基准上优于GPT-5.4；在DynaBench多准则基准上，轨迹级失败检测优于DynaGuard-4B，且逐准则裁决可定位违规规则并支持聚焦升级至更大的思维链评判器。单张H100上，4K token上下文下评估1000条准则耗时<2s、显存<11GB。

Conclusion: CriteriaBank预训练提升跨域迁移能力，尤其在低数据条件下，全量数据时与强直接微调基线持平；VFM兼具精度与效率，适合运行时验证。

Abstract: AI agents are governed by hundreds of natural language criteria, yet existing verifiers cannot efficiently evaluate an agent's output against a large set of such criteria and report which are satisfied, violated, or inapplicable. We introduce the VFM, a tree-attention verifier that encodes the shared context and target text, and scores N natural language criteria in parallel, returning independent per-criterion ternary verdicts. The VFM is pretrained on CriteriaBank, a corpus of 394K (context, target, criterion, verdict) tuples spanning over 321K distinct criterion strings. A finetuned VFM-4B outperforms GPT-5.4 on a synthetic governance benchmark. On the DynaBench multi-criterion benchmark, it outperforms DynaGuard-4B on trace-level failure detection, and its per-criterion verdicts localize the violated rule and support focused escalation to a larger chain-of-thought judge. We show that CriteriaBank pretraining improves cross-domain transfer, particularly in the low-data regime, while matching a strong direct-finetuning control at full data. On a single H100, VFM-4B evaluates 1,000 criteria against a 4K-token context in under 2s at <11 GB VRAM, making it suitable for runtime verification.

</details>


### [185] [Large Language Models Have Not Yet Transformed Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22975)
*Ron Bekkerman,Nichole Li,Bahareh Kargar,Mark Somers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过250万篇全文论文的多维度历时分析发现，2022年底LLM出现后，多数既有科学趋势延续、可检测的断点有限，仅少数维度（如亚洲CS合作团队规模、生物医学论文格式标准化）出现显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2022年末LLM兴起引发对科学未来的担忧，但评估其影响需先厘清科学实践本身已如何演变；同时需区分真实科学变化与测量/格式因素造成的伪变化。

Method: 对2015–2025年五大学科250万篇论文全文做大规模计量分析，沿词汇、结构、认识论、社会四类维度构建指标并追踪时间趋势，检验2023年前后是否存在趋势断点（breakpoint检测）。

Result: 除部分词汇频率变化外，2023年前的趋势大多延续，可检测断点有限；显著例外包括亚洲地区计算机科学合作者团队规模增长明显加速，以及生物医学主题多样性表面下降主要源于论文格式标准化而非主题萎缩。

Conclusion: 研究为在LLM深入应用背景下持续监测全球科学健康度提供了实证基线，并提示需将格式/测量效应与实质科学变化区分开来。

Abstract: The emergence of large language models (LLMs) in late 2022 has raised questions about the future of science, but assessing their impact requires understanding how scientific practices were already evolving. We analyze 2.5 million full-text papers across five disciplines from 2015 to 2025, tracing trends over a broad spectrum of lexical, structural, epistemological, and social dimensions. Aside from frequency shifts in some words, most pre-2023 trends continued with limited detectable breaks. Among the notable exceptions, growth in computer science coauthor teams accelerated significantly in Asia, while apparent declines in biomedical topic diversity largely reflected standardization in article formats. Our study establishes an empirical foundation for monitoring the health of global science as LLM adoption deepens.

</details>


### [186] [JET: Just-Enough Exploration Tuning for Efficient Tool-Calling Agents in Repository-Level Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22539)
*Yoonho Choi,Taehyeon Han,Seonghyeon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现仓库级QA的LLM智能体存在"过度探索"现象（信息已足够仍继续调用工具），提出JET微调方法，通过截断轨迹、插入理由并加权训练来训练智能体"信息足够即作答"，在减少工具调用11-33%的同时基本保持分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级问答中，智能体应在收集足够信息后停止探索并作答，但研究观察发现LLM智能体常常在信息已充分时仍继续调用工具（过度探索）。此外，现有基于专家轨迹的监督微调（SFT）虽能提升任务表现，却会把轨迹中的过度探索行为一并传播给智能体，加剧该问题。

Method: 提出Just-Enough Exploration Tuning（JET）微调方法：将每条训练轨迹在信息变得充分的步骤处截断，插入一段解释"为何在此步即可作答"的理由（rationale），并在微调时对该理由部分施加更高的损失权重。

Result: 在两个仓库级QA基准、两个8B开源权重模型上进行实验：在自生成轨迹上，JET将工具调用减少11-33%，同时基本保持得分；在专家轨迹上，JET比SFT少18-28%的工具调用，并保留SFT的大部分得分增益。两种情况下JET均降低了推理成本。

Conclusion: JET能有效缓解仓库级QA智能体的过度探索问题，在控制工具调用数量与推理成本的同时保持任务性能，优于朴素SFT。

Abstract: In repository-level question answering (QA), an agent explores a code repository with tools and should answer a question once enough information is gathered. Nevertheless, we find that LLM agents often continue to invoke tools even after the gathered information is already sufficient to answer. We call this phenomenon over-exploration. Moreover, even though existing methods improve task performance through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on expert trajectories, SFT also propagates the over-exploration in the trajectories to the agent. To mitigate over-exploration, we propose Just-Enough Exploration Tuning (JET), a fine-tuning method that trains the agent to answer once the gathered information is sufficient. JET truncates each training trajectory at the step where the gathered information becomes sufficient, inserts a rationale that explains why the agent can answer at that step, and fine-tunes the agent with a higher loss weight on the rationale. Experiments on two repository-level QA benchmarks with two open-weight 8B models demonstrate that JET reduces tool calls by 11 to 33% on self-generated trajectories while largely preserving the score. On expert trajectories, JET makes 18 to 28% fewer tool calls than SFT while retaining most of the score gain of SFT. In both cases, JET also lowers the inference cost.

</details>


### [187] [EGS: A Complementary Safety Framework for Educational LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22203)
*Zexuan Fei,Zhengnan Guo,Yiming Luo,Kaiyi Huang,Aimin Zhou,Bo Jiang,Fei Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出教育护栏系统EGS，以查询审核、情景感知教学引导、响应审核三层运行时框架替代二元拒答，在通用与教育基准上提升审核性能、拒答质量与越狱鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在青少年教育场景部署面临有害、偏见、误导或不适龄内容风险；学生提问常含糊或出于好奇心，简单二元拒答无法兼顾安全与教学价值，需要语境化引导。

Method: EGS为轻量、模型无关的运行时框架，与模型内在对齐互补，含三模块：查询审核（按风险等级分流输入）、情景感知教学引导（对灰色地带查询施以类别特定教育干预而非阻断）、响应审核（交付前审计输出）。

Result: 在通用与教育专用基准上，EGS 在多种LLM上均提升审核性能、拒答质量与越狱鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 纵深防御式护栏可在保障安全的同时保留教育LLM所需的教学效用；代码与模型权重将开源以支持社区采用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer strong potential for personalized education, but adolescent-facing deployment raises risks of toxic, biased, misleading, or developmentally inappropriate content. We argue that educational safety should move beyond binary refusal: student queries are often ambiguous or curiosity-driven, requiring contextual guidance rather than simple blocking. We introduce the Educational Guardrail System (EGS), a lightweight, model-agnostic runtime framework that complements intrinsic alignment through query moderation, scenario-aware pedagogical guidance, and response moderation. EGS triages inputs by risk level, redirects gray-zone queries with category-specific educational interventions, and audits outputs before delivery. Experiments across general and education-specific benchmarks show that EGS improves moderation performance, refusal quality, and jailbreak robustness across diverse LLMs. These findings suggest that defense-in-depth guardrails can strengthen safety while preserving the pedagogical utility required in educational LLMs. Our code and model weights will be publicly available to support community adoption.

</details>


### [188] [From Imperfective Paradox to Moorean Absurdity: Single-Chain Reasoning Lock-In and Multi-Candidate Verbalization Across Aspectual and Pragmatic Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22188)
*An-Sheng Jhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文在网络模型 Qwen3-8B 上开展四项预注册实验，考察"单链过早锁定"假说在事件完成性、计划满足与语用评价判断中的可观测后果，发现提示—读出协议（CoT/MCU/MCV/Counterfactual）显著改变完成性与连贯性判断，但内部锁定机制仍属假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言推理与连贯性判断常依赖对事件是否"仅仅发生""达成目的性顶点""满足计划"或"多语用标准下评价"的区分，作者希望检验单链过早锁定假说是否能产生可观测的行为后果，并厘清提示设计与读出方式对判断的影响。

Method: 在 Qwen3-8B-Q8_0 上执行四项预注册密封形式实验（16,640 个正式单元，temperature=0.0），对比 Direct-CoT、NLI-CoT、Multi-Candidate Unweighted (MCU)、Multi-Candidate Weighted (MCW) 与 Multi-Candidate Verbalization (MCV) 等协议；采用实验内 Holm 校正（alpha_fam=0.05）；辅以 1,800 次 OpenAI 消融与 stage="dev" 的 P16.7 跨模型扩展（4,240 计划单元，5 个外部模型，4,222 有效）。

Result: 16 个假说槽中 15 个保持确证地位（12 个在 Holm 校正下拒绝 H0，E3_H3 降为描述性）。实验1：Direct-CoT 与 MCU 在高置信度目的性完成上相差 25.00 pp（Holm p=1.31e-05）；NLI-CoT 在无中断无目的项上崩塌 -91.25 pp（Holm p=1.82e-21）。中文（E2）与英文（E3）在 NLI-CoT 下均出现"中断锚定现实性悖论"。实验4：MCU-CF 对隐性摩尔句连贯性提升 26.64 pp（Holm p=0.0006），Direct-CF−Direct-CoT=29.15 pp，MCU-CoT−Direct-CoT=17.31 pp。跨模型结果显示离散 NLI-CoT 与标量 Direct-CoT 判断发生分离，gpt-4o-mini 在 top_logprobs=20 下取得 1,000/1,000 有效单元。

Conclusion: 确证效应针对的是可观测的提示—读出协议差异，模型内部"过早锁定"仍只是待检验假说；作者公开全部代码、刺激材料、预注册分析计划与验证工具。

Abstract: Natural language entailment and coherence judgments often depend on whether an event merely occurred, reached a telic culmination, satisfied a plan, or was evaluated under multiple pragmatic standards. We test observable consequences of the single-chain premature lock-in hypothesis across four preregistered, sealed formal experiments on Qwen3-8B-Q8_0 (16,640 formal units, temperature=0.0): 15 of 16 hypothesis slots retain confirmatory status (12 rejecting H0 under within-experiment Holm control at alpha_fam=0.05; E3_H3 demoted to descriptive status). In Experiment 1, Direct-CoT vs. MCU differs by 25.00 pp on telic high-confidence completion (Holm p=1.31239e-05), while NLI-CoT collapses by -91.25 pp on uninterrupted atelic items (Holm p=1.81805e-21). Multi-Candidate Verbalization (MCV) elicits K=5 weighted estimates in one call, separating the prompt from unweighted (MCU) and self-weighted (MCW) readouts. Mandarin (E2) and English (E3) expose an Interruption-Anchored Realis Paradox under NLI-CoT. In Experiment 4, MCU-CF raises perceived coherence on implicit Moorean sentences by 26.64 pp over Direct-CoT (Holm p=0.0006), alongside Direct-CF - Direct-CoT = 29.15 pp and MCU-CoT - Direct-CoT = 17.31 pp. A 1,800-call OpenAI ablation and a stage="dev" P16.7 cross-model extension (4,240 planned/collected units across five external models: 4,222 valid, 18 format-missing, 0 transport-missing; <2% missing per slice; OpenAI gpt-4o-mini at top_logprobs=20 achieves 1,000/1,000 valid units) show that discrete NLI-CoT and scalar Direct-CoT judgments dissociate across models. Confirmatory effects concern observable prompt-and-readout protocols; internal premature lock-in remains a hypothesis. All code, stimuli, preregistered analysis plans, and verification tools are available at https://github.com/asj252/llm-judgment-studies.

</details>


### [189] [Disambiguating Moral Circuits and Moral Features in Language Models: Moral content, normative judgment or cue words? Evidence from 14 models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21705)
*Davide Liga,Liuwen Yu,Matteo Magnini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过227组最小对比句、14个开源模型与SAE特征分析，发现改变道德判断的注意力头属于通用的"评价回路"，对各类规范性判断共享同一效果；而SAE特征主要响应"morally"一词。模型隐含表征的是判断类别而非道德本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性方法能定位改变模型道德判断的注意力头与SAE特征，但这些组件究竟响应道德内容、任何规范下的行为评价，还是提示词中的"morally"一词尚不明确，需通过受控对比实验加以区分。

Method: 构建227组短句最小对（经5名标注者验证），两版本分别在道德标准、其他规范（审慎、礼仪、审美、证据）或描述性属性上不同；在14个开源模型（Llama/Gemma/Qwen，1B–14B，base与指令微调）上分别在有/无"morally"条件下测试；结合注意力头消融、SAE特征定位、隐藏状态线性探针与方向消融，并在ETHICS基准400个场景上验证。

Result: 道德答案相关头对道德与其它规范对的效应占比相同（有"morally"时合并比值1.00，97.5%CI [0.92,1.07]；无时为0.90，[0.84,0.96]），且两类判断对这些头的依赖程度相当；无判断请求时同样依赖；在ETHICS上移除这些头消除37–39%的可接受/不可接受差异，随机十头仅1–3%。SAE特征主要响应"morally"而非句意内容。类别信息可从隐藏状态读出；移除承载该方向的向量使道德判定下降数个百分点，且在8个模型中7个降幅大于其他判定。

Conclusion: 模型的道德判断并非由专门的"道德回路"承载，而是复用了通用规范性评价回路；SAE特征层面捕捉的更多是词汇线索而非道德语义，而类别信息以线性方向形式存在并特异性地影响道德判断。作者公开了数据、代码、SAE字典与结果文件。

Abstract: Interpretability methods can locate the attention heads and sparse autoencoder (SAE) features that change the answer a language model gives to a moral prompt, e.g., whether "Stealing medicine for a dying child is morally" continues with "justified" or "wrong". Such a component could respond to moral content, to the judgment of conduct by any norm, or to the word "morally" in the prompt. We built 227 minimal pairs of short phrases, validated by five annotators, whose two versions differ in a moral standard, in another kind of norm (prudence, etiquette, aesthetics or evidence) or in a descriptive quality, and showed them to 14 open-weight models (Llama, Gemma and Qwen, 1B to 14B, base and instruction-tuned), with and without the word "morally". In every model, the heads that change the moral answer belong to an evaluation circuit the models use for every kind of normative judgment we tested: these heads carry the same share of the effect for moral as for other normative pairs (pooled ratio 1.00, 97.5% confidence interval [0.92, 1.07], with the word; 0.90, [0.84, 0.96], without it), and the models need them as much for both. The models need these heads also when nothing in the prompt asks for a judgment, and on 400 scenarios of the ETHICS benchmark, where removing them takes away 37 to 39% of the difference between acceptable and unacceptable scenarios, against 1 to 3% for ten random heads. The SAE features we located respond mostly to the word "morally" rather than to what the phrase is about. The models represent the category of a pair (the standard the annotators assigned to it): it can be read from their hidden states, and, in the eight models we tested, removing the direction that carries it weakens the moral verdict by a few percent, and more than the other verdicts in seven of them. We release the pairs, the ratings, the code, the trained SAE dictionaries and the result files.

</details>


### [190] [Natural Language Programming: Rethinking Language, Memory, and Cognition in the Era of Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23106)
*Giovanni Adorni,Daniele Grosso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出“自然语言编程（NLPg）”概念，将提示词视为外化目标、约束、验证标准与推理状态的动态认知产物，并基于分布式认知、延展心智等理论提出“对话式工作记忆”框架，把BTMA、SKGE、RMR等记忆架构映射到情节、语义、元认知三层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NLP把语言当作计算对象（分析、分类、翻译、生成），而LLM使自然语言成为可执行的认知媒介，用于组织推理、编排问题求解与调控人机混合工作流；现有研究缺乏对“提示即认知编程”这一视角的理论与认识论阐释。

Method: 概念性与理论建构研究：以分布式认知、延展心智、元认知与对话式学习理论为基础，提出NLPg作为传统NLP的理论与认识论延伸；将提示界定为动态认知产物，提出“对话式工作记忆”概念，并将既有记忆架构（BTMA、SKGE、RMR）映射到情节、语义、元认知层次以解释长程对话连贯性。

Result: 构建了NLPg的理论框架，阐明提示与对话状态作为临时外部记忆结构支撑推理连续性与反思性调控；验证了多层记忆架构（情节/语义/元认知）与长程对话一致性之间的对应关系。

Conclusion: 自然语言同时作为交流媒介、可执行规范、认知脚手架与分布式记忆架构，指向一种后符号、后界面的计算语言学研究范式与人机知识系统设计取向。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence is transforming the role of natural language in computational systems. In classical Natural Language Processing (NLP), language was treated as an object of computation: something to analyse, classify, translate, or generate. Large Language Models (LLMs) challenge this perspective, enabling natural language to function increasingly as an executable cognitive medium through which humans structure reasoning, orchestrate problem solving, and regulate hybrid human/AI workflows.
  This paper introduces Natural Language Programming (NLPg) as a theoretical and epistemological extension of traditional NLP. Rather than focusing on language understanding alone, NLPg conceptualises prompts as dynamic cognitive artefacts that externalise goals, constraints, assumptions, validation criteria, and intermediate reasoning states. Prompting is thus not merely a technical interaction technique, but a form of dialogic cognitive programming operating within distributed human/AI memory architectures.
  Building on theories of distributed cognition, extended mind, metacognition, and dialogic learning, the paper proposes the notion of Dialogic Working Memory, describing how prompts and conversational states function as temporary external memory structures supporting reasoning continuity and reflective regulation. We further map existing memory architectures (BTMA, SKGE, RMR) onto this framework, illustrating how episodic, semantic, and metacognitive layers support long-horizon conversational coherence.
  Finally, implications for computational linguistics and AI/mediated knowledge systems are discussed, suggesting a post symbolic and post interface paradigm in which natural language operates simultaneously as communication medium, executable specification, cognitive scaffold, and distributed memory architecture.

</details>


### [191] [What's in a Tree ? Hierarchical $n$-gram models as controlled approximators of language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23093)
*Aritra Das,Francesco Cagnetta,Darshil Doshi,Victor V. Albert,Maissam Barkeshli,Andrey Gromov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 tree-grams：通过二阶与三阶张量的层次化组合构建条件概率张量 p(x_n|x_{1:n-1}) 的自回归生成式 n-gram 模型；n 控制最大上下文窗口、秩超参 d 控制容量，形成可解释、可系统扩展的语言近似器族。实验表明其优于现有 n-gram 基线并可与 Transformer 语言模型竞争，并可作为可控生成器研究 μP 超参迁移、微调性能差距与涌现稀疏性（剪枝 95% 拓扑而无显著性能损失）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化模型常被用作研究语言的合成设定，但其与实际语言语料的相关性仍缺乏探索。作者希望构建一类既贴近真实语言统计、又具备可解释性与可控性的模型族，从而在受控条件下研究语言建模中的容量、上下文范围等关键因素。

Method: 提出 tree-grams：以二阶与三阶张量的层次化（树状）组合构造自回归条件概率张量 p(x_n|x_{1:n-1})；超参 n 限定最大上下文窗口，秩超参 d 控制模型容量，得到可解释且可系统性扩展的近似器族。方法包括：(1) 与 n-gram 基线及 Transformer 语言模型对比；(2) 将 tree-grams 作为生成模型，用中等 n 与 d 生成的较简单数据调优较窄模型，以检验 μP 下的超参迁移鲁棒性；(3) 分析熵与有效相关范围对微调后性能差距的影响；(4) 允许在候选拓扑间路由，观察路由稀疏性并据此剪枝。

Result: tree-grams 优于现有 n-gram 基线，并与基于 Transformer 的深度语言模型表现相当；在 μP 下，用中等 n、d 的 tree-grams 生成的简单数据调优较窄模型可使超参迁移更稳健；熵与相关性的有效范围可解释微调后的性能差距，原因在于 tree-grams 能显式控制视界与容量；在候选拓扑上路由时出现涌现稀疏性，集中式路由允许剪除多达 95% 的拓扑而性能无显著变化。

Conclusion: tree-grams 提供了一类可解释、可系统扩展的语言近似器，兼具竞争力与可控性，可作为生成式工具研究超参迁移、微调性能差距与路由稀疏性；其显式的视界与容量控制使其成为连接合成层次模型与真实语言语料的有用桥梁，并支持大幅拓扑剪枝。

Abstract: Hierarchical models have been proposed as synthetic settings for studying language. However, their relevance to realistic language corpora remains underexplored. In this work, we introduce tree-grams, autoregressive generative $n$-gram models that build the conditional probability tensor $p(x_n \mid x_{1:n-1})$ through a hierarchical composition of order-2 and order-3 tensors. $n$ controls the maximal context window, whereas a rank hyperparameter $d$ controls model capacity, yielding an interpretable and systematically scalable family of approximators of language. We show how tree-grams outperform existing $n$-gram baselines, while remaining competitive with deep transformer-based language models. We demonstrate the utility of such controlled approximations by using tree-grams as generative models. In particular, we show that hyperparameter transfer under $μ$P can be made more robust when narrower models are tuned on simpler data, generated by tree-grams with moderate $n$ and $d$. In addition, we show that entropy and effective range of correlations control performance gap after fine-tuning. This is because tree-grams allow us to explicitly control horizon and capacity. When allowed to route across candidate topologies, we observe emergent sparsity in tree-grams. Such concentrated routing permits pruning upto 95\% of topologies without significant change in performance.

</details>


### [192] [Token Commitment as a Control Surface for Steering Masked Diffusion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21669)
*Ambroise Aigueperse,Hugo Buurmeijer,Carmen Amo ALonso,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将掩码扩散模型中"token承诺（commitment）"（哪些预测被固定为最终结果、每步固定多少）视为一种扩散特有的控制面，提出反事实目标感知承诺与校准自适应预算两种机制，并在LLaDA与Dream-7B家族上验证其可实现单调的目标质量权衡，且可推广至自动代理指标目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时引导方法主要修改激活、logits或去噪轨迹，而把决定预测何时成为最终结果的承诺策略当作固定的解码细节，未加利用；作者认为该策略本身是可解释、可调控的生成控制面。

Method: 将承诺拆解为两个决策：1）承诺哪些token——反事实目标感知承诺，用模型置信度与留一token目标贡献的混合对未解决位置排序；2）每步承诺多少token——由目标进度与置信度驱动的校准自适应预算。仅在临时补全上计算标量分数，因此无需可复用的引导方向。

Result: 在LLaDA家族的语言与视觉语言设置、多目标、两种激活引导方法及无引导对照下，两机制均给出经验上单调的目标质量权衡；自适应调度在同等平均去噪成本下达到与无评分器随机调度相当的竞争性工作点。任务成功型代理目标（对话摘要的NLI蕴含、图像描述的CLIPScore）同样适用，并在Dream-7B上复现了一项目标与一项任务成功目标的行为。

Conclusion: 承诺——哪些临时预测被不可逆地固定以及固定速度——可作为一种可解释的控制面，用于在所测试条件下引导掩码扩散生成。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models repeatedly predict provisional tokens before a sampler decides which predictions become permanent. Existing inference-time steering methods primarily modify activations, logits, or denoising trajectories, while treating this commitment policy as a fixed decoding detail. We instead study token commitment as a diffusion-specific control surface and separate it into two decisions: which tokens to commit and how many tokens to commit at each step. For the first, we introduce counterfactual target-aware commitment, which ranks unresolved positions by a mixture of model confidence and leave-one-token-out target contribution. For the second, we introduce calibrated adaptive budgets driven by target progress and confidence. Across language and vision-language settings in the LLaDA family, multiple targets, two activation-steering methods, and an unsteered control, both mechanisms yield empirically monotonic target-quality trade-offs in every configuration we evaluate. Adaptive schedules also recover competitive operating points relative to scorer-free stochastic schedules matched in average denoising cost. Because both mechanisms require only a scalar score evaluated on a provisional completion, they apply unchanged when the target is an automatic proxy for task success rather than an output attribute: NLI entailment for dialogue summarization and CLIPScore for captioning, neither of which corresponds to a single reusable steering direction. We reproduce the behavior of both mechanisms on a second masked-diffusion family, Dream-7B, for one control target and one task-success target. These results characterize commitment--which provisional predictions become irreversible, and how rapidly--as an interpretable control surface for steering masked diffusion generation under the conditions we test.

</details>


### [193] [GauntletScore: Auditable Fact-Checking That Matches the Best Published Checkers on LLM-AggreFact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22027)
*Ernie Hobbs,Jim Hinson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GauntletScore 是一个基于多模型证据台账与固定贝叶斯规则的句子级事实支持校验引擎，在 LLM-AggreFact 测试集上取得 79.51 平衡准确率，追平榜单最佳（nimble-v4-max 的 79.45），并在 AggreFact-CNN 上以 76.45 显著领先，同时提供可追溯的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验生成句子是否被源文档支持是 AI 写作的标准防护措施，而现有最强的校验器多为单一模型；单模型不仅性能受限，还无法给出判定背后的证据。

Method: 构建 GauntletScore 证据引擎：少量规则与阈值在开发集上设定；向来自不同提供商的独立读者（reader）提出类型化问题，将每个回答记录为证据台账中的一次测量；用固定贝叶斯规则融合，仅当两个读者一致时才计为矛盾。全程冻结引擎、预注册停止计划，测试集仅运行一次。

Result: 在完整 LLM-AggreFact 测试集（11 个数据集、29,320 条主张）上达 79.51 平衡准确率，高于截至 2026-11-15 榜单所有公开平均值，与 nimble-v4-max（79.45）持平（95% 区间 78.47–80.53 覆盖之），并以 1.66/2.17 点优于其三个读者中的两个（区间 0.80–2.53、1.29–3.06）；剔除 AggreFact-CNN 后领先 0.90/1.32 点且区间仍高于零。在 AggreFact-CNN（长新闻对多句摘要）上得分 76.45，为榜单最佳，次优 70.1，六个最优系统为 63.9–67.8。

Conclusion: 多读者独立测量加一致性约束的融合方式可在不牺牲性能的前提下超越最强单模型，并在长文档—多句摘要类格式上优势明显；同时引擎保留每条判定的证据，提供单模型不具备的可解释性与可审计性。作者公开逐条预测与评分脚本。

Abstract: Checking whether a generated sentence is supported by its source document is a standard safeguard for AI-written text, and the strongest published checkers are single models. We describe GauntletScore, an evidence engine whose few rules and thresholds were set on the benchmark's development split. It puts typed questions to independent readers from different providers and records each answer as a measurement in an evidence ledger. It combines the measurements with a fixed Bayesian rule that counts a contradiction only when two readers agree. On the full LLM-AggreFact test split (29,320 claims across 11 datasets), following the leaderboard's approach, GauntletScore achieves 79.51 balanced accuracy, above every average published on the leaderboard as of 15 November 2026. That places it level with the best of them, nimble-v4-max (79.45 from its published dataset scores; the engine's 95% interval, 78.47 to 80.53, contains it). On the same claims it beats two of its three readers used alone (Readers A and B) by 1.66 and 2.17 points (95% intervals 0.80 to 2.53 and 1.29 to 3.06). Without AggreFact-CNN, described below, the leads are 0.90 and 1.32 and both intervals stay above zero. The engine also records the evidence behind every verdict, which a single model does not. Its largest margin is on full-length news articles checked against multi-sentence summaries (AggreFact-CNN), a format that resembles much regulated writing. There it scores 76.45, the best on the leaderboard, against 70.1 for the next best and 63.9 to 67.8 for the six systems with the highest averages. The test split was run once, on an engine frozen before the first test claim, under a stopping plan committed in advance. We release per-claim predictions and the scoring script.

</details>


### [194] [StitchRAG: Constructing Compact and Evidence-Preserving Contexts for RAG with Diffusion-Based Stitching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22766)
*Minsang Kim,Junpyo Lim,Heeje Cho,Seungjun Baek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StitchRAG 通过为每个检索段落自适应分配压缩率、以锚点（答案候选与查询关键词）筛选保留句子、再用文本扩散模型将句子拼接成连贯上下文，在五个开放域QA基准上优于多步与压缩基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长检索上下文带来计算开销，且噪声与无关内容反而损害LLM性能；因此需要在RAG中构建紧凑而信息密集的上下文。

Method: 将每个段落的压缩率设为自适应变量，构造以保留信息与计算成本为权衡的效用最大化问题求解；从段落中抽取答案候选与查询相关关键词作为锚点，在给定尺寸约束下收集包含锚点的句子；最后用文本扩散（text diffusion）将句子"缝合"为连贯自然的上下文。

Result: 在五个开放域QA基准上持续优于多种多步方法与压缩基线；消融实验验证了动态尺寸分配、锚点保留与扩散式拼接各自的有效性。

Conclusion: 按段落动态分配预算、以锚点保障关键信息、并借助生成式拼接恢复连贯性，是实现高保真紧凑RAG上下文的有效路径。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves factuality by grounding large language models (LLMs) in external evidence. However, long retrieved contexts incur computational overhead and can even hurt performance due to noisy or irrelevant content. We propose \textsc{StitchRAG}, a framework for constructing compact context in RAG. \textsc{StitchRAG} builds a faithful, information-dense context while dynamically compressing the retrieved passages. We adapt the size reduction ratio to each passage by formulating a utility maximization trading-off the retained information and computation costs. We then extract answer candidates and query-related keywords from passages as anchors and collect passage sentences containing anchors under the derived size constraint. Finally, we connect or stitch the collected sentences together using text diffusion to form a coherent and natural context. Experiments on five open-domain QA benchmarks show that \textsc{StitchRAG} consistently outperforms diverse multi-step and compression baselines. Extensive ablations show the effectiveness of dynamic size allocation, anchor preservation, and diffusion-based stitching.

</details>


### [195] [OfficePrism: Benchmarking Agents on Single-Domain Operations Across Enterprise Business Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22262)
*Hanfeng Liu,Zhenyu Luo,Dongchao Zhang,Zexi Liu,Zulong Chen,Jinxin Hu,Jia Xu,Zeyi Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OfficePrism 提出以 420 个跨 7 个业务职能的单域企业任务，在统一的 L1–L3 任务结构层级下评估"职能内能力"与"跨职能/系统协调成本"的可分离性，结果表明该基准能有效定位失败，且 L1 稳定最高、L2 普遍优于 L3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽泛的职场评分会把单一业务职能内的能力与跨职能、跨系统的协调额外成本混在一起，导致难以定位失败来源。因此需要一个将协调因素隔离、只考察单域能力的评测前提。

Method: 构建 420 个单域企业任务，覆盖 7 个业务职能，采用基准统一的 L1–L3 任务结构层级；每个任务在全新环境中运行，环境由共享的合成组织 + 单一职能专属域 + 类型化状态访问组成。流水线利用落地记录、业务规则与工具 schema 联合生成中文工单、隐藏的可执行参考见证与期望响应，并经自动过滤与人工标注。同一任务集通过 full-schema 与 search-and-dispatch 两种包进行评测。

Result: 在 124 个 model–harness–package 配置中，单例"自然语言+数据库"联合通过率为 22.6%–76.4%，中位数 67.9%。全部 124 个配置中 L1 最高；122 个配置中 L2 高于 L3。50 个 harness 与模型标签一致的配置下，search-and-dispatch 包在 30 个配置中得分更高、1 个持平、19 个更低。

Conclusion: OfficePrism 通过单域任务与共享层级化结构，将职能内能力与跨职能/系统协调成本解耦，使失败可定位；结果显示任务结构层级对性能有稳定的单调影响，而不同工具接口包的影响方向不一致，说明包设计本身是显著变量。

Abstract: Broad workplace scores can mix competence within one business function with the extra cost of coordinating across functions and systems, making failures hard to localize. OfficePrism evaluates this prerequisite through 420 single-domain enterprise tasks spanning seven business functions under shared, benchmark-defined L1--L3 task-structure levels. Each task runs in a fresh environment combining a shared synthetic organization with one function-specific domain and yped state access. Our pipeline uses grounded records, business rules, and tool schemas to jointly construct a Chinese-language ticket, a hidden executable reference witness, and an expected response, followed by automated filtering and human curation. The same task set is evaluated through full-schema and search-and-dispatch packages. Across 124 model--harness--package configurations, the per-case joint natural-language-and-database pass rate ranges from 22.6% to 76.4%, with a median of 67.9%. L1 is highest in all 124 configurations, and L2 exceeds L3 in 122. Among 50 configurations sharing the same harness and model labels across both packages, search scores higher in 30, ties in one, and scores lower in 19.

</details>


### [196] [Evaluating Semantic Insensitivity in Financial Sentiment Analysis: Implications for Sustainable Financial Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21653)
*Gabriela Cioboata,Teodor-Stefan Bildea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Agent驱动的语义反转攻击框架ASI，利用LLM多智能体流水线对金融新闻进行针对性词汇替换，使FinBERT在90.85%的合格样本上被欺骗；分析揭示模型过度依赖上下文关键词与句法模式而非真正的金融语义逻辑理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FinBERT等金融情感分析模型虽在基准上准确率高，但对细微对抗性文本修改脆弱，威胁自动交易、组合管理与风险评级的算法决策可靠性。现有攻击缺乏金融领域特定的语义操控能力。

Method: 构建Agent驱动的语义反转框架（ASI）：基于LLM的多智能体流水线先筛选符合条件的金融新闻句子，再执行领域特定的定向词汇替换，实现情感极性反转而保持文本自然性；以部署最广泛的FinBERT为攻击对象进行实证评估，并结合定性分析与token级调查分析其失效机理。

Result: 攻击在90.85%的合格案例中成功欺骗FinBERT；模型在金融绩效指标被根本性反转后仍常维持原情感预测。token级与定性分析显示FinBERT过度依赖上下文关键词和句法模式，缺乏对金融语义的真实逻辑理解。

Conclusion: 金融情感模型存在严重的语义逻辑盲区，亟需发展逻辑感知、鲁棒的金融NLP模型，以保障自动化决策系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Financial sentiment analysis is critical for automated trading, portfolio management, and risk assessment, heavily relying on models like FinBERT. Despite high benchmark accuracy, these models remain vulnerable to subtle adversarial text modifications, threatening the integrity of algorithmic decision-making. In this paper, we propose Agent-driven Semantic Inversion (ASI), a novel adversarial attack framework targeting financial sentiment models through domain-specific semantic manipulation. Utilizing an LLM-powered multi-agent pipeline, our approach identifies eligible financial news sentences and applies targeted lexical substitutions. We empirically evaluate ASI against FinBERT, one of the most widely deployed financial sentiment models. Our results reveal a striking vulnerability: the attack successfully deceived FinBERT in 90.85% of eligible cases, with the model frequently maintaining its original sentiment prediction despite the fundamental inversion of financial performance indicators. Through qualitative analysis and token-level investigation, we demonstrate that FinBERT critically over-relies on contextual keywords and syntactic patterns rather than true logical comprehension of financial semantics. Our findings highlight the urgent need for logic-aware, robust financial NLP models to ensure the reliability of automated decision-making systems.

</details>


### [197] [From Preference Data to Personalization: Tracing Sycophancy in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21650)
*Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Yasha Sheynin,Shivam Singhal,Arjun Subramonian,Ankur Samanta,Julian Michael,Smitha Milli,Maximilian Nickel,Claudia Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将谄媚重新定义为“不当同意”（在语境需要纠正、限定或抵抗时仍认可用户主张/偏好/自我评价），提出有效性感知的评审器与3,298条标注探针集SycoDrift，并系统分析人类偏好数据、自动评审规范、上下文个性化三类“同意压力”来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多以“与用户一致/同意”度量谄媚，但同意在用户主张正确、偏好真实或体验需被认可时是恰当的。因此需要区分恰当同意与不当同意，并厘清哪些训练或推理环节会放大不当同意。

Method: 构建有效性感知的谄媚评审器；发布SycoDrift——面向人设、含3,298条带有效性标注的探针，并对用户开放度与风险代价做受控标注。基于该框架分析三类同意压力：(1)人类偏好数据中的验证性偏好；(2)选定值单模型实验中强调型评审量表与抑制型量表对比；(3)14个模型上的离策略评估，检验推理时个性化（尤其紧邻查询匹配的用户画像）的影响。

Result: 人类数据（Chatbot Arena、HH-RLHF、Community Alignment）中对验证性回答的偏好较弱，且集中于同意本就常恰当的场合；强调型评审量表相对抑制型量表产生一致但温和的提升，提示训练期放大的可行机制；14个模型的离策略评估显示推理时个性化加剧不当同意，匹配用户画像置于查询前影响最大。

Conclusion: 谄媚应作为“不当同意”而非“同意”来度量；偏好数据、评审规范与个性化均带有一致但可辨识的同意压力，其中推理时个性化风险最突出，需以有效性感知的评测加以监控。

Abstract: Sycophancy in large language models is often measured as agreement with the user, yet agreement can be appropriate when the user's claim is valid, their preference is genuine, or their experience warrants acknowledgment. We study sycophancy as inappropriate agreement: validating a claim, preference, or self-assessment when the context calls for correction, qualification, or resistance. We introduce a validity-aware sycophancy grader and SycoDrift, a persona-targeted evaluation set of 3,298 validity-annotated probes with controlled annotations for user openness and stakes. We use this framework to analyze three sources of agreement pressure: human preference data, automated judge specification, and in-context personalization. First, preference for validating responses in human datasets (Chatbot Arena, HH-RLHF, Community Alignment) is weak and concentrated where agreement is often appropriate. Second, in a selected-value, single-model experiment, emphasized judge rubrics produce consistent but modest increases relative to paired suppressed rubrics, demonstrating a plausible mechanism for training-time amplification. Third, in off-policy evaluations across 14 models, inference-time personalization increases inappropriate agreement, with the largest effect from matched user profiles placed directly before the query.

</details>


### [198] [Literary Language Mashup: Curating Fictions with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21649)
*Manzanarez Gerardo Aleman,Monroy Raul,Garcia Flores Jorge,Calvo Hiram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出用LLM作为文学小说评估者（GrAImes框架），通过对比LLM与人类评审者对西班牙语微小说及英/西短篇小说的评分，检验二者一致性；结果显示LLM不能替代人类评审（Krippendorff's α<0.66），但可作为编辑质量的初步辅助工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工评估文学作品成本高、可扩展性差，需要探索LLM作为评估者的可行性，并厘清其与人类评判视角的一致性与分歧程度。

Method: 在GrAImes框架内以LLM作为评估者，对人工撰写与AI生成的西班牙语微小说，以及西班牙语和英语短篇小说进行评分；对照对象为5位文学博士教授和16位文学爱好者；通过比较LLM与人类评分的一致性（Krippendorff's α）来评估对齐程度。

Result: LLM评分与人类评审的一致性有限（Krippendorff's α<0.66），表明其无法替代人类评判者；但能提供关于文本编辑质量的初步视角，具备辅助价值。

Conclusion: LLM尚不能取代人类作为文学评估者，但可作为补充性资源用于文学评估的初步筛选；研究同时揭示了该方法学局限与潜力，为AI在文学中角色的讨论提供实证依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an alternative approach to human evaluation of literary fictions by employing LLMs themselves as evaluators within the GrAImes framework. We apply this methodology to assess human-written and AI-generated microfictions in Spanish, to five PhD professors in literature and sixteen literary enthusiasts, and to short stories in both Spanish and English. By comparing the evaluations performed by LLMs with those of human judges, we examine the degree of alignment and divergence between both perspectives, thereby assessing the feasibility of LLMs as auxiliary literary evaluators. Our analysis focuses on the alignment of responses from LLMs with those of human evaluators, providing insights into the potential of LLMs in literary assessment. The conducted experiments reveal that while LLMs cannot be regarded as substitutes for human judges in the evaluation of literary microfictions and short stories, with a Krippendorff's alpha reliability coefficient less than 0.66, they can serve as a valuable tool that offers an initial perspective on the editorial quality of the texts in question. Overall, this study contributes to the ongoing discourse on the role of artificial intelligence in literature, underlining both its methodological constraints and its potential as a complementary resource for literary evaluation.

</details>


### [199] [Can an LLM dream? Testing the overfitted brain hypothesis on an agent with text memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21648)
*Eric Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在基于大模型的智能体（Claude Opus 5.5）上检验 Hoel (2021) 的"梦境扭曲重放经验以防过拟合"假说，发现转录文本损坏在 30 次运行中均未提升知识迁移能力，细节替换损害回忆（76%→60%），随机文本被模型识别并排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验 Hoel (2021) 提出的假说：梦通过扭曲被重放的经验，使大脑不对近期事件过拟合。作者希望在一个可控的 LLM 智能体上对该假说做因果检验，通过人为污染夜间记忆重写的输入来观察是否出现类似"防过拟合"的收益。

Method: 构建一个将所学内容存入文本记忆、每晚依据当日对话转录重写记忆的 LLM 智能体（Claude Opus 5.5），使用 LongMemEval 构建十天的模拟对话。每晚重写前对 5%/10%/20%/40% 的话轮施加两类损坏：(1) 细节损坏——改动一两个细节为合理的错误值，或用同一说话人更早一天的话轮替换；(2) 随机文本损坏——一半被选话轮替换为《白鲸》段落，另一半插入《白鲸》词语。记忆容量上限按人类自由回忆数据设定，从 19,500 tokens 逐夜升至 117,000 tokens，从而强制遗忘。共十种条件、三种对话历史、30 次运行，测量新任务迁移与回忆表现。

Result: 损坏在任何一次运行中都未帮助智能体将早前对话所学迁移到新任务。40% 话轮细节替换使回忆率从 76% 降至 60%；随机文本对回忆无一致影响（40% 条件下配对差异为 0 ± 2 点），因为模型识别其为损坏内容而未写入记忆。被替换的细节即使与其他话轮中的正确值并存，仍被当作事实存储，相关问题的正确率为 40%，无损坏时为 75%。

Conclusion: 预训练模型能够区分损坏文本与真实事件，因此当前设置并非对 Hoel 假说的公平检验。合理的检验应使用难以做出此种区分的"学习者"，例如在损坏日数据上微调的模型，或运行于虚构世界中的智能体。

Abstract: Hoel (2021) proposed that dreams distort replayed experience so that the brain does not overfit to recent events. We test this hypothesis on an LLM agent (Claude Opus 5.5) that stores what it learns in a text memory and rewrites that memory each night from the day's conversation transcript. The agent goes through ten simulated days of conversation built from LongMemEval. We corrupt 5, 10, 20 or 40 percent of the transcript's turns before each nightly rewrite in one of two ways. Detail corruption changes one or two details in a turn to plausible wrong ones, or replaces the turn with one by the same speaker from an earlier day. Random-text corruption replaces half of the selected turns with a passage of Moby-Dick and inserts Moby-Dick words into the other half. A size limit on the memory, rising from 19,500 to 117,000 tokens over ten nights, set from human free-recall data, forces it to forget. We learned that corruption did not help the agent apply what it learned in earlier conversations to new tasks in any of 30 runs (ten conditions, three different conversation histories). Replacing details in 40 percent of turns lowered recall from 76 to 60 percent. Random text had no consistent effect on recall, with a paired difference of 0 $\pm$ 2 points at 40 percent, because the model recognized it as corrupted and left it out of memory. Replaced details were stored as fact even when another turn held the correct value, and the agent answered 40 percent of those questions correctly, compared with 75 percent without corruption. These results suggest that a pretrained model can separate corrupted text from real events, so a fair test needs a learner that cannot make this separation as easily, such as a model fine-tuned on the corrupted days or an agent whose days take place in an invented world.

</details>


### [200] [TUSI: A Citation-Grounded Graph-Based Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21823)
*Marzieh Karimi,Mehran Safayani,Alireza Basiri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出波斯语法律RAG评测基准TUSI-Bench，含开放问答与选择题两类任务，并给出基于法律知识图谱的检索基线，图谱关系可将召回率提升至多8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波斯语法律领域的RAG系统评测困难：现有基准或由法条合成生成、缺乏真实信息需求，或来自非正式法律问答、未与权威法律文本显式关联，既缺乏真实性与依据支撑，也难以覆盖法律推理，无法严谨评估“有据可依”的法律推理能力。

Method: 构建多层法律知识框架，收录7,261条法律条文，整合成文法、法律评注、学理著述与最高法院统一裁判；据此构建连接条文与概念的法律知识图谱以支持结构感知检索。基准包含两项互补任务：TUSI-QA（1,999道开放式问题，要求带明确法律引用的有据回答）与TUSI-Bar（约500道四选项选择题，源自伊朗律师资格考试题）。并提出基于图谱关系的检索基线。

Result: 实验表明，引入图谱关系相比标准基线可将检索召回率提升最多8个百分点，说明结构化法律表示对RAG系统具有重要价值。

Conclusion: TUSI-Bench为波斯语法律领域的有据法律推理评测提供了标准化资源，并为结构化法律智能的后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Evaluating retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in Persian law remains challenging because benchmarks lack realism, grounding, and coverage of legal reasoning. Existing resources are either synthetically generated from statutory texts, lacking natural information needs, or derived from informal legal question--answer sources without explicit linkage to authoritative legal documents, making them unsuitable for rigorous evaluation of grounded legal reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce TUSI-Bench, a structured Persian legal benchmark designed for the evaluation of RAG-based systems. TUSI-Bench is built upon a multi-layered legal knowledge framework containing 7,261 legal articles and integrating codified statutes, legal commentaries, doctrinal writings, and Supreme Court unification rulings. We also construct a legal knowledge graph linking articles and concepts for structure-aware retrieval. Based on this foundation, TUSI-Bench provides two complementary evaluation tasks: (i) TUSI-QA, an open-ended question answering benchmark containing 1,999 questions that requires grounded responses with explicit legal citations, and (ii) TUSI-Bar, a multiple-choice benchmark containing approximately 500 four-option questions derived from Iranian Bar examination questions. Furthermore, we propose a graph-based retrieval baseline that leverages relational structure within the legal knowledge graph. Experimental results demonstrate that incorporating graph-based relations improves retrieval recall by up to 8 percentage points compared to standard baselines, highlighting the importance of structured legal representations for RAG systems. The proposed benchmark provides a standardized resource for evaluating grounded legal reasoning in Persian and enables future research on structured legal intelligence.

</details>


### [201] [RICoT: Recurrent Interleaved Chain-of-Thought for Page Stream Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22382)
*Prashant Khatri,Bhavana Malagavalli,Saiful Haq,Niyati Chhaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将页面流分割(PSS)重构为大模型对整条流的顺序处理过程，提出RICoT提示框架，并发布FinancePSS-205基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法仍把PSS视为对单个或少数相邻页面的边界分类，且商业文档分割API不公开方法，缺乏整体流式视角与公开评测基准。

Method: RICoT在滑动窗口内逐页推理并在移动前提交边界决策，跨窗口传递已读内容的精简记录以控制单次调用成本；同时构建205条真实脱敏金融页面流基准FinancePSS-205。

Result: 在FinancePSS-205上，RICoT在三种开源权重LLM上均匹配或优于相同主干的成对与窗口提示；TABME测试子集上GLM-5达文档F1 0.847、边界F1 0.915，超过已发表零样本结果；2B学生模型两种模式下表现相当，与最强提示模型文档F1差距在0.05以内。

Conclusion: 顺序化、带记忆的提示方式优于局部边界分类范式，且小模型通过蒸馏可逼近强提示模型，为PSS提供可复用框架与公开基准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have renewed interest in Page Stream Segmentation (PSS), splitting a multi-page file into its constituent documents. Yet published LLM methods still frame the task as boundary classification over one or a few neighboring pages, and commercial document-splitting APIs do not disclose their methods. We reimagine PSS for LLMs as a sequential process over the whole stream and propose RICoT (Recurrent Interleaved Chain-of-Thought), a prompting framework that works through the pages as a reader would: within a sliding window it reasons about each page and commits its boundary decision before moving on, and across windows it carries forward a compact record of what it has read, keeping per-call cost bounded. We also introduce FinancePSS-205, a benchmark of 205 real-world, de-identified financial page streams, to be released publicly. On FinancePSS-205, RICoT matches or outperforms pairwise and windowed prompting with the same backbone for each of three open-weight LLMs; on a TABME test subset, RICoT with GLM-5 reaches document F1 0.847 and boundary F1 0.915, above published zero-shot results. A 2B-parameter student, trained on a larger teacher's decisions with and without the page-level reasoning, performs comparably in both modes and comes within 0.05 document F1 of the strongest prompted model on FinancePSS-205.

</details>


### [202] [When Do Typed Decision Models Work in Transportation? Evidence from Travel Choice and Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22926)
*Hongyang Zhang,Boyuan Deng,Siyuan Feng,Kai Chen,Junbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了"类型化决策模型"（直接输出动作选项及选项级概率分布、不生成长文本）在交通领域两类任务中的有效性、鲁棒性与效率，并与传统交通方法、强化学习算法及四款对话式大模型对比，结果显示其潜力与局限并存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通问题通常具有预定义备选方案集合，类型化决策模型因推理成本低、延迟小且规避冗长文本生成，成为有吸引力的候选方案，但其在该场景下的实际表现尚缺乏系统验证。

Method: 选取两类代表性交通任务：个体出行方式选择预测与序贯交通信号控制。以TypeSafe Jev与GPT-6 Luna Decisions为被测对象，对照传统交通方法、强化学习算法和四类基于对话的大语言模型；在Swissmetro数据集上考察预测精度及对输入表示与问题措辞的敏感性，并在闭环信号控制实验中评测性能与推理延迟、计算开销。

Result: 出行方式选择任务中，类型化模型的预测精度低于传统离散选择模型及最强对话式基线，且性能随输入表示与问题框架显著波动；闭环信号控制中，在部分场景可媲美成熟控制器，但在更复杂路网条件下性能退化；同时其推理延迟与计算成本低于所评估的对话式大模型。

Conclusion: 类型化决策模型具备高效零样本交通决策的潜力，但存在精度与鲁棒性短板；其有效应用高度依赖输入设计、任务特性与稳健性评估。

Abstract: Typed decision models combine natural-language understanding with a structured interface that returns a selected action and an option-level probability distribution without generating lengthy textual responses. Their low inference cost and response latency make them attractive for transportation problems with predefined alternatives. This paper investigates their effectiveness, robustness, and efficiency in two representative transportation tasks: individual travel-mode choice prediction and sequential traffic signal control. We systematically evaluate TypeSafe Jev and GPT-6 Luna Decisions against conventional transportation methods, reinforcement learning algorithms, and four chat-based large language models. Experiments on the Swissmetro dataset show that typed decision models achieve lower prediction accuracy than traditional discrete choice models and the strongest chat-based baseline, while their performance varies substantially with input representation and question framing. In closed-loop traffic signal control experiments, typed decision models demonstrate competitive performance against established controllers in several scenarios, but exhibit performance degradation under more challenging network conditions. Furthermore, the typed models achieve lower inference latency and computational cost than the evaluated chat-based large language models, highlighting their potential for efficient, zero-shot transportation decision-making. Overall, these findings demonstrate both the potential and limitations of typed decision models, emphasizing the importance of input design, task characteristics, and robustness evaluation for their effective application in transportation systems.

</details>


### [203] [Visibility Graph Representations of Word Embeddings for Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22685)
*Elif Dorukbaşı,İlker Türker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将文档词向量平均序列视为数值序列，构建自然可见图（NVG）作为中间表示，在受限词表预算下用于新闻主题分类，并在AG News上取得优于BERT基线的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本分类依赖能显式区分类别的表示，而受限（小规模）词表会改变哪种表示最有效；作者希望检验图结构表示在词表预算受限条件下是否具有竞争力。

Method: 词嵌入平均为定长文档向量→按坐标顺序读为数值序列→构建自然可见图；图以有序节点度向量或邻接矩阵两种形式输入分类器。AG News四分类，卡方选择保留100–1500词；对比CBOW、skip-gram、GloVe、FastText、BERT的直接嵌入表示；全连接分类器与卷积分类器两种设置。

Result: 全连接分类器下，度表示在700词起最优，1000词时最高91.24%，1500词时比BERT基线高0.29个百分点；卷积设置下邻接矩阵输入在1000词达86.16%，优于最强静态嵌入的83.12%。

Conclusion: 一旦所选词表覆盖率足够，可见图衍生特征具有竞争力；作者讨论图压缩、坐标排序与分类器选择的作用，并强调需控制实验以确认这些小幅差异的可靠性。

Abstract: Text classifiers depend on representations that expose the distinctions between classes in a form the learning model can use, and a restricted vocabulary changes which representation is most effective.
